*Adrian M. Escobar Ruiz,Miguel E. Gómez Quintanar,Lidia Jiménez-Lara,Jaume Llibre*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了对称四次变形的Tempesta-Turbiner-Winternitz系统，该系统的反平方项产生不可穿透势垒将构型空间分割为不变区域。通过Poincaré非可积性准则证明了对于足够小的非零四次耦合，不存在第二个独立C^2首次积分；有限耦合下的数值计算展示了不变环面破裂和混沌层增长，且奇异势垒抑制了相空间输运但未恢复可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究介于最大超可积Smorodinsky-Winternitz振子和四次耦合Contopoulos振子之间的插值系统的可积性，特别关注反平方势垒对相空间结构和可积性的影响。

Method: 采用Poincaré非可积性准则（通过共振平均化构造相锁定周期轨道，证明其约化Poincaré映射不存在单位特征乘子），结合数值方法（Poincaré截面和有限时间Lyapunov映射）分析系统动力学。

Result: 对于足够小的非零κ，不存在第二个独立的C^2首次积分（局部结果）；有限耦合下，随着能量和四次耦合增加，不变曲线破裂、混沌层增长；与无势垒Contopoulos极限对比，TTW系统的奇异势垒抑制了相空间输运但未恢复可积性。

Conclusion: 该四次变形TTW系统在微扰意义下是不可积的，反平方势垒虽能抑制混沌扩散但不足以恢复可积性，揭示了奇异势垒与非可积动力学之间的复杂关系。

Abstract: We study a symmetric quartic deformation of the classical ($k=1$) Tempesta-Turbiner-Winternitz system, $H=\frac{1}{2}\left(P_X^2+P_Y^2\right)+X^2+Y^2 +\fracγ{X^2}+\fracγ{Y^2} +κ\,X^2Y^2 $, which interpolates between the maximally superintegrable Smorodinsky-Winternitz oscillator and the quartically coupled Contopoulos oscillator. For $γ>0$, the inverse-square terms generate impenetrable walls at ($X=0$) and ($Y=0$), splitting configuration space into invariant sectors. We show that, for sufficiently small nonzero $κ$, resonant averaging produces a phase-locked periodic orbit whose fixed-energy reduced Poincaré map has no unit characteristic multiplier. The Poincaré's nonintegrability criterion then excludes a second independent $C^1$ first integral in any invariant neighbourhood of this orbit. The result is local in phase space and perturbative in $κ$. At finite coupling, Poincaré sections and finite-time Lyapunov maps show the breakup of invariant curves and the growth of chaotic layers with increasing energy and quartic coupling. Comparison with the barrier-free Contopoulos limit shows that the singular TTW walls suppress phase-space transport without restoring integrability.

</details>


### [101] [Localized oscillation of an Euler--Bernoulli beam with time-varying parameters on a visco-elastic foundation: asymptotics, adiabatic invariant, and equivalent Hamiltonian system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22444)
*Ekaterina V. Shishkina,Serge N. Gavrilov,Yulia A. Mochalova*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究欧拉-伯努利梁在粘弹性地基上与阻尼离散振子耦合的局部振荡问题，针对慢变参数系统，在保守情况下通过渐近分析、绝热不变量和哈密顿系统三种方法得到一致的振幅公式，耗散情况下仅用渐近方法获得振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究慢变参数下弹性梁与离散振子耦合系统的局部振荡行为，建立振幅演化的理论框架。

Method: 保守情况采用三种解析方法：渐近分析、基于俘获波作用绝热不变量的方法、等效哈密顿系统方法；耗散情况仅采用渐近分析方法。

Result: 保守情况下三种方法得到一致的振幅公式；耗散情况下通过渐近方法获得了振幅表达式。

Conclusion: 不同分析方法在保守情况下结果一致，验证了振幅公式的正确性；渐近方法适用于耗散情况下的振幅求解。

Abstract: We consider localized oscillation of an Euler--Bernoulli beam on a visco-elastic foundation coupled to a damped discrete oscillator. All parameters of the system independently vary in time in a slow manner. For the conservative case, we use three various analytic approaches. Namely, these are asymptotics, the method based on the adiabatic invariance of the action of a trapped wave, and the consideration of the equivalent Hamiltonian system. All approaches result in the same formula for the amplitude of oscillation. In the dissipative case, we obtain the amplitude of oscillation only utilizing the asymptotic approach.

</details>


### [102] [Minimum Virtual Proper Time and Finite Mass--Charge Matching in QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22666)
*Mustafa Bakr*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限固有时版本的QED，其中每个Schwinger固有时积分具有物理下限s₀=Λ⁻²，通过规范协变生成泛函定义，证明了Ward-Takahashi恒等式精确成立，真空极化有限且严格横向，并验证了Osterwalder-Schrader反射正性及单圈幺正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准QED中存在紫外发散问题，需要重整化处理。作者希望构建一种有限固有时版本的QED，通过引入物理的固有时积分下限（即紫外截断Λ），使理论在有限能量尺度下自然获得紫外有限性，同时保持规范协变性和幺正性。

Method: (1) 构造规范协变的有限固有时生成泛函，所有Schwinger固有时积分保留物理下限s₀=Λ⁻²；(2) 闭费米子圈由规范协变热核行列式定义，开费米子线通过同一有限固有时开线核的泛函微分得到；(3) 利用规范协变性推导Ward-Takahashi恒等式；(4) 证明自由有限固有时传播子满足Osterwalder-Schrader反射正性，并计算Källén-Lehmann谱函数；(5) 通过世界线约束Q≤0实现固定阶欧氏紫外有限性。

Result: (1) 真空极化有限且严格横向；(2) 单圈壳上质量关系给出0.482 MeV（选择Λ=13 TeV）；(3) 自由传播子满足OS正性，Källén-Lehmann谱函数为正，无额外鬼态；(4) 单圈壳上留数Z₂>0，确认费米子扇区幺正性；(5) 单圈顶点修正给出有限反常磁矩，产生标准QED中不存在的O(m²/Λ²)可计算预言。

Conclusion: 该有限固有时QED框架在保持规范对称性和幺正性的前提下，实现了理论的紫外有限性，并给出了标准QED所不具有的新物理预言（如O(m²/Λ²)阶的可计算效应），为构建有限量子场论提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We formulate a finite-proper-time version of QED defined by a gauge-covariant generating functional in which every Schwinger proper-time integral has a physical lower endpoint $s_0=Λ^{-2}$ that is not removed. Closed fermion loops are defined by the gauge-covariant heat-kernel determinant, and open fermion lines are obtained by functional differentiation of the same finite-proper-time open-line kernel. The Ward--Takahashi identity follows exactly from gauge covariance to all orders. The vacuum polarisation is finite and exactly transverse. The on-shell mass relation gives 0.482$$ MeV for an illustrative choice $Λ=13\$ TeV at one loop. The free finite-proper-time propagators are proved to satisfy Osterwalder--Schrader reflection positivity, with a positive Kallen--Lehmann spectral function and no additional ghost states. The on-shell residue $Z_2>0$ at one loop confirms unitarity of the fermion sector, and the perturbative spectral function of the dressed propagator is positive. A worldline bound $Q\le0$ gives fixed-order Euclidean UV finiteness. The one-loop vertex correction gives a finite anomalous magnetic moment, a calculable $O(m^2/Λ^2)$ prediction absent in standard QED.

</details>


### [103] [Why Hadamard states?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22767)
*Eleanor March*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文批判性地审视了弯曲时空中量子场论里Hadamard条件的现有动机，并论证了将其理解为在足够大的可观测量空间上存在良定义算子积的充要条件这一替代性辩护，从而澄清了Hadamard条件与等效原理、Wick多项式及应力能算符期望值良定义性之间的关系，以及Hadamard态为何是"类真空的"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Hadamard条件在弯曲时空量子场论中扮演核心角色（从Wick多项式重整化到Hawking温度推导），但其哲学和基础性依据仍然模糊不清，作者旨在澄清其基础地位。

Method: 首先批判性地考察文献中已有的Hadamard条件动机，然后论证一个替代性辩护：将Hadamard条件理解为在足够大的量子场可观测量空间上存在满足一系列附加条件的良定义算子积的充要条件（证明此前讨论过的结果的逆命题）。

Result: Hadamard条件不仅是必要的，而且是充分的——它保证了足够大的可观测量空间上存在良定义的算子积，从而为Wick多项式和应力能算符期望值等物理量的良定义性提供了基础。

Conclusion: Hadamard条件的最佳理解是作为算子积存在性的充要条件，这厘清了它与等效原理的关系，并解释了为何Hadamard态可被视为"类真空的"。

Abstract: In quantum field theory on curved spacetime, and in locally-covariant quantum field theory, the Hadamard condition is often presented as a necessary condition on 'physically reasonable' states of the quantum field, and plays a central role in many theoretical and foundational applications - ranging from proofs of the renormalizability of Wick polynomials to derivations of the Hawking temperature. Yet despite this, the philosophical and foundational underpinnings of the Hadamard condition remain murky. I critically discuss existing motivations for the Hadamard condition in the literature, before arguing in favour of an alternative justification for the Hadamard condition, according to which it is best understood as a necessary and sufficient condition for the existence of a well-defined operator product on a sufficiently large space of observables of the quantum field, satisfying a variety of further conditions (thus proving a converse to a result which was already discussed in this context). This clarifies the role and status of the Hadamard condition, including its relationship to the equivalence principle, to well-definedness of physical quantities such as Wick polynomials and the expectation value of the stress-energy operator, and the sense in which Hadamard states are 'vacuum-like'.

</details>


### [104] [Compatible Lie conformal bialgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22941)
*Lamei Yuan,Jiansen Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了相容李共形双代数，作为相容李双代数的共形类比。该结构由同一C[∂]-模上的两个相容李共形括号和两个相容共形余括号组成，其任意同时线性组合仍是李共形双代数。发展了相容李共形代数的表示与匹配对，引入了相容李共形余代数，并在有限情形下通过共形对偶建立了对偶性。对于有限秩自由C[∂]-模，证明了相容李共形双代数、标准相容共形Manin三元组与匹配对的等价性。在余边界情形，刻画了决定相容李共形双代数的张量r：要求r的对称部分对两个括号均不变，且满足三个共形Yang-Baxter条件（前两个分别对应两个括号，第三个为相容共形Yang-Baxter条件）。引入相容共形经典Yang-Baxter方程（CYBE），证明其解满足这三个条件但反之不成立。一个例子表明前两个条件推不出第三个；另一个例子表明三个条件同时成立时r仍可能不是相容共形CYBE的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为李共形代数理论引入相容结构，推广相容李双代数的概念到共形情形，建立其代数框架与分类理论。

Method: 通过定义两个相容李共形括号与两个相容共形余括号，建立表示论、匹配对、对偶理论；利用共形Manin三元组和匹配对证明等价性；通过张量r的对称性条件和三类共形Yang-Baxter条件刻画余边界情形。

Result: 证明了有限秩自由情形下相容李共形双代数、标准相容共形Manin三元组与匹配对的等价性；给出了余边界情形张量r的刻画条件（对称性不变性+三个共形Yang-Baxter条件）；揭示了相容共形CYBE解与这些条件之间的蕴含关系（单向而非等价），并给出了反例。

Conclusion: 建立了相容李共形双代数的完整理论框架，明确了余边界情形下各条件之间的逻辑关系，并通过反例揭示了条件之间的独立性。

Abstract: We introduce and study compatible Lie conformal bialgebras as conformal counterparts of compatible Lie bialgebras. Such a structure consists of two compatible Lie conformal brackets and two compatible conformal cobrackets on the same $\C[\partial]$-module, and every simultaneous linear combination of them is again a Lie conformal bialgebra. We develop representations and matched pairs for compatible Lie conformal algebras, introduce compatible Lie conformal coalgebras, and establish their duality through the conformal dual in the finite case. For $\C[\p]$-modules that are free of finite rank, we prove the equivalence among compatible Lie conformal bialgebras, standard compatible conformal Manin triples and matched pairs. In the coboundary case, we characterize the tensors $r$ that determine compatible Lie conformal bialgebras. The characterization requires the symmetric part of $r$ to be invariant with respect to both brackets and three conformal Yang--Baxter conditions to hold. The first two conditions correspond to the two brackets separately, whereas the third is the compatible conformal Yang--Baxter condition. For comparison, we introduce the compatible conformal classical Yang--Baxter equation (CYBE) and show that each of its solutions satisfies these three conditions, while the converse fails. One explicit example shows that the first two conditions do not imply the third. Another shows that all three conformal Yang-Baxter conditions may hold even though $r$ is not a solution of compatible conformal CYBE.

</details>


### [105] [Statistical Physics of Planar Carroll Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23039)
*Fei Huang,Loïc Marsot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究平面Carroll系统的统计物理，发现由于平面Carroll代数的中心扩展，考虑角动量后可定义有意义的统计物理，并推导了均匀旋转圆盘上粒子的配分函数，得出旋转对热平衡不可避免、熵与面积对数相关、压强服从二维理想气体定律等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，在一般维度下，庞加莱统计物理的Carroll极限通常不收敛；作者试图证明在二维平面中，借助Carroll代数的中心扩展并考虑角动量，可以建立有意义的平面Carroll统计物理。

Method: 使用Souriau几何热力学方法，计算均匀旋转圆盘上粒子的配分函数；从对称性出发推导热力学量，并推导相应的有效哈密顿量。

Result: 旋转对平面Carroll系统的热平衡是不可避免的，其中一个中心荷决定旋转方向；熵随圆盘面积对数增长；压强服从二维理想气体定律。

Conclusion: 平面Carroll系统存在定义良好的统计物理，所有结果均可由对称性推导得出，且存在相应的有效哈密顿量。

Abstract: In this article, we define and study the statistical physics of planar Carrollian systems. While it has been shown recently that, for general dimensions, the Carroll limit of Poincaré statistical physics typically does not converge, we show that thanks to the central extensions of the Carroll algebra in the plane, and by considering systems with angular momentum, there exists a well defined notion of planar Carrollian statistical physics.
  Using Souriau's geometric thermodynamics, we compute the partition function for particles on a uniformly rotating disc, and show that rotation is inevitable for thermal equilibrium of planar Carroll systems, with one of the central charges determining the direction of rotation. We derive thermodynamic quantities in particular entropy, which scales logarithmically with the disc area, and pressure, which follows the two-dimensional ideal gas law. Though all results are obtained from symmetry considerations, we also derive the corresponding effective Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [106] [Jacobi exceptional orthogonal polynomials for extended Scarf I potentials with position-dependent mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23264)
*Christiane Quesne*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过点正则变换方法，将位置依赖质量背景下的Scarf I势问题映射为常质量Scarf I势问题，进而利用逆变换构造出与X_m-Jacobi例外正交多项式相关的精确可解有理扩展，并证明了该系统在变形超对称框架下具有变形形状不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决位置依赖质量背景下Scarf I势的精确可解性问题，并探索其与例外正交多项式的关联以及变形超对称性质。

Method: 采用点正则变换将变质量Schrödinger方程映射为常质量方程，再通过逆变换构造有理扩展势；利用变形超对称框架分析形状不变性。

Result: 获得了与X_m-Jacobi例外正交多项式（I、II、III型）相关的精确可解有理扩展势；证明了变质量Scarf I势及其I、II型扩展势均具有变形形状不变性。

Conclusion: 点正则变换是求解变质量背景下Scarf I势的有效工具，得到的扩展势与例外正交多项式自然关联，且变形超对称框架可推广至更一般的变质量系统。

Abstract: We show that the Scarf I potential problem in a position-dependent mass background of the type $m(α;x) = (1 + α\sin x)^{-2}$, $0<α<1$, can be solved by using a point canonical transformation mapping the corresponding Schr\" odinger equation onto that of the Scarf I potential with constant mass. The inverse point canonical transformation then provides some exactly-solvable rational extensions of the Scarf I potential with positive-dependent mass associated with $X_m$-Jacobi exceptional orthogonal polynomials of type I, II, or III. The Scarf I potential problem with position-dependent mass is shown to exhibit a deformed shape invariance property in a deformed supersymmetric framework. Such a property is also valid for extended potentials of type I and II. The results are illustrated with a simple example.

</details>


### [107] [Cantor Spectrum via a Reducibility-Duality Bridge for the Mosaic Almost Mathieu Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23422)
*Jiawei He,Yuan Shan,Yongjian Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究镶嵌准周期Almost Mathieu算子，通过建立对偶框架和推广IDOS与纤维旋转数的对应关系，将问题约化为SL(2,R) cocycles，结合Aubry对偶、可约化理论和Moser-Pöschel论证，证明了所有非临界参数下谱集为Cantor集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解镶嵌Almost Mathieu算子的谱结构，特别是证明其谱集在非临界参数下为Cantor集这一核心性质。

Method: 建立对偶框架，推广IDOS与纤维旋转数的对应关系，将问题约化为SL(2,R) cocycles，结合Aubry对偶、可约化理论和Moser-Pöschel论证。

Result: 证明了所有非临界参数下镶嵌Almost Mathieu算子的谱集为Cantor集。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了对镶嵌准周期模型谱结构的理解，展示了结合对偶性与可约化理论处理奇异Jacobi算子的有效性。

Abstract: We study the mosaic Almost Mathieu operator, a quasiperiodic model that naturally admits a singular strip-Jacobi representation. By establishing a duality framework and extending the correspondence between the integrated density of states and the fibered rotation number to this setting, we obtain an effective reduction to $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ cocycles. As a consequence, combining Aubry duality, reducibility theory, and the Moser--Pöschel argument, we prove that the spectrum is a Cantor set for all noncritical parameters.

</details>


### [108] [Which Waveguide Network Realizes a Prescribed Transmission Profile? An Exact Forward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23645)
*Tristan M. Lawrie*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于规范偏移亥姆霍兹算子的周期性波导网络散射特性的解析可逆波前构建框架，通过傅里叶展开将晶格电抗系数解析映射到图结构参数，实现从目标透射系数到物理结构的闭式逆向设计，并推广到二维图像合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆向设计方法通常依赖数值优化，缺乏从目标透射特性到物理结构的解析映射路径，作者旨在建立一种闭合形式的解析可逆框架，绕开迭代优化过程。

Method: 基于规范偏移亥姆霍兹算子控制的周期性波导网络，精确求解网络透射系数；将晶格电抗展开为傅里叶级数，并建立系数与图架构（键连接、折射率、长度、规范相位）之间的解析映射关系。

Result: 实现了从目标透射系数到物理网络参数的直接解析求解；该方法从一维角度滤波自然扩展到二维图像合成，能够通过精确的频谱控制重建任意透射强度图案。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种从所需波传输轮廓到可物理实现结构的闭式设计途径，克服了传统逆向设计的数值迭代瓶颈，展示了在波前工程和图像合成中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce an analytically invertible framework for wavefront construction based on the scattering properties of periodic waveguide networks governed by a gauge-shifted Helmholtz operator. By determining the exact transmission coefficients of the network, we express the lattice reactance as a Fourier expansion whose coefficients are analytically mapped onto the underlying graph architecture, allowing the required bond connections, refractive indices, lengths, and gauge phases to be determined directly from a prescribed target transmission coefficient. In contrast to conventional inverse-design approaches, the present formulation provides a closed-form route from desired wave transmission profiles to physically realisable structures. The framework extends naturally from one-dimensional angular filtering to two-dimensional image synthesis, where arbitrary transmitted intensity patterns are reconstructed through exact spectral control of the network scattering response.

</details>


### [109] [Position-Space Renormalization and Half-Space Truncations in $φ^4_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23650)
*Majdouline Borji*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了大质量欧几里得φ⁴₄理论中的半空间可观测性，证明重整化关联函数可以与半空间截断相乘而无需额外重整化，并建立了相应的分布极限和Besov空间收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究半空间可观测性在量子场论中的数学严格定义，特别是处理半空间指示函数与重整化关联函数乘积时的奇异性和紫外发散问题，证明无需额外重整化即可得到良定义的分布极限。

Method: 采用位置空间重整化框架，基于Wilson-Polchinski流方程和按奇异性结构分层的幂次计数空间层次结构，导出重整化关联函数的均匀幂次计数界和Besov-Hölder正则性估计。

Result: 证明了半空间截断的光滑近似与重整化关联函数的乘积一致收敛到良定义的分布极限，且极限与紫外截断无关；关联函数在紫外截断趋于无穷时在显式识别的Besov空间中收敛。

Conclusion: 在大质量欧几里得φ⁴₄理论中，半空间可观测性的重整化关联函数无需额外重整化即可与半空间截断相乘，并建立了在Besov空间中的收敛性，为量子场论中边界可观测性的数学基础提供了严格结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study half-space observables in the massive Euclidean $φ^4_4$ theory. We prove that the renormalized correlators can be multiplied by half-space truncations without requiring any additional renormalization. More precisely, products with smooth approximations of the half-space indicator converge to well-defined distributional limits, uniformly in the ultraviolet cutoff. The proof relies on a position-space renormalization framework for the Wilson--Polchinski flow equation based on a hierarchy of power-counting spaces adapted to the singularity structure of the correlators. This yields uniform power-counting bounds and Besov--Hölder regularity estimates for the renormalized correlators. As a consequence, the correlators converge as the ultraviolet cutoff tends to infinity, and the convergence takes place in explicitly identified Besov spaces.

</details>

### [110] [Error Analysis for a Nonconforming Finite Element Discretization of the 3D-CCPF Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20665)
*Clément Adandé,Koffi W. Houédanou,Guy A. Dègla*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对三维各向异性网格上含时耦合管道流问题，进行了先验和后验误差分析，涵盖非协调离散化、不同单元及Crouzeix-Raviart有限元方法变体。在适当正则性假设下推导了H^1模最优先验误差估计，构建了可靠且高效的基于残差的局部误差指示器与全局估计器。效率无条件成立，可靠性依赖于对齐测度概念，并建立了保证估计器最优性的网格条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决三维各向异性网格（极大长宽比单元）上含时耦合管道流问题的误差分析需求，填补现有理论在非协调离散化和不同Crouzeix-Raviart有限元方法变体方面的空白。

Method: 采用先验与后验误差分析框架，基于残差元素构建局部误差指示器和全局估计器，引入对齐测度概念分析可靠性，推导各向异性网格上的最优误差估计。

Result: 获得了H^1类范数下的最优先验误差估计；误差估计器效率无条件成立；可靠性依赖于对齐测度概念；明确了保证估计器最优性的网格条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的后验误差估计器在三维各向异性网格上对含时耦合管道流问题具有可靠性与效率，但可靠性受对齐测度约束，需满足特定网格条件以确保最优性。

Abstract: This paper presents an a-priori and a-posteriori error analysis for the time-dependent case of a coupled continuum pipe-fow in three dimensions on anisotropic meshes (i.e. elements with very large aspect ratio). Our investigation includes nonconforming discretizations as well as different elements and different versions of Crouzeix-Raviart fnite element methods. Under suitable regularity assumptions on the exact solution, we derive optimal a priori error estimates in the H^1-like norm. Leveraging residual elements, local error indicators and a global estimator are generated, demonstrating reliability and efficiency. The efficiency of error estimators holds unconditional, though its reliability involves the alignment measure notion. Mesh requirements are established to ensure the optimality of the error estimator.

</details>


### [111] [Variable Exponent Wasserstein Spaces: Stability of Entropy Convexity and Modified Rényi Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20710)
*Ambroise Soglo,Cyrille Combété,Koffi Wilfrid Houédanou,Léonard Todjihoundé*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了变指数 Wasserstein 空间，证明了 Boltzmann 熵的 (K - C||ε||∞)-凸性，引入修正 Rényi 熵以补偿对数发散，并推导了 Log-Sobolev 和 Talagrand 不等式的扰动版本，将 Lott-Villani 定理推广至变指数情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Lott-Villani 关于 Boltzmann 熵在常指数 Wasserstein 空间中的 K-测地凸性理论推广到变指数情形（指数 p(x)=2+ε(x) 随空间位置变化），探索 Bakry-Émery 张量在变指数设定下的有效曲率作用。

Method: 利用 Lagrangian L(x,v)=|v|^{p(x)} 构造 Wasserstein 距离 W_p，通过分析 W_p² 展开中的对数发散项，引入修正 Rényi 熵进行补偿，建立广义凸性不等式。

Result: 证明了 Boltzmann 熵在变指数 Wasserstein 空间中具有 (K - C||ε||∞)-凸性；得到了 Log-Sobolev 和 Talagrand 不等式的扰动版本。

Conclusion: 经典 Lott-Villani 定理及其推论在运输指数受小扰动时仍然稳健，Bakry-Émery 张量依然是有效曲率的核心量。

Abstract: We study the Wasserstein space $\mathcal{P}(M)$ equipped with a distance $\Wp$ constructed from the Lagrangian $L(x,v)=|v|^{p(x)}$ where $p(x)=2+\varepsilon(x)$ with $\varepsilon$ small. Building on the fundamental work of Lott and Villani on the $K$-geodesic convexity of the Boltzmann entropy in $(\mathcal{P}(M),\Wb)$, we establish a generalized inequality showing that the entropy remains $\left(K - C\|\varepsilon\|_\infty\right)$-convex along $\Wp$-geodesics. We then introduce a modified Rényi entropy that exactly compensates the logarithmic divergence that appears in the expansions of $\Wp^2$, obtaining thus a sharp equivalence that reaveals the Bakry-Émery tensor as the effective curvature in the variable exponent setting. As applications, we derive perturbed versions of the Log-Sobolev and Talagrand inequalities in variable exponent Wasserstein spaces, showing that these fundamental functional inequalities are robust under small perturbations of the transport exponent. This work generalizes the Lott-Villani theorem and its consequences (\emph{J. Lott and C. Villani, Ann. of Math. \textbf{169} (2009), 903-991}) to situations where the transport metric varies spatially.

</details>


### [112] [Inverse initial data reconstruction for a memory convection-diffusion equation via Legendre spatial reduction and Tikhonov regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20875)
*Cong B. Van,Thien P. B. Nguyen,Minh-Binh Tran,Loc H. Nguyen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究了对流扩散记忆方程的初始条件反演问题，提出基于勒让德展开和Tikhonov正则化的计算方法，证明了解的唯一性和正则化解的收敛性，并通过二维数值实验验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从终时刻数据恢复未知初始条件，该问题因包含记忆项（过去状态的拉普拉斯算子）而严重不适定，需要发展有效的理论和数值方法。

Method: 常系数情形：利用傅里叶变换和标量Volterra方程的解析性论证证明唯一性。变系数情形：采用勒让德空间维数约简将原问题转化为有限维终端值系统，再结合Tikhonov正则化和H²惩罚项求解最小二乘问题。

Result: 证明了常系数情形下初始条件的唯一性；在固定截断阶数下，证明了当噪声水平和正则化参数适当选取时，正则化解收敛到有限维最小范数解；二维数值实验验证了方法的性能。

Conclusion: 所提勒让德降维+Tikhonov正则化方法能有效求解带记忆项的对流扩散方程反演问题，具有理论收敛保证和良好的数值表现。

Abstract: We study an inverse initial data problem for a convection-diffusion equation with memory, where the goal is to recover the unknown initial condition from final-time data. The model includes convection, an instantaneous Laplacian term, and a nonlocal-in-time memory term involving the Laplacian of the past states, which leads to a severely ill-posed backward problem. We prove uniqueness in a spatially independent coefficient setting by applying the Fourier transform and using an analyticity argument for a scalar Volterra equation. For the variable-coefficient case, we develop a computational method based on Legendre spatial dimensional reduction and Tikhonov regularization. The solution is approximated by a finite tensor-product Legendre expansion, thereby reducing the inverse problem to a finite-dimensional terminal-value system for the time-dependent coefficients. We solve the reduced problem by a Tikhonov-regularized least-squares method with an $H^2$ penalty. For a fixed truncation order, we prove that the regularized minimizers converge to the finite-dimensional minimum-norm solution as the noise level and the regularization parameter vanish, under a suitable choice of the regularization parameter. Some two-dimensional numerical examples are presented to illustrate the performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [113] [Neural network approximation in discrete dual norms with adaptive test spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20972)
*Tanakorn Udomworarat,Ignacio Brevis,Kristoffer G. van der Zee,Sergio Rojas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出自适应测试空间细化算法，在RVPINNs框架中解决局部奇异性问题，通过可计算的细化指标实现离散与连续Riesz代表元误差的可靠估计，数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RVPINNs中固定的粗测试空间无法解决局部奇异性、陡峭梯度或界面层问题，而初始使用过细测试空间计算效率低下，因此需要自适应细化策略。

Method: 提出自适应算法，仅在离散与连续Riesz代表元误差显著的区域细化测试空间；建立理论自适应策略及其误差界；提出可计算的细化指标，并在饱和假设下证明其作为非可计算误差的可靠且高效估计器。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在椭圆Dirichlet问题上有效，在保持计算效率的同时提高了RVPINNs处理局部奇异性问题的能力。

Conclusion: 自适应测试空间细化是平衡RVPINNs计算效率与精度的有效策略，为处理具有局部特征的问题提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: In robust variational physics-informed neural networks (RVPINNs), the loss function is formulated in terms of the Riesz representative of the variational residual within a discrete test space. This approach guarantees that the loss function is robust with respect to the true error in the energy norm up to a remainder term that depends on both the neural network approximation and the discrete space configuration. However, in problems with localized singularities, steep gradients, or interface layers, a fixed coarse test space may fail to resolve the continuous Riesz representative of the residual during training. Although this can be avoided by using a sufficiently fine test space from the start, doing so may be computationally inefficient. We therefore propose an adaptive algorithm that enriches the test space only where the error between the discrete and continuous Riesz representatives is pronounced. We establish theoretical adaptive strategies within the RVPINN framework and derive their error bounds. Furthermore, we propose a computable refinement indicator and prove that, under the saturation assumption, it serves as a reliable and efficient error estimator for the non-computable discrepancy between the discrete and continuous Riesz representatives. Finally, we propose a practical adaptive algorithm and demonstrate its effectiveness through numerical experiments on elliptic Dirichlet problems.

</details>


### [114] [A Coupled Conforming-Nonconforming Galerkin Method for Poisson's Equation on Curved Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20985)
*Qingguang Guan,Wenju Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种耦合协调-非协调Galerkin方法求解二维曲边域上的Poisson方程，只在曲边边界附近的薄层使用弱Galerkin离散，内部多边形区域使用标准连续Galerkin离散，通过混合插值-投影算子保证相容性，实现了最优误差估计和自由度显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在曲边域上使用标准连续Galerkin方法需要高曲率近似或非结构化网格，而完全使用弱Galerkin方法虽然几何灵活性好但自由度开销大。本文旨在保留弱Galerkin在边界附近的几何灵活性，同时大幅降低非协调自由度数量。

Method: 提出一种耦合协调-非协调Galerkin方法：在曲边边界附近的薄层曲边单元上采用弱Galerkin离散，内部多边形区域使用标准连续Galerkin离散。关键创新是构造了曲边弱Galerkin单元上的混合插值-投影算子，在界面侧结合L²边投影与协调节点插值，确保与连续Galerkin迹的相容性。

Result: 在基本几何假设下证明了能量范数的最优先验误差估计O(h^k)；在椭圆对偶正则性额外假设下证明了L²(Ω)范数的最优估计O(h^{k+1})。数值实验验证了理论收敛阶，并表明相比完全弱Galerkin离散方法，自由度显著减少。

Conclusion: 该耦合方法在保持曲边边界处几何灵活性的同时，有效降低了计算开销，是一种兼具精度与效率的曲边域Poisson方程数值求解方案。

Abstract: A coupled conforming-nonconforming Galerkin method is proposed for Poisson's equation on two-dimensional curved domains. The method applies a weak Galerkin discretization only on a thin boundary layer of curvilinear elements near the curved boundary, while using a standard continuous Galerkin discretization in the polygonal interior. In this way, geometric flexibility is retained where it is needed most, and the number of nonconforming degrees of freedom is significantly reduced. A key ingredient is a mixed interpolation-projection operator on curvilinear weak Galerkin elements, combining $L^2$ edge projections with conforming nodal interpolation on the interface side to ensure compatibility with the continuous Galerkin trace. Based on this construction, we prove an optimal a priori error estimate of order $O(h^k)$ in the energy norm under the basic geometric assumptions of the method, and an optimal $L^2(Ω)$ estimate of order $O(h^{k+1})$ under the additional elliptic dual regularity assumption. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical rates and demonstrate substantial savings in degrees of freedom compared with a fully weak Galerkin discretization.

</details>


### [115] [An Efficient Laguerre Minimum Action Method for Computing Quasi-Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21114)
*Shenghe Huang,Yishuang Yue,Haijun Yu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的拉盖尔谱最小作用量方法（LMAM），用于计算动力系统不动点相关的拟势能，通过在 полубесконечном 时间区间上使用拉盖尔函数离散时间方向，并引入时间重缩放策略提高精度和收敛性，数值实验验证了该方法在常微分和偏微分方程（Allen-Cahn 和 Navier-Stokes）中的准确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小作用量方法在分析小噪声驱动动力系统中稀有跃迁方面具有强大能力，但在无限时域问题中常受限于时间截断和参数敏感性，现有方法性能有限，亟需发展更高效稳定的数值方案。

Method: 基于大偏差框架，将最小作用路径问题在半无穷时间区间上建模，采用拉盖尔函数进行时间方向谱离散；设计了时间重缩放策略以增强拉盖尔谱近似的精度和收敛性；改进了拉盖尔-高斯-拉道求积公式的评估过程，实现大量拉盖尔模态下稳定精确的双精度计算。

Result: 对线性问题给出了精确的数值分析，对非线性情形给出了局部收敛性结果。数值实验涵盖 Allen-Cahn 方程和 Navier-Stokes 方程，验证了方法的准确性和高效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的拉盖尔谱最小作用量方法有效克服了传统方法在无限时域问题中的时间截断和参数敏感性缺陷，为计算动力系统拟势能提供了高精度、高稳定性的数值工具。

Abstract: Minimum action methods provide a powerful framework for analyzing rare transitions in small-noise-driven dynamical systems, but their practical performance is often limited by time truncation and parameter sensitivity in infinite-horizon problems. In this paper, we develop an efficient Laguerre spectral minimum action method (LMAM) for computing quasi-potentials associated with fixed points of dynamical systems. Based on the large deviation framework, the method computes minimum action paths by formulating the problem on a semi-infinite time interval and discretize the temporal direction using Laguerre functions. An appropriate time rescaling strategy is proposed to enhance accuracy and convergence of the Laguerre spectral approximation. To efficiently handle nonlinear terms, we employ an improved procedure for evaluating Laguerre--Gauss--Radau quadrature, which enables stable and accurate double-precision computations with a large number of Laguerre modes. Precise numerical analysis for the linear problem and a local result for the nonlinear case are developed. Numerical experiments including both ordinary and partial differential equations (Allen-Cahn and Navier-Stokes) are presented to illustrate the accuracy and efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [116] [Distributed-memory parallelization of B-spline based s-version of the finite element method via weighted graph abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21142)
*Nozomi Magome,Naoki Morita,Shigeki Kaneko,Naoto Mitsume*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图论的方法，将B样条s型有限元法（BSFEM）中因网格物理重叠产生的复杂连接关系抽象为加权图，从而在分布式内存系统上实现基于区域分解的并行计算，并通过三维稳态泊松方程验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将BSFEM扩展到大规模仿真需要在分布式内存系统上实现基于区域分解的并行计算，但独立网格空间重叠导致的高度复杂交互使得处理器间通信结构的构建极为困难，传统区域分解方法难以适用。

Method: 提出一种数学方法，将网格物理重叠产生的复杂连接关系概括并简化为加权图，将问题归结为图论问题，在此基础上构建适用于BSFEM及SFEM类方法的区域分解并行化算法。

Result: 通过三维稳态泊松方程的数值验证，确认了分布式内存环境下并行计算的实现；同时证明了为图节点分配权重可有效均衡局部计算负载并显著减少总计算时间。

Conclusion: 所建立的图论方法不仅实现了BSFEM及SFEM类方法的并行化，还为其他具有复杂通信结构的方法设计并行化策略提供了通用的理论基础。

Abstract: To extend the B-spline based s-version of the finite element method (BSFEM), which enables locally high-resolution analysis, to large-scale simulations, domain-decomposition-based parallel computation on distributed-memory systems is indispensable. However, the highly complex interactions induced by the spatial overlap of independent meshes make the construction of interprocessor communication structures extremely challenging, thereby posing a major obstacle to the application of conventional domain decomposition approaches. In this study, we propose a method that mathematically generalizes and simplifies the complex connectivity arising from the physical overlap of meshes as a weighted graph. By reducing the problem to graph theory, we successfully construct a domain-decomposition-based parallelization algorithm for BSFEM and SFEM-based methods with complex mesh structures in distributed-memory environments. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, numerical verification is performed using the three-dimensional steady Poisson equation, confirming the realization of parallel computation in a distributed-memory environment. Furthermore, we demonstrate that assigning weights to graph nodes effectively distributes localized computational loads and substantially reduces the total computational time. The graph-theoretic approach established in this study not only enables the parallelization of BSFEM and SFEM-based methods, but also provides a general theoretical foundation for designing parallelization strategies for other methods with complex communication structures.

</details>


### [117] [Exact Nonnegative Matrix Factorization via Cone-Ray Witnesses: Obtuseness Ranking, Saturation Curves, and an Augmented Alt-LP Breakthrough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22451)
*Mithil Ramteke*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合截断SVD、多面锥、双重描述法（DDM）和交替线性规划（alt-LP）的精确非负矩阵分解（NMF）管线，针对小规模精确秩r矩阵，通过闭式见证矩阵M_T在均匀支撑约束下实现微秒级分解，并通过混合策略（闭式解+短SLP交替）将成功率从79/85/58提升至99/95/75（r=4,5,6），同时指出四类扩展瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精确NMF方法在大规模或高秩场景下计算效率低、成功率不稳定，缺乏统一的几何框架来系统刻画分解可行性并高效求解。

Method: 采用锥-射线管线：先对数据矩阵做截断SVD得到低秩近似，再构建非负原像的多面锥，通过双重描述法（DDM）枚举锥射线，利用均匀支撑约束将分解条件Q P^T = I_r转化为r×r见证矩阵M_T的闭式解；当闭式解不可行时，采用交替线性规划（alt-LP）扩展支撑集（k=2最大角度分离射线）最小化松弛变量。

Result: 在m=n=10的100次蒙特卡洛试验中，闭式解在top-200子集上成功率从79/85/58（r=4,5,6）提升至99/95/75；m=n=15时成功率从r=4的37%骤降至r=5~8的0%；Olivetti人脸数据集（400×4096）上DDM步骤超时，揭示扩展瓶颈。

Conclusion: 闭式见证矩阵在均匀支撑约束下可微秒级恢复精确NMF，混合策略显著提升成功率；但面临DDM枚举爆炸、支撑集排序、高维退化及数据集规模四类扩展障碍，需进一步优化。

Abstract: We study exact nonnegative matrix factorization (NMF) of small exact-rank-r matrices via a cone-ray pipeline combining the truncated SVD, the polyhedral cone of nonnegative preimages, the Double Description Method (DDM, via Fukuda's cddlib), and an alternating linear program (alt-LP) for slack minimisation. Under a uniform-support restriction the factorisation constraint Q P^T = I_r reduces to entrywise nonnegativity of an r x r witness matrix M_T = R_T^{-1} (R_K^T)^{-1} for an r-subset pair (T, K) of cone rays; this closed-form witness recovers an exact NMF in microseconds when feasible.
  We characterise feasibility by ranking r-subsets via geometric near-orthogonality ("obtuseness") and walking the top of each list. A 100-trial Monte Carlo at m=n=10 exposes a clean saturation curve: success 44/32/8, 79/85/58, and 79/87/59 of 100 at top-5/200/400 for r=4,5,6 -- beyond top-200 the failures are structural, not budget-limited. Enlarging m,n at fixed r hurts: at m=n=15 success collapses to 37/7/0/0/0 for r=4..8. On Olivetti faces (400x4096) the DDM step itself times out.
  Our main contribution is a hybrid that breaks this ceiling: at each pair we first try the closed-form M_T, and when it is infeasible we augment both supports by k=2 maximally angularly-separated rays and solve for mu,nu>=0 by a short slack-LP alternation. On the same m=n=10 Monte Carlo this lifts success from 79/85/58 to 99/95/75 at r=4,5,6, with cone reconstruction error at or near machine precision. We close with the four scaling walls the pipeline faces and concrete next steps.

</details>


### [118] [A semi-Lagrangian method for the polyatomic ESBGK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21191)
*Klaas Willems,Erik Arlemark,Giovanni Samaey,Axel Klar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的半拉格朗日格式用于求解多原子ESBGK模型，该格式无时间步长限制、渐进保持且刚性精确，在稀薄气体极限下可收敛至欧拉方程，一阶格式可收敛至具有正确输运系数的可压缩纳维-斯托克斯方程，并通过傅里叶、库埃特及移动边界孔板流算例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多原子气体的内能模式显著影响输运系数、松弛过程和非平衡效应，而单原子模型无法准确描述这些行为，因此需要发展高效、稳定的多原子动力学模型数值方法。

Method: 采用半拉格朗日框架（确定性、基于网格）消除线性输运项的时间步长限制；对刚性松弛项使用隐式A-稳定线性多步法，并利用BGK算子结构重构为廉价时间步进格式。

Result: 该方法具有渐进保持性和刚性精确性，一阶格式在努森数趋零时渐近收敛至具有正确输运系数的可压缩纳维-斯托克斯方程；傅里叶和库埃特算例与DSMC结果吻合良好；成功模拟了移动边界的孔板流。

Conclusion: 新格式高效、数值稳定，是模拟多原子气体非平衡流动的有力工具。

Abstract: Polyatomic kinetic models are essential for accurately capturing the thermodynamic behavior of real gases, as internal energy modes significantly influence transport coefficients, relaxation processes, and non-equilibrium effects that cannot be represented by monoatomic models. The polyatomic ESBGK model describes molecular collisions as a relaxation towards a generalized Gaussian distribution with an anisotropic covariance matrix and an exponentially decaying internal energy distribution. We present a new semi-Lagrangian scheme for the polyatomic Ellipsoidal Statistical BGK (ESBGK) model of the Boltzmann equation. The semi-Lagrangian framework, being deterministic and grid-based, removes the time-step restriction associated with the linear transport term by following the method of characteristics. The potentially stiff relaxation term is treated using an implicit A-stable linear multistep method which, owing to the structure of the BGK operator, can be reformulated into a cheap time-stepping scheme. This yields a highly efficient and numerically stable method. The numerical method is asymptotic preserving and stiffly accurate, meaning the scheme asymptotically converges to a scheme for the Euler equations in the vanishing Knudsen limit. In addition, we prove that the first-order scheme, asymptotically converges to the compressible Navier-Stokes equation with correct transport coefficients. Finally, we propose inflow and outflow boundary conditions suitable for BGK-type kinetic equations. We perform simulations of the Fourier and Couette test case to compare the BGK model with Direct Simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC). To conclude, we demonstrate the method on a challenging orifice flow test case with moving boundaries.

</details>


### [119] [A fully discrete Active Flux method for the Euler equations comparing different truly multi-dimensional evolution operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21303)
*Christiane Helzel,Amelie Porfetye*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在已发表的全离散笛卡尔网格Active Flux方法基础上，针对欧拉方程提出了一种新的演化算子，通过移动坐标将线性化欧拉方程简化为声学问题并精确求解，实现了三阶精度，并通过数值实验验证了该方法在不同流态（从激波到低马赫数涡旋）下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有全离散Active Flux方法基础上，引入更精确的演化算子，替代之前使用特征线法得到的近似演化算子，以提高对线性化欧拉方程的处理精度。

Method: 提出在移动坐标下求解线性化欧拉方程，将其简化为声学问题并精确求解；结合局部线性化及线性化误差校正，构建三阶精度格式。

Result: 数值结果表明该方法在不同流态（含激波的间断解结构及低马赫数涡旋结构）下均表现出良好的性能。

Conclusion: 移动坐标方法相比此前基于特征线的近似演化算子更优，结合局部线性化与误差校正，成功构造了非线性欧拉方程的三阶精度Active Flux方法。

Abstract: This article builds upon our recently published fully discrete Cartesian grid Active Flux method for the Euler equations by introducing a new evolution operator for the linearized Euler equations. The evolution of the point value degrees of freedom located at grid cell boundaries and used to compute numerical fluxes is a key component of fully discrete Active Flux methods. Our methods are based on local linearizations of the Euler equations and the use of truly multi-dimensional evolution operators for these linearized problems. Here, we propose to solve the linearized Euler equations in moving coordinates, reducing them to acoustics, which can be solved exactly. We compare this approach with our previous one, in which we used approximate evolution operators derived using the method of bicharacteristics. The moving-grid approach is closely related to recent methods proposed by Barsukow as well as Duraisamy. Our choice of local linearization, as well as correction of linearization errors, enables us to construct third-order accurate methods for smooth solutions of the nonlinear Euler equations. Numerical results illustrate the performance of these methods for different flow regimes, ranging from discontinuous solution structures with shock waves to vortex structures in the low Mach number regime.

</details>


### [120] [Inverse Source Problems for a Class of Fractional Elliptic Equations with Singular Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21375)
*Zewen Wang,Liang Guo,Shufang Qiu,Bin Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有奇异系数的分数阶椭圆方程的反源问题，提出了两种正则化方法（指数型Tikhonov正则化和指数拟边界值正则化），并建立了条件稳定性估计、收敛性分析和数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有奇异系数的分数阶椭圆方程中未知源项的反演问题，这类问题在数学物理中具有重要应用且通常是不适定的。

Method: 建立了Hilbert尺度下与指数算子相关的Hölder型条件稳定性估计；提出了指数型Tikhonov正则化方法和指数拟边界值正则化方法；在正则化参数先验和后验选择下推导了收敛性估计；进行了有限维谱逼近分析。

Result: 两种正则化方法都能稳定地重构未知源项；在低信噪比条件下仍能获得光滑和分段光滑源的准确重建；有限维谱逼近结果表明方法适用于一般平方可积源项，无需指数型源条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的正则化方法对具有奇异系数的分数阶椭圆方程反源问题有效，具有理论上的收敛保证和实际计算中的稳定性。

Abstract: An inverse source problem for a class of fractional elliptic equations with singular coefficients is investigated in this paper. For the corresponding direct problem, a formal solution is derived and the well-posedness of the solution is established. For the inverse problem, a Hölder-type conditional stability estimate is obtained in a Hilbert scale associated with exponential operators. Based on this stability framework, two regularization methods are proposed for reconstructing the unknown source term: the exponential-type Tikhonov regularization method and the exponential quasi-boundary value regularization method. Convergence estimates for the regularized solutions are derived under both a priori and a posteriori choices of the regularization parameter. In addition, finite-dimensional spectral approximation results show that the proposed methods are also applicable to general square-integrable source terms, without requiring the exact source to satisfy an exponential-type source condition. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed methods provide stable and accurate reconstructions for both smooth and piecewise smooth sources even under low signal-to-noise ratio conditions.

</details>


### [121] [QVaR: a Quantum Variational Regularization method for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21392)
*Siiri Rautio,Hjørdis Schlüter,Andreas Hauptmann,Babak Maboudi Afkham*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用量子优化方法求解正则化线性逆问题的统一框架，通过将解空间离散化并将数据保真项和正则项编码为QUBO模型，同时引入量子灵敏度概念建立量子解变异性与经典不适定性的理论联系，并采用小波表示和降阶建模策略缓解硬件限制，实验表明低能解分布能保留逆问题信息但受限于有限时间演化、离散化和硬件噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性逆问题求解方法在计算复杂度上存在瓶颈，量子计算提供了新的求解途径，但仍缺乏统一的框架将正则化逆问题适配到量子优化方法中，且量子解的稳定性与经典不适定性之间的理论关系尚不明确。

Method: 将解空间离散化，将数据保真项和正则项编码为QUBO模型；引入量子灵敏度概念刻画近似量子演化和离散化引起的扰动；推导扰动与逆问题稳定性的界；通过小波表示扩展至变分逆问题；采用参数空间和哈密顿量空间的降阶建模策略缓解硬件限制。

Result: 模拟和物理量子硬件上的数值实验表明，低能解分布保留了底层逆问题的信息，同时揭示了有限时间演化、离散化和硬件噪声带来的限制。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了量子解变异性与经典不适定性之间的理论联系，证明了量子优化方法可用于求解正则化线性逆问题，但当前量子硬件的能力仍对此类方法构成实际限制。

Abstract: We present a tailored framework for solving regularized linear inverse problems using quantum optimization methods. By discretizing the solution space and encoding data fidelity and regularization terms into quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) models, we formulate both Tikhonov- and sparsity-promoting regularized inverse problems within a unified quantum optimization framework. We further introduce a notion of quantum sensitivity that characterizes the effect of perturbations arising from approximate quantum evolution and discretization. We derive bounds relating these perturbations to stability properties of the underlying inverse problem, thereby establishing a theoretical connection between quantum solution variability and classical notions of ill-posedness. The framework is extended to variational inverse problems through wavelet-based representations and complemented by reduced-order modeling strategies in both parameter and Hamiltonian spaces to mitigate current hardware limitations. Numerical experiments on simulated and physical quantum hardware indicate that the resulting low-energy solution distributions retain information about the underlying inverse problem, while also revealing the limitations imposed by finite-time evolution, discretization, and hardware noise.

</details>


### [122] [An FFT-based solver with general boundary conditions for stationary diffusion problems based on Chebyshev collocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21410)
*Juan M. Quecedo,Javier Segurado*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Chebyshev配点框架和FFT的高效鲁棒求解器，用于处理具有一般Neumann和Dirichlet边界条件的扩散型问题。该方法通过Chebyshev多项式逼近与FFT算子结合实现无矩阵实现，并采用层次化细化策略解决细网格收敛问题，适用于均匀/非均匀域及线性/非线性本构方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FFT-based方法多局限于周期性边界条件，难以处理一般Neumann和Dirichlet边界条件；传统Chebyshev配点法在大规模3D问题中面临计算效率低和收敛困难的问题。

Method: (1) 在Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto点上结合Chebyshev多项式逼近与FFT算子，构造无矩阵形式的离散微分算子；(2) 使用LGMRES求解离散化后的线性方程组；(3) 提出基于模态延拓的层次化细化策略，在细网格上克服收敛问题。

Result: 求解Poisson方程时，在均匀1D和3D域上经少量迭代即可收敛到解析解，误差优于DCT/DST方法；层次化策略支持高达256^3的离散化；非均匀域上精度和效率与标准周期性FFT方法相当；计算复杂度保持O(n log n)的FFT标度。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将FFT的高效性与Chebyshev配点法的边界适应性结合，突破了周期性FFT的边界限制，同时保持了近线性的计算复杂度，为大规模扩散问题提供了高效求解方案。

Abstract: An efficient and robust FFT-based solver is proposed for diffusion-type problems with general Neumann and Dirichlet boundary conditions, based on a Chebyshev collocation framework. The method combines Chebyshev polynomial approximations with FFT-based operators to provide a matrix-free implementation of the discrete differential operator at the Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto points. The linear system of equations resulting from the Chebyshev discretization is solved using LGMRES. To overcome convergence problems on fine grids, a hierarchical refinement strategy based on modal prolongation is proposed, enabling the solution of very large 3D problems. The methodology is applicable to homogeneous and heterogeneous domains, as well as to linear and nonlinear constitutive equations.
  The accuracy of the proposed method is analyzed by solving the Poisson equation in homogeneous 1D and 3D domains with general boundary conditions, using manufactured analytical solutions as references. Convergence to the analytical solution is achieved in a few iterations, with smaller errors than those obtained using DCT/DST approaches. Discretizations of up to $256^3$ are achieved thanks to the hierarchical refinement strategy. In the case of heterogeneous domains, the accuracy and efficiency obtained are similar to those of a standard periodic FFT approach. It is found that the computational complexity of the method preserves the FFT scaling, of order $n\log n$, in all the cases studied.

</details>


### [123] [An Aubin-Nitsche Lemma for a positivity-preserving finite element method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21560)
*Gabriel R. Barrenechea,Theophile Chaumont-Frelet*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对椭圆问题的保正离散格式，证明了Aubin-Nitsche引理。由于离散格式的非线性，首先需要构造一个线性化的伴随问题，并通过适当选择权函数将其与原方法关联。该线性化伴随问题结合正则性结果，最终推导出误差在L²范数下的最优阶估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对保正离散格式（一种非线性离散方法）缺乏L²范数下最优误差估计的理论空白，需要建立相应的Aubin-Nitsche引理来填补。

Method: 1) 提出线性化的伴随问题；2) 通过适当选择权函数将伴随问题与原保正离散方法关联；3) 结合正则性结果，证明Aubin-Nitsche引理。

Result: 成功证明了针对保正离散格式的Aubin-Nitsche引理，并推导出L²范数下误差的最优阶估计。

Conclusion: 为非线性保正离散格式的L²误差分析提供了理论框架，拓展了Aubin-Nitsche技巧在非线性离散方法中的应用。

Abstract: In this work we prove an Aubin-Nitsche Lemma for a positivity-preserving discretisation of an elliptic
  problem. Due to the nonlinearity of the discretisation, the result requires as a first step
  the proposal of a linearised adjoint problem that can be linked to the method of
  choice by appropriately selecting weights. This linearised adjoint problem, together
  with a regularity result, allow the proof of an optimal-order error estimate in the $L^2$-norm of
  the error.

</details>


### [124] [A spherical harmonic pseudo-spectral approach to mean curvature flow of surfaces with spherical topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21615)
*Genming Bai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于闭曲面（球面拓扑）平均曲率流的球谐函数伪谱方法，该方法基于单位球面上的全局参数化，对Dziuk方法的弱形式进行球谐函数离散化，并考虑了数值曲面上的求积误差，证明了在初始参数化为解析的情况下位置误差的指数收敛性，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均曲率流的数值方法（如Dziuk的有限元方法）收敛阶有限，缺乏利用谱方法实现高精度（指数收敛）的方案，且尚未有工作系统分析球谐伪谱法在移动几何问题中的收敛性。

Method: 采用球谐函数伪谱方法，将闭曲面表示为球面上的全局参数化，在球谐函数有限维空间中离散化Dziuk的连续弱形式，并显式考虑自主演化数值曲面上的求积误差进行收敛性分析。

Result: 在初始全局参数化为解析的假设下，证明了位置误差的指数收敛性（即收敛速度随球谐函数截断阶数呈指数增长），数值实验与理论结果一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的球谐伪谱方法可实现平均曲率流的高精度指数收敛，且所建立的收敛性分析方法具有通用性，可推广至其他移动域和几何演化问题。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a spherical harmonic pseudo-spectral method for the mean curvature flow of closed surfaces with spherical topology. The evolving surface is represented by a global parametrization over the unit sphere, and the continuous weak formulation underlying Dziuk's method [G. Dziuk, Numer. Math., 1991] is discretized in a finite-dimensional space of spherical harmonics. By explicitly taking into account quadrature errors on the autonomously evolving numerical surface, we prove exponential convergence of the position error under the assumption that the initial global parametrization is analytic. The convergence analysis developed herein is general and could apply to other moving-domain and geometric evolution problems. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical result.

</details>


### [125] [Multilevel Adaptive-Rank Methods for Linear and Nonlinear Systems in the Hierarchical Tucker Format](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21750)
*William A. Sands,Pierson T. Guthrey,Nathan V. Roberts*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了多层自适应秩迭代方法，用于求解高维偏微分方程所导致的线性和非线性系统。主要贡献包括：将Ballani和Grasedyck的投影方法扩展到低秩张量格式中支持灵活预条件；通过将几何多重网格方法适配到低秩场景构建多层预条件策略；将这些技术集成到非精确牛顿框架中求解非线性系统。实验结果表明所提出的方法鲁棒且可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维偏微分方程求解中面临"维数灾难"问题，传统方法计算和存储成本随维度指数增长。低秩张量格式虽然提供了一种压缩表示手段，但高效的预条件子和求解器仍不成熟，尤其缺乏灵活且鲁棒的多层预条件策略。

Method: 1) 扩展Ballani和Grasedyck的投影方法，支持低秩张量格式中的灵活预条件处理；2) 将几何多重网格方法适配到低秩张量设置中，构建多层预条件策略（区别于先前将多重网格作为独立求解器的做法，本文强调其作为预条件子的角色）；3) 将上述技术集成到非精确牛顿框架中处理非线性系统。

Result: 在线性和非线性模型问题上评估了所提方法，多层自适应秩策略提供了鲁棒且可扩展的预条件子，为低秩格式下的高维问题提供了有效的求解器。

Conclusion: 多层自适应秩策略能够产生鲁棒且可扩展的预条件子，是求解低秩格式高维问题的有效手段。

Abstract: We develop multilevel adaptive-rank iterative methods for the solution of linear and nonlinear systems arising from high-dimensional partial differential equations. Our contributions are threefold. First, we extend the projection method of Ballani and Grasedyck [6] to enable flexible preconditioning of high-dimensional linear systems in low-rank tensor formats. Second, we construct multilevel preconditioning strategies by adapting geometric multigrid methods to the low-rank setting. In contrast to prior work, which primarily employs multigrid as a standalone solver, we emphasize its role as an efficient and robust preconditioner. Third, we integrate these techniques within an inexact Newton framework for the solution of nonlinear systems. The proposed methods are evaluated on a range of model problems, including both linear and nonlinear equations, to assess their convergence behavior and computational efficiency. The results demonstrate that multilevel adaptive-rank strategies yield robust and scalable preconditioners, providing effective solvers for high-dimensional problems in low-rank formats.

</details>


### [126] [A Moment--Hankel Rank Method for Identifying the Number of Point Sources in the Heat Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21815)
*Zhiliang Deng,Xiaomei Yang,Ailin Qian*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低频矩-Hankel秩方法，用于从边界通量数据中识别热方程中时不变点源的数量。该方法利用Laplace变换后的边界通量的显式Fourier矩表示，通过低频极限获取有限指数和矩序列，其Hankel矩阵的秩在源位置互异且强度非零的条件下等于源数目。论文还分析了离散和噪声边界数据的影响，给出了稳定数值秩恢复的充分条件，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从边界观测数据中识别热方程中未知点源的数量是一个具有挑战性的反问题，传统方法在源数量未知时往往需要先验假设或迭代验证。本文旨在发展一种无需预先知道源数量、仅利用低频边界通量数据即可直接确定点源数目的分析方法。

Method: 在单位圆盘上利用Laplace变换将边界通量转化为显式Fourier矩表示；取低频极限得到有限指数和矩序列，其中节点编码源位置、权重编码源强度；构造该矩序列的Hankel矩阵并证明其Vandermonde分解性质，从而通过矩阵秩确定源数目；推导均匀矩扰动界，利用Weyl奇异值扰动不等式给出噪声条件下稳定数值秩恢复的充分条件。

Result: 在源位置互异且强度非零的自然假设下，Hankel矩阵的秩精确等于点源数目。数值实验证实了无噪声情况下的精确秩模式，验证了矩噪声下的稳定性阈值，同时揭示了相近源或弱源情况下的分辨率损失。

Conclusion: 低频矩-Hankel秩方法能够有效识别热方程中点源的数量，且对噪声具有一定的鲁棒性。在识别源数目后，同一矩序列还可通过湮灭多项式和Vandermonde重构过程进一步恢复源的位置和强度。

Abstract: We develop a low-frequency moment--Hankel rank method for identifying the number of time-independent point sources in the heat equation from boundary flux data. The method is formulated in the unit disk, where the Laplace-transformed and normalized boundary flux admits an explicit Fourier moment representation. By taking the low-frequency limit, we obtain a finite exponential-sum moment sequence in which the nodes encode the source locations, the weights encode the source strengths, and the number of terms equals the number of point sources. The associated Hankel matrix admits a Vandermonde factorization, and its rank is exactly the source number under the natural assumptions that the source locations are distinct and the source strengths are nonzero. We also analyze the effect of discrete and noisy boundary data. A uniform moment perturbation bound is propagated to the empirical Hankel matrix, and Weyl's singular-value perturbation inequality yields a sufficient condition for stable numerical rank recovery in terms of the smallest nonzero singular value of the ideal Hankel matrix. Numerical experiments confirm the exact rank pattern in the noiseless case, validate the stability threshold under moment noise, and illustrate the loss of resolution for close or weak sources. After the source number is identified, the same moment sequence can be used for location and strength recovery through an annihilating-polynomial and Vandermonde reconstruction procedure.

</details>


### [127] [Spectrally Safe Neural Operator Warm-Starts for Large-Scale Newton Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21828)
*Jaemin Oh,Youngkyu Lee,Jerome Darbon,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 神经算子用于预热牛顿求解器时，即使相对L2误差达到O(10^{-3})，仍可能因局部点违反物理约束导致Jacobian矩阵不定；通过无标签微调（惩罚离散能量）可恢复正定性，实现收敛并加速5.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证神经算子预热牛顿求解器的可靠性，指出低测试误差并不保证初始猜测处于吸引盆内，揭示局部点违反物理约束导致Jacobian矩阵不定性的问题。

Method: 通过相对L2误差O(10^{-3})的神经算子预热求解不可压缩超弹性问题，分析det F的分散性导致Jacobian负特征值；提出无标签微调阶段（惩罚离散能量），结合不精确外层循环进行预热牛顿法。

Result: 未正则化算子在全部加载范围内失败；经微调后Jacobian谱恢复正定，在三维640万自由度问题上实现5.4倍加速。

Conclusion: 仅依靠低L2误差不可靠，需通过物理约束（如能量惩罚）确保Jacobian正定性，才能发挥神经算子预热牛顿求解器的加速效果。

Abstract: Neural operators are increasingly used to warm-start Newton solvers for nonlinear PDEs, on the premise that a low test error places the initial guess inside the basin of attraction. We show that this premise is unreliable. An operator trained to the relative \(L^2\) error \(O(10^{-3})\) can still produce an initial state in which the discrete Jacobian is indefinite, because the mean-squared training controls error on average while leaving localized pointwise violations of the underlying physics. For a nearly incompressible hyperelasticity problem, we trace this to the predicted volume change: the operator disperses \(\mathrm{det} F\) well away from one, and the resulting Jacobian acquires negative eigenvalues even when the predicted field is visually indistinguishable from the reference. At a small scale, this is a nuisance; at a multi-million degree-of-freedom scale, it is disqualifying, since the conjugate gradient and other Krylov solvers needed for memory-feasible Newton steps assume a definite spectrum. We then show that a short, label-free fine-tuning phase -- penalizing the operator against the discrete energy, with no additional solution data -- shifts the Jacobian spectrum back to positive definite. Combined with an inexact outer loop, this gives a warm-started Newton method that converges across the full loading range where the unregularized operator fails, reaching up to 5.4\(\times\) wall-clock speedup over incremental continuation on a 3D problem with 6.4 million degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [128] [A projected walk on spheres method for elliptic equations on high-dimensional embedded manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21883)
*Zhiyuan Hui,Changtao Sheng,Bihao Su,Tao Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种投影漫步球面方法（PWoS），用于求解嵌入流形上的屏蔽泊松方程。该方法通过局部扩展、Green表示和最近点投影，在环境欧几里得空间中实现无网格、高度并行的随机递归，并通过补偿项和自适应半径策略恢复流形的内在几何结构，在多种几何上验证了收敛性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接离散化流形上的Laplace-Beltrami算子，受限于网格生成和几何复杂性，难以高效处理高维、隐式或点云表示的流形。作者旨在开发一种无网格、可并行且能自动适应流形几何结构的随机方法。

Method: 1) 采用局部扩展和局部欧几里得球内的Green表示构建随机递归；2) 通过最近点投影将边界样本映射回流形；3) 引入补偿项修正环境Laplacian与Laplace-Beltrami算子之间的差异；4) 采用由局部几何和边界信息确定的自适应半径策略。

Result: 在边界和闭流形两种设置下建立了均方误差估计；在参数化、隐式、高维（高达1000维）和点云流形上的大量数值实验验证了方法的收敛性和效率。

Conclusion: PWoS方法是一种有效的无网格蒙特卡洛方法，能够处理复杂几何（包括高维和点云流形），具有良好的收敛性和并行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a projected Walk on Spheres method (PWoS) for screened Poisson equations on embedded manifolds. The method employs local extensions together with the Green representation in local Euclidean balls, coupled with a closest-point projection that maps the boundary samples back to the manifold. This formulation yields a meshfree and highly parallelizable stochastic recursion in the ambient Euclidean space, rather than a direct discretization of the Laplace-Beltrami operator on the manifold. To recover the intrinsic geometric structure of the problem, we introduce a compensation term for the discrepancy between the ambient Laplacian of the closest-point extension and the Laplace-Beltrami operator on the manifold, together with an adaptive radius strategy determined by local geometric and boundary information. Under the geometric projection and prescribed compensation accuracy, we establish mean-square error estimates for the proposed Monte Carlo method in both the boundary and closed-manifold settings. Extensive numerical examples on parametrized, implicit, high-dimensional (up to 1000 dimensions), and point-cloud manifolds are presented to illustrate the convergence and efficiency of the proposed method across different geometries.

</details>


### [129] [Second-order $H^1$-norm error analysis for time-fractional advection-dispersion equations based on the fast averaged L1 method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22083)
*Liangcai Huang,Lin Li,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有初始弱奇异性的时间分数阶对流-弥散方程，基于快速平均L1方法进行了误差分析，通过积分因子变换将 tempered 分数阶导数转化为标准Caputo导数，结合指数和近似降低计算成本，建立了稳定性与H1范数收敛性分析，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献中对于具有初始弱奇异性的时间分数阶对流-弥散方程的误差分析尚不充分，需要建立更严格的收敛性理论，并解决 tempered 分数阶导数离散化的数值计算效率问题。

Method: 引入积分因子变换将 tempered 分数阶导数转化为标准Caputo导数，采用快速平均L1方法进行离散化，结合指数和近似(sum-of-exponentials approximation)降低计算和存储成本，通过推导离散系数和累积截断误差的误差估计建立稳定性与收敛性分析。

Result: 建立了高于已有文献的收敛阶的H1范数收敛分析，数值实验验证了理论结果，讨论了分数阶参数α和λ对解的影响，并观察到分数阶情形下的记忆效应和长时间尾现象。

Conclusion: 所提出的快速平均L1方法能有效处理初始弱奇异性，在保证精度的同时降低计算成本，且分数阶方程能捕捉经典整数阶方程无法描述的物理系统中的记忆效应和长时间尾现象。

Abstract: In this paper, based on the fast averaged L1 method, we present an error analysis for time-fractional advection-dispersion equations with a weak singularity at the initial time. An integrating-factor transformation is introduced to convert the tempered fractional derivative into the standard Caputo derivative, which is more suitable for discretization using the fast averaged L1 method. A sum-of-exponentials approximation is then incorporated into the averaged L1 method to reduce computational cost and storage while preserving the desired accuracy. By deriving error estimates for the discrete coefficients and the accumulated truncation errors, we establish the stability and $H^1$-norm convergence analysis, with a convergence order higher than those in the published literature. Numerical examples are tested to validate our theoretical results. The effects of the fractional parameters $α$ and $λ$ on the solution are discussed. The memory effect and long-time tail phenomenon, which are known to exist in real systems yet cannot be captured by classical integer-order equations, are again found in the current fractional case.

</details>


### [130] [A Fractional-Memory Physics-Informed Neural Network with Fast History Compression for Tempered Fractional Coupled Phase-Field Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22191)
*Shubham Kumar,Himanshu Kumar Dwivedi,Matthias Ehrhardt,Rajeev*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出FM-tfPINN框架，通过将回火分数阶记忆直接嵌入神经表示、结合分级时间网格和SOE历史压缩的快速移位残差格式，以及界面感知和残差自适应配点策略，有效解决了回火时间分数阶耦合相场系统（tTFCP）的正向模拟和逆参数辨识问题，在腐蚀相场模型上验证了其准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回火时间分数阶耦合相场系统因同时存在非局部时间算子、弱初始奇异性、移动扩散界面和强耦合多物理场动力学，数值求解极具挑战性；传统分数阶PINN仅通过残差约束施加记忆效应，无法充分捕捉回火分数阶记忆机制。

Method: 提出FM-tfPINN框架：(1) 通过潜在记忆源函数和回火分数阶积分算子将回火分数阶记忆直接嵌入神经表示；(2) 采用基于分级时间网格和指数和（SOE）历史压缩的快速移位残差格式，高效评估回火分数阶算子；(3) 结合界面感知和残差自适应配点策略，提高演化扩散界面附近的分辨率；(4) 统一的物理信息损失函数支持正向预测和逆向参数恢复。

Result: 在回火分数阶腐蚀相场模型（包括一维腐蚀前沿传播、活化控制与扩散控制机制、二维点蚀腐蚀及逆迁移率辨识问题）上验证了方法的有效性，数值结果表明：耦合相场和浓度场的精确恢复、界面诊断指标的稳健预测，以及有限数据下参数的可靠估计。

Conclusion: FM-tfPINN框架有效解决了回火分数阶耦合相场系统的正向模拟和逆问题，将回火分数阶记忆直接嵌入神经表示相较于传统分数阶PINN方法具有显著优势，为处理具有非局部记忆效应的复杂多物理界面问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: Tempered time-fractional coupled phase-field (tTFCP) systems are used to model interfacial phenomena involving memory-dependent transport and relaxation mechanisms. Numerical solutions to these systems are challenging due to the simultaneous presence of nonlocal temporal operators, weak initial singularities, moving diffuse interfaces, and strongly coupled multiphysics dynamics. In this work, we introduce FM-tfPINN (fractional-memory physics-informed neural network), which is used for forward simulation and inverse parameter identification in tempered fractional coupled phase-field systems. Unlike conventional fractional PINNs, which enforce memory effects solely through residual constraints, our framework incorporates tempered fractional memory directly into the neural representation via latent memory-source functions and a tempered fractional integral operator. We develop a fast shifted residual formulation based on graded temporal meshes and sum-of-exponentials (SOE) history compression to efficiently evaluate the tempered fractional operators. This framework combines interface-aware and residual-adaptive collocation strategies, improving resolution near evolving diffuse interfaces. A unified, physics-informed loss formulation allows for the forward prediction and inverse recovery of unknown physical parameters from sparse observations.
  We assess the proposed method on a class of tempered fractional corrosion phase-field models, including one-dimensional corrosion-front propagation, activation- and diffusion-controlled regimes, two-dimensional pitting corrosion, and inverse mobility identification problems. The numerical results demonstrate the accurate recovery of coupled phase and concentration fields, the robust prediction of physically relevant interface diagnostics, and the reliable estimation of parameters from limited data.

</details>


### [131] [Compensated Splitting For Generalized Lyapunov Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22245)
*Hongjia Chen,Ren-Cang Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对广义Lyapunov方程的标准不动点迭代（sFPI）可能发散或收敛慢的问题，提出了一种补偿分裂方案（cFPI），通过降低线性算子谱半径来改善收敛性，数值实验验证了cFPI优于sFPI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准固定点迭代（sFPI）在求解广义Lyapunov方程时，当相关线性算子谱半径大于等于1时可能发散，且收敛速度受限于谱半径大小，需要设计收敛性更好的迭代方案。

Method: 提出一种补偿分裂方案，通过修改原始分裂结构来降低线性算子的谱半径，从而得到补偿固定点迭代（cFPI）。

Result: cFPI在sFPI发散时仍能收敛，在sFPI收敛时收敛速度更快。

Conclusion: 补偿分裂方案能有效改善广义Lyapunov方程迭代求解的收敛性，cFPI相比sFPI具有更优的收敛性能。

Abstract: The generalized Lyapunov equation has a natural splitting that leads to the standard fixed-point iteration (sFPI) for its numerical solution. sFPI is convergent, for an arbitrarily given initial guess, if and only if the spectral radius of the associated linear operator is smaller than $1$. This means that sFPI may diverge if the spectral radius is $1$ or bigger. In this paper, we propose a compensated splitting scheme that aims to reduce the spectral radius so that the resulting compensated fixed-point iteration (cFPI) has a better convergence property, namely, cFPI may still converge even if sFPI does not or cFPI converges faster than sFPI does when the latter is also convergent. Numerical results are presented to demonstrate the superiority of cFPI to sFPI.

</details>


### [132] [Energy Dissipation Analysis of Implicit-Explicit Linear Multistep Methods for Gradient Flows Using General Multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22290)
*Chaoyu Quan,Huaijin Wang,Xuping Wang,Chuanju Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，通过广义乘法器将Dahlquist的G-稳定性理论推广到隐式-显式线性多步法（IMEX-LMM）中，建立了梯度流求解中高阶IMEX-LMM的修正能量耗散性质，首次实现了六阶IMEX-BDF6及七阶以上IMEX-LMM的能量耗散分析，并构造了一个新的八阶能量耗散IMEX-LMM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶IMEX-LMM在梯度流数值求解中广泛应用，但其能量耗散性质（尤其是六阶以上方法）尚未建立。现有理论框架无法直接处理高阶IMEX-LMM的修正能量耗散分析，亟需一个统一的、可系统化验证能量耗散条件的理论工具。

Method: 基于广义乘法器（数值解一阶差分的线性组合）测试格式，推广Dahlquist G-稳定性理论，将修正能量耗散条件转化为乘法器系数的可行性问题；利用单位圆上的正性不等式，通过离散化将问题松弛为有限维线性规划问题，从而系统求解合适的乘法器。

Result: 获得了IMEX-BDF6和七阶IMEX加权移位BDF的乘法器及能量耗散证明；构造了一个新的八阶能量耗散IMEX-LMM及其对应乘法器；数值实验验证了时间精度和修正能量耗散性质。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功建立了六阶及以上IMEX-LMM的首次能量耗散结果，并可推广至线性抛物问题的L²和H¹稳定性分析，为高阶IMEX方法的设计与分析提供了系统路径。

Abstract: A unified framework is proposed to establish the energy dissipation of implicit-explicit linear multistep methods (IMEX-LMMs) applied to gradient flows. This framework is based on testing the schemes with a general multiplier, defined as a linear combination of first-order differences of the numerical solutions. The Dahlquist's G-stability theory is generalized to derive conditions on the coefficients of the IMEX-LMM and the multiplier for modified energy dissipation. Given a fixed IMEX-LMM, it is shown that finding an appropriate multiplier for constructing the dissipative energy can be formulated as a feasibility problem over the multiplier coefficients, where the involved positivity inequalities on the unit circle are relaxed to a finite-dimensional linear programming problem after discretization. Within this framework, two specific multipliers are obtained to establish the energy dissipation of the sixth-order IMEX backward differentiation formula (IMEX-BDF6) and a seventh-order IMEX weighted and shifted BDF, and a new eighth-order energy-dissipative IMEX-LMM is developed together with its multiplier. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first energy-dissipation results for the IMEX-BDF6 method and the IMEX-LMMs of order higher than six. It should be mentioned that this framework can also be applied without difficulty to the $L^2$- and $H^1$-stability analysis of general LMMs for linear parabolic problems. Numerical experiments illustrate the temporal accuracy and modified energy dissipation of the schemes studied.

</details>


### [133] [Optimal $hp$-error estimates and $p$-multigrid convergence for Hybrid High-Order discretizations of the Poisson equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22492)
*Daniele A. Di Pietro,Emil Hössjer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对椭圆问题的混合高阶方法，建立了关于网格尺寸h和多项式阶数k的最优hp误差估计，并首次提出了HHO离散的p-多重网格求解器的严格收敛分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合方法的收敛估计在多项式阶数k上非最优；同时缺乏HHO离散格式下p-多重网格算法的严格收敛理论。

Method: 推导Poisson问题HHO离散的hp-误差估计（在h和k上均达到最优逼近率）；基于此估计设计并分析非继承型p-多重网格求解器（针对静态凝聚后的HHO系统）。

Result: 在h和k上均达到最优逼近率的hp误差估计；改进了其他非协调方法的多重网格理论，首次为HHO离散提供了p-多重网格算法的收敛性分析。

Conclusion: HHO方法的hp误差估计达到了最优逼近率；所提出的p-多重网格求解器具有严格收敛保证，填补了HHO方法在多重网格理论方面的空白。

Abstract: This paper presents two new theoretical results for Hybrid High-Order (HHO) methods applied to elliptic problems. First, we establish $hp$-error estimates for the HHO discretization of the Poisson problem that achieve optimal approximation rates with respect to both the mesh size $h$ and the polynomial degree $k$. These results improve upon previous analyses of hybrid methods, whose convergence estimates were suboptimal in $k$. Second, building on these estimates, we develop and analyze a non-inherited $p$-multigrid solver for the statically condensed HHO system. We prove results that improve upon the corresponding theory available for other non-conforming methods and constitute, to the best of our knowledge, the first rigorous convergence analysis of a $p$-multigrid algorithm for HHO discretizations.

</details>


### [134] [PI-DOSnet: A Physics-Informed Deep Operator-Splitting Network for Evolution Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22514)
*Jizu Huang,Yue Qian,Tao Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出PI-DOSnet，一种融合DOSnet和算子分裂的物理信息算子学习框架，用于高效求解演化偏微分方程。该框架在训练中融入物理约束，无需配对输入-输出数据，通过迭代策略实现长时间推断，并在线性稳定性和近似误差方面进行了理论分析。数值实验表明该方法准确、高效、鲁棒，且在Allen-Cahn方程上即使采用大时间步长也能保持能量稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演化PDE在科学工程中广泛存在，而算子学习作为一种高效的求解范式无需显式时间步积分。然而纯数据驱动的算子学习方法依赖配对输入-输出数据，限制了其适用性。本文旨在提出一种可在无配对数据条件下训练的物理信息算子学习框架。

Method: 基于DOSnet和算子分裂构建物理信息算子学习框架PI-DOSnet，在训练过程中融入物理约束，采用迭代策略进行长时间推断，并进行了线性稳定性与近似误差的理论分析。

Result: 数值实验验证了PI-DOSnet的准确性、效率和鲁棒性；在Allen-Cahn方程上，即使采用大时间步长也能获得能量稳定的解。

Conclusion: PI-DOSnet作为一种融合物理信息的算子学习方法，有效克服了对配对输入-输出数据的依赖，兼具理论保证与数值性能，为演化PDE的高效求解提供了新途径。

Abstract: Evolution partial differential equations (PDEs) describe time-dependent physical systems governed by differential laws and arise widely across science and engineering. In recent years, operator learning has emerged as a powerful and efficient paradigm for solving evolution PDEs by learning mappings between infinite-dimensional function spaces, enabling solution prediction without explicit time-step integration. In this work, we propose PI-DOSnet, a physics-informed operator learning framework built upon DOSnet and operator splitting. Unlike purely data-driven operator learning methods, PI-DOSnet incorporates physical constraints during training, allowing it to operate even in the absence of paired input-output data. Once trained, PI-DOSnet performs long-time inference of PDE solutions through an iterative strategy. We analyze the linear stability and approximation error of PI-DOSnet and demonstrate its accuracy, efficiency, and robustness through multiple numerical experiments. Moreover, for the Allen--Cahn equation, PI-DOSnet achieves energy stable solutions even with a large time-step size.

</details>


### [135] [A Robust Cell-Centered Nodal Integral Method (RCCNIM) for Nonlinear Burgers' Equation: Accurate Formulation, Efficient Implementation, and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22549)
*Nadeem Ahmed,Ram Prakash Bharti*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种改进的单元中心节点积分法(RCCNIM)用于求解非线性Burgers方程，通过对流项的速度评估采用上一时间步的单元平均值替代当前时间步近似，使得系数仅依赖于已知量，从而在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低原始MCCNIM求解非线性Burgers方程时的计算成本，同时保持其精度。

Method: 在离散化前重新构造非线性对流项，将对流速度评估从当前时间步近似改为使用上一时间步的单元平均值，使得代数系统系数仅依赖于已知量，每时间步只需计算一次。

Result: RCCNIM在与MCCNIM的系统性数值比较中达到了相当的精度，同时计算效率更高。

Conclusion: RCCNIM在保持精度的同时提升了计算效率，具备推广至更复杂非线性流体流动问题的潜力。

Abstract: An improved formulation of the recently developed cell-centered nodal integral method (MCCNIM) is proposed for the numerical solution of the nonlinear Burgers' equation. The improved scheme, referred to as RCCNIM, reformulates the nonlinear convection term prior to discretization by evaluating the convective velocity using cell-averaged values from the previous time level, rather than the present-time approximation used in the original MCCNIM formulation. This reformulation leads to a fully algebraic system whose coefficients depend only on known quantities and are therefore evaluated once per time step. As a result, the proposed method significantly reduces computational cost while retaining the accuracy of the original MCCNIM. The performance of RCCNIM is assessed through systematic numerical comparisons with MCCNIM for the nonlinear Burgers' equation. The results demonstrate that RCCNIM achieves comparable accuracy with improved computational efficiency, indicating its potential for extension to more complex nonlinear fluid flow problems.

</details>


### [136] [Counting Triangles of Graphs via Randomized Trace Estimation with Incomplete Matrix-Vector Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22629)
*Soumyadip Ghosh,Lior Horesh,Vasileios Kalantzis,Yingdong Lu,Tomasz Nowicki,Shashanka Ubaru*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在部分观测约束下运行的Hutchinson随机迹估计器变体，通过随机观测邻接矩阵乘积的条目来近似三角形数量，在保证无偏性和可控方差的同时降低分布式异构计算环境中的通信开销和掉队者影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模图的分布式异构计算环境中，传统的Hutchinson迹估计器需要观测矩阵向量乘积的所有条目，这会导致高昂的通信开销和掉队者（straggler）问题。本文旨在解决这一实际部署中的瓶颈，使三角形计数算法更适应现代计算架构。

Method: 提出一种部分观测Hutchinson估计器变体，其中观测条目的数量和身份均为随机；理论分析证明了估计的无偏性、方差界和样本复杂度；在合成和真实世界图上进行实验验证。

Result: 该方法在保持准确三角形计数估计的同时，显著降低了同步成本；理论保证了估计的无偏性和方差可控性；实验表明在合成图和真实图上均有效。

Conclusion: 本文展示了随机化算法对现代计算架构的适应性，为大规模网络分析中的高效模体计数开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Counting triangles in graphs is a fundamental operation in network analysis, underpinning metrics such as clustering coefficients and serving as a signal for community detection, link prediction, and anomaly detection. The standard approach computes the trace of the cube of the adjacency matrix, but explicitly forming $\mathbf{A}^3$ is infeasible for large graphs. Hutchinson randomized trace estimator offers an efficient alternative by approximating the trace through stochastic averaging of quadratic forms, requiring only matrix vector products with $\mathbf{A}$. However, in distributed and heterogeneous computing environments, observing all entries of these products can be costly due to communication overhead and straggler effects. To address this, we propose a new variant of Hutchinson estimator that operates under partial observation constraints, where both the number and identities of observed entries are random. We provide theoretical guarantees on unbiasedness, variance bounds, and sample complexity, and demonstrate through experiments on synthetic and real world graphs that our method achieves accurate triangle count estimates while reducing synchronization costs. This work highlights the adaptability of randomized algorithms to modern computational architectures and opens avenues for efficient motif counting in large scale network analytics.

</details>


### [137] [Interpolations: a linear algebra approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22671)
*Andronick Arutyunov,Nikolay Korgin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的线性代数框架，利用对偶空间将拉格朗日插值、埃尔米特样条和三角插值等标准插值问题统一表述。通过将插值条件表示为有限维函数空间对偶中的线性泛函，建立了通用的存在唯一性定理，推导了对偶基函数的显式公式，并扩展到异质传感器场景下的插值方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有插值方法（拉格朗日、埃尔米特、三角插值）通常被独立处理，缺乏统一的数学框架来揭示其内在联系。本文旨在构建一个统一视角，以便系统性分析和构造各类插值问题。

Method: 采用对偶空间（dual space）的线性代数框架：将插值条件视为有限维函数空间对偶中的线性泛函，推导对偶基函数的显式闭式表达式，并分析退化条件。方法系统化、维度感知，将插值条件的分析与基函数的选择解耦。

Result: 建立了通用的存在唯一性定理，给出了对偶基的闭式表达式，扩展至三角插值（含退化条件分析），并提出了异质传感器场景（不同点指定函数值和导数值）的插值方案。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了跨不同插值问题的统一性，通过运动学和周期函数插值示例验证了其 versatility，预示在轨迹规划、信号处理等领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified linear-algebraic framework for interpolation problems, viewing different standart problems (Lagrange polynomials, Hermite splines, and trigonometric interpolation) with the help of dual spaces. By representing interpolation conditions as linear functionals in the dual of a finite-dimensional function space, we establish a general existence and uniqueness theorem.
  We derive explicit formulas for basis functions dual to given interpolation schemes, enabling efficient construction of interpolants as linear combinations of these bases.
  The approach is extended to trigonometric interpolation, where we provide closed-form expressions for dual bases and analyze degeneracy conditions. We also address practical scenarios involving heterogeneous sensors, where function values and derivatives are specified at distinct points, and propose a corresponding interpolation scheme. The method is systematic, dimension-aware, and decouples the analysis of interpolation conditions from the choice of basis functions.
  We cosider examples from kinematics and periodic function interpolation demonstrate the versatility of the approach, suggesting applications in trajectory planning, signal processing, and beyond.

</details>


### [138] [Factored Sparse Approximate Inverse Preconditioning via Spectral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22742)
*Francesco Brarda,Tianshi Xu,Vassilis Kalantzis,Rui Peng Li,Yuanzhe Xi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对固定模式因式分解稀疏近似逆预条件子的谱优化值选择方法，通过投影Krylov支撑梯度优化谱目标，显著改进了对称不定鞍点系统的预条件效果，并展示了图神经网络预测因子条目的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的稀疏近似逆（SAI）和FSAI构造方法基于代数Frobenius残差准则选择条目，而非直接优化谱目标。本文旨在通过谱目标优化来改进固定模式预条件子的值选择，以更好地聚拢特征值，提升预条件效果。

Method: 1) 对于对称正定系统，优化目标为特征值聚类于+1附近；对于对称不定系统，引入双峰损失函数，将正负特征值分别驱向+1和-1，并惩罚接近零的特征值。2) 推导了投影Krylov支撑梯度，利用Lanczos过程提供谱目标的随机迹估计和精确梯度的Ritz近似。3) 通过分离Rayleigh代理实现梯度计算：前向Lanczos数据固定不变，反向传播仅微分重新计算的Rayleigh商。4) 还讨论了投影Kernel Polynomial Method规则作为有限多项式比较。

Result: 在有限元测试问题上的实验表明，谱值选择方法改进了固定支撑预条件子，尤其对对称不定鞍点系统效果显著。此外，展示了使用图神经网络跨相关矩阵预测可容许因子条目的能力。

Conclusion: 谱目标优化相比传统Frobenius残差准则能更有效地选择固定模式FSAI预条件子的值，特别是对于对称不定系统。所提出的投影Krylov支撑梯度方法避免了直接通过Lanczos递归进行微分，实现了可扩展的矩阵自由梯度计算。

Abstract: In this paper, we study value selection for fixed-pattern factorized sparse approximate inverse preconditioners. Given a prescribed sparsity pattern for a factor $G,$ we choose its admissible entries by optimizing spectral objectives of the congruent preconditioned operator $P(G)=GAG^T.$ This differs from classical sparse approximate inverse and FSAI constructions, which choose entries through algebraic Frobenius-residual criteria. For symmetric positive definite systems, the spectral target is a cluster near $+1.$ For symmetric indefinite systems, where congruence preserves inertia, we introduce a bimodal loss that drives positive and negative eigenvalues toward separated clusters near $+1$ and $-1,$ while penalizing eigenvalues near zero. To make these objectives practical for large sparse matrices, we derive projected Krylov support-gradients. Lanczos runs provide both a stochastic trace estimate of the spectral objective and a Ritz approximation to the exact gradient. We implement the resulting gradient through a detached Rayleigh surrogate: the Lanczos data are computed without gradient tracking and held fixed, while the backward pass differentiates only recomputed Rayleigh quotients with respect to the admissible entries of $G.$ This avoids differentiating through the Lanczos recurrence while returning a matrix-free gradient on the prescribed support. We also discuss a projected Kernel Polynomial Method rule as a finite polynomial comparison. Experiments on finite-element test problems show that spectral value selection improves fixed-support preconditioners, especially for symmetric indefinite saddle-point systems. We further demonstrate a graph neural network model for predicting admissible factor entries across related matrices.

</details>


### [139] [Locally conservative redistribution limiting and applications to the approximation of conservation equations, Part II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22839)
*Jean-Luc Guermond*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种非侵入式、与离散化和PDE无关的限制技术，该技术具有守恒性，可应用于包括谱方法在内的高阶近似，适用于时间依赖/独立问题以及椭圆、抛物和双曲问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有限制技术通常与特定离散化格式或PDE类型绑定，缺乏通用性；同时需要一种非侵入式（即不破坏原有求解器结构）的通用方法。

Method: 提出一种非侵入式、与离散化和PDE类型无关的限制技术，该方法保持守恒性，兼容高阶近似（包括谱方法），可同时处理时间依赖和时间独立问题。

Result: 该方法成功实现了与离散化和PDE类型无关的通用限制，保持了守恒性，适用于多种问题类型（椭圆、抛物、双曲）和数值格式（高阶、谱方法）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种通用、非侵入式且守恒的限制技术，突破了传统限制器对特定离散化和PDE类型的依赖，具有良好的普适性和应用前景。

Abstract: A non-intrusive, discretization and PDE agnostic limiting technique is introduced. The method is conservative and can be applied to high-order approximations including spectral methods. It can be applied to time-dependent and time-independent problems. It can be used for elliptic, parabolic and hyperbolic problems.

</details>


### [140] [Computation of the Fourier transform for a continuous integrable function via NFFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22970)
*Daniel Potts,Manfred Tasche*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用三角采样多项式逼近连续傅里叶变换，并通过非均匀快速傅里叶变换（NFFT）高效求值的方法。基于Ehler等人的工作，推导了傅里叶变换与采样多项式之间的显式ℓ∞误差界，量化了采样宽度和截断参数的影响，在完整频率区间上提供了统一的逼近保证。数值实验验证了理论收敛速率，表明以中等计算量即可获得连续傅里叶变换的精确逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NFFT主要用于三角多项式求值，本文旨在证明其也可作为逼近连续傅里叶变换的有效计算工具，并建立严格的误差理论以指导实际计算。

Method: 首先利用三角采样多项式逼近连续傅里叶变换，然后通过NFFT高效求值；基于Ehler、Gröchenig和Klotz的思想，导出显式的ℓ∞误差界，分析采样宽度和截断参数的影响。

Result: 获得了傅里叶变换与采样多项式之间关于整个频率区间的统一ℓ∞误差界，数值实验验证了理论收敛速率，证明以中等计算量即可实现精确逼近。

Conclusion: 提出的算法框架简单有效，NFFT不仅能用于三角多项式求值，也可作为连续傅里叶变换逼近的高效工具，且相比以往离散或L2误差分析，本文的统一逼近界对实际计算更具直接指导意义。

Abstract: We investigate the approximation of continuous Fourier transforms by
  trigonometric sampling polynomials and their efficient evaluation by the
  nonequispaced fast Fourier transform (NFFT). While the NFFT is traditionally
  used for the evaluation of trigonometric polynomials, we show that it can also
  serve as an effective computational tool for the approximation of Fourier
  transform values.
  Building on ideas of M.~Ehler, K.~Gröchenig, and A.~Klotz \cite{EhGrKl24}, we derive explicit
  $\ell_\infty$ error bounds between the Fourier transform and suitable sampling
  polynomials. The resulting estimates quantify the influence of the sampling
