  width and truncation parameter and provide rigorous accuracy guarantees on
  entire frequency intervals. In contrast to previous analyses focusing mainly on
  discrete or $L_2$-type errors, our results yield uniform approximation bounds
  that are directly relevant for practical computations.
  The derived theory leads to a simple algorithmic framework: first approximate
  the Fourier transform by a trigonometric sampling polynomial and then evaluate this
  polynomial efficiently by the NFFT. Numerical experiments confirm the
  theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate that accurate approximations of
  continuous Fourier transforms can be obtained with moderate computational
  effort.

</details>


### [141] [The Bojanov--Naidenov inequality for quartics and second derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23020)
*Gentian Zavalani*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文解决了Bojanov--Naidenov问题在n=4, k=2情况下的代数多项式情形，证明了对于次数不超过4的实多项式P，其第二导数绝对值的积分不等式成立，且等号仅当P=±T_4时取得。证明过程是初等且有限的，通过插值和凸性分析转化为32种符号组合的显式比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Bojanov--Naidenov问题在代数多项式情况下的特例(n=4, k=2)，即寻找在L¹范数意义下使得多项式二阶导数积分达到最大的极值多项式。

Method: 在T_4的五个极值点处进行插值；利用凸性将问题简化为32种符号选择的组合情形；在每个符号组合下二阶导数为二次多项式，通过显式比较水平集完成证明。

Result: 证明了对于任意t≥0，截断积分不等式成立；进而对任意非递减凸函数φ，φ(|P''(x)|)的积分以φ(|T_4''(x)|)的积分为上界；当φ严格递增且凸时，等号仅当P=±T_4时成立。

Conclusion: 成功解决了Bojanov--Naidenov问题在n=4, k=2时的代数多项式情形，且给出了一个初等且有限的证明。

Abstract: We settle the case $n=4$, $k=2$ of the Bojanov--Naidenov problem for algebraic polynomials. Let $P$ be a real polynomial of degree at most four with $\left\lVert{P}\right\rVert_{C[-1,1]}\leq 1$, and let $T_4(x)=8x^4-8x^2+1$. We prove that, for every $t\geq0$, \[
  \int_{-1}^{1} \bigl(|P''(x)|-t\bigr)_+\,dx
  \leq
  \int_{-1}^{1} \bigl(|T_4''(x)|-t\bigr)_+\,dx . \] This tail estimate implies \[
  \int_{-1}^{1}\varphi(|P''(x)|)\,dx
  \leq
  \int_{-1}^{1}\varphi(|T_4''(x)|)\,dx \] for every nondecreasing convex function $\varphi:[0,\infty)\to\mathbb{R}$. If $\varphi$ is strictly increasing and convex, equality can occur only for $P=\pm T_4$. The proof is elementary and finite. We interpolate at the five extremal points of $T_4$; convexity then reduces the problem to the $32$ sign choices at these nodes. At each vertex the second derivative is a quadratic polynomial, so the remaining work is an explicit comparison of level sets.

</details>


### [142] [A WKB-based fixed-grid method for capturing trait concentration in a dispersal evolution model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23093)
*Weijie Huang,Xinran Ruan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对进化生态学中扩散性状演化模型在稀有突变条件下出现的奇异集中行为，提出了一种基于WKB方法的数值框架，通过将指数集中的性状依赖与平滑振幅变量分离，结合双性状网格实现等技术，在固定网格上高效准确地恢复了选择性状及其集中结构，并建立了稀有突变极限的半离散稳态渐进保持结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散性状演化是进化生态学的核心议题，但在稀有突变条件下自然选择会使性状分布呈现高度集中行为，直接离散化种群密度需要极细的性状网格才能准确识别最适应性状并解析集中分布，存在严重的数值困难。

Method: 发展了一种基于WKB近似的数值框架：将指数集中的性状依赖部分与更平滑的振幅变量分离；结合双性状网格实现及其他专门设计的技术；建立了稳态问题稀有突变极限的半离散固定网格渐进保持结构。

Result: 该方法能够在固定性状网格上高效准确地恢复被选择的性状及其集中结构；数值实验将所提方法与直接密度离散化方法进行了对比，验证了该方法在小区间突变参数下的优势。

Conclusion: 提出的WKB数值框架有效克服了稀有突变条件下扩散性状演化模型的数值困难，在固定网格上即可精确捕捉性状的指数集中结构，为进化生态学中的此类奇异行为问题提供了一种高效、准确的数值求解方案。

Abstract: The evolution of dispersal traits is a fundamental topic in evolutionary ecology, where natural selection may drive the trait distribution toward concentration in the rare-mutation regime. This singular behavior poses a serious numerical difficulty, since direct discretizations of the population density require very fine trait grids to identify the fittest trait and to resolve the concentrated profile accurately. In this paper, we develop a WKB-based numerical framework for a dispersal evolution model. By separating the exponentially concentrated trait dependence from a smoother amplitude variable and combining this WKB representation with dual trait-grid implementation and other specially designed techniques, the method recovers the selected trait and the associated concentration structure accurately and efficiently on fixed trait grids. We establish a semi-discrete stationary fixed-grid asymptotic-preserving structure for the rare-mutation limit of the steady-state problem. Numerical experiments compare the proposed method with direct density discretizations and confirm its advantage in the small-mutation regime.

</details>


### [143] [Turing-region preservation in matrix-oriented splitting methods for reaction-diffusion systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23211)
*Angela Monti,Fasma Diele,Carmela Marangi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对二维两物种反应扩散系统（以Gierer-Meinhardt模型为基准），发展了基于矩阵形式的一阶分裂积分器（IE-S和EX-S族），推导了全离散模态放大矩阵及其Jury条件，揭示了数值积分器会诱导出与连续Turing区域不同的离散Turing区域，可能导致伪影模式或虚假稳定，强调了在解释数值模式前需比较离散与连续Turing区域，属于结构保持数值积分研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展面向两物种反应扩散系统的矩阵形式一阶分裂积分器，并系统分析离散化对Turing不稳定性的影响，揭示数值积分器可能引入伪影或抑制真实模式的问题。

Method: 基于张量积空间离散化的微分矩阵方程，构造IE-S（隐式Euler处理扩散）和EX-S（精确处理扩散）两类分裂积分器，其中反应子步分别采用显式、辛、伴随辛、Poisson和显式变体局部映射近似。从连续扩散驱动不稳定性阈值出发，推导全离散模态放大矩阵及其Jury条件，将连续Turing机制（第一Jury条件）与离散效应（第二Jury条件）分离。

Result: 在连续Turing稳定区域，IMEX可能因违反第二Jury条件产生稳定伪影模式；在真实连续Turing区域，伴随辛族可能虚假稳定从而抑制IMEX正确检测的模式。给出了IMEX保证连续Turing区域保持的时间步条件，指出仅需在Turing稳定区域控制第二Jury条件。

Conclusion: 每个积分器诱导其自身的离散Turing区域，在解释数值模式前必须与连续Turing区域进行比较；这一要求被框架为保持连续问题的定性性质，符合结构保持数值积分的精神。

Abstract: We develop matrix-oriented formulations of first-order splitting integrators for two-species reaction-diffusion systems on 2D domains, using the Gierer-Meinhardt system as a benchmark for discrete Turing instability. Exploiting the differential matrix equation associated with tensor-product spatial discretizations, we obtain the families IE-S and EX-S, where the diffusive flow is treated by implicit Euler or exactly, respectively, and the reaction substep is approximated by explicit, symplectic, adjoint-symplectic, Poisson, and explicit-variant local maps. Starting from the continuous diffusion-driven instability threshold, expressed through the modal relation $J_μ= J^* - μD$, we derive fully discrete modal amplification matrices and their Jury conditions. These conditions separate the continuous Turing mechanism, carried by the first Jury condition, from discrete effects carried by the second. Specializing the analysis to the Gierer-Meinhardt model, we exhibit two opposite pathologies. First, in a continuous Turing-stable regime, IMEX may generate a stable spurious pattern through a violation of the second Jury condition. Second, in a real continuous Turing regime, the adjoint-symplectic family may be spuriously stable and suppress the pattern that IMEX correctly detects. For IMEX, whose first Jury condition reproduces exactly the sign of the continuous Turing polynomial, we give an explicit time-step condition guaranteeing preservation of the continuous Turing region, and show that controlling the second Jury condition is needed only in the Turing-stable regime. These examples show that each integrator induces its own discrete Turing region, which should be compared with the continuous one before interpreting numerical patterns; we frame this requirement as preserving a qualitative property of the continuous problem, in the spirit of structure-preserving numerical integration.

</details>


### [144] [On Runge-Kutta convolution quadrature based fractional variational integrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23302)
*Khaled Hariz Belgacem,Sina Ober-BlÖbaum,Fernando JimÉnez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对分数阶阻尼拉格朗日系统，提出基于Runge-Kutta卷积求积(RKCQ)与高阶Galerkin方法结合的分数阶变分积分器(FVIs)，克服了BD-FCQ方法精度饱和问题，实现了2阶、4阶和6阶精度，并验证了能量衰减等守恒性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于BD-FCQ的分数阶变分积分器由于多步法结构与Galerkin离散内部阶段不兼容，精度饱和在二阶，需开发更高阶的分数阶变分积分器。

Method: 将Runge-Kutta卷积求积(RKCQ)用于分数阶导数逼近，与高阶Galerkin方法结合；特别采用基于Lobatto IIIC的CQ构造分数阶变分积分器。

Result: 所提方案达到2阶、4阶和6阶精度；数值验证了能量衰减等守恒性质及收敛性，二阶方案有理论证明。

Conclusion: RKCQ方法天然兼容基于阶段的离散化，更适合构造高阶分数阶变分积分器；讨论了中点分数阶积分器。

Abstract: Lagrangian systems subject to fractional damping can be incorporated into a variational framework by doubling the state variables and introducing fractional derivatives. Fractional variational integrators based on backward-differentiation convolution quadrature (BD-FCQ), combined with higher-order Galerkin methods, saturate at second-order accuracy because the multistep structure of BDFCQ does not take into account the internal stages of the Galerkin discretization. The main objective of this paper is to develop fractional variational integrators (FVIs) by combining Runge-Kutta convolution quadrature (RKCQ) for the approximation of fractional derivatives with higher-order Galerkin methods. The RKCQ approach is naturally compatible with such stage-based discretizations and is therefore better suited for the construction of higher-order schemes. We are particularly interested in the CQ based on Lobatto IIIC. Preservation properties such as energy decay, as well as convergence properties, are investigated numerically and proved for second-order schemes. The presented schemes reach 2nd, 4th and 6th order of accuracy. A brief discussion on the midpoint fractional integrator is also included.

</details>


### [145] [A Compact Two-Stage Fourth-Order Two-Derivative IMEX Method with Mixed Compatibility, High Implicit-Solve Efficiency,and Enhanced Stiff Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23319)
*Zhixin Huo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对加性分裂刚性演化问题，提出了一种基于双导数公式的紧凑型四阶IMEX方法，仅需一个中间阶段和两次隐式求解即可实现四阶精度，在纯隐式标量极限下稳定性因子呈二次衰减（优于经典方法的线性衰减），并通过数值实验验证了混合一致性、刚性衰减特性及同等隐式求解预算下更小的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典四阶IMEX Runge-Kutta方法在处理加性分裂刚性演化问题时，通常需要多个阶段且耦合阶条件复杂，计算效率受限。本文旨在设计一种更紧凑、高效的四阶IMEX格式。

Method: 基于双导数公式构建紧凑型四阶IMEX格式，通过沿全向量场的时间导数评估实现显式-隐式混合交互，确保非交换分裂系统中的混合兼容性。格式仅含一个中间阶段和两次隐式求解。

Result: 所提方法在纯隐式标量极限下稳定性因子呈二次衰减（经典方法为线性衰减）；在非交换分裂系统、标量刚性模态阻尼、递增刚性测试及一/二维对流扩散高频问题中，均验证了混合一致性、预测的刚性衰减特性，并在强刚性区域以同等隐式求解预算实现了更小的误差。

Conclusion: 本文提出的紧凑型双导数四阶IMEX方法在保持四阶精度的同时，显著提升了每隐式求解的计算效率，并在刚性极限下展现出更强的稳定性衰减特性，适用于非交换分裂刚性演化问题的高效求解。

Abstract: For additively split stiff evolution problems, classical fourth-order IMEX Runge-Kutta methods usually require several stages and complicated coupling order conditions. This paper proposes a compact fourth-order IMEX-type method based on a two-derivative formulation and analyzes its accuracy and stability properties. The proposed scheme incorporates the mixed explicit-implicit interaction directly through temporal derivatives evaluated along the full vector field, which ensures mixed compatibility for non-commuting split systems. With only one intermediate stage and two implicit solves per time step, the method achieves fourth-order accuracy while improving the accuracy obtained per implicit solve compared with classical multi-stage fourth-order IMEX-RK schemes. In addition, the method exhibits stronger damping of stiff modes in the purely implicit scalar limit and in the strong implicit-stiffness limit with a fixed explicit component. In the purely implicit scalar limit, its stability factor decays quadratically as the stiffness increases, whereas a representative classical fourth-order IMEX-RK reference method shows only linear decay. Numerical experiments on non-commuting split systems, scalar stiff-mode damping, increasing stiffness tests, and one- and two-dimensional advection-diffusion high-mode problems confirm the mixed consistency, the predicted stiff decay, and the smaller errors obtained under equal implicit-solve budgets in strongly stiff regimes.

</details>


### [146] [High-Order Invariant-Domain Preserving Continuous Finite Elements via Graph-Poisson Convex Limiting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23397)
*Murtazo Nazarov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于非线性标量守恒律的高阶保不变域连续有限元方法，通过残差粘性高阶离散与低阶保不变域格式的结合，利用图-泊松通量重构和凸限制系数实现高低阶更新融合，再通过基于容量的质量再分配步骤恢复守恒性，数值实验验证了其在光滑区的高阶精度、间断附近的鲁棒性及收敛到熵解的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统高阶格式因非正集中质量和过宽高阶图模板导致的限制，构造一种既保持高阶精度又满足保不变域（如极值原理）特性的数值方法。

Method: 1) 残差粘性高阶离散与低阶保不变域格式结合；2) 基于高阶节点诱导的细P1子网格实现高低阶分离；3) 图-泊松通量重构表示高低阶更新的差异为守恒反称图通量；4) 凸限制系数限制通量；5) 基于容量的质量再分配步骤恢复守恒性并维持预设界。

Result: 数值实验表明方法在光滑区域达到高阶精度，在间断附近保持鲁棒性，对具有挑战性的基准测试收敛到熵解。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功解决了高阶保不变域格式中的关键数值难题，为非线性标量守恒律提供了一种兼顾精度与稳定性的有限元求解方案。

Abstract: We develop a high-order invariant-domain preserving continuous finite element method for nonlinear scalar conservation laws. The method combines a residual-viscosity high-order discretization with a low-order invariant-domain scheme constructed on a fine $\mathbb P_1$ submesh induced by the high-order nodal points. This separation avoids the restrictions caused by nonpositive lumped masses and overly wide high-order graph stencils. The high- and low-order updates are connected by a graph-Poisson flux reconstruction, which represents their difference as conservative antisymmetric graph fluxes. These fluxes are limited using convex limiting coefficients, followed by a capacity-based mass redistribution step that restores conservation while preserving the prescribed bounds whenever sufficient admissible capacity is available. Numerical experiments for smooth and nonsmooth scalar conservation laws demonstrate high-order accuracy in smooth regimes, robustness near discontinuities, and convergence to the entropy solution for challenging benchmarks.

</details>


### [147] [When do perturbed Chebyshev--Lobatto points remain Chebyshev?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23409)
*Hao-Ning Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Chebyshev-Lobatto节点在角度扰动下的稳定性，发现当扰动幅度$n\sigma_n(\log n+1)$足够小时，Lebesgue常数仍保持对数增长；当扰动达到$O(1/n)$量级时则失去稳定性。这一结果对解析插值、Runge现象和伪谱微分有重要推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Chebyshev点在被扰动后，其优良的插值性质（对数增长的Lebesgue常数）能在多大扰动幅度下得以保持。

Method: 利用余弦参数化$x_j=\cos(j\pi/n+\varepsilon_j)$和Bernstein三角多项式不等式，通过角度网格的几何分析而非物理变量的Markov不等式进行证明。

Result: 建立了确定性最坏情况稳定性估计：若$n\sigma_n(\log n+1)$有界且足够小，Lebesgue常数保持对数增长；同时证明了$O(1/n)$量级的扰动会破坏稳定性。

Conclusion: Chebyshev节点在扰动幅度$n\sigma_n\asymp(\log n)^{-1}$量级以下时保持稳定，超过此阈值则性质急剧恶化。该结果对解析插值、Runge现象避免和伪谱微分有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Chebyshev points are distinguished in polynomial interpolation by the logarithmic growth of their Lebesgue constants. This paper asks a simple question: how much can Chebyshev points be perturbed before they cease to behave like Chebyshev points? We study perturbed Chebyshev--Lobatto nodes $x_j=\cos(jπ/n+\varepsilon_j)$, with angular perturbations $|\varepsilon_j|\leq σ_n$. The study is motivated by numerical experiments showing a broad stable region when the mesh fraction $nσ_n$ is small and rapid amplification for larger perturbations; the observed transition region is consistent with the curve $nσ_n\asymp(\log n)^{-1}$. The main result is a deterministic worst-case stability estimate: if $nσ_n(\log n+1)$ is bounded by a sufficiently small constant, then the Lebesgue constant remains logarithmic. The proof uses the cosine parametrization and Bernstein's inequality for trigonometric polynomials, thereby exploiting the angular geometry of the Chebyshev--Lobatto grid rather than a Markov inequality in the physical variable. We also give a worst-case obstruction at the angular mesh scale, showing that perturbations of order $1/n$ cannot be allowed uniformly. Consequences are derived for analytic interpolation in Bernstein ellipses, for the absence of Runge-type divergence in the stable analytic regime, and for pseudospectral differentiation. Numerical experiments illustrate the transition in the Lebesgue constants, the shape of the associated Lebesgue functions, Runge-function interpolants, and finite-precision differentiation errors.

</details>


### [148] [Projection-Based Reconstruction for Achieving High-Order Accuracy from Low-Order DGSEM Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23504)
*Xukun Wang,Suyash Shrestha,Oscar A. Marino,Esteban Ferrer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对基于LGL节点的高阶DGSEM离散格式的校正$\mathbb{P}_n\mathbb{P}_m$ ($c\mathbb{P}_n\mathbb{P}_m$)方法，通过仅演化低阶自由度同时恢复高阶精度，并引入紧凑投影重建算子，在保持$m+1$阶收敛性的同时显著降低计算成本和内存占用，数值实验验证了其在守恒律特别是粘性流中的效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶DGSEM-LGL离散格式在多项式次数增加时，计算成本、内存占用和时间步长限制快速增长，需要一种能够降低这些开销同时保持高阶精度的方案。

Method: (1) 在连续层面推导高阶分量的投影演化，再扩展到全离散DGSEM-LGL框架；(2) 针对LGL quadrature对最高阶Legendre模式不精确的问题引入校正项；(3) 提出紧凑投影重建算子，避免标准方法中求解大规模约束最小二乘系统。

Result: 理论分析表明$c\mathbb{P}_n\mathbb{P}_m$方案可实现预期的$m+1$阶收敛精度；一维和二维守恒律数值实验（含Euler、粘性Burgers及2D各向同性湍流）验证了理论收敛性，并在计算成本下展现了有竞争力的精度，粘性流中效率提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: $c\mathbb{P}_n\mathbb{P}_m$方法能有效恢复高阶DGSEM-LGL的精度，同时大幅降低计算和内存开销，特别适用于粘性流动问题。

Abstract: High-order discontinuous Galerkin spectral element methods (DGSEM) based on Legendre-Gauss-Lobatto (LGL) nodes provide accurate and efficient discretizations for conservation laws. However, their cost, memory footprint, and time-step restrictions increase rapidly when the degree of the polynomial increases. This paper develops a corrected $\mathbb{P}_n\mathbb{P}_m$ ($c\mathbb{P}_n\mathbb{P}_m$) approach for DGSEM-LGL discretizations that aims to recover the accuracy of an $m^{th}$-order approximation while evolving only the degrees of freedom associated with an $n^{th}$-order representation, with $n<m$. The projected evolution of the high-order components is derived first at the continuous level and then in the fully discrete DGSEM-LGL setting. The discrete analysis shows that because LGL quadrature is not exact for the highest Legendre mode, a correction term for that mode is required to preserve the order of convergence. A compact projection-based reconstruction operator is then introduced to recover high-order components without solving the enlarged constrained least-squares systems used in standard reconstruction procedures. For sufficiently smooth solutions, the resulting $c\mathbb{P}_n\mathbb{P}_m$ scheme is shown to achieve the expected $m+1^{th}$ convergence order. Numerical experiments for one- and two-dimensional conservation laws, including Euler, viscous Burgers, and 2D decaying homogeneous isotropic turbulence, confirm theoretical convergence behavior and demonstrate competitive accuracy relative to computational cost, with particularly clear efficiency gains for viscous flows.

</details>


### [149] [A posteriori error bounds for finite element approximations of time-dependent mean field games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23613)
*Iain Smears,Harry Wells*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类时间依赖平均场博弈的稳定化有限元逼近的后验误差界，证明了误差范数与残差对偶范数的等价性，推导了基于残差、时间跳跃和稳定化项的可信且高效的后验误差估计子，并证明稳定化估计子可由残差和时间跳跃估计子控制，从而得到改进的可靠、局部可计算且局部高效的估计子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为时间依赖平均场博弈问题的稳定化有限元数值解提供严格的后验误差控制，确保数值逼近的可靠性和效率。

Method: 首先建立误差范数与残差对偶范数的等价性，然后基于残差估计子、时间跳跃估计子和稳定化项估计子构造后验误差估计子，最后证明在质量集中时间稳定化和仿射保持空间稳定化下，稳定化估计子可由残差和时间跳跃估计子控制。

Result: 得到了可靠且高效的后验误差估计子，并在特定稳定化条件下证明了改进的可靠、局部可计算且局部高效的误差估计。

Conclusion: 本文为时间依赖平均场博弈的稳定化有限元方法提供了严格的后验误差分析框架，所提出的估计子在理论上具有可靠性和效率保证。

Abstract: We present a posteriori error bounds for a general class of stabilized finite element approximations of time-dependent mean field games. We first show the equivalence between the norm of the error and the dual norm of the residual in the coupled Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman and Kolmogorov-Fokker-Planck equations. We then derive a reliable and efficient a posteriori error estimator that is based on residual estimators, along with the temporal jump estimator, and an estimator for the stabilization terms in the numerical discretization. Finally, for stabilizations based on mass-lumping in time and affine-preserving spatial stabilizations, we show that the stabilization estimators can be bounded in terms of the residual and temporal jump estimators, thus yielding an improved reliable, locally computable, and locally efficient estimator.

</details>

### [150] [Old and Bright: The Remarkable Radio Brightening of the Engine-driven SN 2012au Several Years After Explosion Signals the Birth of a PWN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20836)
*Eli Wiston,Raffaella Margutti,A. J. Nayana,Brian D. Metzger,Kohta Murase,Dan Milisavljevic,Itai Sfaradi,Ryan Chornock,Deanne L. Coppejans,Joe Bright,Garrett K. Keating,Giacomo Terreran,Mattias Lazda,Maria R. Drout,Michael Stroh,Lauren Rhodes,Ben Margalit,Jonathan Granot,Fabio De Colle,Michael Bietenholz,Daichi Tsuna,Samantha Wu,Tanmoy Laskar,Edo Berger,Daniel Patnaude,Collin T. Christy*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了引擎驱动的Ib型超新星SN 2012au在爆发后13年内的多波段观测结果。早期（≤190天）射电和X射线演化符合激波与星周介质相互作用的常规模型，但约6.7年后出现显著的射电再增亮。晚期辐射由具有宽且快速演化的谱峰和浅光学薄谱斜率（F_ν∝ν^{-0.31}）的明亮成分主导，表明致密发射区以极慢速度（≤500 km/s）膨胀进入高密度环境，伴随硬电子幂律指数p≈1.6。在任何历元均未探测到软或硬X射线发射。作者认为这些观测无法用非球形激波-CSM相互作用模型充分解释，而新生脉冲星风星云（PWN）的辐射自然解释了射电谱演化和高能限制。结论：SN 2012au是目前发现的最有说服力的年轻新生PWN候选体，可通过甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）观测直接验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究引擎驱动型超新星SN 2012au在爆发后13年内的长期演化行为，特别是晚期射电再增亮现象的物理起源，区分激波与星周介质相互作用模型和脉冲星风星云（PWN）模型。

Method: 对SN 2012au进行爆发后13年内的宽波段（射电到X射线）观测，分析不同时标的辐射演化特征，构建射电频谱演化模型，并与激波-CSM相互作用模型及PWN模型进行对比。

Result: 早期辐射符合常规激波-CSM相互作用模型；晚期（~6.7年后）出现射电再增亮，具有致密发射区、极慢膨胀速度、高密度环境、硬电子谱等特征；未探测到X射线发射；非球形激波-CSM模型无法解释这些极端性质，而新生PWN模型可以自然解释所有观测特征。

Conclusion: SN 2012au是目前发现的最有说服力的年轻新生脉冲星风星云（PWN）候选体，这一结论可通过未来的VLBI观测直接检验。

Abstract: We present the results from an extensive broad-band (radio to X-rays) observing campaign of the engine-driven Type Ib SN 2012au in the first 13 years of evolution. The early-time ($δt\leq{190}$ d) radio and X-ray evolution is well-described by conventional models of a forward shock interacting with a wind-like circumstellar medium ($ρ_{\rm{CSM}}\propto{r}^{-2}$). However, starting at $δ{t}\approx{6.7}$ yr, we detect a significant radio re-brightening. This late-time emission is dominated by a luminous component characterized by a broad and rapidly evolving spectral peak and a shallow optically thin spectral slope, $F_ν\proptoν^{-0.31\pm0.02}$. These properties imply a compact emitting region ($R\lesssim{10}^{16}$ cm) expanding at a remarkably slow velocity ($\lesssim{500}$ km/s) into a high-density environment ($\geq{10}^4 \rm{cm}^{-3}$), accompanied by a hard electron power-law index $p\approx{1.6}$. No soft or hard X-ray emission is detected at any epoch, indicating that high-energy radiation is either strongly absorbed or intrinsically absent. In the context of aspherical shock-CSM interaction models, these observations imply extreme properties of the CSM (geometry, density, total mass) that lack clear astrophysical motivation. Instead, we show that the emergence of radiation from a newborn Pulsar Wind Nebula (PWN) naturally explains the radio spectral evolution and high-energy limits, where the emission is governed by the adiabatic expansion of a relic pair plasma. We conclude that SN 2012au represents the most compelling candidate for a young, newborn PWN discovered to date, a scenario that can be directly tested with pending Very Long Baseline Interferometry (VLBI) observations.

</details>


### [151] [Radio Follow Up of a Sub-threshold GRB in the Sky Localization Area of GW241125](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20885)
*Natalie Gottschlich,Alessandra Corsi,S. Bradley Cenko,Divyajyoti,James DeLaunay,Derek B. Fox,Tanner O'Dwyer,Samuele Ronchini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对与引力波事件GW241125_010116（原S241125n）相关联的候选亚阈值伽马暴进行射电后随观测（VLA）、X射线（Swift/XRT）重分析和光学上限约束，将双黑洞并合中可能存在的相对论性喷流的各向同性能量上限限制在~3×10⁵⁰ erg（n_ISM=1.0 cm⁻³），并讨论了当前射电后随观测在验证BBH-GRB关联中的挑战与诊断能力，展望了下一代地面探测器（Cosmic Explorer、Einstein Telescope）和射电干涉阵（ngVLA、SKA）将极大提升多信使后随能力，有望最终回答BBH是否通过小盘吸积驱动相对论性抛射物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Fermi卫星发现与GW150914时间重合的伽马暴候选体以来，多个双黑洞并合与电磁暂现源之间的候选关联被报道但争议不断。本文旨在通过射电后随观测对另一个候选事件（S241125n/GW241125_010116与Swift/BAT-GUANO亚阈值GRB的时空关联）进行严格检验，以约束BBH并合后相对论性喷流的能量，并为未来多信使观测策略提供指导。

Method: 利用VLA对Swift/BAT-GUANO定位区域进行射电后随观测；重新分析Swift/XRT的X射线数据；结合光学上限进行联合约束。通过射电、X射线和光学多波段数据对可能存在的相对论性喷流的各向同性能量进行建模约束，并在此基础上对下一代探测器网络（Cosmic Explorer、Einstein Telescope）和下一代射电干涉阵（ngVLA、SKA）的探测能力进行展望分析。

Result: 将双黑洞并合中可能存在的相对论性喷流的各向同性能量约束在≤3×10⁵⁰ erg（ISM密度n=1.0 cm⁻³条件下）。

Conclusion: 当前射电后随观测在检验BBH-GRB候选关联中面临挑战但具有重要诊断价值；下一代设施（Cosmic Explorer、Einstein Telescope、ngVLA、SKA）将显著增强多信使协同后随能力，有望最终确定BBH是否通过小盘吸积产生相对论性抛射物。

Abstract: Since the Fermi satellite's identification of a candidate $γ$-ray burst (GRB) temporally coincident with GW150914, several tentative, and often debated, associations between electromagnetic (EM) transients and gravitational-wave (GW) signals from binary black hole (BBH) mergers have been reported. One such event, S241125n (later confirmed as GW241125_010116), was identified during the fourth observing run (O4) of Advanced LIGO and found to be spatially (within large GW localization uncertainty) and temporally coincident with a subthreshold GRB detected by the Swift Burst Alert Telescope Gamma-ray Urgent Archiver for Novel Opportunities (BAT-GUANO). Here, we present results from a radio follow-up campaign targeting the BAT-GUANO localization region, carried out with the Karl G. Jansky Very Large Array (VLA). We also re-analyze Swift/XRT observations of the field, and combine these results with optical upper limits. Our analysis constrains the isotropic kinetic energy of a putative relativistic jet launched in the BBH merger to $\lesssim 3 \times 10^{50}$ erg for $n_{ISM} = 1.0 cm^{-3}$. We also discuss both the challenges and the diagnostic power of radio follow up in assessing candidate BBH-GRB associations, and present projections for analogous radio studies in the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run 5 (O5), and in the era of next-generation ground-based instrumentation. The enhanced sensitivity and localization capabilities of detector networks such as Cosmic Explorer and the Einstein Telescope, paired with the enhanced sensitivity of next-generation radio interferometers such as the next-generation VLA and the Square Kilometre Array, will significantly strengthen coordinated multi-messenger follow-up of BBHs. These next-generation facilities are likely to provide an answer to whether BBHs host relativistic ejecta powered by mini-disk accretion.

</details>


### [152] [Little Red Dots as Supermassive Analogs of SS 433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21105)
*Shuying Zhou,Mouyuan Sun,Xihan Ji,Ya-Ping Li,Luis C. Ho,Roberto Maiolino,Zhen-Yi Cai,Hai-Cheng Feng,Manqi Fu,Wei-Min Gu,Tong Liu,Junfeng Wang,Jianfeng Wu,Yongquan Xue*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出高红移"小红点"（LRDs）是超爱丁顿吸积的银河系微类星体SS 433在大质量、高红移下的类似物，从高倾角视角观测。通过将恒星质量黑洞的超爱丁顿吸积物理标度到超大质量尺度，该模型自然解释了LRD的X射线弱、光学软谱、亚爱丁顿吸积率及巴尔末跳变等观测特征，并预言了巴尔末跳变强度与宽发射线宽度正相关、发射线比连续谱更易变等可检验信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移小红点（LRDs）具有V形能谱分布、宽发射线和显著的巴尔末跳变，其高数密度和巨大黑洞质量表明它们对早期星系和超大质量黑洞的演化至关重要，但其中心引擎的本质仍不确定。

Method: 通过将恒星质量黑洞（SS 433）的超爱丁顿吸积物理标度到超大质量黑洞尺度，结合膨胀吸积盘的自屏蔽几何模型，解释LRD的多波段观测特征。

Result: LRD的X射线弱、光学软谱、亚爱丁顿吸积率及巴尔末跳变均可由高倾角下膨胀吸积盘的自屏蔽几何自然产生；低倾角或低吸积率对应体表现为小蓝点（LBDs）或正常活动星系核。

Conclusion: 该统一标度模型将LRD重新定义为观测早期星系及其中心SMBHs快速吸积驱动增长的关键实验室，并提出了多个可检验的预言。

Abstract: High-redshift little red dots (LRDs) are compact sources characterized by V-shaped spectral energy distributions (SEDs), broad emission lines, and often prominent Balmer breaks. Their high number density and apparently large black hole masses suggest that they are essential to the early evolution of galaxies and supermassive black holes (SMBHs); however, the nature of their central engines remains uncertain. Here, we propose that LRDs are the supermassive, high-redshift analogs of the hyper-Eddington accreting Galactic microquasar SS~433, viewed at high inclinations. By scaling the hyper-Eddington accretion physics from stellar-mass black holes to supermassive scales, we show that the observed LRD features, including X-ray weakness, soft optical SEDs, apparent sub-Eddington accretion ratio, and Balmer breaks, emerge naturally from the self-shielding geometry of a puffed-up accretion disk. In this framework, the broad-line regions are ionized by anisotropic radiation escaping from the inner disk, analogous to the unseen UV/X-ray emission revealed by the W50 nebula in SS 433. Their low-inclination or lower-accretion-rate counterparts would appear as little blue dots (LBDs) or normal active galactic nuclei. Our model predicts that the Balmer break strength positively correlates with the broad-line width, that the emission lines are more variable than the optical continuum, that LRDs are intrinsically more luminous than observed, and that LBDs are more variable than LRDs. This unified-scale model redefines LRDs as the essential laboratories for observing the rapid accretion-driven growth that shaped the early assembly of galaxies and their central SMBHs.

</details>


### [153] [XRISM Time-resolved Fe K$α$ Spectroscopy of NGC 4395: Time-variable Inner-disk Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21180)
*Taiki Kawamuro,Satoshi Yamada,Hirofumi Noda,Yoshiyuki Inoue,Shoji Ogawa,Misaki Mizumoto*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了首次利用XRISM对近邻矮星系NGC 4395中低质量AGN的观测，结合同步NuSTAR数据，在时均光谱中探测到未分辨的中性Fe Kα核及其红翼，并通过相对论性展宽的Fe K分量成功拟合红翼。时变光谱分析揭示盘线轮廓在约400 ks观测期间显著演化，可解释为线发射区内半径变化及约210 ks周期的倾角调制，若归因于倾斜内流的Lense-Thirring进动，则支持较低黑洞质量估计（约9×10^3 M⊙）并暗示中等自旋（a≳0.6）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用XRISM的高分辨率光谱能力研究低质量AGN（NGC 4395）中的相对论性Fe Kα线，探测黑洞附近吸积盘的动力学特征，并约束黑洞质量与自旋参数。

Method: 联合拟合XRISM/Resolve（2-12 keV）和NuSTAR（3-30 keV）光谱，排除Fe K波段（5.5-7.5 keV）以约束连续谱；进行时均光谱分析（探测中性Fe Kα核及红翼）和时变光谱分析（约87 ks时间分辨，总观测约400 ks），用相对论性展宽Fe K分量和盘线模型拟合演化特征。

Result: 时均光谱发现未分辨的中性Fe Kα核（速度宽度≲110 km/s）及其红翼；红翼由相对论性展宽Fe K分量成功再现。时变光谱显示盘线轮廓显著变化，内半径和倾角（约210 ks周期）演化。若解释为Lense-Thirring进动，则黑洞质量约9×10^3 M⊙，自旋a≳0.6。

Conclusion: XRISM能够有效追踪AGN中的相对论性吸积盘动力学，并通过Fe Kα线时变行为约束低质量黑洞的质量与自旋参数。

Abstract: We report the first XRISM observation of the low-mass AGN in the nearby dwarf galaxy NGC 4395 ($M_{\rm BH}\sim10^{4-5}\,M_\odot$), complemented by a simultaneous NuSTAR observation. We constrained the continuum by jointly fitting the XRISM/Resolve (2-12 keV) and NuSTAR (3-30 keV) spectra while excluding the Fe K band (5.5-7.5 keV). Relative to this baseline continuum, the time-averaged Resolve spectrum revealed an unresolved neutral Fe K$α$ core with a velocity width of $\lesssim$110 km s$^{-1}$ and an adjacent redward wing. The red wing was well reproduced by an additional relativistically broadened Fe K component. Furthermore, time-resolved spectroscopy with $\approx$87 ks bins showed that the diskline profile varied significantly over the $\sim$400 ks observation. This evolution can be interpreted in terms of changes in the inner radius of the line-emitting region, together with a possible inclination modulation with a period of $\approx$210 ks. If interpreted as Lense-Thirring precession of a tilted inner flow, the observed period would favor the low end of the black hole mass estimates ($M_{\rm BH}\approx9\times10^3\,M_\odot$) and imply a moderate spin ($a\gtrsim0.6$). These results highlight the capability of XRISM to track relativistic disk dynamics in AGNs.

</details>


### [154] [Probing dipole and quadrupole anisotropy in Gamma-ray bursts from Swift dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21182)
*Vedant Mokal,Shantanu Desai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用Swift卫星的伽马射线暴目录，通过球谐分解分析其角分布，发现原始数据存在显著偶极和四极各向异性，但在考虑观测偏差（曝光图）后，各向异性显著性降至1σ以下，确认了宇宙大尺度各向同性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验宇宙学原理中关于大尺度各向同性的假设是当代宇宙学的关键问题，作者希望通过伽马射线暴这一独立探针来验证该假设的有效性。

Method: 使用Swift卫星的GRB目录，采用HEALPix球谐分解方法估计偶极和四极振幅；通过500次各向同性蒙特卡洛模拟建立零假设；同时利用Swift望远镜指向历史、滚转角和部分编码比例构建曝光图以校正观测偏差。

Result: 原始数据中偶极振幅显著性为2.9σ，四极振幅为7.2σ；在引入曝光图校正观测偏差后，各向异性显著性均降至1σ以下。

Conclusion: Swift数据确认了伽马射线暴天空的统计各向同性，与先前研究一致，并公开了Swift曝光图供后续使用。

Abstract: Testing the validity of the cosmological principle's assumption of large-scale isotropy remains crucial for modern cosmology. We investigate the angular distributions of gamma-ray bursts using the GRB catalog from Neil Gehrels Swift Observatory (Swift) for an independent probe of isotropy. Using the HEALPix spherical harmonic decomposition, we estimate the dipole and quadrupole amplitudes and compare them against the null hypothesis obtained from 500 isotropic Monte Carlo realizations. Our results show 2.9$σ$ dipole and 7.2$σ$ quadrupole amplitude when applied to the raw data. To account for observational biases, we then create an exposure map using the pointing history, roll angle, and the partial coding fraction of the Swift Telescope. Reevaluating the null hypothesis using this map reduces the significance of these anisotropies to less than $1σ$. Therefore, our findings confirm statistical isotropy of the GRB sky using the Swift data, consistent with previous studies. We have also made the Swift exposure map publicly available.

</details>


### [155] [A broadband view of the thermal and non-thermal emission from the embedded massive star cluster RCW 38](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21288)
*Giada Peron,Andrea Bracco,Giovanni Morlino,Silvia Mantovanini,Elena Amato,Daniele Galli,Marco Padovani*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对年轻大质量星团RCW 38进行了多波段观测与建模，发现其伽马射线发射主要来自强子相互作用（质子-分子云碰撞），约束电子-质子比K_ep≲10^{-3}，加速效率≳1%，支持星团是银河系宇宙线质子重要起源的观点，并解释了宇宙线中的²²Ne异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCW 38是首个被观测到仅由星风驱动高能粒子加速的年轻嵌入星团，其伽马射线发射可高达数百GeV。本文旨在表征其伽马射线发射机制，估算加速效率以及加速电子与质子的比例K_ep，以验证星团对银河系宇宙线贡献的假说。

Method: 利用从MHz射电（MWA/GLEAM-X）到GeV伽马射线（Fermi-LAT）的多波段数据，结合Parkes、Planck、IRAS的GHz和THz观测，构建包含8个参数的热和非热辐射模型，采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法进行约束拟合。

Result: 伽马射线发射主要来自强子相互作用（与宿主分子云碰撞）；电子-质子比K_ep≲10^{-3}；加速效率≳1%，该值与解释宇宙线成分（特别是²²Ne异常）所需的值一致。

Conclusion: 星团在至少数TeV能量范围内是银河系宇宙线质子的重要贡献者；未来下一代地面探测器将揭示其在更高能量（如宇宙线膝区）是否也扮演关键角色。

Abstract: Gamma-ray emission has now been detected from a variety of source classes in the Galaxy, including clusters of young massive stars. RCW 38, a very young embedded massive star cluster, is a case of particular interest: its gamma-ray emission detected up to hundreds of GeV, provided the first observational evidence of high-energy particle acceleration powered exclusively by stellar winds. In this work, we aim to characterize the emission mechanisms responsible for the gamma-ray flux in RCW 38 and to provide estimates of the acceleration efficiency, as well as the fraction of accelerated electrons compared to protons, $K_{ep}$. We present the most comprehensive multi-wavelength study of a single star cluster to date. Our analysis ranges from MHz radio observations obtained with the GLEAM-X survey from the Murchison Widefield Array (MWA) to GeV gamma-ray data from Fermi-LAT, and includes GHz and THz measurements from Parkes, Planck, and IRAS. We model the thermal and non-thermal emission of RCW 38 using an eight-parameter model constrained by the Markov chain Monte Carlo method. Our results support an interpretation in which the gamma-ray emission from RCW 38 is produced by hadronic interactions with the host molecular cloud. We derive robust constraints on the electron-to-proton ratio, with $K_{ep} \lesssim 10^{-3}$, and on the acceleration efficiency, estimated to be $\gtrsim$1%, consistent with the values required to explain the cosmic-ray composition, and in particular its $^{22}$Ne anomaly. These results strengthen the idea that stellar clusters play a significant role as contributors to cosmic-ray protons in our Galaxy at least up to energies of a few TeV. Future investigations with the next generation of ground-based detectors will determine whether they also play a relevant role at higher energies, particularly in the context of the cosmic-ray knee.

</details>


### [156] [A New Scaling of Neutron Star Tidal Deformability for Directly Probing the Core Equation of State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21402)
*Jian-Hao Shi,Bao-Jun Cai,Bao-An Li,Yu-Gang Ma*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文发现中子星无量纲潮汐形变参数Λ与中心状态参数X≡Pc/εc之间存在不依赖于具体物态方程的标度关系，使得通过引力波观测的潮汐形变可直接约束中子星核心物态，并给出最大质量中子星Λ的下界（Λ_TOV≳9.2），表明最致密稳定中子星仍与黑洞（Λ_BH=0）有明显区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有引力波观测（如GW170817）中得到的潮汐形变量Λ主要用于约束近两倍饱和核密度的物质压力，而核心物态方程（EOS）对引力波观测几乎不可及。本文旨在建立潮汐形变与核心EOS之间的直接联系。

Method: 采用微扰分析方法，基于标度内禀变量对无量纲恒星结构方程和潮汐响应方程进行分析，不依赖任何特定EOS模型；发现Λ与中心EOS参数X≡Pc/εc之间存在对EOS不敏感的标度关系，并通过大量物理可行的EOS样本进行验证。

Result: 建立了Λ与X之间的普适标度关系；将关系应用于GW170817等事件，可直接确定X；推导出最大质量中子星沿稳定质量-半径序列的严格下界Λ_TOV≳9.2^{+1.2}_{-1.2}。

Conclusion: 潮汐形变参数Λ可作为直接且基本不依赖模型的中子星核心EOS探针，建立了引力波可观测量与超密物质微观物理之间的直接联系。

Abstract: The dimensionless tidal deformability, $Λ$, of neutron stars (NSs), inferred from gravitational-wave (GW) observations, has thus far been used primarily to constrain the pressure of dense matter near twice nuclear saturation density, leaving the core equation of state (EOS) largely inaccessible to inspiral-phase GW observations. We show that the core EOS can be probed directly through $Λ$ using a perturbative analysis of the dimensionless stellar-structure and tidal-response equations formulated in terms of scaled intrinsic variables, without invoking any specific EOS model. We uncover a remarkable EOS-insensitive scaling relation between $Λ$ and the central EOS parameter $\mathrm{X}\equiv P_{\rm c}/\varepsilon_{\rm c}$, where $P_{\rm c}$ and $\varepsilon_{\rm c}$ denote the central pressure and energy density, respectively. The relation is validated against a broad ensemble of physically viable EOSs. Applying it to tidal deformabilities inferred from events such as GW170817 enables a direct determination of $\mathrm{X}$. We further derive a tight lower bound, $Λ_{\rm{TOV}}\gtrsim 9.2^{+1.2}_{-1.2}$, for maximum-mass NSs along stable mass-radius sequences, quantitatively demonstrating that even the most compact stable NSs remain distinctly separated from black holes, for which $Λ_{\rm{BH}}=0$. These findings reveal a previously unrecognized connection between inspiral-phase tidal deformability and the core EOS, establishing a direct link between GW observables and the microphysics of ultradense matter in the strong-gravity regime. The resulting scaling establishes inspiral-phase tidal deformability as a direct and largely model-insensitive probe of the EOS of NS cores.

</details>


### [157] [The Fundamental Planes of Black Hole Activity for High-Synchrotron-Peaked BL Lacertae Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21426)
*Qing-Chen. Long,Ai-Jun. Dong,Qi-Jun. Zhi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过构建69个HBLs样本，研究了射电-X射线相关性和基本平面，发现内在关系为L_R,int ∝ L_X,int^0.68，并证实同步辐射冷却（Syn-c）模型可以解释这种浅斜率关系，表明HBLs的X射线辐射可能来自激波加速的高能电子的快速冷却过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了进一步澄清HBLs（高同步峰BL Lac天体）X射线辐射的起源，区分其是由ADAF主导模式还是喷流同步辐射过程产生。

Method: 构建包含69个HBLs的样本，考虑多普勒聚束效应，重新研究射电-X射线相关性及基本平面，并与同步辐射冷却（Syn-c）理论模型进行对比。

Result: 发现HBLs的内在射电-X射线相关性为L_R,int ∝ L_X,int^0.68，基本平面关系为log L_R,int = (0.57±0.06) log L_X,int + (0.33±0.11) log M_BH + (12.65±2.00)，与Syn-c模型预测的L_R ∝ L_X^{0.6~0.7}一致。

Conclusion: HBLs的X射线辐射主要由同步辐射冷却过程产生，来自激波加速的高能电子的快速冷却，这一结论与近期X射线偏振观测结果一致。

Abstract: The radio--X-ray correlation and Fundamental Plane (FP) of black hole activity can serve as a diagnostic tool for the origin of X-ray emissions. There was a scaling relation between radio and X-ray emissions for High-synchrotron-peaked BL Lacertae objects (HBLs), i.e., $L_{\rm{R}}\propto L_{\rm{X}}^{0.64}$, which can be explained by ADAF-dominated mode or synchrotron cooling (Syn-c). However, many results of studying blazar physics show that the X-ray emissions of HBLs are mainly produced by the synchrotron process of jets. Therefore, Syn-c appears to provide a plausible explanation for this relation. To further clarify the origin of X-ray emissions of HBLs, we constructed a sample containing 69 HBLs in this paper to re-investigate their radio--X-ray correlation and FP. Considering the Doppler beaming effect, we find that the intrinsic radio--X-ray correlation and FP of HBLs are $L_{\rm R,int}\propto L_{\rm X,int}^{0.68}$ and $\log L_{\rm R,int}=(0.57\pm0.06)\log L_{\rm X,int}+(0.33\pm0.11)\log M_{\rm BH}+(12.65\pm2.00)$, respectively. Our results agree with the scaling relation mention above, which suggests these scaling relations are not artificial. By employing the theoretical model of Syn-c, we find that these shallow radio--X-ray correlations and FP are caused by Syn-c, which implies that the X-ray emissions of HBLs may be produced by rapidly cooling, high-energy electrons accelerated at a shock. This is consistent with results of the recent X-ray polarization observations of HBLs. Our results provide the observational evidence of $L_{\rm R}\propto L_{\rm X_{\text{Syn-c}}}^{0.6\sim0.7}$.

</details>


### [158] [Subgrid Modelling for Relativistic Magnetohydrodynamics with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21659)
*William Cook,Sebastiano Bernuzzi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于机器学习的特殊相对论磁流体力学次网格模型，用于模拟磁不稳定性引发的小尺度湍流。通过神经网络训练，在3D开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性模拟中，低分辨率模型能以44倍加速达到4倍分辨率下的磁场放大效果，适用于中子星合并等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决双中子星合并、中子星及吸积盘中磁场不稳定性引发的小尺度湍流难以直接模拟的问题，需发展可嵌入大涡模拟的次网格模型。

Method: 采用神经网络训练，构建首个基于机器学习的特殊相对论磁流体力学次网格模型；通过3D开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性进行先验和后验测试验证性能。

Result: 低分辨率模拟中，该模型能以44倍加速达到4倍分辨率下的磁场放大效果，准确捕获磁场增幅。

Conclusion: 该机器学习次网格模型在相对论磁流体模拟中有效，可推广至广义相对论中子星合并及其他天体物理场景。

Abstract: Resolving the impact of magnetic field instabilities in triggering small scale turbulent flow and the associated rearrangement of the field is of critical importance in understanding multimessenger observables in binary neutron star mergers, and angular momentum transport in neutron stars and accretion disks. Direct simulation of these instabilities are unfeasible, however large-eddy simulations can incorporate the impact of this turbulence with a subgrid model. We present the first machine-learning-based subgrid model for special relativistic magnetohydrodynamics, trained using a neural network. We demonstrate its performance in online simulations of the 3D Kelvin-Helmholtz instability through both a priori and a posteriori tests. Evaluated in a low resolution simulation, our model captures magnetic field amplification of a simulation at 4 times the resolution with a speed-up of a factor 44. This demonstrates the applicability of such methods in general relativistic simulations of neutron star mergers and other scenarios.

</details>


### [159] [Getting Tilted: Random Walk of Binary Black Hole Spin-Orbit Alignment in Dense Star Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21691)
*Maia A. S. Martinez,Christopher E. O'Connor,Fulya Kıroğlu,Frederic A. Rasio*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文挑战了双黑洞自旋-轨道角在密集星团中快速各向同性的假设，通过建模随机游走过程发现自旋-轨道对齐可以在多次强引力 encounters 中幸存，对GWTC-5.0中的对齐趋势和层级合并产物留存有直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"双黑洞自旋-轨道角在密集星团中通过强引力 encounter 快速收敛到各向同性分布"这一普遍假设。

Method: 将双黑洞轨道角动量方向通过连续二元-单体 encounter 的演化建模为单位球面上的随机游走，得到n次 encounter 后方向分布的精确解和各向同性所需 encounter 次数的闭式表达式。通过大规模牛顿点粒子散射实验表征步长分布，并得到平均步长和完整分布的半解析表达式。

Result: 自旋-轨道对齐可以在被抹平之前经历多次强 encounter；最初自旋对齐的双黑洞可以在多次 encounter 后仍保持一定程度的对齐。

Conclusion: 该结果对GWTC-5.0中报告的自旋-轨道对齐趋势以及GW231123等层级合并产物的留存有直接意义。

Abstract: It is commonly assumed that the spin-orbit angles of binary black holes (BBHs) originating from dense stellar environments rapidly converge to an isotropic distribution following a number of strong gravitational encounters. We challenge this assumption by modeling the evolution of the BBH orbital angular momentum orientation through successive binary--single encounters as a random walk on the unit sphere, yielding an exact solution for the orientation distribution after $n$ encounters and a closed-form expression for the number of encounters required to reach isotropy. To characterize the step distribution, we conduct a large suite of Newtonian point-particle scattering experiments with an equal-mass binary, varying the mass of the single, and obtain semi-analytic expressions for both the mean step size and the full distribution of steps. Applying these results to BBHs with initially aligned spins, as may arise from the evolution of primordial massive binaries, we find that spin-orbit alignment can survive several strong encounters before being erased. This has direct implications for the slight trend toward spin-orbit alignment reported in GWTC-5.0 as well as for the retention of hierarchical merger products, such as the components of GW231123.

</details>


### [160] [A grid of fast-rotating, chemically-homogeneous, supernova and/or long-GRB progenitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21824)
*M. Renzo,O. Gottlieb,H. S. Chan,J. A. Goldberg,A. Grichener,K. Sen,N. Shah,E. Farag,Matteo Cantiello*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文使用MESA代码构建了113个快速旋转的大质量恒星模型（Z=0.001, 30-90 M☉），这些模型通过旋转诱导的化学均匀演化，在塌缩时具有大且结构化的角动量，可能形成吸积盘，为研究剥落包层超新星、坍缩星、磁星动力及长伽马射线暴等爆发机制提供了均匀的先驱星模型集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量恒星塌缩与爆发的数值模拟中，核心结构的不确定性是主导因素，而现有先驱星模型网格有限，尤其缺乏考虑旋转和磁场关键作用的高质量模型。

Method: 利用开源恒星演化代码MESA，计算了113个单星模型（初始质量30-90 M☉，初始自转速度0.5-0.99 ω_crit，金属丰度Z=0.001），采用128种同位素核反应网络以追踪硅核燃烧及之后核心的弱反应去轻子化过程。

Result: 模型通过旋转诱导的化学均匀演化，在塌缩起始时（下落速度≤-300 km/s）具有大量且结构化的角动量，足以在原型致密天体周围形成吸积盘。

Conclusion: 该先驱星结构模型集合提供了更新输入物理和算法精度的均匀样本，可用于理解某些类型剥落包层超新星（可能含喷流或宽吸收线）、坍缩星、磁星动力超新星及长伽马射线暴的恒星爆发机制。

Abstract: The understanding of the mechanism(s) by which massive stars collapse and possibly explode is rapidly maturing. Uncertainties in the structure of the stellar core at the onset of collapse are often dominant in numerical simulations, and a limited number of progenitor grids are available. This is especially true for explosions where rotation and magnetic fields play a significant or primary role. We present a grid of 113 single-star models with initial masses $M_{\rm ZAMS}=30-90\,M_{\odot}$ and initial rigid rotation $ω_{\rm ZAMS}=0.5-0.99\,ω_{\rm crit}$ computed at $Z=0.001$ with the open-source stellar evolution code \textsc{MESA}. We adopt a 128-isotope nuclear reaction network capable of following the weak reactions deleptonizing the core during and after silicon core burning. By construction, these models experience rotationally-induced chemically-homogeneous evolution, and reach the onset of collapse ($v_{\rm infall}\lesssim -300\,\mathrm{km\ s^{-1}}$) with large and structured amounts of angular momentum, possibly sufficient to form accretion disks on a proto-compact object. Therefore, these progenitor structures provide a homogeneous set of models with updated input physics and improved algorithmic accuracy to understand stellar explosions of (some types of) stripped-envelope supernovae, possibly jetted and/or broad-lined, collapsars or magnetar-powered, and/or long $γ$-ray bursts.

</details>


### [161] [Do Prompt Gamma-ray Burst Fireball Composition Impact on Afterglow Emission? Cases Study for Long GRBs 080916C/090902B and Short GRBs 090510/130603B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21827)
*Yu Gan,Ren-Jie Xiong,Zi-Qi Wang,Qi-Yu Yan,Liang-Jun Chen,Xiao-Li Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对比分析两类典型长GRB（080916C、090902B）和短GRB（130603B、090510），研究喷流成分对余辉发射的影响。结果表明余辉光变演化与火球成分无关，而取决于环境介质密度；长GRB的早期紫外-光学余辉由级联正负电子对的同步辐射主导，短GRB则不然。火球内能在瞬时阶段转化为喷流动能，成分不在余辉阶段留下可探测痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究伽马射线暴瞬时辐射阶段喷流成分（热/非热主导）是否会影响余辉阶段的辐射特征，即火球成分差异是否会在余辉中留下可观测的"指纹"。

Method: 选取两类GRB（长暴080916C、090902B和短暴130603B、090510），采用包含初级电子和级联电子群辐射的标准正向激波模型，拟合多波段余辉光变曲线，分析粒子加速和辐射物理。

Result: (1) 余辉光变演化与火球成分无关，而取决于环境介质密度；(2) 长GRB早期紫外-光学余辉由γγ湮灭产生的级联正负电子对同步辐射主导；(3) 短GRB早期余辉则无此特征。

Conclusion: 火球内能在瞬时阶段转化为喷流动能，火球成分不在余辉阶段留下可探测痕迹；环境介质密度是塑造余辉发射的关键因素。

Abstract: Broadband observations with the {\em Fermi} mission reveal that a large fraction of gamma-ray burst (GRB) spectra are dominated by non-thermal emission, while a small fraction are dominated by thermal/quasi-thermal emission, likely indicating the difference in jet composition among GRB. By selecting two typical long GRBs (080916C and 090902B) and two short GRBs (130603B and 090510), we present a comparative analysis to investigate whether the composition of prompt GRB jets influences the afterglow emission for bursts originating from both massive star collapse and compact binary mergers. Incorporating emission from both primary and cascade electron populations, we fit the multi-wavelength afterglow lightcurves of these GRBs with the standard forward shock model and analyze the particle acceleration and radiation physics of the jets. Our results show that the afterglow lightcurve evolution with the characteristic parameters is not related to the composition of the GRB fireball but rather depends on the ambient medium density. The early UV-optical afterglows of the two long GRBs are dominated by synchrotron emission from cascaded $e^{\pm}$ pairs produced via the $γγ$ annihilation process, whereas this is not the case for the two short GRBs. These results suggest that the internal energy of the fireball is converted into jet kinetic energy during the prompt phase, and that the fireball composition leaves no detectable footprint on the afterglow jet. Instead, the density of the ambient medium plays an essential role in shaping the afterglow emission.

</details>


### [162] [Intraday Optical Variability of BL Lacertae during its Highly Active 2020-2024 Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21936)
*A. Takey,Ergün Ege,B. M. Mihov,E. G. Elhosseiny,Aykut Ozdonmez,Murat Tekkesinoglu,Hüseyin Er,Aditi Agarwal,L. S. Slavcheva-Mihova,M. Emir Kenger,M. S. Rizk,I. Zead,F. I. Elnagahy,W. A. Badawy,S. K. J. Pacif*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于2020年9月至2024年12月对BL Lacertae天体62夜的多波段（BVRI）光学监测，分析了其日内流量和光谱变化。发现117条光变曲线中88条存在显著变化，最大变化幅度达44.5%（B波段），呈现"越蓝越亮"趋势，并通过对耀斑轮廓的双指数函数建模，推导了粒子加速和冷却时间尺度，约束了发射区的半径、磁场强度及多普勒因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BL Lacertae在2020-2024年期间进入异常活跃阶段，需要高时间分辨率的多波段观测来系统研究其日内时标的流量和光谱变化，以理解喷流发射区的物理过程和粒子加速冷却机制。

Method: 使用埃及、土耳其和保加利亚的四台地面望远镜进行多波段（BVRI）光学监测；采用功率增强F检验和嵌套ANOVA检验评估日内流量变化；对耀斑轮廓用双指数函数建模；基于湍流喷流模型约束发射区半径和磁场强度；分析多波段耀斑的软时间滞后推导多普勒因子和磁场强度。

Result: 117条光变曲线中88条检测到显著变化；B波段最大变幅44.5%；呈现"越蓝越亮"趋势；成功推导了耀斑的上升和衰减时间尺度；限定了发射区半径和磁场强度；检测到多波段耀斑的软时间滞后并得到相应的多普勒因子和磁场强度。

Conclusion: BL Lacertae在2020-2024年处于异常活跃期，日内变化是常见现象，结果揭示了喷流发射区的动态特性，为耀变体变率和喷流物理模型提供了有价值的观测约束。

Abstract: We present an analysis of the intraday flux and spectral variability of BL Lacertae from 2020 September 30 to 2024 December 7, covering its highest recorded brightness and low, intermediate, and high flux states. Our study involved 62 nights of multi-band (BVRI) optical monitoring using four ground-based telescopes located in Egypt, Türkiye, and Bulgaria. We assessed intraday flux variability using the power-enhanced F-test and the nested ANOVA test. Significant variability was detected in 88 out of 117 light curves, consistent with previous studies of this object during active epochs. The maximum variability amplitude is 44.5 per cent in the B band. Spectral analysis reveals a bluer-when-brighter trend during intraday flares, supporting a synchrotron origin for the variable emission. For a subset of well-sampled flares, we model their profiles with a double exponential function, deriving rise and decay time-scales, thereby constraining the characteristic times of particle acceleration and cooling processes within the relativistic jet. Assuming a turbulent jet model, we determined limits on the radii and magnetic field strengths of the emitting regions. We detected soft time lags for a multi-band flare and from their analysis, derived the Doppler factor and magnetic field strength of the corresponding emitting region. Our long-term, high-cadence study confirms that BL Lacertae was in an exceptionally active phase during the 2020-2024 period, with intraday variability being a common phenomenon. The results underscore the dynamic nature of the jet emission region and provide valuable observational constraints for models of blazar variability and jet physics.

</details>


### [163] [Fast Optical Variability of the TeV Blazar PKS 1725+123 Observed by SVOM-VT and Insights from Multi-wavelength Follow-up Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21966)
*Shuo-Yu Liu,Yu-Wei Yu,Ji-Shun Lian,Xin-Ke Hu,Alexis Coleiro,Zhu-Heng Yao,Li-Ping Xin,Jing Wang,Hua-Li Li,Zi-Qi Wang,Jin Zhang,Floriane Cangemi,Bertrand Cordier,Antoine Foisseau,Olivier Godet,Andrea Goldwurm,Diego Gotz,Sebastien Guillot,Xu-Hui Han,Ning Jiang,Cyril Lachaud,Sebastien Le Stum,En-Wei Liang,Pierre Maggi,Yu-Lei Qiu,Jerome Rodriguez,Lian Tao,Jian-Yan Wei,Chao Wu,Liang Zhang,Shuang-Nan Zhang,Shi-Jie Zheng*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了PKS 1725+123这一平谱射电类星体在TeV波段的首次探测，通过SVOM、Fermi-LAT和Swift-XRT等多波段数据分析，发现该源处于历史最高光学通量状态，呈现分钟级光变及"越蓝越亮"现象。基于准同时多波段数据构建了宽带能谱分布，发现其具有异常高的同步辐射峰频，并提出了双区核-鞘喷流模型来解释观测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PKS 1725+123在TeV波段被MAGIC和H.E.S.S.探测到，这是该源的首次TeV探测，需要利用多波段观测数据研究其在高能爆发状态下的物理特性。

Method: 利用SVOM-VT的光学数据、Fermi-LAT的GeV伽马射线数据和Swift-XRT的X射线数据，对PKS 1725+123进行多波段时变分析和能谱建模，并采用双区核-鞘喷流模型拟合宽带能谱分布。

Result: 该源在光学、X射线和GeV伽马射线波段均处于高流强状态；光学通量达到历史最高水平，存在分钟级光变，呈现出"越蓝越亮"的色指数变化；同步辐射峰频异常高于其他FSRQ。

Conclusion: 双区核-鞘喷流模型可以成功再现宽带能谱分布：紧凑区内相对论电子的同步辐射产生光学-X射线发射，同一电子群的逆康普顿散射贡献Fermi-LAT低能端，而扩展区高能电子对紧凑区同步光子的逆康普顿散射贡献Fermi-LAT高能端。

Abstract: PKS 1725+123 is a flat-spectrum radio quasar (FSRQ) with a redshift of $z=0.586$. The detection of this object in the TeV band was reported by the MAGIC telescopes and H.E.S.S. in August 2025. Subsequently, we promptly initiated Target-of-Opportunity observations using the Space-based multi-band astronomical Variable Objects Monitor (SVOM) satellite. By analyzing the observational optical data from SVOM-VT and comprehensively examining the Fermi-LAT and Swift-XRT observational data, it was found that the source is in a high-flux state across the optical, X-ray, and GeV $γ$-ray bands around the time of the TeV detections. Its optical flux reaches a historically unprecedented high level and shows significant variability on timescale as short as minutes. The variability is accompanied by changes in the color index, exhibiting a \textit{bluer when brighter} behavior during the high-flux state. Based on the simultaneous multi-wavelength data, we construct the broadband spectral energy distribution (SED) of the source in the high-flux state. PKS 1725+123 demonstrates a remarkably high synchrotron peak frequency, which is distinctly different from that of other FSRQs. We propose a two-zone spine-sheath jet model to reproduce this SED. The optical--X-ray emission is generated by the synchrotron process of the relativistic electrons within a compact zone. The inverse Compton (IC) scattering processes of the same electron population contribute to the low-energy end of the Fermi-LAT spectrum, while the high-energy end of the Fermi-LAT spectrum is ascribed to the IC scattering of the synchrotron photons within the compact zone by the higher-energy electrons in an extended region.

</details>


### [164] [Multiwavelength variability of the high-energy neutrino candidate PKS 0735+178 over three decades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22190)
*T. Mufakharov,Yu. Sotnikova,V. Vlasyuk,D. Kudryavtsev,A. Pushkarev,A. Mikhailov,M. Khabibullina,Yu. Kovalev,Y. Kovalev,A. Popkov,A. Erkenov,O. Spiridonova,T. Semenova,P. Tsybulev,D. Nezamov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对与中微子事件IC211208A相关联的BL Lac天体PKS 0735+178进行了多波段光变分析，覆盖射电到γ射线波段约30年时标，发现了不同波段间的相关性与时延，探测到~10-11年的特征光变周期，并通过VLBI和射电时延约束了喷流几何结构，表明光变主要由喷流传播效应主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究与高能中微子事件IC211208A相关联的BL Lac天体PKS 0735+178的多波段光变特性，以理解中微子产生的物理机制和喷流中的辐射过程。

Method: 分析覆盖射电（1-230 GHz）、光学和Fermi-LAT γ射线波段约30年的光变曲线，计算波段间的互相关时延，测量VLBI核心偏移和射电时延以约束喷流几何，并检测特征光变周期。

Result: 发现不同波段光变相关，时延从0到1200天向低频增加；IC211208A事件后出现明亮耀发；首次探测到γ射线与光学之间约12天的时延；在射电毫米波段稳健探测到~10-11年的特征光变周期（≥3σ）；VLBI核心偏移与射电时延对喷流几何的估计一致。

Conclusion: 光变特征与不透明度分层的喷流模型一致，长周期调制可能源于喷流基底能量释放的缓慢变化，而个别耀发来自激波向下游传播；进动模型与VLBI约束不符，不能单独解释观测到的光变。

Abstract: We present the multiwavelength variability of the BL Lac object PKS 0735+178, associated with the high-energy neutrino event IC211208A. The light curves cover the radio (1-230 GHz), optical, and Fermi-LAT $γ$-ray bands over a $\sim$30 years time-scale. The light curves are correlated, with delays from 0 to 1200 days increasing towards lower frequencies, consistent with emission from an opacity-stratified jet. The bright flare after the IC211208A event indicates emission from a compact, optically thick region with enhanced activity and more efficient particle acceleration. Short optical and $γ$-ray bursts have been detected very close to the neutrino event, within a few days. The $\sim$12-day lag between the $γ$-ray and optical emissions is detected for the first time, suggesting that the emission regions are not co-spatial. A characteristic variability time-scale of $\sim$10-11 yr is robustly detected in the radio-mm bands ($\geq3σ$), while the optical and $γ$-ray data show weaker, shorter-period signals. The independent constraints from the VLBI core-shift measurements and radio time delays yield consistent estimates of the jet geometry and disturbance propagation, supporting variability governed by jet propagation effects. The long-term modulation is consistent with a slow variation in energy release at the jet base, while individual flares arise from shocks propagating downstream. Jet precession may contribute to the long-term modulation; however, the required viewing angles are inconsistent with the VLBI constraints, indicating that precession alone cannot explain the observed variability.

</details>


### [165] [Crust glass formation reveals the neutron star birth properties in IGR J17480-2446](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22227)
*D. A. Baiko,A. I. Chugunov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: IGR J17480-2446是一个特殊的低质量X射线双星系统，其吸积脉冲星（中子星）具有异常的11 Hz自转频率和极慢的爆发后外壳冷却。研究表明，玻璃层形成是吸积诱导的原始冷晶壳破裂的自然结果，从而确定了吸积物质质量ΔM ≈ 2.4×10⁻⁶ M☉，证实该中子星处于极早期吸积阶段。自转与热态分析揭示了与"再生"中子星（即经历长时间吸积的中子星）相关的独特诞生特性，可能源于电子俘获超新星的中子星形成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释IGR J17480-2446异常的自转频率（11 Hz）和极慢的爆发后外壳冷却现象，特别是探究外层 crust 中低热导率玻璃层的形成机制及其对中子星演化阶段的指示意义。

Method: 通过理论分析论证玻璃层形成是吸积诱导原始冷晶壳破裂的自然结果；基于此模型计算吸积物质质量；结合自转与热态分析推断中子星的诞生性质及其与超新星类型的关联。

Result: 确定吸积物质质量ΔM ≈ 2.4×10⁻⁶ M☉，证实中子星处于极早期吸积阶段；揭示其诞生特性与"再生"中子星相似，但实际尚未经历长时间吸积。

Conclusion: IGR J17480-2446的异常性质可统一解释为极早期吸积阶段的表现，其独特的诞生特性可能与电子俘获超新星形成的中子星有关，为理解中子星形成和演化提供了新视角。

Abstract: IGR J17480-2446 is a low-mass X-ray binary, harboring an exceptional accreting pulsar (a neutron star) with an unusual spin frequency of 11 Hz and a very slow post-outburst crust cooling. The former may imply that it is observed at an early stage of recycling, while the latter was shown to indicate the presence in the outer crust of a low thermal conductivity layer, possibly made of glass. Here we argue that the glass layer formation is a natural result of accretion induced failure of pristine cold crystal crust. This allows us to determine the mass of the accreted material as $ΔM \approx 2.4\times 10^{-6}~M_\odot$, confirming very early accretion stage for this neutron star. An analysis of spin and thermal state reveals a peculiar set of neutron star birth properties which is commonly associated with `recycled' neutron stars, i.e.\ those that have been experiencing prolonged periods of accretion from a companion. We speculate that such birth properties may represent the outcome of neutron star formation in an electron-capture supernova.

</details>


### [166] [Fast targeted gravitational-wave followup search for compact binary mergers using GSTLAL pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22266)
*Leo Tsukada,Noah Zhang,Surabhi Sachdev,Shomik Adhicary,Chad Hanna,Prathamesh Joshi,Divya Singh*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用GstLAL流水线进行紧凑双星并合引力波信号的定向搜索方法，通过融入电磁触发事件的天空定位和时间信息，提升对亚阈值信号的探测灵敏度。注入研究表明，该方法相比标准全天空搜索显著提高了与给定位置和时间一致的信号探测效率，同时保持可控的误报率，为多信使天文学提供了快速后随观测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全天空引力波搜索在电磁触发事件（如短伽马暴）的对应引力波信号探测上灵敏度不足，需要一种利用先验信息进行定向搜索的方法来提升亚阈值信号的探测能力，以支持多信使天文学研究。

Method: 在GstLAL流水线的似然比排名统计量中融入天空定位先验信息，利用外部电磁触发事件提供的天空位置和时间信息对特定区域进行聚焦分析，修改标准似然比公式以加入天空定位先验项。

Result: 注入研究表明，定向搜索相比标准全天空搜索配置在探测效率上有显著提升，能够更有效地识别与给定天空位置和时间一致的信号，同时误报率保持在可控范围内。

Conclusion: 该定向搜索框架能够实现对电磁暂现事件的快速后随观测，在先进引力波探测器时代有效推动多信使天文学的发展。

Abstract: We present a novel method to conduct targeted gravitational-wave searches for compact binary mergers using the GstLAL inspiral pipeline. By incorporating sky localization and timing information from external electromagnetic triggers, we enhance the sensitivity of the search for sub-threshold gravitational-wave signals associated with events such as short gamma-ray bursts. Our approach modifies the standard likelihood ratio ranking statistic to include a sky localization prior, allowing for a more focused analysis on specific regions of the sky. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this method through injection studies, comparing the performance of the targeted search against the standard all-sky search configuration. The results show a significant improvement in detection efficiency for signals consistent with the provided sky location and timing, while maintaining control over false alarm rates. This targeted search framework enables rapid follow-up of electromagnetic transients, facilitating multi-messenger astronomy efforts in the era of advanced gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [167] [EMU discovery of Thunder: a bow-shock PWN powered by PSR J1631-4722 escaping Nimbus SNR (G336.7+0.5)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22354)
*S. Lazarević,C. Maitra,A. Ahmad,R. Z. E. Alsaberi,S. Dai,D. Leahy,M. D. Filipović,P. G. Edwards,O. Kargaltsev,M. Abdelmaguid,J. D. Gelfand,J. West,S. Hutschenreuter,R. Brose,R. Kothes,V. Velović,C. Burger-Scheidlin,B. D. Ball,G. Graham,A. M. Hopkins,G. P. Rowell,S. F. Rahman,Z. J. Smeaton,S. Taziaux*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了在超新星遗迹G336.7+0.5(Nimbus)中发现的一个弓形激波脉冲星风云(PWN)Thunder，由射电脉冲星PSR J1631-4722供能。通过ASKAP EMU巡天首次识别，MeerKAT和ATCA后续观测多波段表征，揭示了一颗高速脉冲星驱动的长约2.7 pc的彗星状星云，具有平坦射电谱、硬X射线光子指数和高有序极化磁场，系统年龄约30-45 kyr，处于晚期Sedov相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超新星遗迹中寻找并表征脉冲星风云，以理解脉冲星与周围环境的相互作用、粒子加速机制以及超新星遗迹的演化阶段。

Method: 利用ASKAP EMU巡天数据识别目标，结合MeerKAT银河平面巡天数据和ATCA 5.5/9 GHz后续观测进行射电成像；分析XMM-Newton存档数据获取X射线对应体；进行射电谱指数、X射线光子指数、偏振分析和磁场强度估计；通过脉冲星定时监测分析自转行为。

Result: 发现了一个长约80角秒(2.7 pc)的彗星状弓形激波PWN；X射线对应体长约50角秒(1.7 pc)；射电谱指数α=-0.27±0.05，X射线光子指数Γ=1.6±0.4；偏振分数高达30%，磁场高度有序排列；磁场强度Beq≈54-140 μG；检测到小幅度自转突变；系统年龄约30-45 kyr。

Conclusion: Nimbus-Thunder系统是一个处在晚期Sedov相（向辐射相过渡）的年轻超新星遗迹-脉冲星风云系统，多波段性质与演化模型一致，为研究脉冲星高速运动、相对论性粒子注入和超新星遗迹演化提供了重要案例。

Abstract: We report the discovery of a bow-shock pulsar wind nebula (PWN), dubbed Thunder, powered by the radio pulsar PSR J1631-4722 and projected within the Galactic supernova remnant (SNR) G336.7+0.5 (Nimbus). The system was first identified in observations from the Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP) Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU) survey and further characterised using MeerKAT Galactic Plane Survey data together with follow-up observations at 5.5 and 9 GHz obtained with the Australia Telescope Compact Array (ATCA). Assuming a distance of 7 kpc, the radio images resolve an elongated ~80 arcsec (2.7 pc) cometary nebula, indicative of a high velocity pulsar. An X-ray counterpart extending ~50 arcsec (1.7 pc) is detected in archival XMM-Newton data. The flat radio spectrum ($α$ = -0.27 $\pm$ 0.05) and hard X-ray photon index ($Γ$ = 1.6 $\pm$ 0.4) indicate synchrotron emission from relativistic particles injected in the pulsar wind. Polarisation analysis reveals a highly ordered magnetic field aligned with the nebular flow, with fractional polarisation reaching up to 30% in the tail. An equipartition estimate gives a PWN magnetic-field strength of Beq $\approx$ 54-140 $μ$G. Pulsar timing over a ~2.2 yr baseline reveals strong timing noise and a small spin glitch with amplitude $Δν/ν$ = 1.10$\times$10$^{-8}$. The SNR shows no clear diffuse X-ray counterpart. The morphology and multiwavelength properties of the Nimbus-Thunder system, along with evolutionary models, constrain the system's age to approximately 30-45 kyr, placing the remnant in the late Sedov phase, approaching the transition to the radiative stage.

</details>


### [168] [Fermi bubbles detected in $\sim$100 TeV neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22387)
*Uri Keshet,Ilya Gurwich*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用 IceCube 12 年高能起始事件数据，发现与 Fermi 气泡和 eROSITA 气泡的 X 射线壳层存在 >4σ 的中微子关联，验证了银河系中心爆发产生的激波可加速离子至 PeV 能量，能量约 10^54.5 erg，加速效率约 10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fermi 气泡是银河系中心爆发产生的强激波，理论预期可产生 >10 TeV 中微子，但早期 IceCube 数据不足以验证该信号。本文旨在利用更长的观测数据检验这一预测。

Method: 使用 IceCube 12 年 HESE 数据，与 Fermi-LAT 和 eROSITA 的 X 射线壳层图进行空间关联分析，分别在银道面外和气泡壳层方向进行统计检验。

Result: 1) IceCube HESE 数据与 Fermi-LAT 天图在银道面外存在 >3σ 关联；2) 利用 eROSITA 定位气泡壳层，在 |b|>30° 区域检测到两个气泡（>4σ），其中东侧 X 射线亮壳层局部过剩 >5σ；3) 信号符合相对论离子携带 ~10^54.5 erg 能量，加速至 >PeV，激波加速效率 ~10%；4) 初步发现 eROSITA 气泡更大壳层也有中微子信号。

Conclusion: 证实了银河系中心爆发产生的强激波强度足以将离子加速至 PeV 能量，并产生可探测的高能中微子信号。结果支持激波加速效率约 10%，且 eROSITA 气泡的外壳层同样存在中微子发射。

Abstract: The Fermi bubbles have been identified as a collimated bipolar outflow emanating from the Milky-Way center, tracing strong forward shocks which extend $\sim10$ kpc from the Galactic disk, originating from a $\sim10^{56\pm1}$ erg outburst. These shocks are sufficiently strong, extended, and energetic to produce a detectable flux of $\gtrsim 10$ TeV neutrinos, especially in their denser north and east, but early IceCube data were insufficient for identifying the signal. We find that IceCube high-energy starting events (HESE 12-year) correlate with the $\textit{Fermi}$-LAT sky map outside the Galactic plane ($>3σ$). Testing for neutrinos coincident with the bubble shells, localized using $\textit{eROSITA}$ data, we detect ($>4σ$) both bubbles at high ($|b|>30^{\circ}$) latitudes, with a local excess ($>5σ$) mainly in their X-ray bright eastern shells. The signal matches the anticipated secondaries of relativistic ions carrying $\sim 10^{54.5}$ erg (with factor $\sim3$ uncertainty) in each bubble, shock-accelerated to $>$PeV energies. The results verify the strength of the shocks, suggesting an ion acceleration efficiency of order $\sim10\%$. We also present preliminary evidence for neutrinos from the even larger shells of the eROSITA bubbles, which encapsulate their younger Fermi-bubble counterparts, carrying a similar energy and confined by shocks nearly as strong.

</details>


### [169] [Unified Mass-Scaled QPO Signatures of Kerr Sen Black Holes from Stellar Mass to Supermassive Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22446)
*Orhan Donmez,G. Mustafa*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究了Kerr-Sen黑洞周围的Bondi-Hoyle-Lyttleton（BHL）吸积过程，发现电荷相关的时空形变会改变激波锥形态、质量吸积率以及类准周期振荡（QPO）的时间行为。计算得到的主频率与多种恒星质量、中等质量和超大质量黑洞系统的观测QPO吻合良好，表明Kerr-Sen黑洞激波锥振荡可能为不同质量尺度黑洞的时变特征提供统一解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kerr-Sen黑洞（弦理论预言的带电荷旋转黑洞）的电荷参数如何影响BHL吸积流的激波锥形态、质量吸积率和QPO时变特征，并探索其能否作为统一框架解释不同质量尺度黑洞的观测QPO。

Method: 在赤道面上对两种自旋参数（a=0.9M和a=0.5M）下的Kerr-Sen黑洞进行相对论BHL吸积的数值模拟，通过对质量吸积率信号计算功率谱密度（PSD）并进行多分量Lorentzian拟合，识别主导QPO模式；通过逆质量标度将数值频率与多类观测QPO比较。

Result: Kerr-Sen形变改变了特征频率和振荡模式的相干性，产生了接近3:2和2:1的近共振谐波结构。数值模拟结果与GRS 1915+105、IGR J17091-3624、M82 X-1、NGC 5408 X-1、RE J1034+396、1H 0707-495和ESO 113-G010等源的观测QPO合理吻合。

Conclusion: Kerr-Sen黑洞激波锥振荡可能为不同质量范围的黑洞提供统一的QPO解释框架，结合流体动力学和时变诊断有助于区分Kerr-Sen几何与Kerr时空的偏差，并可约束尚未完全确定的源的质量和自旋参数。

Abstract: In this study, we numerically investigate Bondi-Hoyle-Lyttleton (BHL) accretion around Kerr-Sen black holes and examine how the charge-related deformation of the spacetime affects the shock-cone morphology, the variation of the mass accretion rate, and the quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO)-like temporal behavior. The relativistic BHL flow is solved numerically in the equatorial plane for two different black hole spin parameters, a = 0.9 M and a = 0.5 M. From the numerically computed mass accretion rate signal, we calculate the power spectral density (PSD) and perform multi-component Lorentzian fits to identify the dominant QPO-like modes excited around the black hole. The results show that the Kerr-Sen deformation shifts the characteristic frequencies, changes the coherence properties of the oscillation modes, and produces near-resonant harmonic structures close to 3:2 and 2:1. By using inverse mass scaling, the numerically computed frequencies are compared with observed QPOs from stellar-mass, intermediate-mass, and supermassive black hole systems. In particular, reasonable agreement between the numerical simulation results and observations is found for the sources GRS 1915+105, IGR J17091-3624, M82 X-1, NGC 5408 X-1, RE J1034+396, 1H 0707-495, and ESO 113-G010. This comparative analysis indicates that Kerr-Sen black hole shock-cone oscillations may provide a unified framework for interpreting timing features over a broad range of black hole masses and may additionally contribute to constraining the mass and spin parameters of sources whose properties are not yet fully established observationally. These findings further imply that combined hydrodynamical and timing diagnostics constitute a promising approach for assessing the extent to which deviations associated with the Kerr-Sen geometry can be empirically distinguished from those of the Kerr spacetime.

</details>


### [170] [Polarization Angle Geodesics in PSRs B1133+16 and B2016+28](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22667)
*M. M. McKinnon*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过重新分析PSR B1133+16和B2016+28的偏振观测数据，发现其偏振角在庞加莱球上分别遵循大圆和小圆弧轨迹，并利用三种偏振模型证明这些测地线主要由正交偏振模转换引起，表明模转换不限于庞加莱球赤道面，圆弧特征可能比以往认知更普遍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验脉冲星偏振模型中关于偏振矢量在庞加莱球上沿大圆或小圆轨迹传播的预测，并探究这些几何特征背后的物理机制。

Method: 重新分析PSR B1133+16和B2016+28的偏振观测数据，在庞加莱球上拟合偏振角轨迹，并结合三种不同偏振模型进行对比解释。

Result: PSR B1133+16的偏振角遵循大圆轨迹；PSR B2016+28的偏振角呈现受自转影响的大圆弧轨迹。三种模型对两颗脉冲星给出相似结论，表明观测到的测地线主要由模转换引起。PSR B2016+28的弧也可解释为椭圆极化模下的矢量旋转。

Conclusion: 模转换不限于庞加莱球赤道面，圆弧和部分圆特征可能比先前认识更普遍。

Abstract: Recent models of pulsar polarization predict that the position and ellipticity angles of the polarization vector can trace portions of a small or great circle on the Poincare sphere. A great circle can arise from a transition in dominance of orthogonal polarization modes, where the relative intensity of the modes changes with pulse longitude. A small circle may be caused by a rotation of the vector, where the phase difference between the modes changes with pulse longitude or wavelength. Observations of PSRs B1133+16 and B2016+28 are reanalyzed to search for these polarization features within their pulse profiles. The polarization angles observed in part of PSR B1133+16 are shown to follow a great circle on the Poincare sphere. The angles observed across the pulse of PSR B2016+28 follow an arc that resembles a portion of a great circle that has been altered by the pulsar's rotation. The observations are interpreted within the context of three different polarization models. All three models produce similar results for both pulsars and indicate that the observed geodesics are caused by mode transitions. The arc observed in PSR B2016+28 can also be interpreted as a vector rotation, provided the modes are elliptically polarized. The observations and accompanying analysis show that mode transitions are not restricted to the equatorial plane of the Poincare sphere and that arcs and partial circles may be more common than previously recognized.

</details>


### [171] [Finite-volume scheme for first-order viscoresistive relativistic magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22691)
*Ruben Lier,Jay Armas,Oliver Porth*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于BDNK理论实现了耗散相对论磁流体动力学的数值方案，通过一阶修正使方程保持因果性，无需引入额外动力学变量。在有限体积格式中处理能量、动量和磁场的耦合耗散，发现超相对论极限下需要额外修正以确保稳定性和因果性，并通过解析基准和二维模拟验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有耗散相对论磁流体力学方案存在因果性问题，需要引入额外动力学变量或高阶修正。BDNK理论提供了一阶修正即可保证因果性的框架，但尚未有完整的数值实现，尤其是在磁场存在的情况下。

Method: 基于BDNK理论，在有限体积格式中实现一阶修正项的数值方案，将磁场视为一形式电荷处理，并针对超相对论极限引入额外修正，开发了高效的初等变量恢复方法。

Result: 最小BDNK项可将扩散方程转化为电报方程类型，但在超相对论极限下需要额外修正才能保证稳定性和因果性。通过解析基准和二维模拟验证了数值方案的准确性。

Conclusion: 提出的数值方案能够有效实现因果且稳定的耗散相对论磁流体动力学模拟，为一阶耗散理论的实际数值应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present a numerical implementation of dissipative relativistic magnetohydrodynamics based on Bemfica-Disconzi-Noronha-Kovtun (BDNK) theory, in which first-order corrections render the equations causal without introducing additional dynamical variables. We show how these corrections can be incorporated in a finite-volume scheme describing the coupled dissipation of energy, momentum, and magnetic field, with the latter treated as a one-form charge. While a minimal set of BDNK terms can convert diffusive equations into telegrapher-type equations, we find that in the ultra-relativistic limit an additional correction is required for the system to behave in a stable and causal manner. With this set of equations, we develop an efficient method for primitive-variable recovery and validate the implementation through an analytical benchmark and various two-dimensional simulations.

</details>


### [172] [Testing the Generalization and Domain Stability of Compact Feature Representations for Photometric Supernova Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22879)
*Anurag Garg*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了超新星光度分类中16维紧凑特征表示的可泛化性和域稳定性。在SPCC数据集中，XGBoost分类器取得F1=0.844、PR-AUC=0.928的良好性能，但在跨调查迁移（SPCC→PLAsTiCC）时性能显著下降。类条件质心分析表明，性能下降主要由特征空间域偏移驱动，而非分类器不稳定。结论是紧凑特征在单一调查内鲁棒，但并非天然的调查不变特征，跨调查部署需要特征协调或域自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超新星光度分类需要模型不仅能在单一调查中表现准确，还能对观测节奏、噪声特性、滤光片覆盖和调查域的变化保持鲁棒。现有紧凑特征表示是否具备这种跨域泛化能力尚不清楚。

Method: 使用SPCC数据集作为参考域，构建16维物理可解释特征（亮度、颜色、变异性、时间演化），采用XGBoost分类器。通过替代分类器、重复重采样、特征扰动、缺失波段代理、缩短时间覆盖以及跨调查迁移（SPCC→PLAsTiCC）等多种实验评估鲁棒性，并利用类条件质心分析诊断域偏移。

Result: 紧凑XGBoost在SPCC内取得强性能（F1=0.844, PR-AUC=0.928）；在重采样和适度扰动下保持稳定；但SPCC→PLAsTiCC直接迁移时性能大幅下降。质心分析显示两个调查的Ia型超新星在特征空间中分布不同，且跨调查的Ia质心偏移大于任一调查内的Ia/non-Ia分离距离。

Conclusion: 紧凑物理可解释特征在单一调查内鲁棒且可用于诊断分析，但并非天然的调查不变特征。跨调查部署需要特征协调、域自适应或限制使用调查稳定特征子集。

Abstract: Photometric classification of supernovae increasingly requires models that are not only accurate within a single survey but also robust to changes in cadence, noise properties, filter coverage, and survey domain. We investigate the generalization and domain stability of a compact 16-feature representation for Type Ia supernova classification. The feature set consists of physically interpretable descriptors of brightness, color, variability, and temporal evolution. Using the Supernova Photometric Classification Challenge (SPCC) dataset as the reference domain, we confirm that a compact XGBoost classifier achieves strong within-survey performance, reaching an F1 score of 0.844 and a PR-AUC of 0.928 on a held-out test set. We then evaluate robustness under alternative classifiers, repeated resampling, feature perturbations, missing-band proxies, shortened temporal coverage, and cross-survey transfer to PLAsTiCC.
  The compact representation remains stable under resampling and moderate perturbations, but direct SPCC$\rightarrow$PLAsTiCC transfer produces a substantial performance degradation. Class-conditional centroid analysis shows that the SPCC and PLAsTiCC Type Ia populations occupy different regions of the compact feature space. The cross-survey Ia centroid shift exceeds the Ia/non-Ia separation within either survey, indicating that the transfer gap is driven primarily by feature-space domain shift rather than classifier instability. These results show that compact physically interpretable features are robust within a survey and useful for diagnostic analysis, but are not automatically survey-invariant. Cross-survey deployment therefore requires feature harmonization, domain adaptation, or restriction to a smaller set of survey-stable features.

</details>


### [173] [Are most detected tidal disruption events partial?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22919)
*Megha Sharma,Daniel J. Price,Alexander Heger,Katie Auchettle*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文使用流体动力学模拟研究恒星被黑洞部分潮汐瓦解事件（TDE），发现质量回落率可超爱丁顿极限，光变曲线比t^{-5/3}更平缓，部分完全TDE事件可能实为部分TDE，零能量部分TDE与重复部分TDE性质相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有数十例TDE事件被探测到，包括重复部分事件。需要理解部分潮汐瓦解事件中恒星残骸的动力学和辐射特征，区分部分与完全TDE事件，并解释观测到的光学/紫外TDE。

Method: 使用Phantom光滑粒子流体动力学（SPH）代码模拟1倍太阳质量恒星绕10^6倍太阳质量黑洞的瓦解过程，考虑导致少于等于50%质量损失的撞击参数，仅考虑零能量轨道。假设辐射被束缚、电子散射主导不透明度、颜色修正系数f_col=1.7，计算温度、光度和黑体半径。

Result: 质量回落率在β≥0.8时超过爱丁顿极限；光变曲线比t^{-5/3}更平缓，接近t^{-9/4}；温度约10^4 K，光学光度约10^{42-44} erg/s，黑体半径10-100 au；β=0.8等熵模拟中X射线光度约10^{44-45} erg/s，半径低于最内稳定圆轨道。

Conclusion: 支持部分TDE可能被误分类为完全TDE的观点；零能量部分TDE与ASSASN-19dj等重复部分TDE性质相似，可解释探测到的光学/紫外TDE。

Abstract: During a tidal disruption event (TDE), a star loses mass due to the tidal gravitational forces of the black hole. In a partial tidal disruption event, a stellar remnant is left behind. Several dozen TDEs have been detected so far, including repeating partial events. We use the Phantom smoothed particle hydrodynamics code to model the disruption of a 1 Msun star around a 10^6 Msun black hole for impact parameters resulting in less than equal to 50 % mass loss. We only consider zero energy orbits. Our simulations show that the mass fallback rate can exceed the Eddington limit for beta greater than or equal to 0.8, allowing debris to obscure the accretion disc by forming a reprocessing layer, similar to full TDEs. The mass fallback rate is shallower than t^{-5/3}, tracking closer to t^{-9/4}. Assuming thermal emission from the debris, that shock heating is trapped, that electron scattering dominates the opacity, and a color correction f_{col} of 1.7, we find temperatures of ~10^4 K, optical bolometric luminosities of ~ 10^{42-44} erg/s and blackbody radii ranging from 10-100 au for our simulations. We compare our values with observations and find support for the previous argument that some TDEs classified as full disruptions might actually be partial. Moreover, our results explain the detected optical/UV TDEs. We also find that our zero energy partial TDEs have properties similar to the repeating partial TDEs such as ASSASN-19dj, ASSASN-14ko, ASSASN-18ul, ASSASN-22ci, AT2020vdq and AT2022dbl. In the beta=0.8, isentropic simulation where radiation is assumed to escape, we find X-ray luminosities of ~ 10^{44-45} erg/s and radii lower than the inner most stable circular orbit.

</details>


### [174] [On the assessment of the disk truncation and detection of type-II bursts from the accreting millisecond X-ray Pulsar IGR J17062-6143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23156)
*Aditya Sow Mondal,Mahasweta Bhattacharya,Swarnendu Jana,Biplab Raychaudhuri,Gulab C. Dewangan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对2022年NuSTAR和NICER观测到的吸积毫秒X射线脉冲星IGR J17062-6143进行了能谱分析。源处于硬谱态，光度约为爱丁顿光度0.2-1.3%。NuSTAR能谱中的连续辐射由幂律成分或高温等离子体的康普顿化发射主导，并明显存在盘反射特征（Fe K线及康普顿驼峰）。反射模型表明盘延伸至中子星表面附近（~7-17 R_g），轨道倾角较低（~20°-40°）。还首次在NICER观测中检测到II型爆发，其轮廓呈现不同模式，能谱可用康普顿化成分描述，爆发起源可能与磁层闸门机制有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对低光度吸积毫秒X射线脉冲星IGR J17062-6143进行详细的能谱分析，探究其在硬谱态下的辐射机制、吸积盘几何结构以及首次检测到的II型爆发的物理特性。

Method: 利用NuSTAR和NICER 2022年的观测数据，进行光谱分析；采用幂律模型和康普顿化模型描述连续辐射；使用relxill和relxillCP自洽相对论反射模型拟合盘反射特征；通过时变分析识别并分类II型爆发；利用康普顿化模型拟合爆发能谱。

Result: NuSTAR能谱显示明显的盘反射特征（宽Fe K线和康普顿驼峰），反射模型揭示吸积盘内边界延伸至~7-17 R_g，轨道倾角~20°-40°；首次检测到II型爆发，呈现mode-0和mode-1两种模式；爆发能谱可由温度1-3 keV的冕的康普顿化描述。

Conclusion: IGR J17062-6143在2022年处于硬谱态，辐射由高温等离子体的康普顿化主导；吸积盘延伸至中子星表面附近且倾角较低；首次检测到的II型爆发可能与磁层闸门机制有关，丰富了对该源吸积过程的理解。

Abstract: We present a spectral analysis of the NuSTAR and NICER observations of the accreting millisecond X-ray pulsar IGR J17062-6143, performed in 2022. The source remained in the hard spectral state during the observations, with a luminosity of about 0.2-1.3$\%$ of the Eddington luminosity. The continuum emission of the NuSTAR spectrum is entirely dominated by a power-law component or by Comptonized emission of disk photons by a plasma with a high electron temperature ($\gtrsim100$ keV). The NuSTAR spectrum also reveals clear evidence of disk reflection, a broad Fe K line around 6-8 keV, and a Compton hump peaking at 20 keV, irrespective of the choice of the continuum models. Our spectral studies suggest a disk extending close to the neutron star surface ($\sim$7-17 $R_{\rm g}$) at low inclination angles ($\sim$20$^\circ$-40$^\circ$), as revealed by a couple of self-consistent relativistic reflection models, relxill and relxillCP. In addition, we detected type-II bursts for the first time in the NICER observation of this source.
