Light curve profiles of type-II bursts exhibit different patterns, mostly associated with the so-called mode-0 and mode-1 type-II bursts. The energy spectra of the persistent (pre-burst) and burst emission are well described by an absorbed Comptonization component, scattering diskbb- and blackbody-distributed photons, respectively, by a corona with a temperature of 1-3 keV. Although the origin of the type-II burst is not very clear, it has been substantially linked to magnetospheric gating of the accretion flow.

</details>


### [175] [When the black holes align: a subpopulation of aligned massive binary black holes observed via gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23305)
*Stefano Rinaldi,Charmaine Wong,M. Paola Vaccaro,Elvir Mislimi,Otto A. Hannuksela,Samson H. W. Leong,Michela Mapelli*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本工作利用非参数方法分析GWTC-5.0数据中双黑洞的主质量与有效自旋联合分布，发现至少存在两个具有不同有效自旋分布的子群体，其中偏向正χ_eff值的子群体指向非球对称动力学环境形成的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不依赖特定模型假设的前提下，探究双黑洞主质量与有效自旋分布中存在的特征，从而更客观地揭示双黑洞群体的真实物理结构。

Method: 采用非参数方法（灵活模型，能够以最小数学假设逼近任意概率密度函数）分析GWTC-5.0数据。

Result: 分析支持存在至少两个独立双黑洞子群体，它们呈现不同的有效自旋分布。

Conclusion: 偏向正χ_eff值的子群体指向在非球对称动力学环境中形成的双黑洞系统。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the features present in the joint primary mass and effective spin distribution of binary black holes without relying on specific modelling assumptions. We make use of non-parametric methods, flexible models capable of approximating arbitrary probability densities with minimal mathematical assumptions, applying it to the newly released GWTC-5.0. Our analysis supports the presence of at least two separate sub-population of binary black holes showing different effective spin distributions: one of them, preferring positive $χ_\mathrm{eff}$ values, points towards the direction of systems formed in a non-spherically-symmetric, dynamical environment.

</details>


### [176] [Galactic Cosmic Ray Transport in the Giant Circumgalactic Medium Halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23317)
*Chao-Ming Li,Andrew M. Taylor*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究银河系周介质（CGM，即巨型晕）对宇宙线（CR）传输的影响，发现巨型晕模型与现有次级/初级宇宙线测量一致，并产生约1/r的空间尾部和更宽的年龄分布，为传统小晕模型提供了物理上更合理的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近观测揭示银河系嵌入一个延伸至数百千秒差距的巨型星系周介质（CGM），这一发现促使研究者探究这种扩展的气体晕作为重子库和宇宙线约束体积的作用，从而质疑传统小晕模型的适用性。

Method: 通过对比巨型晕模型与传统小晕模型中的宇宙线传输特性，分析晕高度与源区范围的关系，并利用次级/初级宇宙线测量数据进行一致性检验，同时评估银河系气体分布不确定性与核散裂截面不确定性的影响。

Result: 巨型晕模型中源区内的宇宙线传输与小晕模型相似，但在更大距离处出现显著差异，表现为约1/r的空间尾部和更宽的年龄分布；该模型与当前次级/初级宇宙线测量一致；银河系气体分布的不确定性与核散裂截面不确定性相当。

Conclusion: 巨型晕模型为传统小晕模型提供了物理上更合理的替代方案，并对银河系弥散伽马射线和中微子发射有重要启示。

Abstract: Recent observations have revealed that the Milky Way is embedded in a massive circumgalactic medium (CGM) extending to several hundred kiloparsecs. Such an extended gaseous halo acts as both a reservoir of baryons and potentially as a confinement volume for Galactic cosmic rays (CRs). We investigate CR transport in this giant Galactic halo and compare its properties with those of conventional small-halo models. In the giant-halo scenario, the halo height is no longer a free parameter, but instead relates to the extent of the source region. We show that CR transport within the source region remains similar to that in small-halo models, while substantial differences emerge at larger distances. In the giant-halo scenario, CRs develop an extended approximately 1/r spatial tail and exhibit a broader age distribution than the small-halo case. This model is shown to be consistent with current secondary-to-primary CR measurements. We further find that uncertainties associated with Galactic gas distributions are comparable to those arising from nuclear spallation cross sections. These results suggest that the giant-halo model provides a physically motivated alternative to conventional small-halo models and may have important implications for diffuse gamma-ray and neutrino emission from the Galactic environment.

</details>


### [177] [Reverberation in the Narrow Fe K$α$ Line in the Seyfert Galaxy NGC 4151 with XRISM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23384)
*Jon M. Miller,Aberahmen Zoghbi,Xin Xiang,Doyee Byun,Missagh Mehdipour,Liyi Gu,Ehud Behar,Laura Brenneman,Elisa Costantini,Luigi Gallo,Matteo Guainazzi,Peter Kosec,Takeo Minezaki,Stephane Paltani*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用XRISM和Swift对Seyfert-1.5星系NGC 4151的时序观测，通过Fe Kα发射线的回声映射测量出约3.5天的时延，并据此估算出黑洞质量约为2.0×10⁷ M⊙，验证了XRISM在AGN回声映射研究中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞附近的电离通量变化会在发射线中产生"回声"效应，为探测中心引擎提供有力手段。NGC 4151的高分辨率X射线光谱和低分辨率数据的时延表明窄Fe Kα线部分起源于光学宽线区附近，因此需要精确测量其时延以约束黑洞质量与几何结构。

Method: 利用XRISM在2024年每隔一天获取的9次观测序列，结合Swift监测驱动光变；采用合适的线核测量BLR成分宽度；通过Swift连续谱与XRISM线通量趋势建模，结合蒙特卡洛模拟计算时延并推导黑洞质量。

Result: BLR成分宽度σ=5.36±0.48×10³ km/s；时延τ=3.5⁺²·⁸₋₁·₇天（对应r=3.6⁺³·⁰₋₁·₇×10³ GM/c²），显著性达2σ；黑洞质量M_BH/f_X=2.0⁺¹·⁴₋₁·₀×10⁷ M⊙。

Conclusion: 测量结果与先前研究及直接光谱拟合一致，表明XRISM能够测量未被遮挡和被遮挡AGN的时延及黑洞质量，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Emission lines that "echo" variations in the ionizing flux produced close to black holes are powerful probes of the central engine. In the Seyfert-1.5 galaxy NGC 4151, high-resolution X-ray spectra and time lags in low-resolution X-ray data suggest that part of the narrow Fe K$_α$ line originates close to the optical broad line region (BLR). We report on a sequence of nine XRISM observations of NGC 4151, obtained every other day in 2024. Swift monitoring was undertaken to sample the driving flux before, during, and after the XRISM sequence. Using suitable line kernels, we measure a mean BLR component width of $σ= 5.36\pm 0.48\times 10^{3}~{\rm km}~ {\rm s}^{-1}$. Modeling the Swift continuum and XRISM line flux trends gives a lag of $τ= 3.5^{+2.8}_{-1.7}$ days ($r=3.6^{+3.0}_{-1.7}\times 10^{3}~GM/c^{2}$ for $M_{BH} = 1.7\times 10^{7}~M_{\odot}$), significant at the $2σ$ level via Monte Carlo simulations, and consistent with prior measurements and direct spectral fits. This lag implies a black hole mass of $M_{BH}/f_{X} = 2.0^{+1.4}_{-1.0}\times 10^{7} M_{\odot}$, where $f_{X}$ is a geometrical factor. A standard optical value for this factor gives a mass that is nominally higher than typical H$β$ mass estimates, but formally consistent. Our results suggest that XRISM can measure lags and black hole masses in both unobscured and obscured AGN.

</details>


### [178] [Neutron Star Mass-Radius Constraints for EXO 0748$-$676 from 2008-2025 Quiescent X-ray Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23466)
*Mingyang Wang,Guobao Zhang,Ang Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对2008-2025年间20次宁静期X射线观测（14次Chandra+6次XMM-Newton）的联合分析，对中子星低质量X射线双星EXO 0748-676中的中子星质量和半径给出了新约束，得到质量1.77^{+0.17}_{-0.22} M⊙、半径12.62^{+0.56}_{-0.74} km，并追踪了2024-2025年爆发后的热演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星的质量和半径是约束致密物质状态方程的关键观测量，但此前对EXO 0748-676的约束较为宽松，且该源的两次宁静期及一次新的爆发事件（2024-2025）为获取更高精度的联合约束提供了独特机会。

Method: 利用20组宁静期X射线光谱数据（0.5-10 keV），采用氢大气模型进行全局MCMC分析，将氢柱密度、中子星质量和半径作为所有观测的共同参数联合拟合，并同时独立拟合两个宁静阶段；进一步引入距离不确定性（7.1±1.2 kpc）给出保守约束。

Result: 全局拟合得到M=1.77^{+0.17}_{-0.22} M⊙、R=12.62^{+0.56}_{-0.74} km（1σ）；联合数据集显著缩减了后验分布的低质量尾端；考虑距离不确定性后，M≃1.41-2.11 M⊙、R≃10.15-15.13 km，倾向于较硬的致密物质状态方程。

Conclusion: 联合多次宁静期观测显著提高了中子星质量和半径的约束精度，特别是消除了低质量尾；两次爆发后的热弛豫对比为进一步研究中子星星壳加热与冷却过程提供了新视角。

Abstract: We present new constraints on the mass and radius of the neutron star in the neutron star low-mass X-ray binary EXO 0748$-$676 obtained from a joint analysis of 20 quiescent X-ray observations obtained between 2008 and 2025, including 14 Chandra and 6 XMM-Newton exposures. These data sample two quiescent episodes separated by the 2024$-$2025 outburst. We model the 0.5$-$10 keV spectra with a hydrogen-atmosphere model, assuming a source distance of 7.1 kpc. In a global Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis in which the hydrogen column density, neutron star mass, and radius are tied across all observations, we obtain a neutron-star mass of $1.77^{+0.17}_{-0.22}\,M_\odot$ and a radius of $12.62^{+0.56}_{-0.74}$ km ($1σ$ credible intervals). We further perform independent fits to the first and second quiescent epochs and find that the combined data set significantly reduces the low-mass tail in the posterior distribution, leading to tighter lower bounds on the neutron-star mass. Incorporating the distance uncertainty of $7.1\pm1.2$ kpc, we conservatively constrain the neutron-star mass and radius to $M\simeq 1.41-2.11~M_{\odot}$ and $R\simeq 10.15-15.13$ km, favoring relatively stiff dense-matter equations of state. We also trace the thermal evolution across two quiescent epochs and find evidence for renewed crust cooling following the 2024$-$2025 outburst, providing a unique opportunity to compare the thermal relaxation behavior after two distinct accretion episodes.

</details>


### [179] [TeV-PeV Gamma-ray and Neutrino Emission in the Galactic Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23476)
*Saikat Das,Nayantara Gupta,Siyao Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究构建了LHAASO观测到的银河系弥散TeV-PeV伽马射线模型，包含来自脉冲星风云的未分辨轻子成分和超新星注入宇宙线质子的强子成分。研究分析了星际辐射场红外成分径向分布的不确定性，及其对伽马射线衰减的影响。替代模型对LHAASO弥散拟合影响较小，但会显著改变中心区域点源在高能段的辐射。未来KM3NeT与伽马射线联合观测可探测内银河宇宙线分布并约束红外辐射场分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究星际辐射场红外成分的径向分布不确定性对LHAASO观测到的银河系弥散TeV-PeV伽马射线建模的影响，并评估这些不确定性对强子-轻子成分分离、伽马射线衰减及中微子辐射预测的连锁效应。

Method: 将LHAASO观测的弥散伽马射线建模为脉冲星风云的未分辨轻子辐射和超新星注入宇宙线质子的强子辐射之和；引入不同径向分布的红外星际辐射场模型，量化其对伽马射线衰减的影响；通过强子归一化推算相关中微子辐射，并与IceCube、ANTARES和KM3NeT观测比较。

Result: 替代红外星际辐射场模型对LHAASO弥散拟合影响适中（因分析排除了银心方向并应用了源掩蔽）；强子中微子辐射与IceCube全天测量一致，银脊区域通量与ANTARES/KM3NeT约束兼容；红外分布差异对内银河中心区域点源（~10 TeV以上）的强子和逆康普顿辐射有显著影响。

Conclusion: 未来KM3NeT与伽马射线对单个源的高能段联合观测，可有效探测内银河宇宙线粒子分布并约束银心附近星际辐射场的径向分布。

Abstract: We model the LHAASO observation of diffuse TeV--PeV $γ$ rays in the Galactic plane as the sum of unresolved leptonic emission from pulsar wind nebulae and hadronic emission from supernova-injected cosmic-ray (CR) protons. We investigate uncertainties in the radial distribution of the infrared component of the interstellar radiation field (ISRF), using profiles with enhanced photon densities in the inner Galaxy. We quantify their effects on $γγ$ attenuation of the diffuse $γ$-ray emission. The alternative ISRF models affect the LHAASO diffuse fit only modestly, as the analysis excludes the Galactic center direction and applies source masks in the Galactic plane. Using the hadronic normalization inferred from the LHAASO fit for various ISRF models, the associated $pp$ neutrino emission remains consistent with the IceCube all-sky measurement, while the flux from the Galactic Ridge region remains compatible with current ANTARES and KM3NeT constraints. Since the modified infrared profiles differ most strongly toward the inner Galaxy, we also examine their impact on inverse-Compton emission from point sources near the central molecular zone. These same models can noticeably modify the hadronic and inverse-Compton $γ$-ray emission above $\sim\!10$ TeV from sources in the central region. Future KM3NeT observations, combined with $γ$-ray measurements of individual sources, can probe the inner-Galaxy CR population and constrain the radial distribution of the ISRF near the Galactic Center.

</details>


### [180] [A Tentative Double Excess in the Gamma-Ray Spectrum of the Fermi Blazar 4FGL J0604.9-0000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23488)
*Shi-Ju Kang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于17.75年的Fermi-LAT观测数据，我们在耀变体4FGL J0604.9-0000的伽马射线谱中发现了一个双超额候选信号，峰值能量分别为~1.5 GeV和~11 GeV，联合局域显著性约4.3σ（4自由度）或4.8σ（2自由度），其能量比~1:7难以用标准天体物理过程解释，但与质量~11 GeV的暗物质湮灭模型一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找耀变体伽马射线谱中可能存在的异常结构，特别是探索无法用标准天体物理过程解释的谱特征，以间接寻找暗物质湮灭信号。

Method: 利用17.75年的Fermi-LAT观测数据对耀变体4FGL J0604.9-0000进行伽马射线能谱分析，采用双高斯分量拟合超额结构，并进行联合似然分析计算检验统计量（TS）和局域显著性。

Result: 发现两个能量超额：低能超额质心E1=1.59±0.07 GeV（宽度固定为仪器分辨率σ1=0.145 GeV），高能超额质心E2=11.15±0.61 GeV（宽度σ2=1.185 GeV，约峰值能量的10%）。两个特征的局域显著性分别为2.6σ和3.7σ（3自由度），联合分析TS≈27，对应局域显著性约4.3σ（4自由度）或4.8σ（2自由度）。

Conclusion: 该双超额候选信号的能量比~1:7难以用标准辐射过程解释，但与质量~11 GeV的暗物质湮灭（如χχ→γγ和χχ→γγ'）的能量特征一致，使该源成为下一代伽马射线望远镜后续观测的有前景目标。

Abstract: Based on 17.75 years of Fermi-LAT observations, we report a tentative double excess in the gamma-ray spectrum of the blazar 4FGL J0604.9-0000, with peak energies at approximately 1.5 GeV and 11 GeV. The two excesses are modeled as double Gaussian components. For the lower-energy excess, the best-fit centroid is $E_1 = 1.59\pm0.07$ GeV with a width fixed to the instrumental resolution ($σ_1 = 0.145$ GeV). For the higher-energy excess, the centroid is $E_2 = 11.15\pm0.61$ GeV and the width is constrained to $σ_2 = 1.185$ GeV (about 10\% of the peak energy), which is comparable to the Fermi-LAT energy resolution at that energy. The two features have local significances of $2.6σ$ and $3.7σ$ (3 dof), respectively. A joint likelihood analysis yields a combined test statistic of TS $\simeq 27$, corresponding to a local significance of approximately $4.3σ$ (4 dof, without correction for trials and the look-elsewhere effect) or about $4.8σ$ (2 dof). To our knowledge, no other active galactic nucleus has been reported to show a similar double-excess candidate. The observed energy ratio of $\sim$1:7 is difficult to explain with standard astrophysical emission processes. However, the energies and their ratio are consistent with dark matter annihilation (e.g., $χχ\toγγ$ and $χχ\toγγ'$) for a particle mass near 11 GeV, making this source a promising target for follow-up observations with next-generation gamma-ray telescopes.

</details>


### [181] [Spectral and timing variability of the transient ultraluminous X-ray source NGC 4631 X-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23498)
*Sinan Allak,Wei Yu,Lorenzo Ducci,Andrea Santangelo,Aysun Akyuz,Faruk Soydugan,Valery Suleimanov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对瞬态超亮X射线源NGC 4631 X-4进行了详细的光谱和时变分析，发现其光度变化超过两个数量级，光谱由多色盘黑体和幂律模型描述，不支持标准薄盘的光度-温度关系，未探测到相干脉动或准周期振荡，短时变以非周期涨落和千秒尺度峰状结构为主，支持恒星质量致密天体上的超爱丁顿吸积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超亮X射线源(ULXs)是研究超爱丁顿吸积的天然实验室，但瞬态ULXs的吸积物理和致密天体性质尚不明确，需要对其光谱和时变特性进行深入研究。

Method: 利用存档的Chandra、XMM-Newton和Swift/XRT观测数据，对NGC 4631 X-4进行详细的光谱拟合（多色盘黑体+幂律模型）和时变分析（功率谱、脉冲搜索等）。

Result: 源呈现显著的光度和光谱变化，光度变化超两个数量级；盘内温度0.9-1.4 keV，光子指数2.0-2.4；偏离标准薄盘的L-T关系；未探测到相干脉动或QPO；短时变以非周期涨落和千秒尺度峰状结构为主，与超爱丁顿吸积流中的团块风和外流几何效应一致。

Conclusion: 光谱和时变特性支持恒星质量致密天体上的超爱丁顿吸积，但现有数据无法区分中子星和恒星质量黑洞吸积体。

Abstract: Ultraluminous X-ray sources (ULXs) are among the best laboratories for studying super-Eddington accretion onto compact objects. We present a detailed spectral and timing analysis of the transient ULX NGC 4631 X-4 using archival Chandra, XMM-Newton, and Swift/XRT observations. The source exhibits pronounced spectral and flux variability on both short and long timescales, with luminosity variations exceeding two orders of magnitude. Its X-ray spectra are well described by absorbed multicolor disk blackbody and power-law models, with characteristic inner disk temperatures of 0.9-1.4 keV and photon indices of 2.0-2.4. The source does not follow the standard luminosity-temperature relation expected for a geometrically thin, optically thick accretion disk. No coherent pulsations or quasi-periodic oscillations are detected, while the short-term variability is dominated by aperiodic fluctuations and kilosecond peak-like structures, consistent with clumpy winds and geometric effects in a super-Eddington accretion flow. Overall, the spectral and timing properties support super-Eddington accretion onto a stellar-mass compact object, although the current data do not allow us to distinguish uniquely between a neutron star and a stellar-mass black hole accretor.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [DPLAN: Minimal Connectivity to Floorplan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21159)
*Rohit Lohani,Krishnendra Shekhawat*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出DPLAN，一种基于图的三阶段框架，从用户定义的房门连接和非邻接约束自动生成无重叠、无空白的矩形或正交楼层平面图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决计算建筑设计中自动楼层平面图生成问题，使用户只需指定房间数量、房门连接要求及房间间非邻接约束，无需精确指定房间位置和形状。

Method: 三阶段流程：(1) 检查并连接用户定义图中断开的组件；(2) 构建双连通平面三角剖分以保证无重叠无空白；(3) 将三角化图转化为楼层平面：矩形平面图通过删除分离三角形（不新增顶点）实现，正交平面图通过引入额外顶点允许直线形房间形状。

Result: 实现了满足房门连接和非邻接约束的楼层平面图生成，支持矩形地块边界，提供了交互式约束指定和平面图可视化的Python原型。

Conclusion: DPLAN框架能有效将房门连接和非邻接约束转化为合规的楼层平面图；未来工作包括支持非矩形地块、基于尺寸的缩放和循环建模。

Abstract: Automated floor plan generation is an important problem in computational architectural design. The goal is to construct a floor plan from user-defined room numbers and door requirements. The user specifies which rooms must share a door and which rooms must not be adjacent. However, these requirements do not determine the exact placement or shape of the rooms. The task is therefore to arrange the rooms in a single floor plan so that all required door connections are satisfied and no rooms overlap. To address this problem, we propose DPLAN (Door Connectivity to Floor Plan Generation), a graph-based prototype that generates floor plans from door and non-adjacency constraints.
  The framework operates in three stages. First, the user-defined graph is examined and, if disconnected, additional edges are added to connect its components. Second, a bi-connected plane triangulation is constructed to ensure the existence of a floor plan without overlapping rooms or empty spaces. Third, the triangulated graph is transformed into floor plans. For rectangular floor plans (RFPs), separating triangles are removed by modifying edges without adding new vertices, thereby avoiding the creation of extra rooms. For orthogonal floor plans (OFPs), separating triangles are removed by introducing additional vertices, allowing rectilinear room shapes.
  By enforcing both door and non-adjacency requirements, the framework generates floor plans that satisfy the given constraints. The method is implemented in Python and includes a prototype for interactive constraint specification and floor plan visualization. Currently, the framework supports rectangular plot boundaries. Future work includes support for non-rectangular plots, dimension-based scaling, and circulation modeling.

</details>


### [183] [Arc-Length Parameterized Interpolating Splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21209)
*Dafna K. Matsegora,Stephen M. Watt*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出了一种迭代算法，用于计算插值点集的弧长参数化样条曲线，适用于任意维度D≥2，并通过平面曲线数值结果进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么不插值原始点，要么返回曲线的参数化不是弧长参数化，无法同时满足插值和弧长参数化两个需求。

Method: 提出一种迭代算法，用于计算插值点集的弧长参数化样条曲线，适用于任意维度D≥2。

Result: 通过平面曲线（2D）的数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够同时实现点插值与弧长参数化，且适用于高维空间。

Abstract: We present an iterative algorithm to compute an arc-length parameterized spline interpolating a set of points. This differs from other methods where the computed spline either does not interpolate the original points or the parameterization is not the arc-length of the returned curves. Our method is applicable in any dimension $D \ge 2$, and we illustrate it with numerical results for plane curves.

</details>


### [184] [Exact and Fast Subset Selection Algorithms for the Bi-objective Integral R2 Indicator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23365)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对双目标优化中基于连续积分R2指标的固定基数子集选择问题，推导了精确的邻接邻居分解，并提出了O(kn²)的Bellman动态规划、O(kn log n)的分治算法以及O(kn)的矩阵搜索算法，证明了转移矩阵的Monge性质，实现了比有限权重向量近似更精确的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R2指标子集选择通常基于有限权重向量的样本平均近似，缺乏精确的连续积分形式解。本文旨在填补这一空白，为双目标情形下基于连续积分R2指标的固定基数子集选择提供精确算法。

Method: 首先对排序后的双目标帕累托前沿近似点进行邻接邻居分解，将积分目标分解为边界项、单反对角修正项和选中邻居转移项；进而建立精确的Bellman动态规划；通过证明转移矩阵具有Monge性质，实现分治算法和阶梯矩阵搜索算法，其中矩阵搜索基于下包络扫描和单交叉转移函数。

Result: 提出了三种精确算法：O(kn²)的Bellman动态规划、O(kn log n)的分治实现以及O(kn)的矩阵搜索实现。通过可复现的Python代码与穷举枚举进行一致性验证。

Conclusion: 本文提供了双目标连续积分R2指标子集选择的精确算法，不同于有限权重向量近似方法，与超体积子集选择等二维基于指标的子集选择工作相关但独立。

Abstract: We study fixed-cardinality subset selection for the exact integral bi-objective $R_2$ indicator with a uniform continuum of weighted Tchebycheff scalarizing functions. The indicator measures the area under the lower envelope of scalarizing losses over weight space, rather than a finite sample average over weight vectors. For a sorted bi-objective Pareto-front approximation, represented by points ordered by increasing first objective and decreasing second objective, we derive an exact adjacent-neighbor decomposition of this integral objective into boundary terms, unary diagonal corrections, and selected-neighbor transition terms. This yields an exact Bellman dynamic program with $O(kn^2)$ running time for selecting $k$ of $n$ candidate points. We then prove that the transition matrix is Monge. This gives a divide-and-conquer implementation with $O(kn\log n)$ running time and, more strongly, a staircase matrix-search implementation with $O(kn)$ running time under constant-time arithmetic comparisons. The matrix-search proof is presented through a lower-envelope sweep over single-crossing transition functions and includes the triangular feasibility condition $i<j$. The algorithms are exact for the continuous integral $R_2$ setting and are distinct from finite-weight-vector approximations, although they are related to earlier exact and dynamic-programming work on two-dimensional indicator-based subset selection, including hypervolume subset selection. Reproducible Python code compares exhaustive enumeration, the direct left-to-right dynamic program, the divide-and-conquer dynamic program, and the matrix-search implementation under explicit consistency checks.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [MAGNIFIED: RL Fine-tuning of Multimodal Large Language Models for Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20641)
*Letian Chen,Yiren Lu,Justin Fu,Yichen Xie,Runsheng Xu,Jyh-Jing Hwang,Ben Sapp,Drago Anguelov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAGNIFIED方法，通过强化学习微调（RLFT）结合token级奖励，将多模态大模型（MLLM）与自动驾驶规划目标对齐，相比监督微调基线降低10.5%重叠率和38.9%偏离道路率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MLLM的下一个token文本预测目标无法满足自动驾驶规划需求，因为它仅鼓励逐token模仿，忽视多步后果及让行等关键规划考量。

Method: 提出MAGNIFIED框架：通过强化学习微调，将预测的token序列映射为车辆轨迹，并从规划奖励中学习，优化真实规划目标而非仅追求token预测准确率。采用Waymo Open Motion Dataset，以栅格化鸟瞰图和token化轨迹作为输入，输出规划导向的轨迹坐标序列。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，相比SFT基线，重叠率降低10.5%，偏离道路率降低38.9%，实现更合规、舒适、高效的驾驶规划。

Conclusion: RLFT方法能有效对齐MLLM与自动驾驶规划目标，超越简单的文本模仿学习，提升规划性能。

Abstract: Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in semantic understanding and common sense reasoning, making them promising candidates for solving planning problems in autonomous driving. However, the next-token text prediction objectives traditionally used in pre-training and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of MLLMs may fall short of fulfilling the planning objectives for autonomous vehicles. The next-token prediction objective merely encourages per-token imitation in text, often irrespective of multi-step consequences and the alignment with crucial planning considerations such as giving space to other road actors. To overcome these limitations, we propose a reinforcement learning fine-tuning (RLFT) approach, MAGNIFIED, that aligns the MLLM-based driving agent with planning objectives by learning from token-level rewards. By mapping a sequence of predicted tokens to corresponding vehicle trajectories and learning from planning rewards, MAGNIFIED optimizes for the true planning objectives rather than focusing solely on token prediction accuracy, enabling the model to refine its understanding of the planning task beyond simple imitation. We validate our approach on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset with a novel setup incorporating rasterized birds-eye views and tokenized trajectories as inputs and planning-oriented outputs. An initial SFT phase establishes a strong baseline in outputting plan trajectories as sequences of X-Y coordinates in text, while subsequent RL fine-tuning substantially enhances planning performance relative to the SFT baseline (demonstrating over a 10.5% reduction in overlap rate and a 38.9% reduction in off-road rate), underscoring the potential of RLFT on MLLMs to achieve vehicle planning that is better aligned with compliant, comfortable, and efficient driving.

</details>


### [186] [TACT-ful: Multi-Channel Terrain Affordance and Compliance Training for Payload-Robust Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20645)
*Thanh Ly,Truong-Duy Dang,Chien Le,Tan-Dzung Do,Phuong Tuan Dat,Cuc T. Trinh,Vien Anh Ngo,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多通道地形代价和多接触感知奖励的仿人机器人结构化地形 locomotion 方法，利用 GPU 并行 DCM 规划器与不对称 actor-critic PPO 策略，结合 Bézier 摆线轨迹与自适应足尖偏置实现足部位姿追踪，并引入虚拟 wrench 注入的下肢柔顺训练以增强负载鲁棒性，在仿真中达到 1.0 m/s 的楼梯速度与 15 kg 负载能力，并完成了硬件定性演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化地形上的立足点选择需要对接触面平面度、坡度陡峭度和运动学可达性进行显式推理，而单通道的高度信号无法捕捉这些属性，因此需要更丰富的多通道地形代价表示。

Method: 1) 多通道地形代价：结合平坦度、陡峭度和速度感知的高度可行性通道，加上前向攀爬奖励；2) GPU 并行 DCM 立足点规划器 + 密集每步可及性奖励用于不对称 actor-critic PPO 训练；3) Bézier 摆线轨迹 + 自适应 apex 偏置，利用反正切引导足底方向通过竖板交叉和踏板着陆，实现足部位置与姿态追踪；4) 下肢柔顺训练：在虚拟负载附着点注入虚拟 wrench，生成物理一致的力和力矩，用 wrench 感知的柔顺目标替换刚性位姿惩罚。

Result: 仿真中：在竖板高达 0.20 m 的楼梯上达到 1.0 m/s 的速度；负载鲁棒性提升至约 15 kg 中心负载及力矩主导的手腕负载，无需微调。并提供定性硬件演示。

Conclusion: 系统端到端训练（标准 PPO，无蒸馏，无师生分阶段），直接从仿真部署到仿人机器人仅需配置更改，验证了多通道地形代价与柔顺训练在结构化地形 locomotion 中的有效性。

Abstract: Foothold selection on structured terrain requires explicit reasoning about contact planarity, surface steepness, and kinematic reachability, properties not captured by a single height-based terrain signal. We propose a multi-channel terrain cost combining flatness, steepness, and velocity-aware height feasibility, plus a forward climb reward, that simultaneously drives a GPU-parallel divergent component of motion (DCM) foothold planner and shapes a dense per-step affordance reward for an asymmetric actor-critic policy trained with proximal policy optimization (PPO) from depth images. A Bézier swing trajectory with adaptive apex bias extends foothold tracking to joint position-and-orientation, using the arc tangent to guide sole orientation through riser crossings and tread landings. To support payload tasks, we introduce a lower-body compliance training procedure in which a virtual wrench is injected at a sampled load attachment point, generating physically consistent force and moment; wrench-aware compliance targets replace rigid pose penalties, and the policy learns to yield to load-induced perturbations without force sensing. The full system trains end-to-end with standard PPO, no distillation, and no teacher-student staging, and is deployed on a humanoid directly from simulation with configuration changes only. In simulation, the policy reaches $1.0~\mathrm{m/s}$ on stairs with risers up to $0.20~\mathrm{m}$ and improves payload robustness up to ${\sim}15~\mathrm{kg}$ centered load and for moment-dominated wrist loads without fine-tuning. We also provide a qualitative hardware demonstration on structured terrain. Project website: https://fai-rl-tech.github.io/tact-locomotion.github.io/

</details>


### [187] [Tessellated Biomes: Distributed Robotic Assemblies for Architectural Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20647)
*Sergio Mutis,Eric Hughes,Gert Duvenhage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Tessellated Biomes，一种结合本地微工厂制造、离散多材料优化和分布式机器人装配的网宇物理框架，实现模块化多材料组件的自适应建造与重构，通过自对齐模块化基元（PLA、木材、混凝土）的数字化制造、压缩型离散结构优化以及四足机器人协作装配，验证了可重构韧性建筑模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统建筑线性生命周期，融合微工厂制造、多材料优化与机器人装配，形成空间自适应的循环建造流程。

Method: (1) 本地微工厂中多材料（PLA、木材、混凝土）自对齐模块化基元的数字化制造；(2) 基于压缩的离散结构聚合与优化；(3) 定制四足机器人协作搬运并装配模块。

Result: 通过离散结构的制造、优化与机器人装配验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: Tessellated Biomes可作为韧性、可重构建筑的模型。

Abstract: This paper presents Tessellated Biomes, a cyber-physical framework for the adaptive robotic construction and reconfiguration of modular multi-material assemblies. It challenges the linear lifecycle of standard construction by fusing (1) local microfactory fabrication, (2) discrete multi-material optimization, and (3) distributed robotic assembly into a unified circular process of spatial adaptation. The research details methods for the digital fabrication of self-aligning modular primitives in multiple materials (PLA, timber, and concrete) produced in local microfactories; the aggregation and optimization of these primitives into compression-based discrete structures; and the deployment of custom quadrupedal robots that collaboratively relocate material into realized physical aggregations. The framework is validated through the fabrication, optimization, and robotic assembly of discrete structures. Together, these results position Tessellated Biomes as a model for resilient, reconfigurable architecture.

</details>


### [188] [Learning Control as Enabling Layer for Embodied Intelligence Research explored with Soft Robotic Swimming in diverse Flow Speeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20660)
*Fabian Schwab,Federico Allione,Bingcheng Wang,Mohamed El Arayshi,Claudio Mucignat,Ivan Lunati,Cristiano Verrelli,Ardian Jusufi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PID-LRLES控制方法，通过线性重复学习估计补偿周期性水动力扰动，在动态水流环境下显著提高软体机器人的周期弯曲跟踪精度和可重复性，并验证了嵌入式软体传感与逐周期学习的协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人在变水动力负载下难以精确控制，传统PID控制在非静态水流中会累积与流速相关的跟踪延迟和试验间变异性，亟需一种能适应动态流动环境的鲁棒控制策略。

Method: 将线性重复学习估计方案（LRLES）与PID控制相结合（PID-LRLES），LRLES将经典积分作用从常数推广至周期非恒定参考，采用极点实部为负的稳定传递函数实现以避免经典重复控制的长期不稳定性；在循环水槽中于5个流速（0-32.6 cm/s）下进行闭环实验，使用嵌入式软电容弯曲传感器以1 kHz控制率运行。

Result: PID-LRLES在所有流速下均比PID基线更紧密地跟踪周期弯曲包络参考，并显著降低了逐次试验RMSE的试验间离散度（配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p=1.8×10⁻⁴，n=25）。

Conclusion: 嵌入式软体本体感知与逐周期学习互为补充：传感器揭示体变形中的周期水动力偏差，学习项在最近振荡周期内吸收该偏差。该方法通过减少与流动相关的控制诱发变异性，为未来研究形态、感知与环境流动对水生 locomotion 的影响提供了基础支持。

Abstract: Soft robots are valuable robophysical platforms for studying body-caudal undulatory locomotion, but their compliant bodies are difficult to control precisely under changing hydrodynamic loading. Conventional proportional-integral-derivative (PID) feedback stabilizes periodic undulation in static water, but can accumulate flow-dependent tracking delay and increasing inter-trial variability when environmental flow becomes non-trivial. Here, we evaluate whether augmenting PID control with a Linear Repetitive Learning Estimation Scheme (PID-LRLES) recovers tracking accuracy and repeatability under dynamic flow. The LRLES generalizes classical integral action from constant to periodic, non-constant references, while using a stable transfer-function realization whose poles have negative real parts to avoid the long-term instability issues of classical repetitive control. Closed-loop experiments were carried out in a recirculating flow tank at five bulk flow speeds spanning 0 to 32.6 cm s^-1, using an embedded soft capacitive bending sensor at a 1 kHz control-loop rate. With controller gains tuned once in static water and then held fixed across all conditions, PID-LRLES tracked the periodic bending-envelope reference more closely than the PID baseline and significantly reduced the inter-trial spread of the per-trial RMSE (paired Wilcoxon signed-rank test, p = 1.8 x 10^-4, n = 25). Embedded soft proprioception and cycle-to-cycle learning act as complementary contributors to robustness: the sensor exposes the periodic hydrodynamic bias in body deformation, while the learning term absorbs it over recent oscillation cycles. By reducing flow-dependent control-induced variability, the approach provides an enabling layer for future robophysical studies seeking to isolate the effects of morphology, sensing, and environmental flow on aquatic locomotion.

</details>


### [189] [MemoryVAM: Integrating Memory into Video Action Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20679)
*Yuxin Jiang,Chang Yu,Yunuo Chen,Xiang Feng,Yin Yang,Nishank Gite,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoryVAM 是一种为视频世界模型策略设计的 episodic memory 机制，通过 Recap-Cue 模块将 CLIP 嵌入压缩为记忆 token，并利用 Cue Gate 估计任务进度，从而解决长序列操作中的非马尔可夫性问题。在 LIBERO-Mem 上将成功率从 5% 提升至 42.5%，在真实机器人上达到 78.3%（计数）、80.0%（空间回忆）和 75.0%（顺序跟踪）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型策略仅依赖短时间观察窗口，当正确动作取决于早期已不可见的事件时，长时域操作变得非马尔可夫，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出 MemoryVAM，包含 Recap-Cue (RC) 模块：Perceiver-based Recap Compressor 将逐帧 CLIP 嵌入压缩为紧凑记忆 token；轻量级 Cue Gate 从记忆和语言指令估计任务完成度。记忆 token 注入视频骨干网络和动作解码器，使策略想象与任务进度对齐。训练使用视频预测、delta 重建辅助损失和 episode 边界监督，无需逐帧进度标签。该机制通过修改交叉注意力注入接口适配 UNet 和 DiT 骨干。

Result: LIBERO-Mem 上平均成功率从 5% 提升至 42.5%；真实机器人：计数任务 78.3%、空间回忆 80.0%、顺序跟踪 75.0%。

Conclusion: 引入 episodic memory 能有效解决视频世界模型策略在长时域操作中的非马尔可夫性问题，显著提升长序列任务的成功率，且该机制具有良好的骨干网络兼容性。

Abstract: Video-world-model policies learn action-relevant representations by predicting future observations. However, they condition on only a short observation window, which renders long-horizon manipulation non-Markovian when the correct action depends on earlier events that are no longer visible. We present MemoryVAM, an episodic memory mechanism for video-world-model policies. We employ a Recap-Cue (RC) module, in which a Perceiver-based Recap Compressor maps per-frame CLIP embeddings into compact memory tokens, and a lightweight Cue Gate estimates task completion from memory and language. These tokens are injected into both the video backbone and the action decoder, aligning policy imagination with episode progress and conditioning actions on history. Our model trains the memory module with video prediction, a delta-reconstruction auxiliary loss, and episode-boundary supervision, requiring no per-frame progress labels. The same mechanism applies to UNet and Diffusion Transformer (DiT) backbones by changing only the cross-attention injection interface. On LIBERO-Mem, our model improves average success from 5% to 42.5%. On real robots, it achieves 78.3% success on counting tasks, 80.0% on spatial recall, and 75.0% on sequential tracking. Project page: https://MemoryVAM.github.io/

</details>


### [190] [JPPD: Joint Prediction_Planning Diffusion with Differentiable Safety Guidance for Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance in Intelligent Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20686)
*Jiahao Wu,Shengwen Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种联合预测-规划扩散框架，将参与者轨迹预测与机器人运动规划统一为条件轨迹生成问题，通过因果Transformer、可微安全势引导和条件流匹配实现共享空间中的安全高效自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享空间自动驾驶系统将问题分解为轨迹预测后接运动规划，形成单向信息流：预测影响规划，但规划结果无法反作用于多智能体演化，导致保守或次优行为。

Method: 1) 将预测与规划联合建模为条件轨迹生成问题，从耦合分布中联合采样所有智能体轨迹；2) 采用因果Transformer与跨轨迹注意力机制；3) 提出可微安全势引导，计算时变占用概率势的梯度直接引导联合采样；4) 使用条件流匹配减少推理步数并保留多模态多样性。

Result: 在场景接地仿真、自然行人回放、Isaac Sim验证及ROS/Orin部署中，联合采样方法在尾部安全性和运行时效率上均优于分离的预测-规划基线。

Conclusion: 联合预测-规划扩散框架通过双向信息交互和可微安全引导，有效提升了共享空间低速自主平台在近碰撞、阻塞时间、行人偏离、急刹车等运营指标上的表现。

Abstract: Shared-space transportation operation requires low-speed autonomous platforms to navigate safely and efficiently among pedestrians, service robots, micromobility users, carts, and other road users. Most existing systems decompose this problem into trajectory prediction followed by motion planning, which creates one-way information flow: predicted participant futures influence the robot plan, but the selected robot plan cannot influence the predicted multi-agent evolution. This paper presents a joint prediction-planning diffusion framework that treats participant prediction and robot planning as a single conditional trajectory generation problem, where the model samples the future robot trajectory and all participant trajectories from one coupled distribution using a causal Transformer with cross-trajectory attention. To replace heuristic repulsive post-processing, the framework introduces differentiable safety potential guidance, a time-varying occupancy-probability potential whose gradient directly guides the joint sampler, and conditional flow matching is used to reduce inference steps while preserving multimodal trajectory diversity. The evaluation emphasizes shared-space operational effects, including near misses, blockage time, induced participant deviation, hard-braking events, and embedded latency, rather than treating average displacement error and final displacement error as the main result. Experiments in scenario-grounded simulation, naturalistic pedestrian replay, Isaac Sim validation, and ROS/Orin deployment show that joint sampling improves tail safety and runtime efficiency over a separated prediction-then-planning baseline.

</details>


### [191] [SafeDojo: Safe Reinforcement Learning for VLA via Interactive World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20698)
*Kai Tang,Peidong Jia,Zhong Chu,Jixian Wu,Rui Ma,Jiajun Cao,Fangyuan Zhao,Sixiang Chen,Yichen Guo,Xiaowei Chi,Chun-Kai Fan,Kevin Zhang,Jinchang Xu,Fubing Yang,Weishi Mi,Xiaozhu Ju,Jian Tang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeDojo是首个基于模型的安全强化学习框架，用于视觉-语言-动作策略，通过世界模型想象学习安全动作，在SafeLIBERO和真实Franka机器人上实现了最优的安全成功率和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法要么需要昂贵的真实世界探索，要么依赖手工设计的安全函数，无法扩展到部署于开放世界物理环境的视觉-语言-动作模型。

Method: SafeDojo在交互式视频世界模型上执行在线强化学习。世界模型生成动作条件化的未来预测，ResNet成功分类器从想象帧估计每步任务进度，轻量级安全头从潜在上下文和动作块预测每步安全成本。通过基于拉格朗日的约束GRPO目标平衡解耦的任务奖励和安全成本信号。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO上，SafeDojo在任务成功、安全成功和执行效率方面均优于推理时间安全、无模型RL和基于模型RL基线，Level I上比最强基线提升8.25个百分点。真实Franka部署中在五个任务上取得了最佳平均任务成功率和安全成功率。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型的安全强化学习是可扩展且可泛化的通向安全具身智能的路径。

Abstract: Safe control is a prerequisite for real-world embodied intelligence, for which safe reinforcement learning has emerged as a promising paradigm. However, existing safe reinforcement learning methods either require costly real-world exploration or depend on hand-crafted safety functions. Neither scales to vision-language-action models deployed in open-world physical environments. We propose SafeDojo, the first model-based safe reinforcement learning framework for vision-language-action policies designed to learn safe actions through world model-based imagination. Specifically, SafeDojo performs online reinforcement learning on top of an interactive video world model. The world model generates action-conditioned future predictions, from which a tailored ResNet success classifier estimates per-step task progress from imagined frames and a lightweight safety head predicts per-step safety costs from latent context together with the proposed action chunk, enabling simultaneous assessment of task execution and trajectory safety. The decoupled task-reward and safety-cost signals are balanced through a Lagrangian-based constrained GRPO objective, enabling coordinated improvement of task success and safety under explicit constraints. On SafeLIBERO, SafeDojo achieves the best aggregate task success, safe success, and execution efficiency among inference-time safety, model-free RL, and model-based RL baselines, with the best average safe-success rate on both levels and an 8.25 percentage-point improvement over the strongest baseline on Level I. Real-world Franka deployment further shows the best average task and safe-success rates across five tasks. Our results position world model-based safe reinforcement learning as a scalable and generalizable path toward safe embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [192] [Real-World Deployment of Massively Parallel Sampling-Based MPC for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20712)
*Magnus Dierking,Joao Carvalho,An Thai Le,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于JAX和MuJoCo MJX的大规模并行采样MPC框架，并在Franka机器人上实现了Push-T操作任务。MTP变体在多模态任务中优于CEM、MPPI等基线方法。在线域随机化分析表明，接触参数可提供可解释的适应信号，而全局物理参数反馈过弱难以实时利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样型MPC多依赖简化动力学或局限于仿真，缺乏从仿真到真实机器人的完整部署研究，且对实时域随机化在接触丰富的操作任务中的有效性缺乏深入分析。

Method: 利用JAX实现大规模并行化计算，结合MuJoCo MJX高保真仿真器，构建采样型MPC框架。提出MTP（多模态轨迹先验）变体进行结构化全局采样，并在Franka Research 3机器人上通过完整的实到仿到实（real-to-sim-to-real）流程执行Push-T操作任务。同时在线评估域随机化在MPC样本预算内的影响。

Result: MTP变体在需要模态切换的任务中，在仿真和硬件上均优于CEM、MPPI和PS等单模态基线方法。接触初始化参数可提供可解释的适应信号，而全局物理参数产生的反馈过弱，无法在典型重规划频率下被可靠利用。

Conclusion: 采样型MPC在接触丰富的操作中面临三个关键挑战：接触敏感性、紧凑的计算预算限制，以及难以实时获取信息丰富的域随机化信号。

Abstract: Sampling-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) is a promising strategy for contact-rich robotic manipulation, combining gradient-free optimization with massively parallel GPU simulation. Yet, most prior work relies on simplified dynamics or remains confined to simulation. We present an MPC framework that leverages JAX for large-scale parallelization and efficient computation, coupled with the high-fidelity MuJoCo MJX simulator, and deploy it on a Franka Research 3 executing the Push-T manipulation task through a complete real-to-sim-to-real pipeline. The MTP variant with structured global sampling outperforms unimodal baselines such as CEM, MPPI, and PS across tasks that require mode switching, both in simulation and on hardware. Furthermore, we evaluate online domain randomization within the MPC sample budget, showing that contact-initiation parameters yield interpretable adaptation signals, whereas global physics parameters provide feedback that is too weak for reliable exploitation at typical replanning frequencies. These findings highlight key challenges for sampling-based MPC in contact-rich manipulation-contact sensitivity, tight compute budgets, and the difficulty of obtaining informative domain-randomization signals in real time.

</details>


### [193] [Coupled Routing and Configuration Optimization for Multi-Viewpoint Robotic Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20739)
*Minh Nhat Vu,Khang Nguyen,Vu Trung Tran,Vien Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，将一组6自由度检测视点转化为9自由度机器人系统的时间最优无碰撞路径。该方法联合优化访问顺序和每个视点的逆运动学配置，使用闭式参数化、双积分器代理和CMA-ES进行全局搜索，再对选定路径进行直接配准轨迹优化，实验验证了可行性和时间优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模块化流水线先固定每个视点的IK配置再构建旅行时间图进行路径规划，这种解耦方式无法实现全局时间最优，且轨迹求解复杂度随视点数量呈二次增长。

Method: 1) 闭式参数化每个视点的自运动流形以保证位姿约束；2) 用闭式可容许双积分器代理近似点对点旅行时间；3) 使用随机密钥编码路径顺序和配置，CMA-ES优化惩罚目标函数；4) 仅对选定路径边进行直接配准轨迹优化以验证动力学可行性和力矩约束。

Result: 在KUKA LBR iiwa配合2自由度线性平台的仿真和实物实验中，验证了可行性、平滑执行，相比模块化和基于距离的基线方法，端到端检测时间显著减少。

Conclusion: 该方法将轨迹求解从二次复杂度降至线性复杂度，消除了模块化流水线的解耦问题，实现了全局时间最优的检测路径规划。

Abstract: We present a unified framework that turns a set of 6-DoF inspection viewpoints into a time-optimal, collision-free route for a 9-DoF robotic system. Unlike modular pipelines that fix a single inverse-kinematics (IK) configuration per viewpoint, build an all-pairs travel-time map, and then route, our method jointly optimizes the visiting order and the per-viewpoint configuration in a single global search. The three-dimensional self-motion manifold of each viewpoint is parameterized in closed form so that the pose constraint holds by construction, the rest-to-rest travel time is approximated by a closed-form admissible double-integrator surrogate, and the tour is encoded by random keys. A derivative-free optimizer (CMA-ES) minimizes a cheap penalized objective over order and configuration, after which direct-collocation trajectory optimization is applied only to the edges of the selected route to certify dynamic feasibility and torque limits, and to return exact timings. This reduces the trajectory solves from quadratic to linear in the number of viewpoints and removes the decoupling that prevents modular pipelines from being globally time-optimal. Simulations and real-robot experiments on a KUKA LBR iiwa with a 2-DoF linear stage validate feasibility, smooth execution, and reduced end-to-end inspection time relative to modular and naive distance-based baselines.

</details>


### [194] [A Digital Twin Framework for Traffic-Aware UAV Pavement Monitoring without Lane Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20742)
*Yamil Uchani,Grace Abigail Luna Verdueta,Mauricio Figueroa,Edwin Salcedo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Unity的无人机路面检测数字孪生框架，集成程序化路面缺陷生成、动态交通与行人、自主无人机导航及路面损伤感知管线。采用YOLOv8n检测器+二级分类器实现五类目标99.26%准确率，并评估了三种遮挡恢复策略在不同交通密度和飞行高度下的表现，其中hover-and-recheck策略在中等/高交通条件下达到97.03%覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低无人机路面检测在实际部署中面临的交通、行人和临时遮挡对缺陷可见性的影响，实现无需封闭车道的交通感知无人机路面巡检。

Method: 构建Unity数字孪生环境，包含程序化路面缺陷生成、动态交通和行人模拟、自主无人机导航；感知模块采用两阶段方法：轻量级YOLOv8n检测器定位缺陷、行人和车辆，二级分类器区分坑洞、单裂缝和鳄鱼裂缝；评估三种遮挡恢复策略（悬停重检、微调位置、跳过再访）。

Result: 感知管线在五类目标上达99.26%准确率；飞行高度对检测覆盖率有显著影响；hover-and-recheck在中高交通条件下达97.03%覆盖率；skip-and-revisit在低交通场景中最优，中空达97.95%覆盖率。

Conclusion: 数字孪生能够有效支持交通感知无人机检测策略的开发和评估，为实际部署前的验证提供可行平台。

Abstract: UAV-based pavement inspection can reduce the cost and risk of road-surface monitoring, but real-world deployment remains difficult when traffic, pedestrians, and temporary occlusions affect the visibility of defects. This paper presents a Unity-based digital twin framework for traffic-aware UAV pavement monitoring without lane closure. The proposed environment integrates procedurally generated road defects, dynamic vehicles and pedestrians, autonomous UAV navigation, and an embedded road-damage perception pipeline. The perception module uses a two-stage approach: a lightweight YOLOv8n detector first localises road defects, pedestrians, and vehicles, while a second classifier distinguishes among potholes, single cracks, and crocodile cracks. On the simulator test set, the full pipeline achieved 99.26% overall accuracy across five classes. The digital twin was then used to evaluate three recovery strategies for occluded road segments: hover-and-recheck, micro-repositioning, and skip-and-revisit. Experiments were conducted across different traffic densities and flight altitudes using coverage, mission time, energy consumption, and revisit ratio as operational metrics. Results show that flight altitude has a strong influence on inspection coverage and that adaptive recovery improves performance under occlusion. In particular, hover-and-recheck achieved the most consistent coverage under medium and high traffic conditions, reaching up to 97.03% coverage, while skip-and-revisit was most effective in low-traffic scenarios, reaching 97.95\% coverage at medium altitude. These results demonstrate that digital twins can support the development and evaluation of traffic-aware UAV inspection strategies before real-world deployment.

</details>


### [195] [Toward Machine Risk Perception: Integrating Trust Calibration and Precursor-Based Risk Estimation for Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20748)
*He Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前兆信号与信任校准的人形机器人主动风险感知框架，通过逻辑-指数（LE）模型刻画事故演化过程，将信任定义为事故概率的倒数，使人形机器人能够实时调整行为，实现从静态阈值安全向动态证据推理的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全范式（基于反应性力或距离限制）无法捕捉人形机器人故障的序列性、不确定性特征，亟需一种能够感知动态风险的前兆驱动框架。

Method: 提出前兆驱动的信任校准框架：采用逻辑-指数（LE）模型耦合多种前兆的逻辑斯蒂增长与时间指数衰减；将信任定义为事故概率的倒数；基于多源数据集（126起事件、241个前兆）识别12种主导事故模式。

Result: 多数事故模式在1秒内通过重叠前兆信号演化；仿真案例（"倒向人类"）验证了LE-信任耦合能够触发早期干预并防止碰撞。

Conclusion: 该方法将人形机器人安全从静态阈值推进到动态证据推理，为工业5.0环境下风险感知与可信人机协作奠定基础。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are emerging as co-workers in smart manufacturing, yet their dynamic, human-like movements introduce safety risks that differ fundamentally from those of fixed or wheeled robots. Conventional safety paradigms based on reactive force or distance limits fail to capture the sequential, uncertain nature of humanoid failures. This study proposes a precursor-driven, trust-calibrated framework to enable proactive humanoid risk perception. Accident evolution is modeled through sequential precursor cues using a Logistic-Exponential (LE) formulation that couples logistic escalation from diverse precursors with exponential decay for temporal dissipation. Trust is defined as the inverse of the estimated accident probability, allowing humanoids to adapt behavior in real time, reducing aggressiveness when risk intensifies, and restoring confidence as stability returns. A multi-source dataset of 126 documented events and 241 precursors revealed twelve dominant accident modes, most evolving through overlapping cues within one second. A simulated case study ("fall-onto-human") demonstrated how the LE-Trust coupling can trigger early intervention and prevent collapse. The results advance humanoid safety from static thresholds toward dynamic, evidence-based inference, establishing a foundation for risk-aware and trustworthy human-robot collaboration in Industry 5.0 environments.

</details>


### [196] [Perturbation-Based Uncertainty for Failure Detection in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20754)
*Yousung Lee,Dongsoo Har*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需标签、模型无关的推理时不确定性估计框架，通过在Transformer隐藏激活中注入高斯扰动来估计认知不确定性，在LIBERO和LIBERO-PRO数据集上优于基于采样的不确定性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型缺乏可靠的不确定性量化方法，尤其在分布偏移场景下。许多现代VLA模型通过回归或流式生成连续动作，无法获得显式预测概率；而现有方法依赖随机动作采样或监督式失败标签，适用范围有限。

Method: 提出标签无关、模型无关的推理时不确定性估计框架。在Transformer隐藏激活中注入高斯扰动，通过扰动前后动作预测的不一致性来估计认知不确定性信号。该方法从贝叶斯角度出发，基于局部模型变体来近似不确定性。

Result: 在LIBERO和LIBERO-PRO数据集上，基于扰动的不确定性在分布偏移下的失败检测任务中一致优于基于采样的不确定性方法。

Conclusion: 提出的扰动式不确定性估计为VLA模型提供了一种实用的不确定性信号，无需标签或特定模型结构即可有效检测分布偏移下的失败。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong performance in robotic manipulation, but reliable uncertainty quantification remains challenging, particularly under distribution shift. Unlike autoregressive policies, many modern VLA models generate continuous actions through regression or flow-based generation, where explicit predictive probabilities are unavailable. Moreover, existing approaches often rely on stochastic action sampling or supervised failure labels, limiting their applicability across diverse pretrained VLA models. In this work, we propose a label-free and model-agnostic framework for inference-time uncertainty estimation through hidden activation perturbations, motivated by Bayesian perspectives on local model variations. Specifically, we inject Gaussian perturbations into transformer hidden activations and estimate epistemic signals from disagreement across perturbed action predictions. Experiments on LIBERO and LIBERO-PRO show that perturbation-based uncertainty consistently improves failure detection under distribution shift compared to sampling-based uncertainty, providing a practical uncertainty signal for VLA models.

</details>


### [197] [UNSEEN: Uncertainty-aware Navigation via Sparse Estimation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20755)
*Tommaso Faraci,Marco Camurri,Daniele Fontanelli,Luigi Palopoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UNSEEN，一个统一的、感知不确定性的视觉导航框架，仅使用前视相机，通过紧耦合定位、建图与规划，在资源受限平台上实现了鲁棒的未知环境导航，相比SOTA方法定位精度提升最高45%，任务成功率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航方法多采用松耦合模块化流程，依赖多模态传感器或对感知质量的强假设，在运动模糊、低纹理、光照变化等条件下性能严重退化，且未能一致地在导航栈中传播不确定性。

Method: 提出UNSEEN框架，包含：(1) 以6Hz频率估计稀疏地图和机器人位姿及其不确定性；(2) 在滚动时域中联合优化任务进度和估计精度的轨迹规划。仅使用前视单目相机，实现定位、建图与规划的紧耦合。

Result: UNSEEN-SLAM减少绝对平移误差9.8%；UNSEEN-Plan相比SOTA提升估计精度最高45%；任务成功率达到100%。仿真与真实世界实验均验证了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式传播不确定性并联合优化感知与规划，视觉导航可以仅凭单目前视相机在未知环境中实现高鲁棒性和高成功率，降低系统复杂度与部署成本。

Abstract: Visual navigation in unknown environments remains a core challenge in mobile robotics, especially for resource-constrained platforms. Most existing approaches rely on loosely coupled modular pipelines and strong assumptions on perception quality or environmental structure, often resorting to multi-modal sensor suites that increase system complexity and deployment cost. Vision-only navigation offers a lightweight alternative, but its performance degrades severely under motion blur, low texture, and illumination changes, largely because they neglect the tight coupling between commanded motion and perception. While perception-aware methods partially address this issue, they typically optimize individual modules and fail to propagate uncertainty consistently across the navigation stack. In this paper, we present UNSEEN, a unified uncertainty- and perception-aware navigation framework that explicitly couples localization, mapping, and planning using only a front-mounted camera. UNSEEN estimates sparse maps and robot poses with associated uncertainties at 6Hz, and leverages them to plan trajectories that jointly optimize task progress and estimation accuracy in receding-horizon. Simulations and extensive real-world experiments in unknown environments demonstrate the robustness of the proposed approach, with UNSEEN-SLAM reducing absolute translational error by 9.8% and UNSEEN-Plan improving estimation accuracy by up to 45% compared to state-of-the-art methods, while achieving a 100% task success rate.

</details>


### [198] [Mind the Privileged-to-Camera Gap: Actor-Centric Sidecar Supervision for Camera-First Open-Loop Waypoint Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20772)
*Feeza Khan Khanzada,Jaerock Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RU-sidecar方法，利用模拟器中的道路使用者侧边标签（actor grounding、hindsight relevance、short-horizon motion）作为辅助监督信号，在开环路点预测任务中将FDE从1.815m降至1.223m（降幅32.6%），且不增加推理输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯路点监督无法显式监督邻近道路使用者的actor级表征，导致模型对周边动态交通参与者的感知不足，限制了路点预测精度。

Method: 在训练阶段引入模拟器导出的侧边标签（actor grounding、privileged hindsight actor relevance、selected-actor short-horizon motion）作为辅助监督，推理时仅使用多视角RGB、自车状态和路径命令。

Result: RU-sidecar将FDE从基线1.815m降至1.223m（降幅32.6%），在1445/1494条路线上优于基线；含至少4个有效侧边actor的样本上降幅29.1%，含弱势道路使用者的样本上降幅30.0%。

Conclusion: 利用模拟器侧边标签对actor级表征进行监督学习可显著提升开环路点预测精度，但特权模拟器状态与纯相机输入之间仍存在性能差距，且结论仅限于开环仿真诊断。

Abstract: Camera-first autonomous-driving models predict future ego waypoints from images, ego-state features, and route commands, but waypoint supervision alone does not explicitly supervise actor-level representations of nearby road users. We study this as supervised representation learning for open-loop waypoint prediction. The deployable model uses multi-view RGB, ego state, and route command at inference. During training, simulator-derived sidecar labels supervise actor grounding, privileged hindsight actor relevance relative to the logged ego trajectory, and selected-actor short-horizon motion; these labels are never inference inputs. We evaluate route-disjoint splits with matched architecture, optimizer, validation criterion, checkpoint selection, and three seeds. A plain waypoint-only RGB baseline obtains 1.815$\pm$0.02 m final displacement error (FDE), and the matched no-teacher non-sidecar RGB control obtains 1.716$\pm$0.02 m. Road-user sidecar supervision (RU-sidecar) reduces FDE to 1.223$\pm$0.01 m, a 32.6% reduction over the plain baseline and 28.7% over the matched no-teacher non-sidecar RGB control. It improves over the plain baseline on 1445/1494 routes and over the matched no-teacher non-sidecar RGB control on 1417/1494 routes. Actor-conditioned slices show gains in all nonempty subsets, including 29.1% reduction for samples with at least four valid sidecar actors and 30.0% when a vulnerable road user is present. Optional simulator-state teacher alignment reaches 1.186$\pm$0.15 m FDE, but higher seed variability makes it secondary. Non-deployable simulator-state diagnostics remain stronger, indicating a privileged-to-camera gap. The evidence is limited to open-loop simulation diagnostics.

</details>


### [199] [World Action Models: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20781)
*Qiuhong Shen,Shihua Zhang,Yue Liao,Qi Li,Zhenxiong Tan,Shizun Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了世界动作模型（WAMs）的边界与分类，提出了两种互补的组织视角（生成内容视角和架构分解视角），揭示了WAMs并非简单的视频生成加动作头，而是在表征丰富度与计算/内存/延迟/动作标签成本之间权衡的设计范式，并指出领域正朝着"生成更少的未来但保留控制所需"的方向发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）领域发展迅速，涌现出基于视频生成模型和基于语言/视觉语言骨干的不同路线，导致世界模型、视频生成模型、动作接地视频世界模型、视觉-语言-动作策略和WAMs之间的边界模糊不清，亟需系统性的梳理与统一框架。

Method: 采用两种互补的视角组织现有工作：(1) 生成内容视角——渲染未来、潜在未来、无视频生成的动作推理；(2) 架构分解视角——预测基质、骨干网络、动作耦合方式、部署模式。以此为基础统一讨论可交互性、因果性、持久性、物理合理性和泛化性。

Result: 发现一致的设计模式：WAMs不是简单的视频生成器加动作头，而是预测-动作方法，其设计选择在表征丰富度与计算、内存、延迟和动作标签成本之间进行权衡。领域趋势是生成更少的未来内容，同时保留控制所需的信息。

Conclusion: 该综述为WAMs领域提供了统一的概念框架和分类体系，揭示了核心设计权衡，指出了数据、评估和开放性挑战，并预测了领域发展方向。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) are embodied predictive-action models that make a forecast of the future available to action. Recent WAMs repurpose large video generation models, and a parallel line relies on language or vision-language backbones without a video-generation core. This rapid expansion has blurred the boundary among broad world models, video generation models, action-grounded video world models, Vision-Language-Action policies, and WAMs. This survey gives the field a common account. It first clarifies these boundaries, then organizes existing works through two complementary views. The first view asks what each method is required to generate, spanning rendered futures, latent futures, and video-generation-free action reasoning. The second view decomposes each method by predictive substrate, backbone, action coupling, and deployment regime. This anatomy supports a unified discussion of interactability, causality, persistence, physical plausibility, and generalization, followed by data, evaluation, and open challenges. Across these axes, a consistent design pattern emerges: WAMs are not simply video generators with action heads, but predictive-action methods whose design choices trade representational richness against compute, memory, latency, and action-label cost. The field is moving toward methods that generate less of the future while preserving what control requires. The survey homepage is available at https://world-action-models.github.io/.

</details>


### [200] [Geometric Entropy: When Trajectory Diversity Helps and Hurts in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20871)
*Qian Luo,Ruizhe Liu,Pei Zhou,Xunzhe Zhou,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出几何熵（Geometric Entropy, H_G）量化演示轨迹的形状多样性，发现模仿学习性能与H_G呈倒U型关系：适度多样性提升鲁棒性，过度多样性导致策略歧义。最优熵随任务难度、数据量和先验增强而降低，预训练VLA模型呈单调递减趋势。H_G可用于演示数据集的快速预训练审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏系统衡量演示轨迹中形状多样性如何影响模仿学习性能的量化指标，需要一种任务无关的度量来评估轨迹多样性，并揭示其与学习性能之间的关系。

Method: 提出几何熵（H_G），通过对齐目标帧归一化外在变化（如目标姿态、工作空间尺度），量化轨迹的内在形状多样性。在多种IL架构、模拟与真实机器人接触式操作任务上评估H_G与成功率的关系。

Result: 发现成功率与H_G呈一致的倒U型关系；最佳熵值随数据量增加、任务变简单或先验增强而降低；预训练VLA模型呈单调递减趋势。

Conclusion: H_G可作为演示数据集质量的快速审计工具，为校准演示数据提供简单指导，使其处于可学习区间。

Abstract: We study how trajectory-shape diversity in demonstrations affects imitation learning (IL) performance across models, tasks, and data scales. We introduce Geometric Entropy (H_G), a task-agnostic metric that quantifies the intrinsic diversity of transit trajectories after normalizing away extrinsic variation, such as goal pose and workspace scale, via target-frame alignment. Across multiple IL architectures and both simulated and real-robot contact-rich manipulation tasks, we observe a consistent inverted-U relationship between success and H_G: increasing geometric diversity improves robustness in low-diversity regimes but degrades performance once diversity induces strategy ambiguity. Moreover, the optimal entropy shifts toward lower values as task mastery increases through more data, easier tasks, or stronger priors, and for a pretrained vision-language-action model the trend becomes effectively monotonic decreasing. Practically, H_G enables fast pre-training auditing of demonstration datasets and offers a simple guideline for calibrating demonstrations toward the learnable regime.

</details>


### [201] [Vesta: A Generalist Embodied Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20905)
*Johan Bjorck,Zhiqi Li,Yunze Man,Jing Wang,An-Chieh Cheng,Sifei Liu,Shihao Wang,Zhiding Yu,Abhishek Badki,Stan Birchfield,Valts Blukis,Yevgen Chebotar,Siyi Chen,Sicong Leng,Yu-Cheng Chou,Tianli Ding,Boyi Li,Zhengyi Luo,Hang Su,Jonathan Tremblay,Tingwu Wang,Bowen Wen,Jimmy Wu,Xianghui Xie,Hanrong Ye,Hongxu Yin,K. R. Zentner,Liangyan Gui,Yu-Xiong Wang,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Vesta是一个统一的具身通才模型，通过单一基础模型整合了定位、空间推理、导航和长周期规划，在多个基准上平均超越专业SOTA模型20%以上，超越最佳专家集成10%以上，并在真实世界机器人任务中提升成功率35%以上，证明通才模型是比组合专家更可行、可扩展且更优的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界机器人需要整合定位、空间推理、导航和长周期规划等多个能力，但部署多模型栈计算成本高昂且容易产生级联错误，因此需要统一的单模型解决方案。

Method: 提出了Vesta模型，包含两个关键组件：(1) 大规模多样化精选语料库，用于诱导空间基础能力；(2) 简单的多模态记忆机制，使模型能够在扩展的时间跨度上进行推理。

Result: 在多个基准上，Vesta平均超越单个SOTA基线20%以上，超越每类别最佳基线的集成10%以上；在需要记忆和推理的真实世界机器人任务中，任务成功率提升35%以上。

Conclusion: 单一通才模型是可行的、可扩展的，并且是比组合多个专家模型更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in open-world environments must seamlessly integrate localization, spatial reasoning, navigation, and long-horizon planning. While specialist models excel at individual tasks, deploying a multi-model stack is computationally expensive and prone to cascading errors. We present Vesta, a unified embodied generalist that consolidates these capabilities into a single foundation model. Our approach combines a diverse and massive curated corpus designed to induce spatial grounding and a simple multimodal memory harness that enables reasoning over extended time horizons. Across diverse benchmarks, Vesta on average beats individual SOTA baselines by >$20\%$ and beats an ensemble of per-category-best baselines by $>10\%$ -- thus demonstrating that a generalist model can match or exceed specialists. On real-world robotic tasks requiring memory and reasoning, Vesta improves task success by >35\%. Our work thus demonstrates that a single generalist is a feasible, scalable, and arguably preferable alternative to combining specialists.

</details>


### [202] [Learning-Based Modeling of Soft Robots via Cosserat Rod Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20958)
*Mohammad Ali,Nithin Senthur Kumar,Eric J. Barth,Thomas Beckers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端口-汉密尔顿高斯过程回归框架，结合Cosserat杆理论与哈密顿物理，实现了对平面杆状软体机器人的精确、能量一致的动力学建模与仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人动力学建模面临挑战：基于第一性原理的模型耗时且表达能力有限，而数据驱动模型缺乏可解释性和物理一致性。现有方法难以兼顾精度与物理一致性。

Method: 将Cosserat杆理论与哈密顿力学结合高斯过程回归，构建端口-汉密尔顿框架。该模型通过数据驱动推断学习杆动力学，同时保持系统的能量结构（如能量守恒、无源特性）。

Result: 数值仿真表明，该方法能够实现平面杆状软体机器人的精确且能量一致的动力学表征。

Conclusion: 该框架为复杂连续介质力学建模提供了一条鲁棒且可解释的途径，具有良好潜力。

Abstract: Modeling soft robot dynamics is challenging due to their continuum structure and typically nonlinear dynamics. Creating models based on first-order principles is typically time-demanding, and their expressiveness is limited, whereas data-driven models lack interpretability and physical consistency. This work aims to overcome these challenges by introducing a port-Hamiltonian Gaussian Process Regression framework for learning and simulating the dynamics of planar, rod-like soft robots. In detail, the proposed model integrates Cosserat rod theory and Hamiltonian physics with data-driven inference to preserve the system's energy structure while accurately learning the rod dynamics. Numerical simulations show that we can achieve accurate and energy-consistent representations of a rod-like soft robot, showing the potential for a robust and interpretable pathway for modeling complex continuum mechanics.

</details>


### [203] [Heterogeneous Policy Networks for Composite Robot Team Communication and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20962)
*Esmaeil Seraj,Rohan Paleja,Luis Pimentel,Kin Man Lee,Zheyuan Wang,Daniel Martin,Matthew Sklar,John Zhang,Zahi Kakish,Matthew Gombolay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HetNet（异质策略网络），利用异质图注意力网络实现异构机器人团队的高效通信与协调，在多个领域实现5.84%-707.65%的性能提升，同时将通信带宽降低200倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL方法多采用同质图网络，无法有效建模异构智能体（不同状态、动作、观测空间）间的异质通信模式，导致通信效率低下甚至损害团队性能。

Method: 基于异质图注意力网络构建HetNet，支持异构机器人团队扩展；通过端到端训练学习高效的二值化消息传递机制，实现异构协作策略的学习。

Result: 相比最优基线方法，HetNet在多个领域实现5.84%至707.65%的性能提升，同时通信带宽需求降低200倍。

Conclusion: HetNet在异构多智能体团队的协调与通信策略学习上达到新SOTA，验证了异质性建模和高效二值化通信的有效性。

Abstract: High-performing human-human teams learn intelligent and efficient communication and coordination strategies to maximize their joint utility. These teams implicitly understand the different roles of heterogeneous team members and adapt their communication protocols accordingly. Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) has attempted to develop computational methods for synthesizing such joint coordination-communication strategies, but emulating heterogeneous communication patterns across agents with different state, action, and observation spaces has remained a challenge. Without properly modeling agent heterogeneity, as in prior MARL work that leverages homogeneous graph networks, communication becomes less helpful and can even deteriorate the team's performance. In the past, we proposed Heterogeneous Policy Networks (HetNet) to learn efficient and diverse communication models for coordinating cooperative heterogeneous teams. In this extended work, we extend Heterogeneous Policy Networks (HetNet) to support scaling heterogeneous robot teams. Building on heterogeneous graph-attention networks, we show that HetNet not only facilitates learning heterogeneous collaborative policies but also enables end-to-end training for learning highly efficient binarized messaging. Our empirical evaluation shows that HetNet sets a new state of the art in learning coordination and communication strategies for heterogeneous multi-agent teams by achieving an 5.84% to 707.65% performance improvement over the next-best baseline across multiple domains while simultaneously achieving a 200x reduction in the required communication bandwidth.

</details>


### [204] [Duet: Dual-Robot Understanding via Efficient Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20990)
*Yiqi Zhao,Ruohai Ge,Celina Shiyu Wang,Junjie Ye,Muchen Xu,Minhao Li,Sergey Zakharov,Basile Van Hoorick,Vitor Campagnolo Guizilini,Leonidas Guibas,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DUET是一个双机器人移动操作学习框架，通过VR遥操作和人类协作演示数据采集，结合Action Chunking Transformer预训练+微调范式，在减少人类数据收集工作量的同时取得了优于纯机器人数据训练方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双机器人协作能完成单机器人无法实现的任务（如协同搬运、交接），但数据采集是训练这类系统的主要瓶颈。现有方法缺乏高效的数据收集手段。

Method: 1) 构建统一的双本体同步VR遥操作系统进行域内异构机器人数据收集；2) 开发人类-人类协作追踪管线记录协作移动操作先验；3) 基于Action Chunking Transformer的架构，先在高效的人类演示数据上预训练协作策略，再在少量真实机器人遥操作轨迹上微调。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人和Dexmate Vega1移动操作器上的四个协作任务基准测试中，利用人类先验的方法在任务性能上优于纯机器人数据训练的基线，同时遥操作数据收集速度平均提升5.4倍，且在所有任务上表现不低于纯机器人数据训练的策略。

Conclusion: 利用人类协作先验不仅提升了任务性能，还显著减少了数据收集所需的人类工作量，验证了人类-人类演示预训练+少量机器人数据微调范式的有效性。

Abstract: Dual-robot collaboration enables tasks that exceed the reach and payload of a single robot, such as collaboratively transporting objects across environments and executing coordinated handovers. Data acquisition is the primary bottleneck for training these systems. To this end, we introduce DUET, a dual-robot learning framework for mobile manipulation. For efficient data collection, we create a unified dual-embodiment synchronized VR-based teleoperation system for in-domain heterogeneous robot data collection. We further develop a complementary tracking pipeline that records human-human coordination and collaborative mobile manipulation priors. To allow efficient learning, we introduce an Action Chunking Transformer based architecture that first pretrains collaborative policies on efficient human-human demonstrations, before finetuning them on a minimal set of real-robot teleoperation trajectories. We develop a benchmark of four collaborative tasks to evaluate our framework using a Unitree G1 humanoid and a Dexmate Vega1 mobile manipulator. The results demonstrate that harnessing human priors not only yields superior task performance compared to baselines trained only on robot data, but also reduces the total human effort required for data collection. Our human data collection pipeline achieves 5.4x acceleration on average from teleoperation, but we perform equally or better than robot-only data trained policies across all tasks. Our project page is available at https://zhaoy37.github.io/Duet/.

</details>


### [205] [Inductive Generalization for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20999)
*Annabella Macaluso,Haochen Zhang,Ishaan Masilamony,Yingshan Chang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"归纳泛化"概念，通过逐步增加任务难度的分布外测试评估视觉运动策略的泛化能力。实验表明，现有最先进的视觉-语言-动作模型在传统领域偏移测试中表现良好，但在归纳泛化测试中失败，揭示了一个与数据/模型缩放正交的学习挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略的泛化评估主要依赖已知分布上的插值测试（如光照、杂乱、多样物体），未能真正衡量策略的归纳能力。作者认为需要测试策略在逐步变难的分布外任务变体上的归纳容量。

Method: 借鉴语言模型中基于轴评估的思路（如序列长度、计数），提出一个可复用的形式化评估协议，用于测量任意操作策略的归纳泛化能力，并在现有范 paradigm 上建立基线。

Result: SoTA的视觉-语言-动作模型在传统领域偏移（如干扰物）上表现出泛化，但在归纳泛化测试中失败。

Conclusion: 归纳泛化暴露了一类与数据/模型缩放正交的学习挑战，解决这些挑战对于实现通用机器人至关重要。

Abstract: Understanding the generalization capabilities of visuomotor policies is essential in the development of capable robotic agents. Generalizable models learn structures that transfer across domains. However, in practice, visuomotor policies test performance by interpolation on known distributions using unstructured domain shifts (e.g. lighting, clutter, diverse objects). We argue that to measure generalization capabilities we must instead test the inductive capacity of policies on progressively harder, out-of-distribution task variants. We call this inductive generalization, drawing directly on how axis-based evaluation has revealed inherent generalization limitations in language models (e.g. sequence length, counting) arXiv:2502.00197 . We provide a reusable and formal evaluation protocol for measuring inductive generalization in any manipulation policy, and establish baselines showing that existing paradigms fail this test; e.g. SoTA Vision-Language-Action models and find that policies that appear to generalize to prior domain shifts (distractors, etc) fail inductive generalization tests. These results expose a class of learning challenges orthogonal to those addressed by data and model scaling in robot learning, yet are imperative to solve in order to realize general purpose robots.

</details>


### [206] [R2HandoverSim: A Simulation Framework and Benchmark for Robot-to-Human Object Handovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21011)
*Hanxin Zhang,Abdulqader Dhafer,Hongbiao Dong,Zhou Daniel Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了R2HandoverSim，一个机器人对人（R2H）物体交接的仿真基准，包含标准化评估协议、四种基线方法比较、30人用户研究，并验证了仿真结果与真实评估的相关性，同时提出了五个互补性评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: R2H交接方法虽发展迅速，但缺乏标准化评估协议，导致无法进行客观比较。

Method: 构建仿真基准R2HandoverSim，系统比较四种基线方法的共享抓取姿态预测；通过30名参与者进行用户研究，分析基线性能并验证仿真与真实评估的相关性；提出五个互补性评估指标。

Result: 仿真结果与真实世界评估结果相关；五个互补指标（规划可行性、可达性、抓取稳定性、抓取适应性、安全性）比单一成功率更能反映用户感知的交接质量。

Conclusion: R2HandoverSim提供了标准化评估工具，五个互补指标可更全面评估R2H交接质量。

Abstract: We present R2HandoverSim, a simulation benchmark for robot-to-human (R2H) object handovers. Although R2H handover methods have advanced rapidly, the lack of standardized evaluation protocols impedes objective comparison. Our benchmark enables reproducible evaluation by systematically comparing four baselines on their predicted shared grasp poses. We conduct a user study with 30 participants, analyze baseline performance, and show that simulation results correlate with real-world evaluation outcomes. Crucially, five complementary metrics (planning feasibility, reachability, grasp stability, grasp affordance, and safety) better reflect user-perceived handover quality than overall success rate alone. Website and code: https://robot-future.github.io/r2handoversim/.

</details>


### [207] [BayesFP: Posterior Estimation for Flow-Based Policies via Feynman-Kac Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21014)
*Sreevardhan Sirigiri,Weiming Zhi,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯后验采样的约束轨迹生成方法，利用Feynman-Kac修正器框架扩展至确定性流匹配策略，实现无需重训练的推理时约束优化，在模拟和真实操作任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要在保持模仿学习专家行为的基础上，满足推理时才指定的安全约束和任务目标，现有方法需重训练或无法统一处理扩散模型和流匹配策略的约束生成问题。

Method: 将约束轨迹生成建模为贝叶斯后验采样，以专家演示分布为先验，推理时成本函数导出的似然进行倾斜调整；利用Feynman-Kac修正器框架扩展至确定性流匹配策略，实现无需重训练的统一采样器。

Result: 在Diffusion Policy、GR00T-N1.6和π₀.₅等预训练检查点上验证，在模拟和真实操作任务中（包括推理时引入的非凸障碍物规划）均取得改进，并在零样本任务上优于基础π₀.₅。

Conclusion: 提出的统一推理时采样方法无需重训练即可为扩散和流匹配策略施加约束，有效提升了机器人轨迹生成的可行性与优化性。

Abstract: Robots must generate trajectories that remain faithful to learned expert behavior while satisfying safety constraints and task-specific objectives specified only at inference time. We formulate constrained trajectory generation for pretrained diffusion and flow-matching policies as Bayesian posterior sampling, with the learned demonstration distribution as a prior and an inference-time, cost-derived likelihood tilting it toward feasible, optimal trajectories. To sample from this posterior without any retraining of the base policy, we leverage the Feynman--Kac corrector framework, originally formulated for diffusion models, and extend it to deterministic flow-matching policies. The result is a unified, inference-time, retraining-free sampler for diffusion and flow policies. We validate the approach on pretrained Diffusion Policy, GR00T-N1.6, and $π_{0.5}$ checkpoints across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, including planning around non-convex obstacles introduced at inference time, and show improvements over the base $π_{0.5}$ on zero-shot tasks.

</details>


### [208] [Membrane-based Acoustic Microrobots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21047)
*Fatih Kocabas,Cemal Polat Avdar,Prithvi Venkatesh,Yunus Alapan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于柔性PDMS薄膜密封微腔的声学微型机器人，解决了传统气泡型声学微型机器人因气体溶解导致寿命短、共振频率漂移的问题。该设计实现了超过24小时的稳定微流推进，结合磁性颗粒可实现低强度磁场定向控制，并展示了~100微米尺度的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声学微型机器人依赖被困在微腔中的气泡产生微流，但气泡会因气体快速溶解而导致共振频率偏移和寿命受限，亟需一种能够长期稳定运行的设计方案。

Method: 在微腔上方键合一层柔性PDMS薄膜以物理隔绝气体扩散，通过薄膜振动产生微流；同时将磁性微粒嵌入结构体以实现磁场定向控制。

Result: 膜基声学致动器在连续运行超过24小时后仍能保持稳定的流场和推进性能；在2 mT低强度磁场下实现了方向可控的微型游动；设计架构可缩小至~100微米。

Conclusion: 提出的膜基方案为开发高耐久性的声学微致动器和微型机器人提供了可靠框架，使其能够胜任长期任务。

Abstract: Acoustic microrobots have emerged as a promising frontier for targeted drug delivery and minimally invasive medicine due to their high-power density and biocompatibility. Despite wide-ranging designs, conventional acoustic microrobots mostly rely on air microbubbles trapped within confined microcavities within the robot body, which suffer from limited operational longevity due to rapid gas dissolution and resultant shifts in resonance frequency. In this paper, we propose a robust, membrane-based acoustic microrobot that overcomes these limitations by employing a thin flexible Polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS) membrane bonded over confined microcavities for microstreaming. The introduced design physically prevents gas diffusion, ensuring stable performance over extended periods at high actuation voltages. We systematically characterized the membrane-based acoustic actuator longevity, demonstrating consistent streaming and propulsion for over 24 hours of continuous operation. In addition, by embedding magnetic microparticles into the structural body, these actuators were successfully employed as microswimmers with directional control using low-intensity (2 mT) external magnetic fields. Finally, we demonstrate the scalability of the proposed design architecture down to ~100 um. This membrane-based approach establishes a reliable framework for the development of high-endurance acoustic microactuators and microrobots capable of performing long-term tasks.

</details>


### [209] [ReFPO: Reflow Regularization for Flow Matching Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21086)
*Ge Wang,Yibo Peng,Fan Feng,Shenhao Yan,Chengsi Yao,Jiahao Yang,Honghao Cai,Yiming Zhao,Xi Li,Jinke Ren,Shuguang Cui,Yatong Han,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReFPO通过在FPO中引入显式Reflow正则化，实现了一种单行代码即可完成的在线强化学习方法，在不增加计算开销的情况下稳定了PPO式训练，并在多种物理仿真任务中提升了性能与离散化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的策略梯度方法（FPO）在训练稳定性和推理质量上存在不足，作者发现FPO的梯度更新可视为隐式的优势加权Reflow过程，但缺乏显式的几何约束，导致CFM代理比率尖峰和训练不稳定。

Method: ReFPO在FPO基础上引入显式Reflow几何正则化项，仅需修改一行代码即可实现。该方法将优势引导更新与路径整流（path rectification）协同，通过显式正则化抑制CFM代理比率尖峰，稳定PPO式训练。

Result: 在GridWorld、MuJoCo Playground和高维Humanoid Control任务中，ReFPO相比基线方法提升了平均性能和离散化鲁棒性，实现了高保真单步推理，其性能常匹配或超越多步推理结果。

Conclusion: ReFPO为基于流的生成策略提供了一种可扩展且稳定的训练方法，证明了显式几何正则化在流匹配强化学习中的有效性。

Abstract: We present Reflow-regularized Flow Matching Policy Gradients (ReFPO), a simple online RL method that adds explicit Reflow regularization to FPO for efficient flow-based control. We uncover a key structural property: the gradient updates in Flow Matching Policy Gradients (FPO) can be interpreted as an implicit advantage-weighted Reflow process, providing a new geometric perspective on flow-based policy gradients. Building on this insight, ReFPO introduces an explicit geometric regularizer that can be implemented with a single line of code change without incurring additional computational overhead or auxiliary distillation stages. By synergizing advantage-guided updates with path rectification, our method reduces CFM proxy-ratio spikes, stabilizes PPO-style training, and enables high-fidelity one-step inference that often matches or exceeds multi-step performance. We experimentally demonstrate that ReFPO improves average performance and discretization robustness across GridWorld, MuJoCo Playground, and high-dimensional Humanoid Control tasks, providing a scalable and stable approach for generative policies in complex physical simulations.

</details>


### [210] [MV-WAM: Manifold-Aware World Action Model with Value Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21088)
*Jintao Chen,Peidong Jia,Qingpo Wuwu,Jiaming Liu,Mengfei Du,Chun-Kai Fan,Xiaowei Chi,Hao Chen,Chengyu Bai,Zezhong Qian,Hao Wang,Jiajun Cao,Weishi Mi,Xiaozhu Ju,Jian Tang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MV-WAM框架，通过跨模态因果掩码、流形感知优化和进度-价值调节机制，解决机器人操作中视觉与动作模态的结构异质性问题，在仿真和真实场景中显著提升了策略的泛化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在分布内场景表现良好，但在分布外场景下性能下降严重。作者认为根本原因在于视觉与动作模态之间存在结构不匹配，其固有的异质流形导致联合优化在分布偏移时不成比例地损害动作鲁棒性。

Method: 提出MV-WAM端到端框架，包含三个核心创新：1）跨模态因果掩码，将动作层次化地锚定在预测视频帧和双模态价值函数token上；2）流形感知优化方案，显式建模跨模态结构异质性；3）进度-价值调节机制，估计任务完成度、检测预测帧与生成动作之间的不对齐，并通过价值引导回滚实现自主纠错。

Result: 在RoboTwin仿真中，无随机动作监督下随机场景平均成功率55.7%，超过最强基线29.3%。在双臂机器人的四个真实任务中平均成功率达77.5%。

Conclusion: 流形感知的跨模态对齐是实现鲁棒策略泛化的关键，为可部署的机器人操作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Achieving robust and generalizable manipulation across diverse environments remains a fundamental challenge in embodied robotics. Recent world action models achieve strong in-domain performance, yet their gains do not extend proportionally to out-of-distribution scenarios. We attribute this to a structural mismatch between visual and action modalities, whose intrinsically heterogeneous manifolds cause joint optimization to disproportionately degrade action robustness under distribution shift. To address this, we propose MV-WAM, a novel end-to-end framework that jointly models visual prediction, action generation, and value estimation designed to effectively leverage video priors during both training and inference for enhanced action generalization. Key to this unification is a cross-modality causal mask that hierarchically grounds actions in predicted video frames and value function tokens in both modalities. To further narrow the generalization gap, MV-WAM adopts a manifold-aware optimization scheme that explicitly accounts for the structural heterogeneity across modalities. Finally, MV-WAM introduces a progress-value regulation mechanism that estimates task completion and detects misalignment between predicted frames and generated actions, enabling the policy to autonomously identify execution deviations and recover through value-guided rollback. On the RoboTwin simulation, MV-WAM achieves a 55.7% mean success rate on random scenarios without any randomized action supervision, outperforming the strongest baseline by 29.3%. MV-WAM achieves a 77.5% mean success rate across four real-world tasks of varying difficulty on a dual-arm robot. Our results demonstrate that manifold-aware cross-modal alignment is essential for robust policy generalization, offering a path toward deployable robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [211] [How Should a Robot Configure Its Laser Scanner for Inspection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21093)
*Zhiling Chen,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,Yang Zhang,Jiong Tang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SenseHD是一种机器人传感系统，将扫描仪配置形式化为指令条件化的传感决策，通过超维关联记忆选择稳定传感参数，在真实机器人检测平台上显著提升了检测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人检测研究主要集中在运动规划和视角规划上，而传感参数的配置方法未得到充分探索，尽管这些参数对测量质量有决定性影响。

Method: SenseHD将传感参数视为离散的传感动作，并通过超维关联记忆（Hyperdimensional Associative Memory）选择稳定的传感区间，采用指令条件化的传感决策范式，而非直接预测精确参数值。

Result: 在真实机器人检测平台上，SenseHD能够稳健地选择适当的传感配置，显著提升检测可靠性，且相比基线方法更轻量高效。
