Learned trajectory predictors capture richer interactions but are short-horizon and tend to be unstable when run in closed loop. We present Enactor, an actor-centric generative model for closed-loop intersection microsimulation. The model focuses on vehicles; pedestrians are included as context that can influence vehicle decisions but not predicted. Dynamic actors and lane polylines are encoded in polar coordinates referenced to the intersection center. A transformer with separate spatial and temporal attention blocks predicts a distribution over each actor's next-step motion ($s$, $α$). Training uses a closed-loop curriculum so the model is exposed to its own predictions. We evaluate Enactor in two regimes. In a 4000-second simulation-in-the-loop test at two intersection geometries, Enactor controls every dynamic vehicle against a continuously refreshing actor set rather than the fixed cohort that learned trajectory predictors are usually evaluated against. It recovers the SUMO data generator's speed and travel-time distributions with KL divergence over an order of magnitude lower than a recent transformer baseline on travel time, and substantially lower on speed (roughly $5\times$ lower at Site 1), and reduces red-light violations relative to the same baseline by more than an order of magnitude. An ablation isolates the leader rear-bumper feature as the change with the largest effect on intersection-aware safety metrics. We also evaluate on real-world field data and apply the same architecture to naturalistic vehicle trajectories from a fish-eye camera at a signalized intersection and evaluate it on multi-horizon predictive tasks. Enactor outperforms a constant-velocity baseline at every horizon evaluated.

</details>


### [321] [KEMO: Event-Driven Keyframe Memory for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation with VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23589)
*Yihan Zeng,Minghao Ye,Yiyuan Chen,Yide Shentu,Philipp Wu,Zike Yan,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KEMO是一个轻量级即插即用记忆框架，通过结合机器人运动学和视觉过滤自动检测任务相关状态变化的关键帧，并将其编码为紧凑的时间有序记忆令牌，通过交叉注意力和门控残差融合与当前视觉特征结合，用于VLA策略训练。在多种真实双臂操作任务上，相比无记忆基线（如π_{0.5}），KEMO将任务成功率和阶段完成率分别提升23.6%和34.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域机器人操作中，相似观测可能出现在不同执行阶段，而适当动作取决于已完成的先前操作。现有记忆增强方法要么保留密集历史需要压缩，要么主要依赖近期上下文可能丢弃早期任务相关事件，无法有效解决这种歧义性。

Method: KEMO框架包含三个核心组件：1) 事件检测：结合机器人运动学和视觉过滤自动检测任务相关状态变化的关键帧；2) 记忆编码：将选中的关键帧编码为紧凑的时间有序记忆令牌；3) 记忆融合：通过交叉注意力和门控残差融合将记忆令牌与当前视觉特征整合，用于VLA训练。同时，检测到的事件还定义了关键转换附近的高权重训练样本。

Result: 在多种真实双臂操作任务（2-6个评分子任务，轨迹长度830-2846步，时长28-95秒）上，KEMO相比无记忆基线（π_{0.5}）将聚合任务成功率提升23.6%，阶段完成率提升34.1%。消融实验表明，事件驱动的关键帧选择优于均匀采样和近期帧保留，门控融合和关键帧对齐的损失加权提供互补增益。

Conclusion: KEMO通过事件驱动的关键帧记忆机制有效解决了长时域操作中的观测歧义性问题，为VLA策略提供了一种轻量级、即插即用的记忆增强方案，显著提升了长时程复杂操作任务的性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation remains challenging because similar observations may occur at different execution stages, while the appropriate action depends on previously completed operations. Memory can address this ambiguity by enabling policies to infer task progress from execution history. However, existing memory-augmented approaches often either retain dense histories that require compression or rely primarily on recent context that may discard earlier task-relevant events. In this work, we propose propose KEMO, a lightweight plug-in memory framework that automatically selectively preserves keyframes associated with task-relevant state changes for VLA policies. KEMO combines robot kinematics with visual filtering to detect events, encodes the selected keyframes as compact temporally ordered memory tokens, and integrates them with current visual features through cross-attention and gated residual fusion for VLA training. The detected events also define higher-weight training samples near critical transitions. We evaluate KEMO on various real-world dual-arm manipulation tasks spanning 2 to 6 scored subtasks, and trajectory length ranging from 830 steps to 2846 execution steps (durations from 28 to 95 seconds). Compared with the memory-free baseline (e.g., $π_{0.5}$), KEMO improves aggregate Task Success Rate by 23.6\% and Stage Completion Rate by 34.1\%. Ablations show that event-driven keyframe selection outperforms uniform sampling and recent-frame retention, while the proposed gated fusion and keyframe-aligned loss weighting provide complementary gains.

</details>


### [322] [Real-Time Multimodal Activity-Aware Error Detection in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23593)
*Seyed Hamid Reza Roodabeh,Zongyu Li,Homa Alemzadeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，利用多模态输入（视频、运动学数据和文本提示）进行手术执行错误检测，在JIGSAWS和SAR-RARP50数据集上F1分数分别提升5%和16.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有执行错误检测方法仅使用视频数据，忽视了手术流程层级结构中细粒度的活动描述和错误类型，且未充分利用多模态互补信息。

Method: 构建统一框架，融合视频、运动学数据和描述性文本提示；通过活动提示（activity prompting）整合手势级活动、器械-物体交互和错误定义的描述性语言；引入基于手术活动标签预训练的视觉编码器生成活动感知视觉嵌入，并对比CLIP与传统图像嵌入的效果；将运动学数据与视频和文本模态无缝集成。

Result: 在JIGSAWS数据集上F1分数提升5%，在SAR-RARP50数据集上提升16.6%，优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合精心设计的文本提示与多模态数据能有效提升手术错误检测的准确性，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery improves surgical precision but introduces complexity, making technical error detection essential for ensuring patient safety. Current executional error detection methods using video data often overlook fine-grained contextual descriptions of activities and error types within the hierarchical structure of surgical procedures. They also under-utilize complementary multimodal information. We propose a unified framework for executional error detection that leverages multimodal input, including video, kinematics, and descriptive textual prompts. Through activity prompting, we integrate descriptive language in gesture-level activities, instrument-object interactions, and error definitions. We also introduce activity-aware visual embeddings derived from vision encoders pretrained on surgical activity labels to compare the effectiveness of contrastive language-image embeddings with traditional image-based embeddings for error detection. By seamlessly integrating kinematic data with video and textual modalities, our framework significantly improves error detection performance. Achieving up to 5\% and 16.6\% F1 score improvements over state-of-the-art baselines on the JIGSAWS and SAR-RARP50 datasets, respectively, we demonstrate the value of combining curated textual prompts with multimodal data for accurate error detection.

</details>


### [323] [Autonomous Subsea Cable Search and Tracking with Graph-Optimised Priors and Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23606)
*Ibrahim Fadhil Djauhari,Adrian Bodenmann,Samuel Simmons,Cailei Liang,David White,Susan Gourvenec,Tom Bennetts,Darryl Newborough,Blair Thornton*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用不确定先验路由图的水下电缆搜索与跟踪方法，通过基于图的优化、物理约束的悬链线模型和半监督分类器，使AUV能在部分埋设和小直径电缆条件下实现连续视觉跟踪，现场试验中成功跟踪120米测试电缆的59%并实现丢失后恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球通信依赖海底电缆基础设施，但这些电缆易受自然灾害和人类活动破坏。AUV虽能有效检测长距离裸露电缆，但电缆路由图的不确定性、小直径和部分埋设使连续跟踪面临挑战，因此需要一种能在不确定性条件下高效搜索和跟踪电缆的方法。

Method: 1) 基于图的优化：持续更新电缆路由以保持与视觉观测一致性；2) 物理约束的悬链线模型：根据观测距离约束路由不确定性，考虑敷设深度、直径和密度等电缆参数，将搜索空间限制在物理可行区域；3) 半监督分类器：在AUV上实时运行进行电缆检测；4) 受限搜索空间下的丢失恢复机制。

Result: 使用南安普顿大学Smarty200 AUV进行现场试验，在初始路由图中存在故意错误的情况下成功定位电缆，更新路由使其与观测一致，通过视觉跟踪检查了120米测试电缆的59%，并在跟踪丢失后成功恢复。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效应对电缆路由图不确定性、小直径和部分埋设等挑战，通过物理约束的搜索空间提高了搜索效率，并具备跟踪丢失后的自主恢复能力，显著提升了AUV海底电缆检测的可靠性和实用性。

Abstract: Global communications rely on subsea cable infrastructure that remains vulnerable to damage from natural hazards and human activity. Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) offer an efficient means to inspect long sections of exposed cable, but uncertainty in cable route maps, small cable diameters and partial burial makes continuous tracking a challenge. This paper presents a novel cable search and tracking method that leverages uncertain prior cable route maps. Graph-based optimisation continuously update the cable route to remain consistent with visual observations. Route uncertainty is constrained as a function of distance from observations using physics-based catenary models that account for cable parameters (i.e., lay depth, diameter, and density), bounding the search space to physically feasible regions and improving search efficiency. Cable detection is performed using a semi-supervised classifier running in real-time on-board a camera-equipped AUV. These detections both update the graph-based optimisation and enable visual cable tracking. When tracking is lost due to misclassification, burial or imperfect control, the bounded search space enables efficient recovery. The approach was demonstrated in field trials using the University of Southampton's Smarty200 AUV. The system successfully located the cable despite deliberate errors in it initial cable route map, updating this to be consistent with observations and using visual tracking to inspect up to 59% of a 120m test cable, with successful recovered after tracking loss.

</details>


### [324] [RECALL: Recovery Experience Collection for Active Lifelong Learning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23617)
*Ulas Berk Karli,Tesca Fitzgerald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向VLA模型的主动持续学习范式，使用不确定性引导的数据收集替代被动模仿学习，能提升微调效率，但引发灾难性遗忘问题；通过评估回放混合与弹性权重巩固等持续学习技术，揭示了不确定性引导恢复数据的学习可塑性与旧知识保留之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动模仿学习需要机器人先失败才能触发数据收集、无法有效指导哪些状态需要监督，且浪费标注资源在模型已能胜任的任务部分。作者希望提出更高效的数据收集与微调方式。

Method: 1）提出主动、不确定性引导的数据收集范式用于VLA微调；2）对比主动收集与被动收集数据的效果；3）研究回放数据混合与弹性权重巩固等持续学习技术以缓解遗忘问题。

Result: 不确定性引导的数据收集比被动演示更高效；但仅在主动收集的恢复数据上微调会导致灾难性遗忘；回放混合与弹性权重巩固等技术存在可塑性与知识保留之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 不确定性引导的恢复演示能提升VLA模型的适应效率，但将针对性新数据融入大规模机器人策略仍面临开放挑战；本文为自回归VLA的主动持续学习提供了实证研究基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly fine-tuned through passive imitation learning, where additional demonstrations are collected for tasks where the policy performs poorly. This approach incurs several downsides: it requires the robot to fail before data collection is triggered, provides little guidance about which states require supervision, and wastes demonstrator effort on redundant parts of the task where the policy already performs well. In this paper, we propose an active, continual learning paradigm for VLAs. We demonstrate that active, uncertainty-guided data collection leads to more efficient fine-tuning than when using passively-collected demonstrations. However, we also find that fine-tuning only on actively-collected recovery data leads to catastrophic forgetting. We evaluate techniques for continual learning, including replay-based data mixing and elastic weight consolidation, and identify tradeoffs between plasticity to uncertainty-guided recovery data and retention of previously learned behaviors. Overall, our work contributes an empirical study of active continual learning for autoregressive VLAs, establishing that uncertainty-guided recovery demonstrations can improve adaptation efficiency while also revealing open challenges when targeted new data is incorporated into large robot policies.

</details>


### [325] [dVLA-RL: Reinforcement Learning over Denoising Trajectories for Discrete Diffusion Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23623)
*Yuhao Wu,Yitian Liu,Weijie Shen,Mishuo Han,Wenjie Xu,Haotian Liang,Zhongshan Liu,Yinan Mao,Lei Xu,Xinping Guan,Ru Ying,Ran Zheng,Wei Sui,Xiaokang Yang,Wenbo Ding,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出dVLA-RL方法，将强化学习引入离散扩散VLA模型，通过将去噪过程建模为MDP并优化路径联合概率而非边际概率，实现了统一的多任务步长调度，在LIBERO上达到99.7%成功率，在RoboTwin 2.0上相比SFT提升30.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散VLA模型仅使用监督微调（SFT）训练，强化学习（RL）用于策略微调的潜力尚未被探索，而dVLA中最终动作的边际概率难以求解是RL应用的核心障碍。

Method: 将去噪过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），将学习目标从边际动作概率转为采样路径的联合概率（即步级转移概率的乘积），并基于此提出统一步长调度方法，根据任务复杂度自适应调整去噪步数以优化成功率和计算效率。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到99.7%成功率；在RoboTwin 2.0上相比SFT基线提升30.6%，与强World-Action Model基线竞争。

Conclusion: 通过路径联合概率的RL训练和统一步长调度，有效释放了离散扩散VLA模型的策略优化潜力，在多项机器人操控任务上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have established a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation by grounding control into the semantic reasoning of VLMs. Prevailing architectures typically model actions continuously via diffusion or flow processes, or discretely through either autoregressive generation or parallel decoding. Recently, Discrete Diffusion VLAs (dVLAs) have emerged as a distinct alternative, unifying vision, language, and action into a single discrete token space via masked generative modeling. While combining iterative refinement with unified representations, its training has thus far been restricted to Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), leaving the potential of Reinforcement Learning (RL) for further policy refinement largely unexplored. A fundamental challenge in RL for dVLAs is that the marginal probability of the final action generated by dVLAs remains intractable. To solve this problem, we propose \textbf{dVLA-RL}, shifting the learning objective from the marginal action probability to the joint probability of the sampled generation path. Specifically, by modeling the denoising process as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), we mathematically formulate this path probability as a product of step-wise transitions. This trajectory-level objective provides a unified formulation that natively accommodates variable denoising steps. Leveraging this intrinsic fexibility, we introduce a unified step scheduling approach for complex multi-task learning, tailoring denoising steps to specific task complexities to maximize both success rates and computational effciency. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our approach achieves a success rate of \textbf{99.7\%} on LIBERO. Furthermore, it establishes strong VLA-based results on RoboTwin 2.0 by delivering a \textbf{30.6\%} improvement over the SFT baseline, remaining competitive with strong World-Action Model baselines.

</details>


### [326] [Learning to See While Learning to Act: Diffusion Models for Active Perception in Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23625)
*Kuancheng Wang,Vaibhav Saxena,Shuo Cheng,Yotto Koga,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出See2Act模仿学习方法，通过在测试时主动推断视角序列来解耦动作去噪与视角优化，在严重遮挡下提升操作性能达34%，并实现零样本仿真到真实环境的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法大多假设完全可观测性，但实际场景中物体常被遮挡，机器人需要同时执行搜索和操作行为，而有限演示下学习这种耦合行为仍然困难。

Method: 提出See2Act方法，将动作预测与主动推断的视角序列条件化，耦合动作去噪与视角优化。策略利用离线演示中锚定到关键帧动作的相机位姿进行训练，隐式学习在何处观察的同时学习如何行动。

Result: 在Ravens中，策略在严重遮挡下恢复信息性视角；在RLBench上相比先前方法性能提升高达34%。真实世界中，在数字孪生中收集50个演示，利用深度观测实现零样本仿真到真实迁移，策略能处理显著遮挡。

Conclusion: 学习到的视角推理能力使得在部分可观测性下实现鲁棒的操作成为可能。

Abstract: Most imitation learning methods assume full observability in table-top settings. In practice, objects are often occluded, requiring robots to both search and act, and learning this coupled behavior from limited demonstrations remains challenging. We propose See2Act, an imitation learning approach that conditions action prediction on a sequence of actively-inferred viewpoints at test time, by coupling action denoising with viewpoint refinement. The policy is trained using camera poses anchored to keyframe actions from offline demonstrations, enabling implicit learning of where to see, while learning how to act. We empirically demonstrate that in Ravens the policy recovers informative viewpoints under severe occlusions, and on RLBench tasks it improves performance by up to 34% over prior methods. In the real world, we collect 50 demonstrations in a digital twin and achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on pick-and-place tasks using depth observations. The policy handles significant occlusions, showing that learned viewpoint reasoning enables robust manipulation under partial observability.

</details>


### [327] [Flatness Preserves Instruction Following in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23641)
*Haochen Zhang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过平坦性保持优化（Sharpness-Aware Minimization）来微调VLA模型，解决了标准微调导致的语言指令忽视问题（指令盲区），在无需额外数据或架构修改的情况下，将指令跟随性能提升超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在下游机器人数据微调时，有限的数据导致梯度稀疏、损失景观尖锐，模型出现"指令盲区"——忽视语言指令而依赖视觉捷径，亟需一种不依赖额外数据或架构修改的解决方案。

Method: 在VLA微调过程中应用Sharpness-Aware Minimization（SAM）平坦性保持优化，通过寻找损失景观中更平坦的区域，增强模型对权重空间扰动的鲁棒性，从而在完全相同的数据上实现更优的指令跟随能力。

Result: 在多个仿真和真实世界基准上，指令跟随性能提升超过60%，且与现有引导技术互补，无需额外数据、架构修改或重新训练。

Conclusion: 平坦性保持优化是解决VLA模型指令盲区的有效方法，其效果源于更平坦的损失景观带来的泛化鲁棒性，且与现有方法正交可组合。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have the potential for open-world generalization by leveraging pretrained vision-language representations, yet downstream finetuning on limited robot data often degrades these representations, leading to brittle policies that ignore language instructions in favor of visual shortcuts, a failure mode we term instruction blindness. We hypothesize that standard finetuning with limited data applies gradients to a sparse set of points, which manifests as a sharp loss landscape with high-curvature minima. We propose to address this directly through flatness-preserving optimization while finetuning on the exact same data, where learning a flatter landscape results in a model more robust to perturbations in the weight space. Specifically, we demonstrate that simply applying sharpness-aware minimization during VLA finetuning significantly improves instruction following by over 60% across multiple simulation and real-world benchmarks without additional data, architectural modification, or retraining. We further analyze the effect of selective sharpness, quantify its effects, and show that our approach is complementary to existing guidance techniques. Project page can be found at https://haochenz11.github.io/papers/flatness-vla/.

</details>


### [328] [A Reduced Order Model for Emergent Mechanics in Woven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23658)
*Anvay A. Pradhan,Evgueni T. Filipov,Talia Y. Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种降阶模型，通过节点和四种物理可解释的刚度单元（轴向变形、面内解捻、编织束间剪切、摩擦滑移）来模拟编织结构，精度达5%以内，并能模拟泊松效应、渐进拉出、应力局域化及可编程各向异性等连续方法无法捕捉的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编织结构模型要么过度均质化丢失微观交互细节，要么计算成本过高无法实用，亟需一种兼具物理透明性与计算效率的降阶模型来填补这一空白。

Method: 将编织束交互表示为节点系统，引入四种刚度单元（轴向、解捻、剪切、摩擦滑移）；通过单位胞元特征值分析验证各元素的必要性；基于三点弯曲和剪切实验数据标定刚度参数。

Result: 模型在多种编织宽度和间距下与实验数据吻合度达5%以内；成功展示了泊松响应、渐进拉出力降阶、三种撕裂构型下的应力局域化以及空间梯度刚度实现的可编程力学各向异性。

Conclusion: 该降阶模型物理透明、计算高效，可作为编织结构材料分析与设计的实用工具，实现可编程力学响应。

Abstract: Woven structures exhibit rich mechanical behaviors including anisotropic stiffness, shear-induced locking, and crimp interchange that emerge purely from the geometric arrangement of individual weavers rather than from constituent material properties. Existing models either homogenize these interactions or resolve them at prohibitive computational cost. We introduce a reduced-order model that bridges this gap by representing individual weaver interactions through a system of nodes and four physically interpretable stiffness elements capturing axial deformation, in-plane uncrimping, inter-weaver shear, and frictional slip. Eigenvalue analysis of the unit cell confirms that the lowest-energy deformation modes correspond directly to known weave-specific phenomena, and that each element is necessary for a complete kinematic and mechanistic description. Element stiffness parameters are calibrated against empirical three-point bending and shear data, achieving agreement within 5% across varied weaver widths and spacings. The validated model is then applied to demonstrate capabilities beyond the reach of continuum approaches including: the emergent Poisson's response arising from crimp interchange, stepwise force reduction during progressive weaver pullout, stress localization under three distinct tearing configurations, and programmable mechanical anisotropy through spatially graded weaver stiffness. The physical transparency and computational efficiency of the framework position it as a practical tool for the analysis and design of woven architected materials with programmable mechanical response.

</details>


### [329] [CoorDex: Coordinating Body and Hand Priors for Continuous Dexterous Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23680)
*Sikai Li,Shuning Li,Zhenyu Wei,Yunchao Yao,Chenran Li,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoorDex是一个学习流水线，通过将高维身体和灵巧手控制转化为协调的潜在残差控制，使类人机器人能够在移动中执行高自由度灵巧操作任务（如抓取瓶子、开冰箱门等），克服了传统“走走停停”模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统类人机器人的移动操作被简化为“走走停停”的分离过程，且依赖低自由度末端执行器，缺乏在移动中进行高自由度灵巧操作的能力。

Method: CoorDex从全身和手部仿真演示开始，训练特权运动跟踪教师模型，将其蒸馏为基于本体感觉的潜在先验，再以冻结的潜在先验作为动作空间进行下游残差强化学习。协调的潜在残差策略通过共享任务上下文和分离的身体-手部残差头组合这些先验。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人（配备20自由度WUJI手）上实现了移动中的灵巧操作，包括非停式瓶子抓取搬运、移动中开冰箱门、方块抓取旋转等。消融实验表明，联合空间PPO、联合空间手部控制和单块潜在预测在同一奖励预算下均失败。

Conclusion: 潜在先验接口和协调残差结构使得高维、密集接触的移动操作任务变得可训练，为类人机器人实现复杂移动灵巧操作提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation is often simplified into a stop-and-go process: walking to an object, stopping to manipulate it, and then resuming locomotion. It also commonly relies on low degree-of-freedom (DoF) end effectors that behave like an open-close grasp primitive. We introduce CoorDex, a learning pipeline that converts high-dimensional body and dexterous hand control into coordinated latent residual control, enabling high-DoF dexterous loco-manipulation on the move. Starting from simulated whole-body and hand demonstrations, CoorDex trains privileged motion tracking teachers for the humanoid body and dexterous hand, distills them into proprioception-conditioned latent priors, and uses the frozen priors as the action space for downstream residual reinforcement learning. A coordinated latent residual policy composes these priors through shared task context and separate body-hand residual heads, preserving natural whole-body motion while improving finger-level contact reliability. CoorDex enables a Unitree G1 humanoid with a 20-DoF WUJI hand to execute dexterous manipulation while in motion, including non-stop bottle grasping and carrying, fridge door opening on the move, and cube pick-and-turn. Ablations on the walk-grasp-carry task show that joint-space PPO, joint-space hand control, and monolithic latent prediction all fail under the same reward budget, while the latent-prior interface and coordinated residual structure make high-dimensional contact-rich loco-manipulation trainable. Project Page: https://skevinci.github.io/coordex/

</details>


### [330] [LaST-HD: Learning Latent Physical Reasoning from Scalable Human Data for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23685)
*Jiaming Liu,Yinxi Wang,Chenyang Gu,Siyuan Qian,Xiangju Mi,Hao Chen,Jiawei Chen,Qingpo Wuwu,Xiaoqi Li,Nuowei Han,Yiming Zhang,Xuheng Zhang,Yang Yue,Yeqing Yang,Lei Wang,Peng Jia,Hao Tang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaST-HD提出了一种人-to-机器人动作学习范式，通过联合嵌入空间对齐人手和机器人动作，结合低成本数据手套OOL Glove和混合训练策略，仅需20分钟数据即可实现90%+的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人手示范提供了直接的物理交互数据，但跨形态的鲁棒迁移需要超越几何对齐，解决人手与机器人之间物理动力学的底层对齐问题。

Method: 1) 训练辅助的以动作为条件的世界模型，在共享前向动力学空间中统一人手和机器人轨迹；2) 开发低成本动作捕捉手套OOL Glove采集高保真人手数据；3) 提出渐进式混合训练策略（混合人机共训练+人手在线校正后训练）。

Result: 在仅有20分钟OOL手套数据的情况下，LaST-HD在新型环境中实现了超过90%的准确率；仅凭人手示范即可泛化到新物体、新场景和新位置。

Conclusion: 通过跨形态的共享潜在推理空间对齐物理动力学，结合高质量人手数据采集和渐进式训练，LaST-HD有效弥合了人手与机器人之间的形态鸿沟，显著提升了数据效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Human-hand demonstrations provide a direct and scalable source of physical interaction data for robot learning. While manual retargeting is indispensable for establishing kinematic action correspondence across different morphologies, robust transfer requires going beyond geometry to address the underlying alignment of physical dynamics between human and robot manipulation. To address this, we introduce LaST-HD, a novel human-to-robot action learning paradigm that extends reasoning-before-acting VLA by aligning human-hand and robot demonstrations in a shared latent reasoning space. Rather than mimicking human kinematics, LaST-HD trains an auxiliary action-conditioned world model on unpaired human-hand and robot trajectories to synthesize unified latent targets. After aligning cross-embodiment representations in this shared forward-dynamics space, these targets supervise LaST-HD's latent reasoning process, enabling it to internalize shared physical dynamics and drive efficient human-hand action learning. Moreover, we develop Out-of-Lab (OOL) Glove, a low-cost motion-capture glove tailored to LaST-HD for human-hand data collection. The captured human data provide precise keypoints and serve as universal action supervision across grippers and dexterous hands. Armed with the aligned latent space and high-fidelity human-hand data, we develop a progressive mixed-to-human training recipe comprising mixed human-robot co-training and human-hand online correction post-training. Through mixed co-training, LaST-HD improves generalization to novel objects, scenes, and positions using only human-hand demonstrations. With online correction, LaST-HD further adapts to novel environments and achieves over 90\% accuracy using only 20 minutes of OOL glove data.

</details>


### [331] [LIBERO-Safety: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Physical and Semantic Safety in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23686)
*Rongxu Cui,Zongzheng Zhang,Jingrui Pang,Haohan Chi,Jinbang Guo,Saining Zhang,Shaoxuan Xie,Xin Jin,Yao Mu,Jiaolong Yang,Guocai Yao,Xianyuan Zhan,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIBERO-Safety提出了一个参数化安全基准和关键帧驱动的数据生成管道，构建了包含19,664条无碰撞演示的大规模数据集，并系统评估了8个VLA和2个具身基础模型，揭示了泛化-安全权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在严格约束下的操作安全性尚未得到充分验证，现有方法受限于人工遥操作的可扩展性瓶颈，缺乏大规模安全关键场景的基准测试。

Method: 1) 提出参数化安全基准，程序化生成具有全面随机性的安全关键场景；2) 开发关键帧驱动的数据生成管道，解决可扩展性问题；3) 构建大规模严格无碰撞演示数据集（19,664条），包含广泛域随机化；4) 进行跨范式系统评估。

Result: 1) 构建了大规模安全数据集；2) 系统评估了8个VLA和2个具身基础模型；3) 揭示了泛化-安全权衡：高多样性训练虽产生更安全的轨迹，但任务成功受限于次优轨迹合成和语义对齐问题。

Conclusion: LIBERO-Safety通过提供可扩展管道、鲁棒数据集和深刻的失败模式分析，为开发安全可靠的VLA模型奠定了关键基础。

Abstract: Despite the impressive manipulation capabilities of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, their operational safety under strict constraints remains largely unverified. To address this, we introduce a parametric safety benchmark to procedurally generate safety-critical scenarios with comprehensive stochasticity. To overcome the scalability bottlenecks of human teleoperation, we develop a novel keypose-driven data generation pipeline. Leveraging this infrastructure, we curate a large-scale dataset of 19,664 strictly collision-free demonstrations with extensive domain randomization. We then conduct a systematic cross-paradigm evaluation of eight VLA and two embodied foundation models. Our analysis reveals a critical generalization-safety tension: although high-diversity training fosters safer trajectories, task success remains fundamentally bottlenecked by sub-optimal trajectory synthesis and semantic misalignment. By providing a scalable pipeline, a robust dataset, and profound failure-mode insights, LIBERO-Safety establishes a crucial foundation for developing safe and reliable VLA models.

</details>


### [332] [AutoDex: An Automated Real-World System for Dexterous Grasping Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23689)
*Mingi Choi,Gunhee Kim,Jisoo Kim,Taeksoo Kim,Taeyun Ha,Jongbin Lim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoDex是一个自动化真实世界数据采集系统，它通过闭环流程（感知、执行、标记、重置）自动收集灵巧抓取的物理验证数据，无需人工干预，显著提升数据收集效率和抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取需要物理验证的真实世界数据，但遥操作耗时且存在操作者偏差，仿真数据虽然廉价但无法保证接触有效性。现有方法难以在无人干预下规模化收集真实物理验证数据。

Method: AutoDex构建了自动化闭环数据收集系统：利用20相机密集感知克服手-物遮挡，执行碰撞监控的机器人运动，对抓取进行升降成功/失败标记，并在试验间主动重置物体状态以暴露更多稳定姿态。支持可替换的候选抓取生成器，下游系统可通过检索和可行性过滤查询数据库。

Result: 在100个不同物体上收集了3,593次抓取试验（Allegro和Inspire手），包含多视角同步观测和机器人状态日志。匹配500次轨迹采集时，AutoDex耗时10.3小时，遥操作耗时49.4小时，吞吐量提升4.8倍。从AutoDex验证数据库中检索的抓取成功率为76%，而纯仿真验证仅为34%。

Conclusion: 自动化真实世界数据采集系统可以高效、规模化地获取物理验证的灵巧抓取数据，显著优于遥操作和纯仿真方法，为下游抓取策略提供了可靠的数据基础。

Abstract: Learning robust dexterous grasping requires real-world data that records the physical outcomes of grasp attempts. Such data is hard to obtain at scale: teleoperation yields valid physical outcomes but is slow and operator-biased, while simulation-based generation is cheap and scalable but cannot certify contact validity. A natural solution is to generate candidate grasps and verify them on real hardware, but this scales only if the entire collection loop (perception, execution, labeling, and reset) runs without human intervention. We present AutoDex, an automated real-world data-collection system that closes this loop: for each candidate from a replaceable generator, it localizes the object under severe hand-object occlusion with dense 20-camera perception, executes collision-monitored robot motions, labels lift-and-hold success or failure, and actively resets the object between trials to expose additional candidates across stable poses. The result is a reusable database of physically labeled grasp trials that downstream systems can query by retrieval and feasibility filtering. Using AutoDex, we collect 3,593 grasp trials across Allegro and Inspire hands on 100 diverse objects, with synchronized multi-view observations and robot-state logs. For a matched 500-trajectory collection, AutoDex requires 10.3 h versus 49.4 h for teleoperation, yielding a 4.8x throughput improvement, and grasps retrieved from the AutoDex-validated database succeed 76% versus 34% for simulation-only validation. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>

### [333] [Charged kaon and proton multiplicities in semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering with 11 GeV electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20745)
*P. Bosted,W. Armstrong,H. Bhatt,D. Dutta,R. Ent,D. Gaskell,S. Jia,E. Kinney,H. Mkrtchyan,S. Ali,R. Ambrose,D. Androic,C. Ayerbe Gayoso,A. Bandari,V. Berdnikov,D. Bhetuwal,D. Biswas,M. Boer,E. Brash,A. Camsonne,M. Cardona,J. P. Chen,J. Chen,M. Chen,E. M. Christy,S. Covrig,S. Danagoulian,M. Diefenthaler,B. Duran,C. Elliot,H. Fenker,E. Fuchey,J. O. Hansen,F. Hauenstein,T. Horn,G. M. Huber,M. K. Jones,M. L. Kabir,A. Karki,B. Karki,S. J. D. Kay,C. Keppel,V. Kumar,N. Lashley-Colthirst,W. B. Li,D. Mack,S. Malace,P. Markowitz,M. McCaughan,E. McClellan,D. Meekins,R. Michaels,A. Mkrtchyan,C. Morean,G. Niculescu,I. Niculescu,B. Pandey,S. Park,E. Pooser,B. Sawatzky,G. R. Smith,H. Szumila-Vance,A. S. Tadepalli,V. Tadevosyan,R. Trotta,H. Voskanyan,S. A. Wood,Z. Ye,C. Yerom,X. Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了在Jefferson Lab Hall C使用10.2和10.6 GeV电子束轰击液氢和液氘靶测得的带电Kaon和质子的SIDIS多重性数据，分析了其运动学依赖性和方位角调制，并与理论预测和蒙特卡洛模拟进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过测量带电Kaon和质子的SIDIS多重性及其方位角调制，研究强子化过程中的横向动量依赖因子化（TMD）适用性，检验现有碎裂函数（如DSS）的预测能力，并探究软中心区域的其他物理过程贡献。

Method: 在Jefferson Lab Hall C使用HMS和SHMS谱仪，采集10.2和10.6 GeV电子束轰击10 cm长液氢和液氘靶的数据。在运动学区间$(0.3<x<0.6, 3<Q^2<6 \text{ GeV}^2, 0.3<z<0.7, P_t<0.6 \text{ GeV})$对每个$(x, Q^2, z, P_t)$ bin将多重性拟合成$M_0[1+A\cos(\phi^*)+B\cos(2\phi^*)]$形式。

Result: Kaon的$M_0$与$\pi$的比值：$K^+$与DSS碎裂函数预测合理一致，$K^-$则大多远低于预测；Kaon的方位角调制$A$和$B$与零一致。质子的$M_0$与$\pi$的比值在最低$W^2$处比TMD预测高一个数量级以上，在最高$W^2$处降至约两倍；质子与氘靶结果无显著差异；质子$A$值一致为正（均值约0.01），$B$与零一致。

Conclusion: Kaon数据支持在更高$W^2$区域应用TMD因子化，而质子数据则凸显软中心区域非TMD过程的重要贡献；实验结果与Lund蒙特卡洛预测趋势一致，表明现有碎裂函数对$K^-$的描述需要改进。

Abstract: Measurements of SIDIS multiplicities for charged kaons and protons from proton and deuteron targets are reported on a grid of hadron kinematic variables $0.3<z<0.7$ and $P_{t}<0.6$ GeV for leptonic variables $0.3<x<0.6$ and $3<Q^2<6$ GeV$^2$. Data were acquired in 2018-2019 at Jefferson Lab Hall C with 10.2 and 10.6~GeV electron beams impinging on 10-cm-long liquid hydrogen and deuterium targets. Electrons (hadrons) were detected in the HMS (SHMS) spectrometers. Multiplicities were fitted for each bin in $(x,~Q^2,~z,~P_{t})$ as $M_0[1+A\cos(φ^*)+B\cos(2φ^*)]$. The kaon kinematic range spans the regions where transverse-momentum-dependent factorization can be applied in SIDIS, and a `soft' central region where other processes are of critical importance. The kaon to pion ratios of $M_0$ are in reasonable agreement with predictions using the DSS fragmentation functions for $K^+$, but are mostly well below them for $K^-$. The kaon azimuthal modulations are consistent with zero. The kinematic range for protons is centered on the `soft' central region. The proton-to-pion multiplicity ratios are more than an order-of-magnitude larger than TMD predictions at the lowest value of $W^2$, decreasing to as little as a factor of two at the highest value of $W^2$. No significant difference is observed between proton and deuteron targets. These trends are consistent with Lund Monte Carlo predictions. The proton values of $A$ are consistently positive, with an average value of approximately 0.01, while $B$ is consistent with zero.

</details>

S. Vismaya,L. Vitale,V. Vobbilisetti,R. Volpe,M. Wakai,S. Wallner,M. -Z. Wang,A. Warburton,M. Watanabe,S. Watanuki,C. Wessel,X. P. Xu,B. D. Yabsley,S. Yamada,W. Yan,W. P. Yan,J. Yelton,K. Yi,J. H. Yin,K. Yoshihara,C. Z. Yuan,J. Yuan,L. Yuan,Y. Yusa,L. Zani,F. Zeng,M. Zeyrek,B. Zhang,X. Zhao,V. Zhilich,J. S. Zhou,Q. D. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,L. Zhu,R. Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次在B⁰→π⁰π⁰衰变中测量到混合诱导的CP破坏，利用Belle II实验的1.9亿个Υ(4S)→B⁰B¯⁰事件和一种利用量子纠缠的新方法，在无需重建B⁰→π⁰π⁰衰变位置的情况下实现了时间依赖分析。测得混合诱导CP破坏系数S_{π⁰π⁰}=0.61_{-0.79}^{+0.75}(stat)±0.11(syst)，其精度需传统分析中二十倍的数据量；直接CP破坏系数C_{π⁰π⁰}=0.05±0.28(stat)±0.07(syst)。这些结果显著改进了从B→ππ衰变对夸克混合矩阵CP破坏相位φ₂的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CP破坏测量是检验粒子物理标准模型并寻找新物理的重要探针。B⁰→π⁰π⁰衰变中的混合诱导CP破坏此前从未被测量过，且该通道由于中性π介子重建困难，传统分析方法需要大量数据。作者旨在利用量子纠缠的新方法，以远少于传统分析所需的数据量实现首次测量。

Method: 使用Belle II实验采集的1.9亿个Υ(4S)→B⁰B¯⁰事件，提出一种利用B⁰B¯⁰对量子纠缠的新方法，无需重建B⁰→π⁰π⁰衰变位置即可进行衰变时间依赖的分析，从而测量混合诱导CP破坏系数S和直接CP破坏系数C。

Result: 测得S_{π⁰π⁰}=0.61_{-0.79}^{+0.75}(stat)±0.11(syst)，精度相当于传统分析需二十倍数据量；C_{π⁰π⁰}=0.05±0.28(stat)±0.07(syst)。

Conclusion: 首次实现了B⁰→π⁰π⁰衰变中混合诱导CP破坏的测量，新方法大幅提升了数据利用率，显著改进了对夸克混合矩阵CP破坏相位φ₂的约束，展示了量子纠缠技术在粒子物理中的有效应用。

Abstract: Measurements of charge-parity (CP) violation in quark transitions provide stringent tests of the current theory and sensitive probes of particles or interactions beyond it. We report the first measurement of mixing-induced CP violation in $B^0\toπ^0π^0$ decays. The measurement uses 190 million $Υ(4S)\to B^{0}\overline{B}{}^0$ events collected with the Belle II experiment at the SuperKEKB asymmetric-energy electron-positron collider. A new approach that exploits the quantum entanglement of the $B^{0}\overline{B}{}^0$ pair enables a decay-time-dependent analysis without reconstructing the $B^0 \to π^0π^0$ decay position. We measure the mixing-induced CP-violating coefficient to be $S_{π^{0}π^{0}} = 0.61^{+0.75}_{-0.79}\,(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.11\,(\mathrm{syst})$, attaining a precision that would require a data set twenty times as large with a conventional analysis. We also measure the direct CP-violating coefficient to be $C_{π^{0}π^{0}} = 0.05 \pm 0.28\,(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.07\,(\mathrm{syst})$. These results substantially improve the constraints from $B \to ππ$ decays on the CP-violating phase $φ_2$ of the quark-mixing matrix using far less data than had previously been assumed necessary.

</details>


### [335] [Analysis note: Long-range near-side correlation in $e^+e^-$ with $W$-boson-pair events at 183-209 GeV with ALEPH archived data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21094)
*Tzu-An Sheng,Yu-Chen Chen,Yi Chen,Kyle Sun,Jason Chiu,Michael Peters,Marcello Maggi,Austin Baty,Anthony Badea,Chris McGinn,Jesse Thaler,Gian Michele Innocenti,Jingyu Zhang,Paoti Chang,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ALEPH探测器在LEP2能量区间（√s=183-209 GeV）采集的e⁺e⁻碰撞数据，测量了两粒子角关联，重点关注W⁺W⁻过程的贡献。研究发现，当带电粒子多重数高于50时，出现了类似脊(ridge)的调制结构，偏离蒙特卡洛预测；当多重数从30开始，符号化的v₂类谐波系数由负变正，同样偏离基线。这有助于区分高多重数下长程关联的起源是来自W⁺W⁻产生的复杂色弦结构，还是仅由更高碰撞能量导致的多重数增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在e⁺e⁻碰撞中，高多重数事件的两粒子角关联此前被观测到与MC预测存在偏差，暗示即使在最简单的碰撞系统中也可能出现长程近侧关联。然而，这种偏差的物理起源尚不明确：是源于W⁺W⁻过程中更复杂的色弦结构，还是单纯由于更高碰撞能量带来的更高末态多重数。本文旨在通过增强W⁺W⁻过程的贡献来区分这两种竞争性解释。

Method: 使用ALEPH探测器在LEP2计划中采集的e⁺e⁻碰撞数据（√s=183-209 GeV），分析两粒子角关联函数。在宽赝快度范围和全方位角上，按带电粒子多重数分档计算关联函数。进一步将关联函数展开为傅里叶级数，提取谐波系数vₙ，与存档的蒙特卡洛样本预测进行比较。

Result: 多重数高于50时，观察到类似脊(ridge)的调制结构，偏离MC预测。多重数从30开始，符号化的v₂类谐波系数由负变为正，同样偏离MC基线。

Conclusion: 高多重数下观测到的长程关联偏离MC预测的现象，可能更倾向于与更高碰撞能量带来的高末态多重数相关，而非特定于W⁺W⁻过程的复杂色弦结构，但仍需进一步研究确认其物理机制。

Abstract: Events characterized by a high multiplicity of charged particles have been a central focus in the study of collective behavior across both large and small collision systems. A previous measurement of two-particle angular correlations in $e^+e^-$ collisions at center-of-mass energies up to $\sqrt{s} = 209$ GeV, using LEP2 data, revealed discrepancies with Monte Carlo (MC) predictions at high multiplicity, suggesting the possible emergence of long-range near-side correlations even in the simplest collision system. Unlike at lower energies, where quark-antiquark production dominates, $W^+W^-$ processes become increasingly important at multiplicities above 30. On the one hand, the observed excess in long-range correlations may reflect the more complex color-string configurations arising from $W^+W^-$ production. On the other hand, it can simply arise from the higher final-state multiplicity made possible by the increased collision energy, independent of the underlying production mechanism.
  To discriminate between these competing interpretations, we present a measurement of two-particle angular correlations in $e^+e^-$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 183-209$ GeV, with a focus on enhancing the contribution from $W^+W^-$ processes. The analysis uses data collected by the ALEPH detector during the LEP2 program. Correlation functions are evaluated across a broad range of pseudorapidities and full azimuth, in bins of charged-particle multiplicity. A ridge-like modulation is seen for multiplicity above 50, deviating from the MC reference. In addition, the correlation functions are further decomposed into a Fourier series, and the resulting harmonic coefficients $v_n$ are compared with predictions from the archived Monte Carlo sample. For multiplicity starting from 30, the signed $v_2$-like proxy goes from negative to positive, also deviating from the MC baseline.

</details>


### [336] [Towards LLM-Powered Automation of a Dark Matter Constraint Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21658)
*Lanqing Yuan,Karthik Ramanathan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的自动化流水线，用于从arXiv论文中提取暗物质约束曲线并生成代码形式的拉取请求，在346篇论文基准测试中实现了90.5%的耦合类型分类准确率和0.33 dex的中位耦合残差，但尚未有AI生成的限制数据被合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质约束存储库是社区关键基础设施，但现有最广泛使用的存储库由志愿者维护，随着新结果加速涌现，这种模式面临可持续性风险。

Method: 构建LLM流水线，监控arXiv、提取论文中的限制曲线、将其集成到代码中、并打开供人工审查的PR。核心包括：将每次提取视为通过共识投票聚合的噪声样本、使用物理代理助手GPD实现物理约定标准化层、以及采用区分提取误差与不可比性的评分方法。

Result: 在346篇论文基准上，耦合类型分类准确率90.5%，中位耦合残差0.33 dex（48%曲线误差在2倍以内），平均质量范围覆盖率76%。困难集中在罕见耦合类型（宏平均残差1.1 dex）。流水线已部署并产生了限制提案，但尚未有任何合并。

Conclusion: 该流水线在主流耦合类型上表现良好，但罕见类型仍是难点；AI生成科学数据的治理本身是一个未解决的问题。

Abstract: Dark matter constraint repositories are critical community infrastructure, giving experimentalists and theorists a shared landscape of existing bounds. Yet the most widely-used repositories are maintained by individual volunteers, creating a sustainability risk as the pace of new results accelerates. We present a large language model (LLM) pipeline that monitors arXiv, extracts limit curves from papers, integrates them as code, and opens pull requests (PRs) for human review. On a 346-paper benchmark whose ground truth is the upstream-curated repository itself, the pipeline classifies the coupling type correctly for 90.5% of papers and reaches a median coupling residual of 0.33 dex (a factor of two for 48% of curves), with 76% mean mass-range coverage. This is driven by treating each extraction as a noisy sample reconciled through consensus voting, a physics convention canonicalization layer built with the agentic physics assistant Get Physics Done (GPD), and a scoring methodology that separates genuine extraction error from non-comparability. The remaining difficulty is concentrated in rare coupling types with idiosyncratic conventions (macro-averaged residual 1.1 dex). The pipeline is deployed and has generated limit proposals; none have merged. Governance of AI-generated scientific data is itself an unsolved problem.

</details>


### [337] [Accelerating Discovery: Model-Agnostic Likelihoods for the Reinterpretation of Particle Physics Results and their Application to the Belle II $B^{+}\to K^{+}ν\barν$ Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22215)
*Lorenz Gärtner*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本论文开发、验证并应用了一种新的重解释方法，通过可分发似然函数实现快速推理替代理论参数，在保持统计严谨性的同时大幅降低计算需求。将该方法应用于Belle II的$B^+ \to K^+ ν\barν$测量，进行了弱有效理论重解释和轻新物理搜索两项分析，并公开发布模型无关似然函数，推动高能物理开放科学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理实验本质上依赖于假设，而理论模型激增导致理论与实验验证之间的速度不匹配。现有重解释方法存在局限：完全重分析需要巨大计算资源和专业知识，简化方法则牺牲准确性。因此需要一种既能保持统计严谨性又能降低计算开销的新方法。

Method: 该方法根据替代假设与零假设的理论预测比率，对重建变量和运动学变量的联合分布进行重新加权。从基于直方图的似然函数出发，为任意理论模型生成更新后的模板，同时保留变量间的相关性和系统不确定性。

Result: 通过示例验证了方法的有效性，并将其应用于Belle II $B^+ \to K^+ ν\barν$测量（与标准模型存在$2.7σ$偏差）。完成了两项分析：弱有效理论重解释以约束Wilson系数，以及通过$B^+ \to K^+ X$二体衰变搜索轻新物理。

Conclusion: 公开发布模型无关似然函数为实验粒子物理开放科学建立了重要先例。该方法通过促进快速理论验证，民主化实验数据访问，提升科研投资回报，并推进了高能物理中FAIR数据原则的实施。

Abstract: Experimental results in high-energy physics are inherently hypothesis-dependent, as collider analyses are designed to test specific theoretical frameworks. The proliferation of theoretical models creates a mismatch between the pace of theory development and experimental validation, motivating the reinterpretation of existing results in terms of alternative hypotheses. Existing strategies face limitations: full reanalysis requires enormous computational resources and experimental expertise, while simplified methods sacrifice accuracy by neglecting kinematic variations and systematic uncertainties.
  This thesis develops, validates, and applies a novel reinterpretation method that preserves statistical rigour while dramatically reducing computational requirements, enabling rapid inference on alternative theoretical parameters through distributable likelihoods.
  The method reweights the joint distribution of reconstruction and kinematic variables according to the ratio of theoretical predictions of alternative to null hypotheses. Starting from a histogram-based likelihood, this produces updated templates for any theoretical model while preserving correlations and systematic uncertainties. The method is validated through examples and applied to the Belle II $B^+ \to K^+ ν\barν$ measurement ($2.7σ$ tension with the Standard Model). Two analyses are presented: a Weak Effective Theory reinterpretation constraining Wilson coefficients, and a light new physics search via ${B^+ \to K^+ X}$ two-body decays.
  The public release of the resulting model-agnostic likelihoods establishes an important precedent for open science in experimental particle physics. By facilitating rapid theoretical validation, this approach democratizes experimental data access, enhances scientific return on investments, and advances FAIR Data principles in high-energy physics.

</details>

X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约100.87亿J/ψ事例，通过J/ψ→γη'、η'→π⁺π⁻η过程寻找带电轻子味 violating 衰变η→e^±μ^∓，未发现信号，在90%置信水平上设定分支比上限为6.8×10⁻⁷，比此前最好结果提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准模型中，带电轻子味 violating (cLFV) 过程被强烈抑制，因此任何实验观测都将是新物理的确凿证据。η→e^±μ^∓作为cLFV衰变过程，其搜索有助于检验轻子味守恒定律并寻找超出标准模型的新物理。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在质心能量√s=3.097 GeV处采集的(10.087±0.044)×10⁹ J/ψ事例，通过J/ψ→γη'伴随η'→π⁺π⁻η的级联衰变过程，对η→e^±μ^∓进行搜索。采用统计分析方法，在未观测到显著信号的情况下，基于90%置信水平设定分支比上限。

Result: 未观测到η→e^±μ^∓信号，在90%置信水平上给出分支比上限为B(η→e^±μ^∓)<6.8×10⁻⁷，相较于此前世界最好结果提升了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该结果显著提高了对η介子带电轻子味 violating衰变的敏感度，为轻子味守恒定律提供了更严格的实验检验，对超出标准模型的新物理空间进行了进一步压缩。

Abstract: Based on $(10.087 \pm 0.044) \times 10^{9}~J/ψ$ events collected at the center-of-mass energy $\sqrt{s} = 3.097$~GeV with the BESIII detector, we search for the charged lepton flavor violating decay $η\to e^{\pm}μ^{\mp}$ through the process $J/ψ\to γη'$ with $η' \to π^{+} π^{-} η$. No signal is observed, and an upper limit on the branching fraction is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(η\to e^{\pm}μ^{\mp}) < 6.8 \times 10^{-7}$ at the 90\% confidence level. This result improves the previous best limit by one order of magnitude.

</details>


### [339] [Searches for resonant and nonresonant multi-Higgs production in ATLAS and CMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22461)
*Emmanouil Vourliotis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了ATLAS和CMS实验在LHC上关于多希格斯粒子产生（包括谐振和非谐振模式）的最新搜索结果，重点涉及希格斯玻色子自耦合测量及其对标准模型外物理的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量希格斯玻色子自耦合是LHC计划的关键物理目标之一，可直接探测希格斯势的形状，并对超出标准模型的物理（BSM）提供敏感度。双希格斯产生探测三线性自耦合，三希格斯产生则敏感于四线性耦合，此外重标量衰变至希格斯对的研究也受BSM模型驱动。

Method: 利用ATLAS和CMS实验在LHC上收集的数据，对多希格斯产生过程进行搜索，涵盖谐振产生（如重标量衰变至希格斯对或希格斯玻色子与新标量）和非谐振产生（如双希格斯和三希格斯产生）模式，涉及多种未态衰变通道。

Result: 展示了ATLAS和CMS实验关于多希格斯产生的最新搜索结果，包括谐振和非谐振两种产生模式。

Conclusion: ATLAS和CMS的最新多希格斯产生搜索结果为希格斯势形状和BSM物理提供了直接探测手段，当前结果已对希格斯自耦合和重标量参数空间给出了限制。

Abstract: The measurement of the Higgs boson self-coupling is one of the key physics goals of the LHC program, providing direct access to the shape of the Higgs potential and offering sensitivity to physics beyond the Standard Model (BSM). Higgs pair production probes the trilinear self-coupling, while triple Higgs production is sensitive to the quartic coupling. In addition, resonant searches for heavy scalars decaying to pairs of Higgs bosons or to a Higgs boson and a new scalar are motivated by a broad class of BSM models. Recent results on multi-Higgs production searches from the ATLAS and CMS experiments at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) are presented, covering both resonant and nonresonant production modes across a variety of final states.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上收集的26 fb⁻¹数据，在3.510至4.946 GeV范围内47个质心能量点测量了e⁺e⁻ → p\bar{p}反应的Born截面，以此寻找衰变到p\bar{p}终态的粲偶素（类粲偶素）态，并分析了质子角分布以提取质子电磁形状因子。以6.6σ显著性观测到ψ(3770) → p\bar{p}衰变，并在4.2 GeV附近观测到一个结构（对ψ(4160)或Y(4230)假设的显著性分别为4.6σ或4.8σ，目前无法区分）。此外，在大时间类光动量转移下更精确地提取了形状因子比值|G_E/G_M|和磁形状因子|G_M|。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找衰变到p\bar{p}终态的粲偶素（类粲偶素）态，并精确测量质子电磁形状因子，以深入理解粲偶素态在开粧区域的衰变机制以及质子的内部结构。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上收集的47个质心能量点（3.510–4.946 GeV，积分亮度26 fb⁻¹）的e⁺e⁻碰撞数据，通过测量e⁺e⁻→p\bar{p}反应的Born截面寻找粲偶素态，并通过分析质子极角分布提取电磁形状因子比值|G_E/G_M|和磁形状因子|G_M|。

Result: 以6.6σ显著性观测到ψ(3770)→p\bar{p}衰变；在4.2 GeV附近观测到结构，对ψ(4160)和Y(4230)假设的显著性分别为4.6σ和4.8σ（目前无法区分）；在大时间类光动量转移下更精确地提取了|G_E/G_M|和|G_M|。

Conclusion: 这些结果为理解开粧区域粲偶素（类粲偶素）态的衰变机制以及质子的内部结构提供了重要的实验见解。

Abstract: We search for possible charmonium(-like) states decaying into the $p\bar{p}$ final state by studying the Born cross sections of the $e^+e^-\to p\bar{p}$ reaction, and we determine the proton electromagnetic form factors by analyzing the proton angular distribution. The measurement is performed using a sample of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected at 47 center-of-mass energies from 3.510 to 4.946 GeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 26 fb$^{-1}$, recorded by the BESIII detector collected at the BEPCII collider. The decay $ψ(3770)\to p\bar{p}$ is observed with a significance of 6.6$σ$ including systematic uncertainties. Furthermore, a structure near 4.2 GeV is observed with significances of $4.6σ$ or $4.8σ$ for the $ψ(4160)$ or $Y(4230)$ hypotheses including systematic uncertainties, respectively; these interpretations cannot presently be distinguished. In addition, the moduli of the form factor ratios $|G_{E}/G_{M}|$ and of the magnetic form factors $|G_{M}|$ are extracted by analyzing the proton polar angle distribution with higher precision at large time-like squared momentum transfer. These results provide important experimental insights into both the decay mechanisms of charmonium(-like) states in the open charm region and the internal structure of proton.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的26 fb⁻¹积分亮度数据，利用能量扫描技术测量了47个质心能量点（3.510-4.946 GeV）的e⁺e⁻→p\bar{p}反应Born截面和有效形状因子。首次在高精度下确定了大时间型动量转移区域的电磁形状因子比值|GE/GM|和磁形状因子|GM|的模量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究粲偶素（类粲偶素）态在开粲阈值以上的性质，以及质子电磁形状因子的底层动力学机制。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集数据，采用能量扫描技术，在47个质心能量点（3.510-4.946 GeV）进行测量。通过分析质子极角分布来提取形状因子比值和磁形状因子模量。

Result: 首次在大时间型动量转移区域高精度确定了|GE/GM|和|GM|的模量。

Conclusion: 这些结果为理解开粲阈值以上的粲偶素（类粲偶素）态性质以及质子电磁形状因子的动力学提供了重要见解。

Abstract: We report a measurement of the Born cross section and the effective form factor for the $e^+e^-\to p\bar{p}$ reaction at 47 center-of-mass energies between 3.510 and 4.946 GeV. The measurement is performed using the energy-scan technique and is based on data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 26 fb\(^{-1}\) collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider. For the first time, the moduli of the electromagnetic form factor ratio $|G_{E}/G_{M}|$ and of the magnetic form factor $|G_{M}|$ are determined with high precision by analyzing the distribution of the polar angle of the proton at a large timelike momentum transfer.
  These results provide essential insights into the nature of charmonium(-like) states above the open-charm threshold and the dynamics underlying the proton electromagnetic form factors.

</details>


### [342] [Ferromagnetic broadband sensing of axionlike dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22814)
*Chenhao Peng,Dmitry Budker,Yuanning Gao,Xinran Li,Jia Liu,Wei Ji,Jing Shu,Zhenxing Tang,Liheng Wang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于双共振模式的悬浮磁体磁力计，利用硬磁阵列和软铁磁屏蔽构建混合铁磁平台，在40-3000 Hz频率范围内搜索类轴子暗物质，在276 Hz附近达到最佳灵敏度0.7 fT，对应的光子耦合上限g_{aγ}~10^{-7} GeV^{-1}，比此前直接探测限改进了四个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 悬浮粒子系统具有极低耗散和对磁场、加速度的超高灵敏度，在基础物理研究（特别是轴子和类轴子粒子探测）方面具有巨大潜力。高灵敏度和宽带宽是此类探测的关键需求，现有方法在带宽上存在局限。

Method: 采用 engineered double-resonance mode（工程化双共振模式）实现悬浮磁体磁力计，有效线宽比先前方法宽约三个量级；结合硬磁阵列增强轴子诱导信号，以及软铁磁屏蔽抑制环境磁噪声，构成混合铁磁平台进行暗物质搜索。

Result: 在40-3000 Hz频率范围内对类轴子暗物质的光子耦合g_{aγ}进行搜索，建立了新的直接探测上限；最佳灵敏度在276 Hz附近，磁力计达到0.7 fT磁场分辨率，对应g_{aγ}~10^{-7} GeV^{-1}，比此前直接上限改进超过四个数量级。

Conclusion: 该高带宽悬浮传感器不仅在类轴子暗物质搜索中取得重要突破，还可广泛应用于生物传感和精密测量等领域。

Abstract: Levitated particles have demonstrated ultrahigh sensitivity to magnetic fields and accelerations owing to their extremely low dissipation. Such systems have strong potential for fundamental physics research, particularly for the detection of axions and axionlike particles, well-motivated dark matter candidates spanning a broad mass range. In this context, both high sensitivity and large bandwidth are essential. Here, we demonstrate a levitated magnet magnetometer based on an engineered double-resonance mode, achieving an effective linewidth at its optimal sensitivity that is approximately three orders of magnitude broader than those of previous approaches. Together with a hard-magnet array that enhances the axion-induced signal and soft-ferromagnetic shielding that suppresses environmental magnetic noise, this system constitutes a hybrid ferromagnetic platform for axionlike dark matter searches. We search for axionlike dark matter through its photon coupling $g_{aγ}$ over the $40$-$3000\,\mathrm{Hz}$ frequency range and establish new direct limits in this frequency band. The best sensitivity is achieved near the upper resonance around $276\,\mathrm{Hz}$, where the magnetometer reaches a magnetic-field resolution of $0.7\,\mathrm{fT}$, corresponding to a limit of $g_{aγ}\sim10^{-7}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. At this frequency, this result improves upon previous direct limits by more than four orders of magnitude. The demonstrated high-bandwidth levitated sensor may also enable a broad range of applications, including biological sensing and precision measurements.

</details>


### [343] [Observations of Low-Energy-Electron Production and Experimental Characterization of the Test-Mass Charging Process in the LISA Gravitational Reference Sensor with the BART Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22979)
*Francesco Dimiccoli,Francesco Venturelli,Valerio Ferroni,Antonella Cavalleri,Teodoro Klaser,Matteo Tomasi,Davide Dal Bosco,Enrico Verroi,Rita Dolesi William J. Weber*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LISA任务中测试质量充电噪声受低能二次电子发射影响，本文通过粒子加速器实验直接验证了这一假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA Pathfinder观测到的充电噪声显著高于预发射模拟预测，表明已有模型中遗漏了关键物理过程，需要识别并验证缺失的机制以提高未来引力波探测的噪声建模精度。

Method: 采用粒子加速器实验，在类LISA引力参考传感器几何结构中，测量质子诱导的测试质量充电作为测试质量静电势的函数，直接检验低能二次电子（LEE）发射假说。

Result: 实验验证了低能二次电子发射是影响LISA测试质量充电噪声的关键过程，充电率与测试质量静电势存在依赖关系。

Conclusion: 低能二次电子发射是导致LISA Pathfinder充电噪声高于预期的核心机制，未来LISA任务必须将此过程纳入充电噪声模型以确保引力波探测的精度。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is a space-based gravitational-wave observatory that uses free-falling test masses as inertial references to detect milliHertz frequency signals. Interactions between test masses and galactic or solar energetic particles cause charge buildup and Coulomb forces, source of acceleration noise that must be accurately modeled. LISA Pathfinder measurements showed that the Poissonian test mass charging noise was considerably larger than that in pre launch simulations, indicating missing physical processes in early models. Emission of low energy secondary electrons (LEE) from test mass and housing surfaces has been proposed as a key mechanism affecting charging and the sensor electrostatic response. We report a particle accelerator based experiment that directly tests the LEE hypothesis by measuring proton induced test mass charging in a LISA like Gravitational Reference Sensor geometry as a function of the test mass electrostatic potential.

</details>


### [344] [Recent Hadron Spectroscopy Results from the Belle and Belle II experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23170)
*Elisabetta Prencipe*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文总结了Belle~II实验在强子谱学方面的最新进展，包括底偶素、粲偶素物理、五夸克态搜索以及重子谱学研究，并展示了结合Belle和Belle~II联合数据样本（超过1.5 ab⁻¹）在精确测量方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Belle~II实验积累的高统计量数据（积分亮度超过BaBar和Belle），结合Belle与Belle~II的联合数据样本，在强子谱学领域实现前所未有的精确度，探索奇特强子态（如Υ(10753)结构、隐粲/隐奇异五夸克态）及激发态重子（Ω(2012)、Λ_c激发态）的性质。

Method: 基于SuperKEKB非对称能量正负电子对撞机，Belle~II探测器采集数据；采用Belle与Belle~II联合数据分析方法，利用超过1.5 ab⁻¹的综合数据样本进行强子谱学研究。

Result: Υ(10753)结构研究取得新进展；开展了隐粲和隐奇异五夸克态的搜索；获得了Ω(2012)和激发态Λ_c的重子谱学最新结果。

Conclusion: Belle~II实验已展现出利用联合数据样本实现高精度强子谱学分析的强大潜力，未来有望借助完整数据样本在奇特强子和重子谱学领域取得更多突破。

Abstract: The Belle~II experiment, the major upgrade of the Belle detector, is currently collecting data at the SuperKEKB asymmetric-energy $e^+e^-$ collider in Tsukuba, Japan. With an integrated luminosity of 575\,fb$^{-1}$, Belle~II has already surpassed the maximum luminosity accumulated by both BaBar and Belle. Recent analyses combining Belle and Belle~II data sets have delivered results of unprecedented precision, demonstrating the strong potential of the unified data sample, which now exceeds 1.5\,ab$^{-1}$.
  In this contribution, recent advances in hadron spectroscopy are presented. Highlights include new results in bottomonium and charmonium physics, with particular emphasis on the structure of the $Υ(10753)$, searches for hidden-charm and hidden-strangeness pentaquarks, and the latest developments in baryon spectroscopy, including studies of the $Ω(2012)$ and excited $Λ_c$ states. Prospects for future spectroscopy analyses exploiting the full Belle and Belle~II data sets are also discussed.

</details>

Wei,A. J. White,S. Wolbers,T. Wongjirad,K. Wresilo,W. Wu,E. Yandel,T. Yang,L. E. Yates,H. W. Yu,G. P. Zeller,J. Zennamo,S. Zhai,C. Zhang,Y. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用MicroBooNE探测器首次测量了氩气上缪子中微子带电电流共振相互作用，通过横向运动学不平衡变量对末态相互作用模型进行约束，发现现有模型无法同时再现所有观测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子-原子核截面测量对改进相互作用模型以及支持DUNE、Hyper-Kamiokande等新一代精密振荡实验至关重要。重子共振中微子相互作用在DUNE能谱峰值附近占主导，但目前相关测量匮乏，特别是氩靶上的共振过程尚未被实验约束。

Method: 使用MicroBooNE探测器收集数据，选取缪子中微子带电电流共振类相互作用事件，通过重建缪子、领头质子和中性π子的动量，构建横向运动学不平衡变量来进行分析。

Result: 首次实现了氩靶上中微子共振类相互作用的测量，对π⁰-质子末态进行了全面表征。关键结论是：目前考虑的各类模型均无法同时复现所有测量观测量。

Conclusion: 现有中微子-原子核相互作用模型在描述共振过程中的末态相互作用方面存在系统性不足，急需进一步改进以支持未来精密中微子振荡实验的需求。

Abstract: Neutrino-nucleus cross-section measurements are needed to improve interaction modeling and to enable precision neutrino oscillation measurements in upcoming experiments such as the Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE), Hyper-Kamiokande, and the Short-Baseline Neutrino program. Baryon-resonance neutrino interactions constitute a dominant contribution near the peak of the DUNE neutrino energy spectrum. We present the first measurement of muon neutrino charged-current resonance-like interactions on argon using transverse kinematic imbalance variables with the MicroBooNE detector. These observables are highly sensitive to the modeling of final-state interactions. This measurement probes kinematic imbalances using the reconstructed momenta of the muon, leading proton, and neutral pion. A comprehensive characterization of the $π^0$-proton final state is presented; however, none of the models considered are able to simultaneously reproduce all measured observables.

</details>

Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用LHCb实验收集的9 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，在双缪子不变质量平方(q²)区间内对B±→π±μ+μ-衰变的分支比和CP不对称性进行了微分测量，并报告了B±→π±μ+μ-与B±→K±μ+μ-分支比的比值。测量结果与标准模型预测一致，一致性在1.4σ到3.8σ之间，取决于模型假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过测量B±→π±μ+μ-衰变的分支比和CP不对称性，检验标准模型预测，寻找可能的超出标准模型的新物理信号。

Method: 基于LHCb实验在7 TeV、8 TeV和13 TeV质心能量下收集的9 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，在双缪子质量平方(q²)区间内对B±→π±μ+μ-衰变进行微分分支比和CP不对称性测量，并与B±→K±μ+μ-衰变的分支比进行比较。

Result: 测得的B±→π±μ+μ-分支比与标准模型预测兼容，但一致性程度取决于模型假设，在1.4σ到3.8σ之间。

Conclusion: 当前测量结果与标准模型预测一致，但存在一定张力，需要进一步数据以确认或排除新物理信号。

Abstract: Differential measurements of the branching fraction and $C\!P$ asymmetry of the decay $B^\pm\to π^\pmμ^+μ^-$ are performed in intervals of the dimuon mass squared, $q^2$, using proton-proton collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 9 fb$^{-1}$ recorded by the LHCb experiment at centre-of-mass energies of 7 TeV, 8 TeV and 13 TeV. The ratios of the branching fractions of $B^\pm\to π^\pmμ^+μ^-$ and $B^\pm\to K^\pmμ^+μ^-$ decays are also reported in the same $q^2$ intervals. The measured branching fractions are compatible with the predictions of the Standard Model, with consistency varying between 1.4$σ$ and 3.8$σ$ depending on the model assumptions.

</details>
