### [347] [Partition function zeros of quantum many-body systems: perturbative results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20808)
*Muhammad Sedik,B Sriram Shastry*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于微扰理论，提出了一种系统性的方法来寻找量子多体系统的杨-李配分函数零点，并将该方法应用于一维Hubbard模型，与精确对角化结果进行了比较，同时提出了实验探测这些零点的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了系统性地找到量子多体系统中杨-李配分函数零点，并探索其在有限温度下的可计算性及实验可探测性。

Method: 基于arXiv:2504.01880中引入的有限温度微扰理论方法，从热电子格林函数中提取依赖于波矢和温度的复数虚能量，收集所有$\vec{k}$下的虚能量得到杨-李零点。将该方法应用于一维Hubbard模型（不同边界条件），在微扰论二阶下与精确对角化结果对比。提出一个可用于实验探测Hubbard环零点的物理量，并用8位点Hubbard环的精确对角化作为示例。

Result: 在微扰论二阶下，该方法得到的一维Hubbard模型杨-李零点与精确对角化结果吻合较好；提出了可用于实验探测Hubbard环零点的物理量，并通过8位点Hubbard环的精确对角化验证了探测方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该微扰方法为寻找量子多体系统杨-李零点提供了系统性的途径，与精确对角化结果一致，且提出的实验探测方案有望在Hubbard环等体系中实现。

Abstract: A systematic method to find the Yang-Lee partition function zeros of quantum many-body systems based on perturbation theory at finite temperatures was recently introduced in arXiv:2504.01880. This method identifies wave-vector and temperature-dependent complex virtual energies obtainable from the thermal electronic Greens function. The collection of virtual energies over all $\vec{k}$ yield the Yang-Lee zeroes. We apply this approach to the one-dimensional Hubbard model for different boundary conditions. We compare the results obtained by this method up to second-order in perturbation theory with the results found by exact diagonalization. We also propose a quantity that could be used for experimental detection of these zeros of a Hubbard ring. An example of the detection method is presented using exact diagonalization of the 8-site Hubbard ring.

</details>


### [348] [Displacement-Field-Driven Semimetal-Superconductor Transition in Magic-Angle Twisted Trilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20849)
*Bokai Liang,Jing-Yu Zhao,Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了魔角三层石墨烯（MATTG）中位移场调控的相关现象，揭示了位移场的主要效应是诱导狄拉克锥能量位移和自掺杂，并预言了从半金属到超导态的转变，为电场可调超导、Mott物理和重费米子行为提供了统一图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究魔角三层石墨烯（MATTG）中位移场调控的关联现象的物理机制，特别是解决传统重费米子模型（Kondo晶格模型）无法直接应用的困境，理解位移场为何能调控超导、Mott绝缘体等丰富相图。

Method: 采用奴隶粒子理论（slave-particle theory），在填充数ν=±2下进行具体计算，分析位移场D对能带结构和相互作用的影响。

Result: 发现位移场的主要效应是诱导狄拉克锥能量位移和自掺杂进入TBG扇区；增大D驱动从半金属到超导态的转变；讨论了D对ν=±2附近超导的增强作用以及相图的粒子-空穴不对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作为MATTG中电场可调的超导、Mottness和重费米子行为提供了一个统一的理论图景。

Abstract: Magic-angle twisted trilayer graphene(MATTG) hosts versatile displacement-field-tuned correlated phenomena. MATTG consists of a dispersive Dirac cone which hybridizes with the flat band from a twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) sector. The hybridization strength increases with the displacement field $D$ and naively one may expect D-driven heavy fermion physics. However, the TBG Hubbard bands have a momentum-selective Mott gap, which is small at the $Γ$ point due to the band topology, and a rigid local moment description as in the familiar Kondo lattice model is invalid. Here we show that the dominant effect of the displacement field is to induce an energy shift of the Dirac cone and self-doping into the TBG sector. We illustrate this picture in a concrete calculation using a slave-particle theory at the filling $ν=\pm 2$. We find that increasing $D$ drives a transition from a semimetal into a superconducting state. We also discuss the enhancement of the superconductivity by $D$ near $ν=\pm2$ and the particle-hole asymmetry of the phase diagram. Our results provide a unified picture for electric-field-tunable superconductivity, Mottness, and heavy-fermion-like behavior in MATTG.

</details>


### [349] [Importance of effective Coulomb interactions for $T_c$ in cuprates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21323)
*Jakša Vučičević,Upendra Kumar,Chia-Nan Yeh,Miguel A. Morales,Malte Rösner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模系统研究，收集约40种铜氧化物超导体的Tc和晶体结构数据，利用DFT能带计算和MLWF/cRPA方法下涌到单带Hubbard模型和三带Emery模型，并结合统计分析，发现Tc与模型参数（特别是单带模型中的t'和三带模型中氧p轨道库仑耦合）存在显著关联，表明氧位点对高温超导的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体在常压下具有最高的超导Tc，但其Tc在不同材料间差异显著，目前不清楚晶体结构中的哪些性质对Tc影响最大，而这一理解是实现高温超导材料设计的基础。

Method: 收集约40种不同铜氧化物化合物的Tc和晶体结构数据；采用全自动流程进行DFT能带计算；基于MLWF和cRPA方法下涌到单带Hubbard模型和三带Emery模型；应用全面无偏的统计分析研究实验Tc与计算模型参数之间的相关性。

Result: 数据表明可能需要更复杂的模型来描述所有铜氧化物，但Tc与模型参数的简单函数关系良好；验证了单带模型中t'与实验Tc的相关性，发现Tc在t'低于某个有限值时消失；在整个数据集中，Tc与三带模型中氧p轨道上的库仑耦合相关性最强，凸显了铜氧平面中氧位点的重要性。

Conclusion: 单带模型中的t'和三带模型中氧p轨道的库仑耦合是影响铜氧化物超导体Tc的关键因素，氧位点在高温超导中扮演重要角色。

Abstract: Cuprate superconductors exhibit the highest observed superconducting $T_c$ at atmospheric pressure. However, the magnitude of $T_c$ varies significantly between different cuprates. At present, it is unclear what properties of the crystal structure affect $T_c$ most strongly, yet such an understanding must underpin any efforts toward high-$T_c$ materials design. To address this issue, we perform a large scale systematic study, employing a combination of data collection, state-of-the-art numerical methods, and statistical analysis. We identify about 40 different cuprate compounds, and we compile detailed data about their $T_c$'s and crystal structures from literature and the available databases. Using a fully automated procedure, for each compound we compute the DFT bandstructure and downfold it to two of the most commonly studied low-energy lattice models, namely the single-band Hubbard and the three-band Emery models. The downfolding is based on the approach of MLWFs and cRPA. Finally, we apply a thorough and unbiased statistical analysis to investigate the correlations between the experimentally measured $T_c$'s and the computed parameters of our theoretical models. Our data indicates that more sophisticated models might be needed to describe all cuprates on the same footing. Nevertheless, we find that $T_c$ scales well with simple functions of model parameters. We confirm a previously observed trend that $t'$ in the single-band model correlates with the experimental $T_c$, and we find that $T_c$ appears to vanish below a finite value of $t'$, in agreement with recent ground-state calculations for the Hubbard model. However, we find that the coupling strength also plays a role: throughout our entire dataset, $T_c$ correlates the most with the Coulomb coupling on the $p$-orbitals in the 3-band model, highlighting the importance of the oxygen sites in the copper-oxide planes.

</details>


### [350] [Localization pattern of a mobile impurity in the disordered Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21499)
*A. V. Sadovnikov,A. N. Rubtsov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了一个移动杂质与无序Kitaev链的耦合，发现杂质在不同拓扑相中的局域化行为存在差异：拓扑相中部分局域化，平庸相中更尖锐地局域到单点；边缘杂质偏好源于Majorana二聚体结构；无序会破坏这种偏好。未发现杂质局域模式与拓扑的直接一一对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究移动杂质能否作为探针区分Kitaev链在不同化学势无序下的拓扑相与非拓扑相。

Method: 小周期性链采用精确对角化（ED），强相互作用开链采用密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）；通过IPR_d、杂质密度分布等量表征局域化行为。

Result: 拓扑相中杂质部分局域化且IPR_d平滑增长，平庸相中杂质近乎单点局域化；开链近Kitaev sweet spot时杂质密度边缘局域，化学势增加则杂质扩散至体态；Majorana二聚体结构解释边缘偏好；无序可抑制边缘偏好而将杂质钉扎在体态。

Conclusion: 杂质对宿主系统相敏感，但局域化模式与拓扑无严格一一对应，仅存在间接关联。

Abstract: We study a mobile impurity coupled to a Kitaev chain with chemical-potential disorder and ask whether the impurity behavior distinguishes different regimes of the host system. Exact diagonalization calculations for small periodic chains shows that in the deep topological regime the impurity localizes only partially, with a smooth increase of $\mathrm{IPR}_d$, whereas in the deep trivial regime it undergoes a much sharp transition to nearly single-site localization. For open chains at strong interaction, DMRG shows edge-localized impurity density near the Kitaev sweet spot. With increasing chemical potential, the impurity weight spreads into the bulk and eventually becomes almost uniform. We explain the edge preference analytically from the Majorana-dimer structure: a bulk impurity rearranges two neighboring dimers, while an edge impurity affects only one. Disorder competes with this clean edge bias and can pin the impurity in the bulk. Thus, the impurity is sensitive to the regime of the host system, although we do not find a strict one-to-one correspondence between the impurity localization pattern and the host topology. Instead, the disorder-averaged behavior suggests only an indirect correlation between impurity localization and the underlying phase of the chain.

</details>


### [351] [Many-body quantum geometric effects and entanglement at the 3D metal-insulator quantum phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21648)
*Jason Y. Yan,Xiaoyu Guo,R. Bhandia,T. F. Rosenbaum,Natalia Drichko,N. P. Armitage*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过磷掺杂硅中三维金属-绝缘体量子相变的光学电导率测量，展示了量子几何长度可作为实验探针。该长度在相变临界点不连续跳变至无穷大，反映了三维体系中紫外主导求和规则对临界涨落不敏感的特性，并为赫兹菲尔德金属化判据提供了量子力学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何作为凝聚态物理中的统一概念，已在非线性拓扑响应、平带超导等理论中发挥作用，但其在无序相互作用体系中的实验探测仍具挑战。本文旨在通过光学电导率的第一个负矩（与量子Fisher信息及多体纠缠相关）来实验探测量子几何，特别是针对三维金属-绝缘体量子相变。

Method: 提取光学电导率第一个负矩作为量子几何长度的实验探针，测量磷掺杂硅从绝缘体到金属的量子相变过程中的光谱响应，分析该长度随掺杂浓度和温度的变化行为。

Result: 量子几何长度在远离相变时接近磷施主的玻尔半径（原子尺度）；接近相变时该长度增强但非连续发散，而是跳跃至无穷大；这种不连续性源于三维体系对临界涨落不敏感的紫外主导求和规则。

Conclusion: 量子几何长度增强反映了施主极化率的"膨胀"效应，修正后的Clausius-Mossotti描述与发散的介电响应更加吻合；该工作为赫兹菲尔德金属化判据提供了量子力学基础，并展示了量子几何在无序相互作用体系中的可实验测量性。

Abstract: Quantum geometry has emerged as a unifying concept across condensed matter physics, underlying phenomena from nonlinear topological response to flat-band superconductivity. While usually formulated within band theory, quantum geometry remains meaningful in disordered interacting systems~\cite{resta1999electron}. Here we show that the first negative moment of the optical conductivity -- proportional to the zero temperature quantum Fisher information as a bound on the multipartite entanglement -- provides an experimental probe of quantum geometry across the three-dimensional metal-insulator quantum phase transition in phosphorus-doped silicon. We extract a quantum geometric length $\ell$ that characterizes the local wavefunctions. Far from the transition, this length is almost coincident with the Bohr radius of the hydrogenic phosphorus donors, reflecting their atomic-scale quantum geometry. Approaching the transition, $\ell$ is enhanced, but does not diverge continuously like a correlation length; it jumps discontinuously to infinity at the critical point. This reflects the UV domination of the sum rule in three dimensions that renders it insensitive to the critical fluctuations driving the diverging dielectric constant and correlation length. Its enhancement demonstrates a ``puffing" of the donor polarizability volume of quantum geometric origin, which yields a quantum geometric corrected Clausius-Mossotti description in closer agreement with the diverging dielectric response and provides a quantum mechanical foundation for the century-old Herzfeld metallization criterion.

</details>


### [352] [Trial wavefunction for fractional quantum spin Hall insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21652)
*Omri Lesser,Chao-Ming Jian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对自旋相反的费米子占据半填充共轭Chern能带时与多分量朗道能级不同的相互作用物理，提出了一个具有Z₄拓扑序的分数量子自旋霍尔态的变分波函数。该波函数基于一对解耦的Moore-Read Pfaffian态及其共轭态中的中性费米子激子凝聚，通过耦合手性和反手性Ising共形场论引入变分质量参数，描述复合费米子的种间s波配对和种内p波配对。蒙特卡洛模拟显示，该Z₄态在参数空间的大片区域中能量上优于解耦的共轭Pfaffian态和替代的激子凝聚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半填充共轭Chern能带中的费米子因不可避的种间碰撞而无法使用Halperin型波函数，与多分量朗道能级不同。此前实验在扭曲MoTe₂中暗示了分数自旋霍尔电导1/2，其对应的最小拓扑序为Z₄拓扑序。因此需要构造一个具体的变分波函数来实现这种Z₄分数量子自旋霍尔相。

Method: 基于一对解耦的Moore-Read Pfaffian态及其共轭态中中性费米子激子凝聚构建变分波函数；耦合手性和反手性Ising共形场论；引入变分质量参数描述复合费米子的种间s波配对和种内p波配对；使用球形几何上的蒙特卡洛采样评估能量；明确计算由于涉及朗道能级混合带来的动能惩罚。

Result: 相图显示所提出的Z₄态在参数空间的大片区域中能量上有利，优于解耦的共轭Pfaffian态和替代的激子凝聚态。该Z₄拓扑序与电荷守恒、S_z守恒、时间反演对称性以及实验暗示的分数自旋霍尔电导1/2兼容。

Conclusion: 为Z₄分数量子自旋霍尔相提供了具体的微观波函数实现，并提出了一条构建此类态家族的路线。

Abstract: Fermions with opposite spins occupying half-filled conjugate Chern bands exhibit interaction physics distinct from their multi-component Landau-level counterparts with the same chirality. This is largely due to unavoidable inter-species collisions that preclude the Halperin-type wavefunctions available in multi-component Landau levels. In this work, we propose and evaluate a variational wavefunction for a fractional quantum spin Hall state with Z_4 topological order in a pair of conjugate Landau levels. This Z_4 topological order has previously been shown to be the minimal topological order compatible with charge conservation, $S_z$ conservation, time-reversal symmetry, and the fractional spin Hall conductance 1/2 suggested by previous twisted MoTe$_2$ experiments. Our construction is based on the condensation of an anyonic exciton formed by the neutral fermionic excitations in a decoupled pair of Moore-Read Pfaffian state and its conjugate. By coupling the chiral and anti-chiral Ising conformal field theories associated with the two spin species, we introduce a variational mass parameter in the Z_4 trial wavefunction that captures the inter-spin-species s-wave pairing of composite fermions alongside the intra-spin-species p-wave pairing. We assess the energetics of this trial state using Monte Carlo sampling on a spherical geometry. Because the coupled state intrinsically involves Landau-level mixing, we explicitly evaluate the resulting kinetic energy penalty. Our phase diagram reveals that the proposed Z_4 state becomes energetically favorable in a sizable region of parameter space, over both the decoupled pair of conjugate Pfaffian states and an alternative exciton condensate state. These results provide a concrete microscopic wavefunction realization of this Z_4 fractional quantum spin Hall phase, and propose a route to constructing additional families of such states.

</details>


### [353] [Quantum oscillations and Dirac dispersion in tunable kagome lattice Lu$_{1-y}$Y$_y$(Nb$_{1-x}$Ta$_x$)$_6$Sn$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21694)
*Keenan Avers,Phineas Sobel,Lochlan Joyce,Jared Dans,Prathum Saraf,Ram Kumar,Shanta Saha,Peter Zavalij,Johnpierre Paglione*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了Lu$_{1-y}$Y$_y$(Nb$_{1-x}$Ta$_x$)$_6$Sn$_6$双合金体系的单晶生长与表征，研究了其电荷密度波演化、电输运行为及相图。通过新型生长技术获得高质量单晶，观察到Y替代对CDW的逐渐抑制以及量子振荡，揭示了费米面减小、有效质量与费米波矢之间的标度关系，暗示狄拉克型色散的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome晶格体系具有平带、范霍夫奇点、线性色散狄拉克/外尔点等对称性保护的能带结构特征，是强关联电子物理的丰富平台。受1-6-6系列化合物中电荷密度波演化的启发，作者希望通过双合金化体系精细调控晶格、基态和电子色散性质，以深入理解Kagome晶格的新奇态和相互作用。

Method: 采用新型晶体生长技术制备了Lu$_{1-y}$Y$_y$(Nb$_{1-x}$Ta$_x$)$_6$Sn$_6$双合金体系的高质量单晶（沿c轴方向延伸）；通过Y替代和Ta替代进行化学调控；测量了磁阻和磁化强度中的量子振荡；分析了费米面演化、有效质量和准粒子色散。

Result: Y替代逐渐抑制了电荷密度波序；在整个合金系列中观察到量子振荡；费米面尺寸随两种替代而减小；有效质量与费米波矢之间存在标度关系，表明体系处于狄拉克型色散区域。

Conclusion: 该双合金体系能够在最小化无序影响的前提下精细调控Kagome晶格的晶格参数、基态和电子色散性质，为进一步理解Kagome晶格的新型量子态和相互作用开辟了道路。

Abstract: Kagome lattice crystal systems present interesting symmetry-protected band structure features such as flat bands, van Hove singularities, and linearly dispersing Dirac/Weyl points that provide a rich playground for strongly correlated electron physics. Motivated by the rich properties and charge density wave evolution through the 1-6-6 series of compounds, we present our results in single crystal growth and characterization of the of Lu$_{1-y}$Y$_y$(Nb$_{1-x}$Ta$_x$)$_6$Sn$_6$ double-alloy system, including evolution of the charge density wave transition, electrical transport behavior and resultant phase diagrams. Using a novel growth technique, the synthesis of high quality crystals with extended length along the crystallographic $c$-axis allows us to follow the gradual suppression of charge density wave (CDW) order with Y substitution, and observe quantum oscillations in both magnetoresistance and magnetization throughout the series. We review the evolution of Fermi surfaces, effective masses and quasiparticle dispersion through the alloy series, revealing a decrease in size of Fermi surfaces that trends with both substitutions, and a scaling between effective mass and Fermi wavevector that suggests a regime with Dirac-like dispersion. The ability to fine-tune crystallographic, ground state and electronic dispersion properties of the \lit\ system with minimal impact of disorder opens a path torward further understanding the nature of the kagome lattice and its novel states and interactions.

</details>


### [354] [Spectroscopic fingerprints of a ferroaxial charge density wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21872)
*Jiangchang Zheng,Zhongyi Zhang,Fazhi Yang,Josh Leeman,Luanjing Li,Zihan Lin,Zijian Fei,Tianhao Guo,Siyu Heng,Xin Liang,Leslie M. Schoop,Junzhang Ma,Hoi Chun Po,Berthold Jäck*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过线偏振角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）和扫描隧道显微镜（STM）对LaTe$_3$中电荷密度波（CDW）相的研究，揭示了具有混合$p_x$-$p_z$轨道特征的轨道间CDW，并证明其包含显著的铁轴分量，打破了所有垂直镜面对称性。研究建立了基于单个原子散射的STM和ARPES协同光谱方法，用于识别和表征隐藏的多分量电子序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规CDW可承载奇异的集体模式和非平凡拓扑，是量子物质研究的新前沿。尽管实验已在稀土三碲化物中发现铁轴CDW的证据，但其底层电子结构和序参量对称性的明确形式仍不清楚，缺乏光谱层面的直接洞察。

Method: 使用线偏振ARPES测量重构费米面上的光谱间隙；利用STM进行准粒子干涉（QPI）映射，并通过选择性沉积原子散射中心增强信号；基于QPI特征在费米面轨道子空间内分析序参量对称性。

Result: ARPES揭示了重构费米面上复杂的光谱间隙分布；STM-QPI直接显示出具有混合$p_x$-$p_z$轨道特征的轨道间CDW；序参量对称性分析表明该CDW具有显著的铁轴分量，打破所有垂直镜面对称性。

Conclusion: 建立了基于单个原子散射的STM和ARPES协同光谱方法来识别和表征隐藏的多分量电子序；证明了LaTe$_3$中存在具有铁轴分量的混合CDW相。

Abstract: Unconventional charge density waves (CDWs) with complex order parameters can host exotic collective modes and non-trivial topologies. They have emerged as a new frontier in the study of quantum matter. Recent experiments on rare-earth tritellurides have reported evidence for a ferroaxial CDW through the detection of characteristic Raman modes. This phase, often regarded as a hidden order, has been recognized to arise from the coupling between charge and orbital degrees of freedom in these materials. Yet, spectroscopic insight into its underlying electronic structure and the explicit form of its order parameter symmetry has remained elusive. Here, we present results from linearly polarized angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) measurements of the CDW phase in LaTe$_3$. Our ARPES measurements reveal a complex landscape of spectral gaps across the reconstructed Fermi surface, while our STM-based quasiparticle interference (QPI) mapping, enhanced through the selective deposition of atomic scattering centers, directly reveals an inter-orbital CDW with mixed $p_x$-$p_z$ orbital character. The detailed analysis of the QPI characteristics in terms of the order parameter symmetry within the orbital subspace of the Fermi surface suggests a mixed CDW phase with substantial ferroaxial component, which breaks all vertical mirror symmetries. More broadly, our work establishes a powerful spectroscopic pathway, based on scattering off individual atoms, for identifying and characterizing hidden, multi-component electronic orders in quantum materials using STM and ARPES measurements.

</details>


### [355] [Magneto-ionic control of topological transport in SrRuO3 via band topology engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21999)
*Xuanchi Zhou,Xiaohui Yao,Xiaomei Qiao,Guowei Zhou,Wenjing Huo,Shuang Li,Huihui Ji,Xiaohong Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过磁离子控制方法在4d轨道SrRuO₃体系中实现了拓扑输运的可逆调控，并成功区分了反常霍尔效应与拓扑霍尔效应的贡献，为低功耗、可重构全氧化物自旋电子器件提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现对拓扑输运现象的可逆主动调控，并清晰区分拓扑霍尔效应与反常霍尔效应对净霍尔效应的贡献，是当前自旋电子学的重要目标，但面临巨大挑战。

Method: 在具有强自旋轨道耦合和巡游铁磁性的SrRuO₃体系中，利用可控质子化或氧空位引入实现磁离子控制，通过能带填充调控改变费米能级相对于避免能带交叉的位置。

Result: 实现了反常霍尔效应极性的可调反转温度；通过广泛离子掺杂产生了可逆切换的驼峰状霍尔异常；提供了该信号源于反演对称性破缺驱动的拓扑霍尔效应而非双通道反常霍尔效应的证据。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了磁离子控制作为动态工程化拓扑输运的有效范式，为在强自旋轨道耦合铁磁体中按需控制拓扑输运提供了调控手段，有望用于低功耗、可重构的全氧化物自旋电子器件。

Abstract: The interplay between spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and nontrivial band topology in ferromagnets gives rise to a rich landscape of topological transport phenomena such as anomalous Hall effect (AHE) and topological Hall effect (THE). One central goal in modern spintronics lies in the realization of the active control over topological transport phenomena in a reversible fashion, while unambiguously disentangling respective contributions of THE and AHE to the net Hall effect remains a formidable challenge. Here we establish magneto ionic control as a powerful paradigm for dynamically engineering topological transports in a 4d-orbital SrRuO3 system with sizable SOC and itinerant ferromagnetism. Harnessing controllable protonation or oxygen vacancy incorporation, the Fermi-level upshift relative to avoided band crossings are realized through band filling control, giving rise to tunable reversal temperature of AHE polarity. Of particular note is the emergence of hump like Hall anomalies through extensive ionic doping that can be reversibly switched, irrespective of AHE polarity, providing evidence for a THE signal driven by broken inversion symmetry rather than a two channel AHE. Our findings provide a viable tuning knob for Berry curvature engineering, enabling on demand control of topological transports in strong SOC ferromagnets for low power, reconfigurable all oxide spintronic devices.

</details>


### [356] [Field-tunable quadruple-$Q$ states driven by momentum-space frustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22133)
*S. Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过研究正方晶格上具有四类对称序矢的动量空间自旋模型，揭示了双线性-双二次耦合作用下磁场驱动的多Q磁序相图，发现了单Q、双Q及多种不等价四重Q态之间的连续相变，并阐明了四重Q态中相位锁定、振幅分布和非共面性如何产生不同的实空间拓扑自旋纹理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中心对称巡游电子系统中，多Q磁性能够形成复杂的自旋晶体和非共面自旋纹理，但如何有效稳定多样化的四重Q自旋晶体仍缺乏系统性理解。本文旨在探索动量空间阻挫和双二次耦合作为构建高阶自旋纹理的一般性机制。

Method: 在正方晶格上构建包含四个对称性相关的序矢的动量空间自旋模型，引入双线性和双二次相互作用，外加垂直磁场。采用模拟退火方法进行数值优化，获得磁场依赖的相图，并通过分析相位锁定、振幅分布和非共面性表征不同四重Q态的内部结构。

Result: 获得了完整的磁场-温度相图，识别出单Q、双Q以及多种不等价的四重Q态之间的连续相变序列。发现具有相同波矢量的四重Q态在相位锁定方式、振幅分布和非共面性上存在显著差异，产生不同的实空间标量自旋手性模式。

Conclusion: 动量空间阻挫与双二次耦合是稳定多样化四重Q自旋晶体的有效途径，为在中心对称巡游磁体中设计和理解高阶自旋纹理提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Multiple-$Q$ magnetism in itinerant electron systems enables complex spin crystals and noncoplanar textures even in centrosymmetric settings. We study a minimal momentum-space spin model on a square lattice with four symmetry-related ordering wave vectors, including bilinear and biquadratic interactions under an out-of-plane magnetic field. Using simulated annealing, we obtain the field-dependent phase diagram and identify successive transitions among single-$Q$, double-$Q$, and multiple inequivalent quadruple-$Q$ states. The quadruple-$Q$ manifold exhibits rich internal structures: the states sharing the same wave vectors differ in phase locking, amplitude distribution, and noncoplanarity, leading to distinct real-space textures and scalar spin chirality patterns. Our results demonstrate that momentum-space frustration and biquadratic coupling provide an efficient route to stabilizing diverse quadruple-$Q$ spin crystals, offering a general framework for higher-order spin textures in centrosymmetric itinerant magnets.

</details>


### [357] [Van Hove Singularity and Phase Instability: Exploring the Role of Electron Correlation in the Magnetic Behavior of $\mathrm{Fe}_{16}\mathrm{N}_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22256)
*Peter Stoeckl,Przemyslaw Wojciech Swatek,Jian-Ping Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用GGA+U框架下的密度泛函理论研究Hubbard参数U和J对α''-Fe₁₆N₂磁性的影响，发现电子结构对U和J高度敏感，揭示了费米能级附近的van Hove奇异点与材料结构及热相不稳定性相关，表明U的选取不仅使饱和磁化和磁晶各向异性趋近实验值，还揭示了相亚稳态的潜在电子机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe₁₆N₂具有巨饱和磁化强度，是环保无稀土永磁体的候选材料，但第一性原理计算难以一致地复现实验观测的高饱和磁化强度，且磁晶各向异性值变化很大，需要系统研究关联效应对其磁性的影响。

Method: 采用GGA+U框架下的密度泛函理论，系统调控Hubbard参数U和J，研究其对Fe₁₆N₂电子结构和磁性的影响。

Result: 电子结构对U和J高度敏感；费米能级附近存在van Hove奇异点，与材料的结构和热相不稳定性相关；U的选取可同时调控饱和磁化和磁晶各向异性能趋近实验值。

Conclusion: U的选择不仅使磁性能趋近实验值，还揭示了相亚稳态背后的电子机制，为理解Fe₁₆N₂的关联驱动磁行为提供了框架，并为其稳定性和性能优化指明了路径。

Abstract: The ordered iron nitride phase $α''-\mathrm{Fe}_{16}\mathrm{N}_2$ is a promising candidate for environment-friendly, rare-earth-free permanent magnets due to its demonstrated giant saturation magnetization ($M_s$). However, first-principles electronic-structure calculations have struggled to consistently reproduce experimentally-observed high $M_s$, and have yielded highly variable magneto-crystalline anisotropy (MCA) values. In this work, we employ Density Functional Theory under the GGA+$U$ framework to study the effect of the Hubbard parameters $U$ and $J$ on the magnetic properties of $\mathrm{Fe}_{16}\mathrm{N}_2$. We demonstrate that the electronic structure exhibits high sensitivity to these parameters, specifically uncovering a van Hove singularity near the Fermi level ($E_F$), inherently tied to the material's structural and thermal phase instability. By linking this topological anomaly to the calculated magnetic properties, we demonstrate that the selection of $U$ not only tunes $M_s$ and MCA energy towards experimental values but also reveals an underlying electronic mechanism potentially responsible for the phase's metastability. This provides a framework for understanding the correlation-driven magnetic behavior of $\mathrm{Fe}_{16}\mathrm{N}_2$ and offers a pathway for optimizing its stability and performance in practical applications.

</details>


### [358] [Magnetovolume effect in SrRu1-xCoxO3 (x = 0.0, 0.05)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22257)
*M. Manikandan,R. Mahendiran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用微波辐照法快速合成了SrRu1-xCoxO3 (x=0, 0.05)和CaRuO3多晶样品，研究了其磁性、磁电阻、热膨胀和磁致伸缩性能。微波合成的SrRuO3在TC=160 K处发生铁磁相变，呈现金属性电阻和负磁电阻，性能与传统加热24小时合成的样品相当。降温时线性热膨胀从顺磁态的常规收缩转变为铁磁态的自发膨胀（类因瓦效应）。外加磁场导致各向同性的长度膨胀（正磁体积效应），体积磁致伸缩在10 K/50 kOe下为40 ppm，在TC附近达到最大值60 ppm。Co掺杂削弱了自发热膨胀，磁致伸缩在10 K时各向异性，80 K以上恢复各向同性。结果表明磁体积效应与RuO6八面体倾斜/旋转与掺杂Co2+离子的自旋轨道相互作用之间的竞争有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索微波辐照法快速合成钙钛矿氧化物SrRuO3及其掺杂体系，并系统研究其磁性能、磁致伸缩和热膨胀行为，揭示磁体积效应的物理机制。

Method: 采用微波辐照法（<1小时）快速合成多晶SrRu1-xCoxO3 (x=0, 0.05)和CaRuO3，并测量其磁性、磁电阻、线性热膨胀和磁致伸缩性能。

Result: 微波合成SrRuO3的铁磁转变温度为160 K，呈现金属性电阻和负磁电阻；降温时发生从顺磁收缩到铁磁自发膨胀的转变（类因瓦效应）；外加磁场产生各向同性正磁体积效应，体积磁致伸缩在10 K/50 kOe下为40 ppm，TC附近达60 ppm；Co掺杂削弱自发热膨胀，低温磁致伸缩各向异性，80 K以上恢复各向同性。

Conclusion: SrRu1-xCoxO3中的磁体积效应源于RuO6八面体的倾斜/旋转与掺杂Co2+离子自旋轨道相互作用之间的竞争。

Abstract: Polycrystalline SrRu1-xCoxO3 (x = 0.0, 0.05) and CaRuO3 were rapidly synthesized (< 1 hour) by microwave irradiation of oxide powders, and their magnetic, magnetoresistance, thermal expansion, and magnetostriction properties were investigated. The microwave-synthesised SrRuO3 exhibits a ferromagnetic transition at TC = 160 K, metallic-type resistivity, and negative magnetoresistance, with magnitudes comparable to those of a sample synthesized by conventional heating over 24 hours. Upon lowering the temperature from 300 K, the linear thermal expansion shows a transition from the usual contraction in the paramagnetic state to spontaneous expansion in the ferromagnetic state (invar-like effect). The application of an external magnetic field at a fixed temperature results in isotropic expansion of the length, implying a positive magnetovolume effect. The volume magnetostriction is 40 ppm at 10 K in a magnetic field of 50 kOe, and it reaches a maximum value of 60 ppm close to TC. The spontaneous thermal expansion is diminished in SrRu0.95Co0.05O3. While the magnetostriction is anisotropic at 10 K, the isotropic behaviour is recovered above 80 K, and the maximum value of the positive magnetovolume is comparable to that of the parent compound. Our results suggest that the magnetovolume effect in SrRu1-xCoxO3 is related to competition between robust tilting/rotation of RuO6 octahedra and spin-orbit interaction of the doped Co2+ ions

</details>


### [359] [Evolution of anisotropic magnetostriction in LaMn1-xCoxO3 (x= 0.1-0.9)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22268)
*M. Manikandan,R. Mahendiran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了微波合成的LaMn1-xCoxO3系列多晶样品（x=0.1-0.9）的磁致伸缩和磁性能，发现所有组分中横向磁致伸缩为正、纵向为负，各向异性磁致伸缩远大于体积磁致伸缩；各向异性磁致伸缩在x=0.5时达到最大值1221 ppm（10 K, 50 kOe），认为与高自旋Co2+离子的非零轨道矩有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LaMn1-xCoxO3系列材料在宽组分范围内的磁致伸缩和磁性能，揭示组分变化对磁致伸缩行为的影响机制。

Method: 采用微波辐照氧化物前驱体法合成LaMn1-xCoxO3多晶样品（x=0.1-0.9），测量平行和垂直于磁场的磁致伸缩（l_par和l_per），计算各向异性磁致伸缩（l_anis）和体积磁致伸缩（l_vol），并结合饱和磁化强度进行组分依赖性分析。

Result: 所有组分中l_par为负、l_per为正，l_anis >> l_vol；l_anis在10 K、50 kOe下随x先增后减，x=0.5时达最大值1221 ppm，且组分依赖性关于x=0.5不对称；饱和磁化强度随x单调递减，但在x=0.6处出现突变。

Conclusion: x=0.5处最大各向异性磁致伸缩归因于高自旋Co2+离子的最大比例及其非零轨道矩，而Mn3+/4+或Co3+离子作用较小；组分依赖性源于结构变化、Mn和Co离子价态及磁相互作用的改变。

Abstract: Polycrystalline samples of LaMn1-xCoxO3 series over a wide compositional range (x = 0.1 - 0.9) were synthesized by microwave irradiation of oxide precursors and their magnetic and magnetostrictive properties were investigated. Magnetostrictions parallel (l_par) and perpendicular (l_per) to the applied magnetic field were measured to estimate anisotropic(l_anis) and volume magnetostrictions(l_vol). In all the compositions, l-par (l_per) is negative (positive) and (l_anis) >> (l_vol), suggesting dominance of anisotropic lattice distortion under a magnetic field. The value of l_anis at 10 K for H = 50 kOe initially increases with x from 178 ppm for x = 0.1 to a maximum of 1221 ppm at x = 0.5 before decreasing for higher x. The composition dependence of magnetostriction is asymmetric about x = 0.5, the decrease for x > 0.5 is more steeper than for x < 0.5 whereas saturation magnetization decreases monotonically with increasing x except for an abrupt change at x = 0.6. The largest anisotropic magnetostriction observed for x = 0.5 is attributed to the presence of high-spin Co2+ ions with a non-zero orbital moment in the maximum fraction whereas Mn3+/4+ or Co3+ ions play minor roles. The composition dependence of magnetostriction is suggested to arise from changes in the structure, valence states of Mn and Co ions and magnetic interactions among them.

</details>


### [360] [Pairing-induced Bloch oscillations in an interacting Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22284)
*E. S. Ma,Z. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Kitaev链中强近邻相互作用会抑制单粒子跳跃但增强配对，形成Wannier-Stark梯级。该体系可映射为锯齿晶格上的横场Ising模型，Wannier-Stark态对应铁磁/反铁磁相间的局域畴壁，在无纵向场时仍展现Bloch振荡，体现了涌现的Stark多体局域化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Kitaev链中近邻相互作用诱导的奇特动力学行为，特别是强相互作用下涌现的Wannier-Stark梯级及其背后的物理机制，为理解Stark多体局域化提供新的范例。

Method: 通过自旋-费米子对偶性将模型映射到锯齿晶格上的横场Ising模型，结合数值模拟时间依赖可观测量对理论结论进行验证。

Result: 发现强近邻相互作用抑制单粒子跳跃但增强配对，形成Wannier-Stark梯级；Wannier-Stark态对应铁磁/反铁磁相间的局域畴壁，并在无纵向场时产生Bloch振荡。

Conclusion: 该研究为Stark多体局域化提供了新的例证，揭示了近邻相互作用在Kitaev链中诱导的丰富物理现象，并为实验验证提供了清晰的物理图像和指导。

Abstract: We study the peculiar dynamics of the Kitaev chain induced by nearest-neighbor (NN) interaction. We show that a strong NN interaction suppresses single-particle hopping but enhances pairing, resulting in a Wannier-Stark ladder. Based on the spin-fermion correspondence at the symmetry point, the model maps to a transverse field Ising model on a zigzag lattice, providing a clear physical picture and guiding experimental verification. The Wannier-Stark state corresponds to a localized domain wall between ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic phases. It exhibits Bloch oscillation even in the absence of a longitudinal field, in contrast to previous works. Numerical simulations of time-dependent observables verify these conclusions. Our findings provide an example demonstrating emergent Stark many-body localization.

</details>


### [361] [Neural Polaron: Learning Quasiparticle Operators in Quantum Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22644)
*Shang-Shun Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经极化子拟设，通过神经网络多体算符作用于基态波函数来表征准粒子激发，在J1-J2海森堡模型上精确再现了磁振子色散和谱权重，捕捉到了(π,0)反常等非平凡多体特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统的动力学性质难以研究，因为激发态信息通常超出基态波函数所能提供的内容。现有方法需要独立学习激发态波函数，计算成本高且物理透明度低。

Method: 引入神经极化子拟设：通过紧凑的神经头（建立在预训练基态网络的特征图上）参数化局域缀饰算符，而非学习独立的激发态波函数。该算符构建自然包含平移对称性、动量分辨率和准粒子局域性，同时分离基态关联与激发特异性缀饰。

Result: 在方格子J1-J2海森堡模型上，该方法精确再现了磁振子色散和谱权重，覆盖了广泛的阻挫范围。特别地，成功捕捉到了(π,0)反常及其随J2/J1增加而逐渐软化的非平凡多体特征。

Conclusion: 神经算符为将神经量子态从基态性质扩展到动力学响应提供了一条物理透明的路径。

Abstract: Understanding dynamical properties of quantum many-body systems remains a central challenge because excitations generally require information beyond a ground-state wave function. Here we introduce a neural polaron ansatz that represents quasiparticle excitations by neural many-body operators acting on a correlated ground state. Instead of learning an independent excited-state wave function, the method parameterizes a local dressing operator through a compact neural head defined on the feature map of a pretrained ground-state network. This operator-based construction builds in translation symmetry, momentum resolution, and quasiparticle locality, while separating ground-state correlations from excitation-specific dressing. We benchmark the method on the square-lattice $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model, where it accurately reproduces magnon dispersions and spectral weights over a broad range of frustration. In particular, it captures nontrivial many-body features including the $(π,0)$ anomaly and its progressive softening with increasing $J_2/J_1$. These results establish neural operators as a physically transparent route for extending neural quantum states from ground-state properties to dynamical response.

</details>


### [362] [Strain-tuned orbital-dependent electronic correlations in FeTe thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22799)
*Hyunjee Song,Sangjae Lee,Keun-Yeol Park,Jaehyun Park,Suyoung Lee,Yeonjae Lee,Jinyoung Kim,Jaeung Lee,Celesta S. Chang,Younsik Kim,Changyoung Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过对FeTe薄膜施加拉伸应变，利用角分辨光电子能谱观察到d_xy和d_z²轨道之间的谱权重转移，揭示了轨道选择性莫特相（OSMP），并发现了与化学掺杂不同的应变诱导效应，其中d_xz轨道的演化展示了电子跳迁机制对电子性质的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁硫族化合物中的轨道依赖电子相互作用和强电子关联导致丰富现象，以往多通过Se掺杂研究FeTe中的轨道选择性，但化学掺杂可能引入杂质干扰；本文旨在通过施加拉伸应变精确控制体系，以无杂质干扰的方式研究FeTe中的涌现行为。

Method: 对FeTe薄膜施加拉伸应变，利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）观测电子结构变化。

Result: 观察到d_xy和d_z²轨道间的谱权重转移，证实轨道选择性莫特相（OSMP）的存在；发现不同于化学掺杂的应变诱导效应；揭示d_xz轨道演化与电子跳迁机制对电子性质的决定性作用。

Conclusion: 外延应变与FeTe电子结构演化之间存在直接关联，应变可作为独立于化学掺杂的有效调控手段。

Abstract: Iron chalcogenides exhibit rich phenomena which are governed by orbital-dependent electronic interactions and strong electronic correlation. In particular, many studies have explored orbital selectivity in FeTe through Se doping. Here, applying tensile strain to thin films allows us to precisely control the system without other impurities that may arise from chemical doping to investigate the emergent behaviors in FeTe. Using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, we observe a spectral weight transfer between $d_{\rm xy}$ and $d_{\rm z^{2}}$ orbitals, evidence of an orbital-selective Mott phase (OSMP). Beyond OSMP, we reveal hitherto unobserved strain-induced effects, distinct from chemical doping. The evolution of $d_{\rm xz}$ orbital demonstrates how electron hopping mechanism plays an important role in defining the electronic properties of the system. Our findings highlight a direct correlation between epitaxial strain and the evolution of electronic structures in FeTe.

</details>


### [363] [Isometrization of Tensor Network States via Gauge Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22816)
*Zhiyu Jiang,Hiroshi Ueda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种规范传播方法，将通用张量网络态近似转换为具有指定正交中心的等距张量网络态形式。在一维中传播精确，但在高维网络中，通过将局部张量或收缩的局部簇近似为等距因子与输出腿因子张量积的结构化项，并将等距因子保留、输出腿因子吸收到相邻张量中。通过在随机张量和Kitaev自旋液体的环气张量表示上的基准测试，证明了该方法通过增加保留项数或扩大局部簇可降低局部残差和累积传播误差。该方法可作为变分isoTNS算法的初始化器或预处理器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通用张量网络态转换为具有指定正交中心的等距张量网络态形式在多维网络中面临挑战，因为局部步骤有多个输出方向，残差因子通常不可分离为独立的单键贡献。现有方法缺乏有效的局部截断准则和实用途径来精炼传播结果。

Method: 提出规范传播方法，通过近似分解局部张量或收缩的局部簇为结构化项（等距因子乘以输出腿因子的张量积），保留等距因子在当前位点，将输出腿因子沿传播方向吸收到相邻张量中。通过增加保留项数或扩大局部簇实现精炼。

Result: 在随机张量上验证了方法的有效性；在Kitaev自旋液体的环气张量表示上，两个结构化项即可将局部残差降至数值精度，将局部对象从2入2出扩展到4入2出和6入2出簇可降低局部截断误差和有限蜂窝规范传播中的累积误差。

Conclusion: 传播兼容的局部分解是近似等距化有用的构建模块，可作为变分isoTNS算法的潜在初始化器或预处理器。

Abstract: We introduce a gauge-propagation approach for approximately converting generic tensor-network states into an isometric tensor-network state form with a prescribed orthogonality center. In one dimension, this propagation is exact because the non-isometric factor produced by a QR or singular-value decomposition is supported on a single virtual bond. In higher-dimensional networks, however, a local step can have several outgoing directions, and the residual factor is generally not separable into independent single-bond contributions. We address this local obstruction by approximating a local tensor, or a contracted local cluster, by structured terms consisting of an isometric factor multiplied by a tensor product of output-leg factors. The isometric factor is retained at the current site or cluster, while the output-leg factors are absorbed into neighboring tensors along the propagation directions. This construction provides a local truncation criterion for gauge propagation and a practical route to refinement by increasing the number of retained terms or enlarging the local cluster. Benchmarks on random tensors and on the loop-gas tensor representation of the Kitaev spin liquid show that this refinement reduces both local residuals and accumulated propagation errors. For the loop-gas tensor, two structured terms reduce the local residual to numerical precision, and enlarging the local object from 2-in-2-out to 4-in-2-out and 6-in-2-out clusters lowers both local truncation errors and accumulated errors in finite honeycomb gauge propagation. These results identify propagation-compatible local decomposition as a useful building block for approximate isometrization and as a potential initializer or preconditioner for variational isoTNS algorithms.

</details>


### [364] [Spiral spin liquid resilient to quantization in the frustrated honeycomb antiferromagnet GdZnPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23007)
*Xun Chen,Rui Bian,Yuqian Zhao,Haijun Liao,Weiqiang Yu,Yi Cui,Yuesheng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过核磁共振研究，发现GdZnPO在中间磁场区域(~3-12T)表现出无对称性破缺的自旋液体态，在低场下(<3T)出现弱条纹序但伴随强烈涨落，证实了高自旋阻挫磁体中自旋液体行为的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究阻挫磁体GdZnPO是否能在量子涨落下形成自旋液体等新奇量子相，特别是验证其作为螺旋自旋液体候选材料的理论预测。

Method: 利用核磁共振(NMR)技术测量GdZnPO的局部磁化和自旋动力学，在极低温(0.033 K)和不同磁场条件下进行实验。

Result: 中间磁场(~3-12T)观察到空间均匀磁化和持续低能自旋涨落，无对称性破缺；低场(<3T)下约0.25K出现弱条纹序，但自旋-晶格弛豫率在低温极限下几乎与温度无关且异常大，表明强涨落持续存在。

Conclusion: 自旋-7/2量子化微弱地 lifted 螺旋简并，稳定了微妙的磁序同时保留了强动力学和自旋液体现象，GdZnPO是高自旋阻挫磁体中研究自旋液体的理想平台。

Abstract: Frustrated magnets host strong quantum fluctuations that can suppress conventional magnetic order and give rise to exotic quantum phases such as spin liquids. In some cases, however, quantum fluctuations lift classical degeneracies and stabilize ordered states via an order-by-quantum-disorder mechanism. The spin-7/2 honeycomb antiferromagnet GdZnPO has recently been proposed as a spiral spin-liquid candidate arising from cooperative fluctuations among a subextensively degenerate manifold of spiral states. Here, we investigate the local magnetization and spin dynamics in GdZnPO using nuclear magnetic resonance. In an intermediate field regime between $\sim$3 T and the saturation field ($\sim$12 T), we observe a spatially uniform magnetization and persistent low-energy spin dynamics down to 0.033 K, with no detectable symmetry breaking, providing spectroscopic evidence for a spin-liquid state. At lower fields below $\sim$3 T, a weak stripe order emerges below $\sim$0.25 K; however, strong fluctuations persist, as indicated by a nearly temperature-independent and unusually large spin-lattice relaxation rate in the low-temperature limit. Our results demonstrate that spin-7/2 quantization weakly lifts the spiral degeneracy, stabilizing subtle magnetic order while preserving robust dynamics and spin-liquid phenomenology. These findings establish GdZnPO as a promising platform for exploring spin liquids in high-spin frustrated magnets down to the lowest accessible temperatures.

</details>


### [365] [Angular-time evolution and edge-spin dynamics in the Haldane phase of the S=1 bilinear-biquadratic chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23117)
*Takuma Kaise,Kouichi Okunishi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了S=1双线性-双二次链在开放边界条件下基态的角时间演化（由纠缠哈密顿量生成的参数时间演化），通过矩阵乘积态表示计算了Haldane相中的角时间自旋关联函数，提取了由Z₂×Z₂对称性导致的近二重简并纠缠谱主导的振荡模式，并验证了该模式与系统部分施加均匀磁场时的有效边缘自旋动力学的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究纠缠哈密顿量生成的角时间演化（参数时间演化）特性，并建立其与边缘自旋动力学的联系，以深化对Haldane相中拓扑性质的理解。

Method: 利用矩阵乘积态（MPS）表示基态波函数，计算角时间自旋关联函数；通过比较子系统尺寸依赖性，将数值结果与均匀磁场下的有效边缘自旋动力学进行精确对比。

Result: 在Haldane相中提取了由Z₂×Z₂对称性相关的近二重简并纠缠谱主导的振荡模式，并数值验证了该模式与均匀磁场下边缘自旋动力学的对应关系。

Conclusion: 角时间演化中的主导振荡模式可归因于纠缠谱的近二重简并性，且该模式与系统在磁场下的边缘自旋动力学存在精确对应。

Abstract: We investigate the angular-time evolution -- a parameter-time evolution generated by the entanglement Hamiltonian -- for the bipartitioned ground state of the S=1 bilinear-biquadratic chain under the open boundary condition with the up edge spin. Using a matrix-product-state representation of the ground-state wavefunction, we calculate the angular-time spin correlation functions $\langle S_n^{α}(τ)S_{n'}^{α}(0)\rangle$ in the Haldane phase, and extract its dominant oscillation mode attributed to the nearly two-fold-degenerate entanglement spectrum associated with the $\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry. We also compute the effective edge-spin dynamics under a uniform magnetic field applied to the system part and numerically verify its correspondence to the dominant angular-time mode by precisely comparing the subsystem-size dependence of their amplitudes.

</details>


### [366] [Wess-Zumino terms in 0+1 SU(N) superspin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23234)
*J. S. Morales,M. N. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统地介绍了量子系统中具有SU(N)对称性的Wess-Zumino项，从SU(2)自旋相干态路径积分中的Berry相位出发，扩展到SU(3)和SU(4)超自旋相干态构造，并讨论了其在凝聚态物理中的应用，包括SU(N)海森堡模型、自旋-轨道耦合系统、多极交换相互作用等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子系统中SU(N)对称性的WZ项提供一个自洽的、完整的入门介绍，强调几何、拓扑与凝聚态物理应用之间的相互作用，填补从SU(2)到更高维SU(N)对称性系统之间理论框架的衔接空白。

Method: 从SU(2)自旋相干态路径积分出发推导Berry相位作为WZ项，引入拓扑项的几何起源及其与积分上同调类的关系，将Berry曲率识别为规范U(1)丛的第一陈类；随后扩展到SU(N)超自旋相干态构造，将相空间识别为CP^{N-1}，推导SU(3)和SU(4)的显式局域WZ项；附录提供超自旋语言与具体物理嵌入之间的代数对应。

Result: 建立了从SU(2)到SU(N)的WZ项统一框架，给出了SU(3)和SU(4)的显式局域WZ项表达式，提供了多极生成子基和多种SU(4)参数化等实用代数工具。

Conclusion: 该笔记为理解量子系统中SU(N)对称性的拓扑项提供了完整的数学和物理框架，对凝聚态物理中具有高阶对称性的系统（如自旋-轨道耦合、多极序）的理论研究具有重要的工具性价值。

Abstract: These notes present a self-contained introduction to Wess-Zumino (WZ) terms in quantum systems with $SU(N)$ symmetry, emphasizing the interplay between geometry, topology, and condensed-matter applications. We begin with the $SU(2)$ spin coherent-state path integral, where the Berry phase appears as a WZ term encoding the symplectic structure of the Bloch sphere. This example is then used to introduce the geometric origin of topological terms, their relation to integral cohomology classes, and the role of Berry curvature as the first Chern class of the canonical $U(1)$ bundle. We next discuss physical realizations in which such geometric terms affect dynamics, including adiabatic Berry phases and geometric quantum noise in magnetic quantum dots. A substantial part of the notes is devoted to the condensed-matter motivation for higher $SU(N)$ symmetries, covering $SU(N)$ Heisenberg models, $SU(4)$ spin-orbital and spin-pseudospin systems, multipolar exchange interactions, and higher-spin multipolar orders. Finally, we develop the 0+1-dimensional $SU(N)$ superspin coherent-state construction, identify the phase space with $CP^{N-1}$, and derive explicit local WZ terms for $SU(3)$ and $SU(4)$. The appendices provide algebraic dictionaries connecting the abstract superspin language with concrete physical embeddings, including multipolar generator bases and several useful $SU(4)$ parametrizations.

</details>


### [367] [Signatures of unconventional magnetism in the layered metallic ferromagnet LaCrSb$_3$ from ferromagnetic resonance spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23413)
*J. J. Abraham,S. Samanta,R. Kolay,V. Singh,R. Nath,B. Büchner,V. Kataev,A. Alfonsov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过X射线衍射、静态磁化和宽频电子自旋共振/铁磁共振实验，研究了层状金属铁磁体LaCrSb₃的磁性和结构性质。研究发现：无结构相变但存在强磁弹耦合、顺磁区以上存在准静态短程关联（低维磁性特征），以及铁磁共振模式揭示的两个磁子晶格。基于正交铁磁和反铁磁子晶格相互作用的唯象模型定量解释了FMR激发，支持了此前中子衍射提出的自旋强倾斜图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LaCrSb₃具有大的反常霍尔效应、自旋晶格强倾斜和特殊的自旋重取向转变等独特物性，但其微观磁性机制尚不清楚。本文旨在通过多种实验手段（尤其是宽频ESR/FMR）深入理解该体系的非常规磁性起源。

Method: 采用变温X射线衍射、静态磁化测量以及宽频率、宽磁场、宽温区的电子自旋共振（ESR）和铁磁共振（FMR）实验，结合正交铁磁与反铁磁子晶格相互作用的唯象模型进行理论分析。

Result: 1) X射线衍射显示无结构相变至15K，但在居里温度T_C≈126K处存在强磁弹耦合；2) ESR结果表明在T_C以上存在准静态短程自旋关联，是低维磁性的典型特征；3) FMR模式的频率-场图强烈支持存在两个磁子晶格；4) 正交铁磁与反铁磁子晶格相互作用的唯象模型定量解释了FMR激发。

Conclusion: FMR结果证实了LaCrSb₃中存在两个耦合的磁子晶格（正交铁磁/反铁磁），支持了此前中子衍射提出的自旋强倾斜图像，并呼吁发展微观模型以解释该材料的非常规磁性。

Abstract: LaCrSb$_{3}$ is a metallic ferromagnet with a layered crystal structure demonstrating intriguing electronic and magnetic properties, such as large anomalous Hall effect, strong canting of the spin lattice, and a peculiar spin-reorientation transition. Here, we report the results of the temperature-dependent x-ray diffraction, static magnetization, and in particular electron spin resonance (ESR) and ferromagnetic resonance (FMR) experiments carried out over a wide range of frequencies, magnetic fields, and temperatures. Though x-ray data reveals no structural transition down to 15 K, a strong magneto-elastic coupling is detected across the ferromagnetic transition at $T_{\rm C} \simeq 126$ K. ESR results indicate a presence of the quasi-static short-range correlations extending far above $T_{\rm C}$, which is a typical fingerprint of the low-dimensional magnetism. The frequency-field diagram of the FMR modes mapped below $T_{\rm C}$ strongly suggests presence of two magnetic sublattices in LaCrSb$_{3}$. A quantitative understanding of the FMR excitations was achieved within a phenomenological model of interacting orthogonal ferro- and antiferromagnetic sublattices which was earlier proposed to explain unusually strong spin canting observed by neutron diffraction [E.~Granado et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. 89, 107204 (2002)]. The FMR results corroborate this scenario and call for the development of the underlying microscopic model of unconventional magnetism in LaCrSb$_{3}$.

</details>


### [368] [Resolving support-mismatch by local basis rotation in variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23657)
*Jia-Lin Chen,Zhen Fan,Canhui Yan,Yantao Wu,Tao Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种局部基旋转采样方案，解决了变分蒙特卡洛中带电局域算符引发支持失配问题，使得长时量子动力学和动态结构因子的计算成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域淬火后的实时动力学编码了光谱实验中的响应函数，但变分蒙特卡洛计算长期面临支持失配问题：带电局域算符的投影作用导致大量组态消失，但这些组态仍可能贡献动力学，从而产生偏倚估计并冻结演化。

Method: 提出局部基旋转采样方案，通过在采样基中进行局部旋转来恢复丢失的支持，不改变底层变分动力学；该方案可无缝集成到现有变分蒙特卡洛算法中。

Result: 基准测试表明旋转采样准确捕捉长时量子动力学，实现了一维动态结构因子的变分蒙特卡洛计算和二维无偏局域算符淬火动力学计算。该方案也适用于基态变分蒙特卡洛计算。

Conclusion: 局域基旋转采样有效解决了带电局域算符导致的支持失配问题，将变分蒙特卡洛的适用性扩展到实时量子动力学和动态结构因子计算领域。

Abstract: Real-time dynamics after a local quench by a charged operator encodes the response functions measured in spectroscopic experiments, yet they have long posed a challenge for variational Monte Carlo calculations. The obstacle is a support mismatch: the projective action by a charged local operator forces an exponentially large number of configurations to vanish, but these configurations may still contribute to the dynamics, biasing the estimators and freezing the evolution at the very first step. This difficulty is an artifact of the chosen sampling basis, and the support mismatch generated by a charged local operator is itself local. We demonstrate that the missing support can be restored by a local rotation of the sampling basis, without changing the underlying variational dynamics. We propose a local basis-rotation sampling scheme that resolves the support-mismatch problem and can be readily incorporated into existing variational Monte Carlo algorithms. Benchmarks show that rotation sampling accurately captures long-time quantum dynamics, enabling variational Monte Carlo calculations of dynamical structure factors in one dimension and unbiased local-operator quench dynamics in two dimensions. We also show that this resolution of the support-mismatch problem extends beyond real-time dynamics, and may also be helpful for ground state variational Monte Carlo calculations.

</details>


### [369] [Emergent Andreev Reflection from a Lattice Duality Defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23684)
*Atsushi Ueda,Tokiro Numasawa,Boris De Vos,Masataka Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明了横场伊辛链中的马约拉纳平移缺陷在连续极限下等价于一种类安德烈夫反射边界条件，并揭示了该缺陷与双通道近藤问题、Maldacena-Ludwig单极子散射以及轴U(1)_A对称电荷翻转边界的深层联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在建立格点模型中的对偶性缺陷与超导界面中安德烈夫反射之间的类比关系，为某些强关联问题中的边界条件提供微观格点解释。

Method: 从横场伊辛链的马约拉纳表示出发，构造折叠格点模型，引入边界马约拉纳杂质实现交错马约拉纳链的单格点平移；在连续极限下将该平移映射为手征费米子宇称缺陷，进而与复费米子的安德烈夫边界条件相联系。

Result: 成功将格点上的马约拉纳平移缺陷转化为连续理论中的类安德烈夫边界条件；为Emery-Kivelson边界（双通道近藤问题）和Maldacena-Ludwig单极子散射提供了微观格点解释；将该边界识别为Kitaev链SPT相与无能隙链之间的界面；同时实现了轴U(1)_A对称的电荷翻转边界。

Conclusion: 格点对偶性缺陷可以产生与超导界面类似的电荷共轭边界条件，为理解强关联物理中的特殊边界态提供了统一的格点框架。

Abstract: Andreev reflection converts an incoming fermion into an outgoing hole and is usually tied to a superconducting interface. We show that an analogous charge-conjugating boundary condition emerges from a purely lattice duality defect. Starting from a Majorana representation of the transverse-field Ising chain, we construct a folded lattice model in which a boundary Majorana impurity implements a one-site translation of a staggered Majorana chain. In the continuum, this translation becomes a chiral fermion-parity defect: it flips the sign of the only left-moving Majorana mode while leaving the right-moving mode unchanged. When the two Majorana modes are recombined into a complex fermion in the folded geometry, this sign flip becomes the Andreev-like boundary condition. Our lattice formulation gives a microscopic interpretation of the Emery--Kivelson boundary of the two-channel Kondo problem and of Maldacena--Ludwig monopole scattering, while identifying the boundary as the interface between a Kitaev-chain SPT phase and a gapless chain. The same Majorana translation defect also provides a lattice realization of an axial $U(1)_A$-symmetric charge-flip boundary.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [370] [Surveying the adaptive landscapes of 10,000 antibodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21351)
*Daniel PGH Wong,Aleksandra M. Walczak,Thierry Mora*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无参数群体遗传学框架，利用公共克隆型中趋同亲和力成熟的统计特征来识别抗体中的有益突变。通过对20名健康个体中超过10,000个公共克隆型的分析，研究者发现了广泛存在的克隆型依赖性选择信号，估计了V基因上单碱基位点的突变流行度与适应度效应，并揭示了二者之间的权衡关系。这些推断的适应性景观能够复现SARS-CoV-2和流感特异性抗体的趋同突变统计特征，并可用于评估现有抗体语言模型的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前仅在少数抗体中探索了适应性景观（即改善抗原结合等功能特性的抗体特异性突变集合），缺乏在大规模抗体库中系统识别有益突变位点及其变异模式的方法，这限制了治疗性抗体的设计。

Method: 开发了一个无参数群体遗传学框架，利用具有相似未突变序列的B细胞谱系（公共克隆型）中趋同亲和力成熟的统计特征来识别有益突变。对20名健康个体中超过10,000个公共克隆型进行多谱系分析，估计V基因上单碱基位点的突变流行度和适应度效应。

Result: 发现广泛存在的克隆型依赖性选择信号；估计了V基因上单碱基位点的突变流行度与适应度效应，揭示二者之间的权衡关系；推断的适应性景观能复现SARS-CoV-2和流感特异性抗体的趋同突变统计特征；现有抗体语言模型主要由非选择性信号主导，但通过简单的重整化程序可暴露与预测一致的克隆型依赖性正选择信号。

Conclusion: 该框架能在大规模抗体库中系统识别有益突变并构建适应性景观，为治疗性抗体设计提供指导，同时为评估和改进抗体语言模型提供了基准。

Abstract: Affinity maturation is the Darwinian process by which antibodies improve antigen binding through somatic hypermutation and selection. The adaptive landscape, which defines the set of antibody-specific mutations that improve functional characteristics like antigen binding, has been explored in only a handful of antibodies. Identifying the sites of adaptive mutations in a given antibody sequence, and how these sites vary across the antibody repertoire, can inform the design of therapeutic antibodies. We develop a parameter-free population genetic framework that leverages the statistics of convergent affinity maturation in B cell lineages sharing similar naive sequences, called public clonotypes, to identify beneficial mutations. Applying this framework to more than 10,000 public clonotypes represented by multiple lineages across 20 healthy individuals, we identify widespread signatures of clonotype-dependent selection of individual mutations. We estimate the prevalence and typical fitness effects of mutations across the V gene at the single-site level, uncovering a general tradeoff between prevalence and fitness effect. These inferred landscapes broadly reproduce the statistics of convergent mutation in antibodies specific to SARS-CoV-2 and influenza. Finally, we use our framework to benchmark predictions from existing antibody language models, and show that while these models are dominated by non-selective signatures, a simple renormalization procedure can expose signatures of clonotype-dependent positive selection consistent with our predictions.

</details>


### [371] [Evolutionary Entropy Shapes Reproductive Lifespan in Age-Structured Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22001)
*Jorge Buescu,Saber Elaydi,Henrique Oliveira*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了进化熵（evolutionary entropy）作为衡量年龄结构种群生活史中繁殖贡献时间组织的指标。通过对130个动物物种的Leslie矩阵模型分析，发现繁殖窗口通常集中在熵最大化所选择的年龄类附近。核心结果是约简原理：在Euler-Lotka归一化下，生育力和存活率的乘法缩放不影响进化熵和世代时间。研究表明，繁殖分布几何尾部的临界阈值决定了种群是否具有唯一的有限熵最大化端点。在130个物种中，多数物种的预测与观察到的繁殖中位数完全一致，超过90%的预测误差在三类繁殖类以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的人口统计指标（净繁殖数、渐近增长率）衡量的是终生替代和增长，但无法量化繁殖分布在时间上的离散程度。进化熵弥补了这一空白，提供了衡量繁殖时间组织的新视角。作者希望揭示年龄结构种群中繁殖窗口组织的普遍规律，并验证熵最大化原理在跨物种中的适用性。

Method: 1) 基于Leslie型年龄结构人口矩阵建立数学框架，定义进化熵作为生长调整后繁殖分布的时间离散度指标。2) 推导有限种群和开放群体Leslie模型的显式熵函数，包括几何繁殖尾部情况。3) 在Euler-Lotka归一化下证明进化熵和世代时间的约简不变性。4) 对130个动物物种进行实证测试，将仅从人口矩阵计算得到的熵预测与独立的生活史变量进行比较，并进行系统发育校正。

Result: 1) 进化熵由归一化的成熟后繁殖分布决定，而非绝对存活率、生育率或幼体死亡率。2) 几何繁殖尾部存在尖锐临界阈值：种群要么具有唯一的有限熵最大化端点，要么熵趋近于仅由首次繁殖年龄决定的渐近值。3) 在130个物种中，预测与观察到的繁殖中位数对于大多数物种完全一致，超过90%的物种预测误差在三类繁殖类以内，且经过系统发育校正后关联依然显著。

Conclusion: 进化熵提供了一个跨分类群的定量规律性描述，几何繁殖分布在其中扮演核心角色。该理论补充了经典的人口统计指标，揭示了年龄结构种群中繁殖时间组织的基本原理。研究结果支持熵最大化作为预测年龄结构种群繁殖分布组织的有效框架。

Abstract: Evolutionary entropy measures the temporal organization of reproductive contributions along the life cycle of an age-structured population. We develop a mathematical and empirical framework showing that, in iteroparous animal populations represented by Leslie-type demographic matrices, reproductive windows are frequently organized near the age classes selected by entropy maximization.
  Evolutionary entropy complements the classical net reproductive number and asymptotic growth rate: whereas these measure lifetime replacement and growth, entropy measures the temporal dispersion of the growth-adjusted reproductive distribution.
  Our central result is a reduction principle: under Euler--Lotka normalization, evolutionary entropy and generation time are invariant under multiplicative rescaling of survivorship and fertility on the reproductive interval. The relevant entropy is determined not by absolute survivorship, fertility, or juvenile mortality, but by the normalized post-maturity reproductive distribution.
  We derive explicit entropy functionals for finite and open-group Leslie models, including geometric reproductive tails. For the geometric regime, governed by we prove a sharp critical threshold separating populations with a unique finite entropy-maximizing endpoint from those whose entropy increases toward an asymptotic value in terms solely of the age at first reproduction.
  The theory is tested on 130 animal species. Entropy-derived predictions, computed from the demographic matrices alone, are compared with independent life-history variables. Predicted and observed reproductive medians coincide exactly for a majority of species, over 90% are predicted within three reproductive classes, and associations remain strong after phylogenetic correction. These results identify a quantitative regularity across taxa, with geometric reproductive distributions playing a central role.

</details>


### [372] [Inferring and Predicting Clade-Level Relative Transmission Fitness in Seasonal Influenza A Using Differential Population Growth Rate and Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22279)
*Ursula Senam Nkonu,Richard Annan,Letu Qingge,Hong Qin*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究应用种群差异增长率（DPGR）框架分析2014年至2026年全球H3N2和H1N1流感监测数据，估算分支水平相对传播适合度，并训练卷积神经网络从全基因组序列预测DPGR，实现了流感适合度从基因组预测到生物学解释的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性甲型流感快速进化导致新分支取代原有谱系，给监测和疫苗评估带来困难，亟需一种可解释的监测信号来量化分支间相对传播适合度。

Method: 采用DPGR框架结合滑动窗口回归识别共循环期并重建分支间相对适合度关系；训练基于完整基因组的亚型特异性卷积神经网络预测DPGR，利用共形预测量化不确定性，通过SHAP定位基因组贡献区域。

Result: DPGR成功恢复了两种亚型的谱系更替规律；H3N2亚分支K在多个区域的适合度高于疫苗株背景；基因组模型对H3N2（R²=0.9577）和H1N1（R²=0.9871）的预测准确度高；解释分析揭示了血凝素抗原位点及内部基因的贡献。

Conclusion: DPGR是一种可解释的监测信号，流感适合度可以通过统一框架实现基因组预测与生物学解释的衔接。

Abstract: Seasonal influenza A evolves rapidly, allowing newly emerged clades to replace previously dominant lineages and complicate surveillance and vaccine evaluation. Here, we applied the Differential Population Growth Rate (DPGR) framework to GISAID-derived H3N2 and H1N1 surveillance data collected from 1 January 2014 to 12 February 2026, including the 2025-2026 influenza season, to estimate clade-level relative transmission fitness across continents and within the United States. We identified windows of co-circulation with sliding-window regression, reconstructed relative-fitness relationships among clades, and compared inferred growth advantages with independent WHO and CDC surveillance patterns. We further trained subtype-specific convolutional neural networks on complete viral genomes to predict DPGR from sequence, quantified predictive uncertainty with conformal prediction, and used SHAP to localize genomic contributors to fitness. DPGR recovered recurrent lineage turnover in both subtypes and consistently identified the emerging H3N2 subclade K as fitter than the 2025-2026 vaccine-lineage background across multiple regions. Genome-based models predicted DPGR accurately for H3N2 ($R^2 = 0.9577$) and H1N1 ($R^2 = 0.9871$), while interpretation highlighted known haemagglutinin antigenic sites together with contributions from internal genes. These results support DPGR as an interpretable surveillance signal and show that influenza fitness can be linked to genomic prediction and biological interpretation in a unified framework.

</details>


### [373] [quaint: An R Package for detecting introgression across a phylogeny using discordant gene tree topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22561)
*Ethan A. Baldwin,James H. Leebens-Mack*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为"quaint"的R包，该包基于ABBA-BABA框架，利用基因树拓扑结构而非核苷酸位点模式来检测杂交渐渗，为系统发育基因组学研究提供了可重复的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂交成种和渐渗杂交是重要的进化现象，但现有的D统计量等方法基于核苷酸位点模式，且缺乏利用基因树拓扑结构进行渐渗检测的软件包。

Method: 开发了一个R包，在ABBA-BABA框架下，通过总结基因树不一致性模式来推断渐渗，输入为一组基因树和物种树。

Result: 该R包克服了基于位点方法的局限性，能够在广泛的系统发育基因组学背景下检测渐渗。

Conclusion: quaint包为研究网状进化的研究者提供了一个可访问且可重复的工具。

Abstract: Premise: Hybrid speciation and introgressive hybridization are increasingly recognized as important evolutionary phenomena across the tree of life. One widely used class of methods to detect introgression includes D statistics and related methods which employ the ABBA-BABA test using nucleotide site patterns. Recent studies have applied this theoretical framework to phylogenomic datasets using gene tree topologies instead, but no software packages using this method have been developed.
  Methods and Results: An R package was developed to facilitate the inference of introgression given a set of gene trees and a species tree. Using an ABBA-BABA framework, this package summarizes patterns of gene tree discordance to infer introgression across large phylogenies.
  Conclusions: Using gene tree topologies, quaint overcomes the limitations of site-based methods, enabling the detection of introgression across broad phylogenomic contexts. This R package provides an accessible and reproducible tool for researchers investigating reticulate evolution.

</details>


### [374] [Upstream reciprocity versus downstream reciprocity: Catalyzing cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22582)
*Vikash Kumar Dubey,Sagar Chakraborty*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过基于智能体的仿真，研究了间接互惠的两种形式——上行互惠（基于经验）和下行互惠（基于声誉）在有限和结构化群体中的联合动力学，发现更新机制（全局/局部）对互惠行为者的存亡起决定性作用，并揭示了一个跨所有更新规则和群体大小的鲁棒结构特征：存在一个最优网络度数使上行互惠最大化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接互惠的两种经典形式（上行互惠与下行互惠）通常被孤立研究，它们在有限和结构化群体中的联合动态在很大程度上未被探索。

Method: 采用基于智能体的仿真，智能体行为类型分为背叛者、上行互惠者或下行互惠者，使用不同的进化更新机制（全局/局部更新经验和声誉）来模拟群体状态的时域演化。

Result: 更新机制对互惠行为者的命运起决定性作用；全局或局部更新可导致从丰富的行为共存到仅下行互惠者主导的不同结果；发现存在一个最优网络度数使上行互惠最大化，这反映了合作聚类与暴露于背叛者之间的根本性拉锯。

Conclusion: 下行互惠对上行互惠的影响取决于更新机制（可促进或抑制），但下行互惠对合作的净效应总体上是正向的。

Abstract: Why would anyone help a stranger, knowing they may never meet again? Indirect reciprocity offers one of the most compelling evolutionary answers, yet its two canonical forms -- upstream reciprocity (experience-based), and downstream reciprocity (reputation-based) -- have been studied mostly in isolation. Their joint dynamics in finite and structured populations remain largely unexplored. Here, we fill this gap using agent-based simulations in which an agent is behaviourally either defector, upstream reciprocator, or downstream reciprocator, and the agents' population state is temporally updated using different evolutionary update mechanisms. We show that update mechanism plays a surprisingly decisive role in shaping the fate of downstream and especially upstream reciprocators. Whether agents' experiences and reputations are updated globally or locally can shift outcomes from rich behavioural coexistence to the dominance of downstream reciprocators alone. Intriguingly, we uncover a robust structural feature that persists across all the explored update rules and population sizes: an optimal network degree at which upstream reciprocity is maximized, reflecting a fundamental tug-of-war between cooperative clustering and exposure to defectors. Our results highlight that while downstream reciprocity can either foster or inhibit upstream reciprocity depending on the update mechanism, its net effect on cooperation remains largely positive.

</details>


### [375] [When do correlations reflect biological similarity in ecological dynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22949)
*Akiva Goldberg,Nadav M. Shnerb*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文分析了生态群落中物种生物学相似性与丰度相关性之间的关系，发现随机Lotka-Volterra模型无法满足生物学约束下的随机驱动假设，而消费者-资源模型虽能自然纳入生物学随机性，但丰度相关性的解释取决于噪声进入系统的路径，表明丰度相关性无法简单作为生态位重叠的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群落生态学中常用丰度时间序列相关性作为物种生物学相似性或生态位重叠的代理指标，尤其是在微生物群落研究中。然而，这一做法的理论基础尚不明确，本文旨在严格分析生物学相似性与丰度相关性之间的关系，检验这一推断的有效性。

Method: 作者在随机群落模型框架下进行分析，要求不同物种受到的随机驱动与其生物学相似性成比例相关，并考察这一假设在两种主流模型——随机Lotka-Volterra模型和消费者-资源模型——中的实现情况及其对丰度相关性的影响。

Result: 1) 随机Lotka-Volterra框架通常无法满足随机驱动与生物学相似性成比例相关的要求；即使满足，丰度相关性也不携带生态位重叠信息。2) 消费者-资源模型能自然纳入生物学层面的随机性，但丰度相关性的解释取决于噪声路径：直接作用于消费者的噪声与通过资源介导的波动编码不同的生物学信息。

Conclusion: 丰度相关性不能简单作为生态位重叠或生物学相似性的代理指标，其解释高度依赖模型结构和噪声引入方式。这对随机生态群落的建模以及从群落时间序列进行推断具有重要启示。

Abstract: The structure of competitive ecological communities is shaped by the strength of interactions between species, which in turn reflects their biological similarity. At the same time, the stochastic forcing that drives abundance fluctuations is itself biologically grounded: species that are more similar may be expected to respond more similarly to environmental variation. This motivates the increasingly common use of correlations in abundance time series, particularly in microbial communities, as proxies for biological similarity or niche overlap. Here we analyze the relation between biological similarity and abundance correlations in stochastic community models. We require that the stochastic forcing acting on different species be correlated in proportion to their biological similarity, and ask how such forcing is reflected in abundance correlations. We show that this requirement cannot, in general, be satisfied within the widely used stochastic Lotka-Volterra framework, and that even when it is, abundance correlations carry no information about niche overlap. In contrast, consumer-resource models provide a natural framework for biologically grounded stochasticity. In this setting, however, the interpretation of abundance correlations depends strongly on the pathway through which noise enters the system: direct forcing of consumers and resource-mediated fluctuations encode different biological quantities. These results have implications both for the modeling of stochastic ecological communities and for understanding what can, and cannot, be inferred from correlations in community time series.

</details>


### [376] [Bayesian modelling of herd-level infection dynamics in cattle: Local spread as the primary driver of Salmonella Dublin persistence on Öland](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23148)
*Stefan Widgren,Ivana Ewerlöf,Arianna Comin,Robert Johansson,Stefan Engblom*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究利用贝叶斯仿真推断与粒子马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法，基于散装奶罐样本数据分析了瑞典厄兰岛都柏林沙门氏菌在牛群中的感染动态，估算基本再生数（R₀）和有效再生数（Rₜ），发现大多数牧场R₀<1但部分牧场R₀>1，Rₜ平均接近1表明地方性稳定存在，且仅限制感染牛只移动不足以将Rₜ降至1以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 都柏林沙门氏菌是一种牛适应性人畜共患血清型，导致动物福利问题和经济损失，在瑞典厄兰岛的控制尤为困难，需要深入了解其传播动态以评估现行控制措施的有效性并识别可降低流行率的干预手段。

Method: 采用贝叶斯仿真推断方法，基于散装奶罐样本结果，将感染过程建模为动态状态空间模型，并应用粒子马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法推断潜在的感染动态，估算基本再生数（R₀）和有效再生数（Rₜ）。

Result: 大多数厄兰岛牧场的R₀<1，表明感染引入后预计会自行消退；但部分牧场R₀>1，传播风险较高；平均Rₜ≈1，表明存在稳定的地方性流行；仅限制感染牛只移动不足以将Rₜ降至1以下，因为局部传播与牛群内传播对感染压力的贡献各占约50%。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯数据驱动分析能够为都柏林沙门氏菌的控制与根除提供循证决策支持，需同时干预局部传播和牛群内传播才能有效降低流行水平。

Abstract: Salmonella Dublin (S. Dublin), a zoonotic serotype adapted to cattle, causes animal welfare issues and economic losses. The disease has proven particularly challenging to control in Öland, Sweden. This study uses Bayesian simulation-based inference of bulk tank milk sample results to analyse the S. Dublin infection dynamics in Öland cattle. The infection process was formulated as a dynamic state-space model and particle Markov-chain Monte Carlo methods were applied to infer the underlying infection dynamics and estimate the basic reproduction number ($R_0$) as well as the effective reproduction number ($R_t$). These metrics provide insight into transmission dynamics, enabling assessment of the effectiveness of the current S. Dublin control in Swedish cattle and identification of interventions that may reduce the prevalence.
  The results show that most holdings on Öland have $R_0 < 1$, indicating that infection is expected to die out after introduction. However, in a subset of holdings $R_0 > 1$, and there the risk for spread of S. Dublin is higher. Furthermore, the analysis reveals that on average, $R_t \approx 1$, suggesting a stable endemic presence unless effective interventions are implemented. In addition, the results show that it is insufficient to restrict the movements of infected cattle on Öland to bring $R_t < 1$, as local spread and within-herd transmission contribute equally to the force of infection (approximately 50% each). These findings demonstrate how Bayesian data-driven analysis can support evidence-based decision making for the control and eradication of S. Dublin in cattle.

</details>


### [377] [From Lab to Landscape: Assessing the Impact of Pesticides on Pollinator Populations Based on Laboratory Data by Combining ALMaSS and BufferGUTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23470)
*Florian Schunck,Agnieszka Bednarska,Leonhard Bürger,Christopher John Topping,Andreas Focks,Xiaodong Duan*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过将BufferGUTS模型与ALMaSS景观模拟系统耦合，以独居蜂Osmia bicornis暴露于农药Closer（有效成分sulfoxaflor）为案例，展示了机理模型在个体基模型框架中如何支持环境风险评估，弥合实验室实验与真实景观尺度风险评估之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前环境风险评估（ERA）使用实验室衍生的毒性阈值，未考虑种群和景观动态，可能不足以在保护非靶标生物的同时实现害虫防治的平衡。为克服这一局限性，需开发更精细的评估工具。

Method: 将动物-景观-人类模拟系统（ALMaSS）与BufferGUTS模型耦合，利用实验室存活数据（局部和口服暴露）校准BufferGUTS参数，并将其嵌入ALMaSS框架中的模型生物，通过景观模拟外推不同暴露水平下sulfoxaflor对种群动态的影响。

Result: BufferGUTS成功高精度集成到ALMaSS中，实现了每日存活概率的计算。即使极端施药率也只导致可忽略的种群效应，但探索性分析表明，若考虑所有生命阶段，农药驱动的幼虫死亡率可能产生更严重的影响。

Conclusion: 机理建模嵌入个体基模型框架可通过结合暴露与效应来支持ERA，构建基于系统的风险评估工具，从而弥合受控实验室实验与现实景观尺度风险评估之间的差距，为下一代ERA提供方法支撑。

Abstract: Pesticides are designed to eradicate pests from crops, fulfilling an important role in the current agricultural system. However, nature conservation requires that pesticide applications are protective for non-target organisms, which provide ecosystem services on the other hand. Environmental risk assessment (ERA) is supposed to strike this balance, but the current use of laboratory derived toxicity thresholds in the landscape context, without consideration of population and landscape dynamics might be too coarse to achieve this task. Here, we propose to overcome this limitation by coupling the Animal, Landscape, and Man Simulation System with the BufferGUTS model for non-target arthropods. We conducted a case study of the solitary bee Osmia bicornis exposed to the pesticide formulation Closer (a.i. sulfoxaflor) to assess the integration. Laboratory survival data of topical and oral exposure to Closer were used to calibrate BufferGUTS models. The resulting parameters were used to parametrise model organisms in ALMaSS simulations to extrapolate the effects of sulfoxaflor at different exposure levels on population dynamics. The integration of BufferGUTS into ALMaSS landscape simulation was achieved with high numerical precision, allowing for the calculation of daily survival probabilities for model organisms in the ALMaSS framework. We found that even extreme application rates only led to negligible population effects in ALMaSS simulations, but an exploratory analysis of pesticide-driven larval mortality showed that effects might be more severe when all life stages are considered. The work demonstrates how mechanistic modelling embedded into individual based modelling frameworks can support ERA by combining exposure and effect in systems-based ERA tools, bridging the gap between controlled laboratory experiments and realistic landscape-scale risk assessments for next generation ERA.

</details>
